const fs = require("fs");

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u8(p) {
  return buf[p];
}

function u32(p) {
  return (
    buf[p] |
    (buf[p + 1] << 8) |
    (buf[p + 2] << 16) |
    (buf[p + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = u8(p++);
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sectionName(id) {
  return {
    0: "CUSTOM",
    1: "TYPE",
    2: "IMPORT",
    3: "FUNCTION",
    4: "TABLE",
    5: "MEMORY",
    6: "GLOBAL",
    7: "EXPORT",
    8: "START",
    9: "ELEMENT",
    10: "CODE",
    11: "DATA",
  }[id] || `SECTION_${id}`;
}

console.log("TRACE FUNC 3317 REFERENCE STORES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("TARGET    : FUNC 3317");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codePayload = 0;
let codeSize = 0;

while (p < buf.length) {
  const id = u8(p++);
  const sz = readULEB(p);
  p = sz.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = start + sz.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    codePayload = start;
    codeSize = sz.value;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (!codeStart) {
  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse code section and locate every function body.
// Imports = 83 from previous proven analysis.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let q = codePayload;
const bodyCount = readULEB(q);
q = bodyCount.next;

const IMPORTS = 83;

const bodies = [];

for (let body = 0; body < bodyCount.value; body++) {
  const sz = readULEB(q);
  q = sz.next;

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = q + sz.value;

  const funcIndex = IMPORTS + body;

  bodies.push({
    funcIndex,
    bodyStart,
    bodyEnd,
    size: sz.value,
  });

  q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("CODE BODIES :", bodies.length);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Scan each function for stores with offset=0.
// We deliberately inspect:
//   36 = i32.store
//   37 = i64.store
//   3a = i32.store8
//   3b = i32.store16
//
// Atomic prefix FE:
//   FE 17 ... = i32.atomic.store
//   FE 18 ... = i64.atomic.store
//
// Also record surrounding bytes.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function hex(n, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function dumpAround(at, before = 18, after = 18) {
  const s = Math.max(0, at - before);
  const e = Math.min(buf.length, at + after);

  let out = [];

  for (let x = s; x < e; x++) {
    out.push(buf[x].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
  }

  return out.join(" ");
}

function parseMemarg(pos) {
  const a = readULEB(pos);
  const b = readULEB(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: b.value,
    next: b.next,
  };
}

const hits = [];

for (const fn of bodies) {
  let x = fn.bodyStart;

  while (x < fn.bodyEnd) {
    const op = u8(x);

    // i32.store
    if (op === 0x36) {
      const m = parseMemarg(x + 1);

      if (m.offset === 0) {
        hits.push({
          func: fn.funcIndex,
          addr: x,
          kind: "i32.store",
          align: m.align,
          offset: m.offset,
          bytes: dumpAround(x),
        });
      }

      x = m.next;
      continue;
    }

    // i64.store
    if (op === 0x37) {
      const m = parseMemarg(x + 1);

      if (m.offset === 0) {
        hits.push({
          func: fn.funcIndex,
          addr: x,
          kind: "i64.store",
          align: m.align,
          offset: m.offset,
          bytes: dumpAround(x),
        });
      }

      x = m.next;
      continue;
    }

    // i32.store8
    if (op === 0x3a) {
      const m = parseMemarg(x + 1);

      if (m.offset === 0) {
        hits.push({
          func: fn.funcIndex,
          addr: x,
          kind: "i32.store8",
          align: m.align,
          offset: m.offset,
          bytes: dumpAround(x),
        });
      }

      x = m.next;
      continue;
    }

    // i32.store16
    if (op === 0x3b) {
      const m = parseMemarg(x + 1);

      if (m.offset === 0) {
        hits.push({
          func: fn.funcIndex,
          addr: x,
          kind: "i32.store16",
          align: m.align,
          offset: m.offset,
          bytes: dumpAround(x),
        });
      }

      x = m.next;
      continue;
    }

    // Atomic instructions.
    // FE <subopcode> <memarg>
    if (op === 0xfe) {
      const sub = readULEB(x + 1);
      const m = parseMemarg(sub.next);

      // Known atomic stores:
      // 0x17 = i32.atomic.store
      // 0x18 = i64.atomic.store
      if (
        m.offset === 0 &&
        (sub.value === 0x17 || sub.value === 0x18)
      ) {
        hits.push({
          func: fn.funcIndex,
          addr: x,
          kind:
            sub.value === 0x17
              ? "i32.atomic.store"
              : "i64.atomic.store",
          align: m.align,
          offset: m.offset,
          bytes: dumpAround(x),
        });
      }

      x = m.next;
      continue;
    }

    x++;
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return [v >>> 0, p];
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8, code = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const [n, q] = u(p);
  p = q;
  if (id === 10) {
    code = p;
    break;
  }
  p += n;
}

p = code;
const [count, q0] = u(p);
p = q0;

const targets = new Set([3336, 2034, 91, 119, 113, 122, 2057]);

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
  const [size, q] = u(p);
  p = q;

  const func = 83 + i;
  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  if (func !== 6991 && func !== 3336) {
    p = end;
    continue;
  }

  console.log(`\n============================================================`);
  console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
  console.log(`============================================================`);

  for (let x = start; x < end; x++) {
    if (b[x] !== 0x10) continue;

    const [callee] = u(x + 1);

    if (!targets.has(callee)) continue;

    console.log(
      `CALL ${callee} @ 0x${x.toString(16)}`
    );

    console.log(
      [...b.subarray(Math.max(start, x - 100), Math.min(end, x + 12))]
        .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ")
    );

    console.log("--------------------------------------------");
  }

  p = end;
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_FUNCS = new Set([374, 376]);

function uleb(buf: Buffer, p: number) {
    let v = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) {
            return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function opcodeName(op: number) {
    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",
        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
        0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
        0x2f: "i32.load16_u",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",
        0x38: "f32.store",
        0x39: "f64.store",
        0x3a: "i32.store8",
        0x3b: "i32.store16",
        0x3c: "i64.store8",
        0x3d: "i64.store16",
        0x3e: "i64.store32",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",
        0x48: "i32.lt_s",
        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",
        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x73: "i32.xor",
        0x74: "i32.shl",
        0x75: "i32.shr_s",
        0x76: "i32.shr_u"
    };

    return names[op] ?? `opcode_${hex(op)}`;
}

console.log("TRACE FUNC 374 / 376 STORE ADDRESS DATAFLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = 8;

// locate code section
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const s = uleb(wasm, p);
    const size = s.value;
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

let q = codeStart;

const fc = uleb(wasm, q);
const functionCount = fc.value;
q = fc.next;

for (let func = 0; func < functionCount; func++) {

    const bs = uleb(wasm, q);
    const bodySize = bs.value;
    q = bs.next;

    const bodyStart = q;
    const bodyEnd = q + bodySize;

    if (!TARGET_FUNCS.has(func)) {
        q = bodyEnd;
        continue;
    }

    let x = bodyStart;

    const lc = uleb(wasm, x);
    const localDeclCount = lc.value;
    x = lc.next;

    const locals: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount; i++) {
        const n = uleb(wasm, x);
        x = n.next;

        const type = wasm[x++];

        for (let k = 0; k < n.value; k++) {
            locals.push(type);
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
    console.log(`BODY = ${hex(bodyStart)} .. ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
    console.log(`INSTR = ${hex(x)} .. ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
    console.log("LOCAL DECL COUNT =", localDeclCount);
    console.log("TOTAL EXTRA LOCALS =", locals.length);
    console.log("============================================================");

    /*
      Print every instruction byte and decode the important
      memory/global operations.

      This is intentionally raw + local-context oriented.
    */

    let i = x;

    while (i < bodyEnd) {

        const at = i;
        const op = wasm[i++];

        let detail = "";

        if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
            const r = uleb(wasm, i);
            i = r.next;
            detail = `local=${r.value}`;
        }
        else if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
            const r = uleb(wasm, i);
            i = r.next;
            detail = `global=${r.value}`;
        }
        else if (
            op === 0x28 ||
            op === 0x29 ||
            op === 0x2c ||
            op === 0x2d ||
            op === 0x2e ||
            op === 0x2f ||
            (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e)
        ) {
            const align = uleb(wasm, i);
            i = align.next;

            const offset = uleb(wasm, i);
            i = offset.next;

            detail =
                `align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`;
        }
        else if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = uleb(wasm, i);
            i = r.next;

            detail = `value=${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`;
        }
        else if (
            op === 0x10 ||
            op === 0x0c ||
            op === 0x0d
        ) {
            const r = uleb(wasm, i);
            i = r.next;
            detail = `index=${r.value}`;
        }

        console.log(
            `${hex(at)}  ${opcodeName(op).padEnd(18)} ${detail}`
        );
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET CONSTANT SEARCH");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of [0x195350, 0x195354]) {

    const b = [
        target & 0xff,
        (target >>> 8) & 0xff,
        (target >>> 16) & 0xff,
        (target >>> 24) & 0xff
    ];

    let hits = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i + 3 < wasm.length; i++) {

        if (
            wasm[i] === b[0] &&
            wasm[i + 1] === b[1] &&
            wasm[i + 2] === b[2] &&
            wasm[i + 3] === b[3]
        ) {
            hits++;

            if (hits <= 10) {
                console.log(
                    `TARGET ${hex(target)} RAW HIT @ ${hex(i)}`
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log(
        `TARGET ${hex(target)} TOTAL RAW HITS = ${hits}`
    );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace413IndirectCallers.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_MIN = 4057;
const TARGET_MAX = 4188;

function readULEB(
    buf: Buffer,
    p: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
        }
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function parseSections(buf: Buffer) {
    const sections: any[] = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const s = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = s.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + s.value;

        sections.push({
            id,
            start,
            end,
            size: s.value
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

/*
 * Skip a single WebAssembly instruction immediate.
 *
 * This decoder is used only to identify real instruction boundaries.
 * It does not execute anything.
 */

function skipInstruction(
    buf: Buffer,
    opPos: number
): { next: number; isCallIndirect: boolean; typeIndex?: number; tableIndex?: number } {

    let p = opPos;

    const op = buf[p++];

    /*
     * call_indirect
     *
     * 0x11 typeidx tableidx
     */
    if (op === 0x11) {
        const typeInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = typeInfo.next;

        const tableInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = tableInfo.next;

        return {
            next: p,
            isCallIndirect: true,
            typeIndex: typeInfo.value,
            tableIndex: tableInfo.value
        };
    }

    /*
     * No-immediate instructions.
     */
    const noImm = new Set<number>([
        0x00, 0x01,

        0x0b, 0x0f,

        0x1a, 0x1b,

        0x45,
        0x46, 0x47, 0x48, 0x49,
        0x4a, 0x4b, 0x4c, 0x4d,
        0x4e, 0x4f, 0x50, 0x51,
        0x52, 0x53, 0x54, 0x55,
        0x56, 0x57, 0x58, 0x59,
        0x5a, 0x5b, 0x5c, 0x5d,
        0x5e, 0x5f, 0x60, 0x61,
        0x62, 0x63, 0x64, 0x65,
        0x66, 0x67, 0x68, 0x69,
        0x6a, 0x6b, 0x6c, 0x6d,
        0x6e, 0x6f, 0x70, 0x71,
        0x72, 0x73, 0x74, 0x75,
        0x76, 0x77, 0x78, 0x79,
        0x7a, 0x7b, 0x7c, 0x7d,
        0x7e, 0x7f,

        0x80, 0x81, 0x82, 0x83,
        0x84, 0x85, 0x86, 0x87,
        0x88, 0x89, 0x8a, 0x8b,
        0x8c, 0x8d, 0x8e, 0x8f,
        0x90, 0x91, 0x92, 0x93,
        0x94, 0x95, 0x96, 0x97,
        0x98, 0x99, 0x9a, 0x9b,
        0x9c, 0x9d, 0x9e, 0x9f,
        0xa0, 0xa1, 0xa2, 0xa3,
        0xa4, 0xa5, 0xa6, 0xa7,
        0xa8, 0xa9, 0xaa, 0xab,
        0xac, 0xad, 0xae, 0xaf,
        0xb0, 0xb1, 0xb2, 0xb3,
        0xb4, 0xb5, 0xb6, 0xb7,
        0xb8, 0xb9, 0xba, 0xbb,
        0xbc, 0xbd, 0xbe, 0xbf,
        0xc0, 0xc1, 0xc2, 0xc3,
        0xc4, 0xc5, 0xc6, 0xc7,
        0xc8, 0xc9, 0xca, 0xcb,
        0xcc, 0xcd, 0xce, 0xcf,
        0xd0
    ]);

    if (noImm.has(op)) {
        return {
            next: p,
            isCallIndirect: false
        };
    }

    /*
     * block / loop / if
     *
     * blocktype:
     *   0x40
     *   valtype
     *   signed type index
     */
    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        if (b >= 0x40) {
            return {
                next: p,
                isCallIndirect: false
            };
        }

        if (
            b === 0x7f ||
            b === 0x7e ||
            b === 0x7d ||
            b === 0x7c ||
            b === 0x7b ||
            b === 0x70 ||
            b === 0x6f
        ) {
            return {
                next: p,
                isCallIndirect: false
            };
        }

        /*
         * type index encoded as signed LEB.
         */
        const t = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = t.next;

        return {
            next: p,
            isCallIndirect: false
        };
    }

    /*
     * br / br_if
     */
    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
        const x = readULEB(buf, p);
        return {
            next: x.next,
            isCallIndirect: false
        };
    }

    /*
     * br_table
     */
    if (op === 0x0e) {
        const n = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = n.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value + 1; i++) {
            const x = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = x.next;
        }

        return {
            next: p,
            isCallIndirect: false
        };
    }

    /*
     * return_call / return_call_ref etc.
     */
    if (op === 0x12 || op === 0x13) {
        const x = readULEB(buf, p);

        return {
            next: x.next,
            isCallIndirect: false
        };
    }

    /*
     * call
     */
    if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = readULEB(buf, p);

        return {
            next: x.next,
            isCallIndirect: false
        };
    }

    /*
     * local.get/set/tee
     * global.get/set
     */
    if (
        op === 0x20 ||
        op === 0x21 ||
        op === 0x22 ||
        op === 0x23 ||
        op === 0x24
    ) {
        const x = readULEB(buf, p);

        return {
            next: x.next,
            isCallIndirect: false
        };
    }

    /*
     * memory load/store family
     *
     * memarg = align + offset
     */
    if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
        const a = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = a.next;

        const o = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = o.next;

        return {
            next: p,
            isCallIndirect: false
        };
    }

    /*
     * memory.size / memory.grow
     */
    if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
        const x = readULEB(buf, p);

        return {
            next: x.next,
            isCallIndirect: false
        };
    }

    /*
     * i32.const
     */
    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = readULEB(buf, p);

        return {
            next: x.next,
            isCallIndirect: false
        };
    }

    /*
     * i64.const
     *
     * signed LEB, but length decoding is identical for our boundary use.
     */
    if (op === 0x42) {
        const x = readULEB(buf, p);

        return {
            next: x.next,
            isCallIndirect: false
        };
    }

    /*
     * f32.const
     */
    if (op === 0x43) {
        return {
            next: p + 4,
            isCallIndirect: false
        };
    }

    /*
     * f64.const
     */
    if (op === 0x44) {
        return {
            next: p + 8,
            isCallIndirect: false
        };
    }

    /*
     * ref.null
     */
    if (op === 0xd0) {
        return {
            next: p + 1,
            isCallIndirect: false
        };
    }

    /*
     * ref.func
     */
    if (op === 0xd2) {
        const x = readULEB(buf, p);

        return {
            next: x.next,
            isCallIndirect: false
        };
    }

    /*
     * prefixed instructions 0xfc / 0xfd / 0xfe
     *
     * Decode enough to safely cross common immediates.
     */
    if (op === 0xfc || op === 0xfd || op === 0xfe) {

        const sub = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = sub.next;

        /*
         * 0xfc bulk-memory / saturating conversions
         */
        if (op === 0xfc) {

            const s = sub.value;

            /*
             * saturating conversions 0..7
             */
            if (s <= 7) {
                return {
                    next: p,
                    isCallIndirect: false
                };
            }

            /*
             * memory.init
             * dataidx + memidx
             */
            if (s === 8) {
                const a = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = a.next;

                const b = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = b.next;

                return {
                    next: p,
                    isCallIndirect: false
                };
            }

            /*
             * data.drop
             */
            if (s === 9) {
                const a = readULEB(buf, p);

                return {
                    next: a.next,
                    isCallIndirect: false
                };
            }

            /*
             * memory.copy
             */
            if (s === 10) {
                const a = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = a.next;

                const b = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = b.next;

                return {
                    next: p,
                    isCallIndirect: false
                };
            }

            /*
             * memory.fill
             */
            if (s === 11) {
                const a = readULEB(buf, p);

                return {
                    next: a.next,
                    isCallIndirect: false
                };
            }

            /*
             * table.init
             */
            if (s === 12) {
                const a = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = a.next;

                const b = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = b.next;

                return {
                    next: p,
                    isCallIndirect: false
                };
            }

            /*
             * elem.drop
             */
            if (s === 13) {
                const a = readULEB(buf, p);

                return {
                    next: a.next,
                    isCallIndirect: false
                };
            }

            /*
             * table.copy
             */
            if (s === 14) {
                const a = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = a.next;

                const b = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = b.next;

                return {
                    next: p,
                    isCallIndirect: false
                };
            }

            /*
             * table.grow / table.size / table.fill
             */
            if (s === 15 || s === 16 || s === 17) {
                const a = readULEB(buf, p);

                return {
                    next: a.next,
                    isCallIndirect: false
                };
            }

            return {
                next: p,
                isCallIndirect: false
            };
        }

        /*
         * SIMD:
         *
         * Most SIMD opcodes have either no immediate,
         * a 16-byte lane mask, or a memarg.
         *
         * For the call_indirect scan we conservatively handle
         * the common forms.
         */
        if (op === 0xfd) {

            const s = sub.value;

            /*
             * SIMD memory operations.
             */
            const simdMem = new Set<number>([
                0x00, 0x01, 0x02, 0x03,
                0x04, 0x05, 0x06, 0x07,
                0x08, 0x09, 0x0a, 0x0b,
                0x0c, 0x0d, 0x0e, 0x0f,
                0x10, 0x11, 0x12, 0x13,
                0x54, 0x55, 0x56, 0x57,
                0x58, 0x59, 0x5a, 0x5b,
                0x5c, 0x5d, 0x5e, 0x5f
            ]);

            if (simdMem.has(s)) {
                const a = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = a.next;

                const b = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = b.next;

                return {
                    next: p,
                    isCallIndirect: false
                };
            }

            /*
             * v128.const
             */
            if (s === 12) {
                return {
                    next: p + 16,
                    isCallIndirect: false
                };
            }

            /*
             * shuffle
             */
            if (s === 13) {
                return {
                    next: p + 16,
                    isCallIndirect: false
                };
            }

            /*
             * lane-index instructions.
             */
            if (
                (s >= 21 && s <= 34) ||
                (s >= 84 && s <= 115)
            ) {
                return {
                    next: p + 1,
                    isCallIndirect: false
                };
            }

            return {
                next: p,
                isCallIndirect: false
            };
        }

        /*
         * Atomic / threads prefix.
         *
         * Most atomic memory operations use memarg.
         */
        if (op === 0xfe) {
            const a = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = a.next;

            const atomicNoImm = new Set<number>([
                0x00, 0x01, 0x02, 0x03
            ]);

            if (atomicNoImm.has(sub.value)) {
                return {
                    next: p,
                    isCallIndirect: false
                };
            }

            const x = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = x.next;

            const y = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = y.next;

            return {
                next: p,
                isCallIndirect: false
            };
        }
    }

    /*
     * Unknown opcode.
     *
     * Return one byte forward. The caller will report it as a
     * decoder anomaly rather than silently trusting it.
     */
    return {
        next: p,
        isCallIndirect: false
    };
}

function parseCodeBodies(
    buf: Buffer,
    codeSection: { start: number; end: number }
) {
    let p = codeSection.start;

    const bodyCount = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = bodyCount.next;

    const bodies: {
        functionIndex: number;
        bodyStart: number;
        codeStart: number;
        codeEnd: number;
    }[] = [];

    /*
     * First 83 function indexes are imports.
     */
    const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

    for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount.value; i++) {

        const bodySize = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = bodySize.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = p + bodySize.value;

        const localCount = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = localCount.next;

        for (let j = 0; j < localCount.value; j++) {
            const n = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = n.next;

            /*
             * local type = one byte valtype/ref type
             */
            p++;
        }

        bodies.push({
            functionIndex: IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + i,
            bodyStart,
            codeStart: p,
            codeEnd: bodyEnd
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return {
        bodyCount: bodyCount.value,
        bodies,
        cursor: p
    };
}

export async function run() {

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SEGMENT 413 INDIRECT CALL TRACE");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log("");
    console.log("WASM size :", buf.length);

    const sections = parseSections(buf);

    const element = sections.find((x) => x.id === 9);
    const code = sections.find((x) => x.id === 10);

    if (!element) {
        throw new Error("Element section not found");
    }

    if (!code) {
        throw new Error("Code section not found");
    }

    /*
     * ----------------------------------------------------------
     * ELEMENT TABLE
     * ----------------------------------------------------------
     */

    let p = element.start;

    const segCount = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = segCount.next;

    const flags = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = flags.next;

    if (flags.value !== 0) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected element flags ${flags.value}`
        );
    }

    const offsetOpcode = buf[p++];

    if (offsetOpcode !== 0x41) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected offset opcode ${hex(offsetOpcode)}`
        );
    }

    const offset = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = offset.next;

    const endOpcode = buf[p++];

    if (endOpcode !== 0x0b) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected offset end opcode ${hex(endOpcode)}`
        );
    }

    const countInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = countInfo.next;

    const count = countInfo.value;

    const table = new Map<number, number>();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const fn = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = fn.next;

        table.set(offset.value + i, fn.value);
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("Element segments :", segCount.value);
    console.log("Table offset     :", offset.value);
    console.log("Function count   :", count);
    console.log(
        "Element cursor   :",
        hex(p),
        "expected",
        hex(element.end)
    );

    /*
     * ----------------------------------------------------------
     * CODE BODIES
     * ----------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const parsed = parseCodeBodies(buf, code);

    console.log("");
    console.log("Code bodies      :", parsed.bodyCount);
    console.log("Expected         : 9398");
    console.log(
        "Code cursor      :",
        hex(parsed.cursor),
        "expected",
        hex(code.end)
    );

    /*
     * ----------------------------------------------------------
     * INDIRECT CALL TRACE
     * ----------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" RESOLVED CALL_INDIRECT → SEGMENT 413 FUNCTIONS");
    console.log("============================================================");

    let totalIndirect = 0;
    let relevant = 0;
    let decodeErrors = 0;

    const hits: any[] = [];

    for (const body of parsed.bodies) {

        let q = body.codeStart;

        while (q < body.codeEnd) {

            const opPos = q;

            let ins;

            try {
                ins = skipInstruction(buf, q);
            } catch (e) {
                decodeErrors++;

                console.log("");
                console.log(
                    "DECODER ERROR @",
                    hex(q),
                    "function",
                    body.functionIndex
                );

                break;
            }

            if (ins.next <= q || ins.next > body.codeEnd) {
                decodeErrors++;

                console.log("");
                console.log(
                    "INVALID INSTRUCTION BOUNDARY @",
                    hex(q),
                    "function",
                    body.functionIndex
                );

                break;
            }

            if (ins.isCallIndirect) {

                totalIndirect++;

                const tableIndex = ins.tableIndex!;

                const resolved = table.get(tableIndex);

                if (
                    resolved !== undefined &&
                    resolved >= TARGET_MIN &&
                    resolved <= TARGET_MAX
                ) {

                    relevant++;

                    const hit = {
                        caller: body.functionIndex,
                        instruction: opPos,
                        typeIndex: ins.typeIndex,
                        tableIndex,
                        target: resolved
                    };

                    hits.push(hit);

                    console.log("");
                    console.log(
                        `CALL_INDIRECT #${relevant}`
                    );
                    console.log(
                        " caller function :",
                        body.functionIndex
                    );
                    console.log(
                        " instruction     :",
                        hex(opPos)
                    );
                    console.log(
                        " type index      :",
                        ins.typeIndex
                    );
                    console.log(
                        " table index     :",
                        hex(tableIndex)
                    );
                    console.log(
                        " resolved target :",
                        resolved
                    );
                }
            }

            q = ins.next;
        }
    }

    /*
     * ----------------------------------------------------------
     * SUMMARY
     * ----------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SUMMARY");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log(
        "Total call_indirect :",
        totalIndirect
    );

    console.log(
        "Relevant matches    :",
        relevant
    );

    console.log(
        "Decoder errors      :",
        decodeErrors
    );

    console.log("");

    if (hits.length > 0) {

        console.log(
            "Unique target functions:"
        );

        const uniqueTargets =
            [...new Set(hits.map(x => x.target))];

        uniqueTargets.sort(
            (a, b) => a - b
        );

        for (const fn of uniqueTargets) {

            const n =
                hits.filter(
                    x => x.target === fn
                ).length;

            console.log(
                `  function ${fn} <- ${n} indirect call(s)`
            );
        }

    } else {
        console.log(
            "No resolved indirect calls reached functions 4057..4188."
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" END");
    console.log("============================================================");
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace4154Chain.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 4154;
const NEXT_FUNC = 1475;
const IMPORT_FUNC = 83;
const CALLER_FUNC = 6491;

const FIRST_CALL_INDIRECT = 0x00229661;

function readULEB(
    buf: Buffer,
    pos: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (pos < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos,
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB EOF");
}

function readSLEB(
    buf: Buffer,
    pos: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (pos < buf.length) {
        b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: pos,
    };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8): string {
    return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function opcodeName(op: number): string {
    const m: Record<number, string> = {
        0x00: "unreachable",
        0x01: "nop",
        0x02: "block",
        0x03: "loop",
        0x04: "if",
        0x05: "else",
        0x0b: "end",
        0x0c: "br",
        0x0d: "br_if",
        0x0e: "br_table",
        0x0f: "return",
        0x10: "call",
        0x11: "call_indirect",
        0x1a: "drop",

        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",

        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2a: "f32.load",
        0x2b: "f64.load",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",

        0x3f: "memory.size",
        0x40: "memory.grow",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",

        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x73: "i32.xor",
        0x74: "i32.shl",
        0x75: "i32.shr_s",
        0x76: "i32.shr_u",

        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",
    };

    return m[op] ?? `op_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function parseSections(buf: Buffer) {
    const sections: any[] = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;

        const payload = p;
        const end = payload + r.value;

        sections.push({
            id,
            payload,
            size: r.value,
            end,
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

function parseTypes(
    buf: Buffer,
    section: any
) {
    const types: any[] = [];

    let p = section.payload;

    const countR = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = countR.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < countR.value; i++) {
        const form = buf[p++];

        if (form !== 0x60) {
            types.push({
                index: i,
                form,
            });
            continue;
        }

        const paramR = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = paramR.next;

        const params: number[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < paramR.value; j++) {
            params.push(buf[p++]);
        }

        const resultR = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = resultR.next;

        const results: number[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < resultR.value; j++) {
            results.push(buf[p++]);
        }

        types.push({
            index: i,
            params,
            results,
        });
    }

    return types;
}

function parseImports(
    buf: Buffer,
    section: any
) {
    const imports: any[] = [];

    let p = section.payload;

    const countR = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = countR.next;

    let functionIndex = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < countR.value; i++) {
        const moduleLen = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = moduleLen.next;

        const module = buf
            .subarray(p, p + moduleLen.value)
            .toString("utf8");

        p += moduleLen.value;

        const nameLen = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = nameLen.next;

        const name = buf
            .subarray(p, p + nameLen.value)
            .toString("utf8");

        p += nameLen.value;

        const kind = buf[p++];

        if (kind === 0x00) {
            const typeR = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = typeR.next;

            imports.push({
                importIndex: i,
                functionIndex,
                module,
                name,
                typeIndex: typeR.value,
            });

            functionIndex++;
        } else if (kind === 0x01) {
            const elemType = buf[p++];

            const flagsR = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = flagsR.next;

            if (flagsR.value === 0) {
                p += 1;
                p += 1;
            } else {
                p += 1;
                p += 1;
            }
        } else if (kind === 0x02) {
            const flagsR = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = flagsR.next;

            p = readULEB(buf, p).next;

            if (flagsR.value & 1) {
                p = readULEB(buf, p).next;
            }
        } else if (kind === 0x03) {
            p += 1;
            p += 1;
        }
    }

    return imports;
}

function parseCode(
    buf: Buffer,
    section: any
) {
    const bodies: any[] = [];

    let p = section.payload;

    const countR = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = countR.next;

    const imported = 83;

    for (let i = 0; i < countR.value; i++) {
        const sizeR = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = sizeR.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = p + sizeR.value;

        const localCountR = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = localCountR.next;

        const locals: any[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < localCountR.value; j++) {
            const nR = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = nR.next;

            const type = buf[p++];

            locals.push({
                count: nR.value,
                type,
            });
        }

        bodies.push({
            func: imported + i,
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            instrStart: p,
            instrEnd: bodyEnd,
            locals,
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function decode(
    buf: Buffer,
    start: number,
    end: number
) {
    let p = start;

    while (p < end) {
        const at = p;
        const op = buf[p++];

        try {
            if (op === 0x10) {
                const r = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = r.next;

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  call              func=${r.value}`
                );
                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x11) {
                const t = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = t.next;

                const table = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = table.next;

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  call_indirect     type=${t.value} table=${hex(table.value)}`
                );
                continue;
            }

            if (
                op === 0x20 ||
                op === 0x21 ||
                op === 0x22 ||
                op === 0x23 ||
                op === 0x24
            ) {
                const r = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = r.next;

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  ${opcodeName(op).padEnd(18)} ${r.value}`
                );
                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x41) {
                const r = readSLEB(buf, p);
                p = r.next;

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  i32.const         ${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`
                );
                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x42) {
                const r = readSLEB(buf, p);
                p = r.next;

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  i64.const         ${r.value}`
                );
                continue;
            }

            if (
                op === 0x28 ||
                op === 0x29 ||
                op === 0x2a ||
                op === 0x2b ||
                op === 0x36 ||
                op === 0x37
            ) {
                const a = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = o.next;

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  ${opcodeName(op).padEnd(18)} align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`
                );
                continue;
            }

            if (
                op === 0x0c ||
                op === 0x0d ||
                op === 0x02 ||
                op === 0x03
            ) {
                const r = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = r.next;

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  ${opcodeName(op).padEnd(18)} ${r.value}`
                );
                continue;
            }

            console.log(
                `${hex(at)}  ${opcodeName(op)}`
            );
        } catch {
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)}  ${opcodeName(op)} [decode-stop]`
            );
            break;
        }
    }
}

export async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" FUNCTION 4154 CHAIN TRACE");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();

    const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    const sections = parseSections(buf);

    const typeSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 1);
    const importSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 2);
    const codeSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

    if (!typeSection || !importSection || !codeSection) {
        throw new Error("Required section missing");
    }

    const types = parseTypes(buf, typeSection);
    const imports = parseImports(buf, importSection);
    const bodies = parseCode(buf, codeSection);

    console.log("WASM size :", buf.length);
    console.log("Types     :", types.length);
    console.log("Imports   :", imports.length);
    console.log("Bodies    :", bodies.length);
    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" IMPORT FUNCTION 83");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const imp83 = imports.find(
        x => x.functionIndex === IMPORT_FUNC
    );

    if (imp83) {
        console.log("Function :", IMPORT_FUNC);
        console.log("Module   :", imp83.module);
        console.log("Name     :", imp83.name);
        console.log("Type     :", imp83.typeIndex);

        const t = types[imp83.typeIndex];

        if (t) {
            console.log(
                "Params   :",
                t.params?.map((x: number) => hex(x, 2)).join(" ")
            );

            console.log(
                "Results  :",
                t.results?.map((x: number) => hex(x, 2)).join(" ")
            );
        }
    } else {
        console.log("IMPORT 83 NOT FOUND");
    }

    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" FUNCTION 4154");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const f4154 = bodies.find(
        x => x.func === TARGET_FUNC
    );

    if (!f4154) {
        throw new Error("Function 4154 not found");
    }

    console.log(
        "Range :",
        `${hex(f4154.instrStart)} .. ${hex(f4154.instrEnd)}`
    );

    console.log(
        "Size  :",
        f4154.instrEnd - f4154.instrStart
    );

    const funcTypeIndex = (() => {
        const functionSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 3);

        if (!functionSection) return -1;

        let p = functionSection.payload;

        const countR = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = countR.next;

        const definedIndex = TARGET_FUNC - 83;

        if (
            definedIndex < 0 ||
            definedIndex >= countR.value
        ) {
            return -1;
        }

        for (let i = 0; i < definedIndex; i++) {
            p = readULEB(buf, p).next;
        }

        return readULEB(buf, p).value;
    })();

    console.log(
        "Type index :",
        funcTypeIndex
    );

    if (types[funcTypeIndex]) {
        console.log(
            "Params     :",
            types[funcTypeIndex].params
                ?.map((x: number) => hex(x, 2))
                .join(" ")
        );

        console.log(
            "Results    :",
            types[funcTypeIndex].results
                ?.map((x: number) => hex(x, 2))
                .join(" ")
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("DECODE:");

    decode(
        buf,
        f4154.instrStart,
        f4154.instrEnd
    );

    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" FUNCTION 1475");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const f1475 = bodies.find(
        x => x.func === NEXT_FUNC
    );

    if (!f1475) {
        throw new Error("Function 1475 not found");
    }

    console.log(
        "Range :",
        `${hex(f1475.instrStart)} .. ${hex(f1475.instrEnd)}`
    );

    console.log(
        "Size  :",
        f1475.instrEnd - f1475.instrStart
    );

    console.log();
    console.log("DECODE:");

    decode(
        buf,
        f1475.instrStart,
        f1475.instrEnd
    );

    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" CALLER 6491 / FIRST INDIRECT CALL");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const f6491 = bodies.find(
        x => x.func === CALLER_FUNC
    );

    if (!f6491) {
        throw new Error("Function 6491 not found");
    }

    console.log(
        "Function 6491 range:",
        `${hex(f6491.instrStart)} .. ${hex(f6491.instrEnd)}`
    );

    const windowStart = Math.max(
        f6491.instrStart,
        FIRST_CALL_INDIRECT - 120
    );

    const windowEnd = Math.min(
        f6491.instrEnd,
        FIRST_CALL_INDIRECT + 40
    );

    console.log(
        "Window:",
        `${hex(windowStart)} .. ${hex(windowEnd)}`
    );

    console.log();

    decode(
        buf,
        windowStart,
        windowEnd
    );

    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" RAW BYTES AROUND FIRST CALL");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const rawStart = Math.max(
        f6491.instrStart,
        FIRST_CALL_INDIRECT - 48
    );

    const rawEnd = Math.min(
        f6491.instrEnd,
        FIRST_CALL_INDIRECT + 16
    );

    for (
        let p = rawStart;
        p < rawEnd;
        p += 16
    ) {
        const e = Math.min(p + 16, rawEnd);

        const bytes: string[] = [];

        for (let q = p; q < e; q++) {
            bytes.push(
                buf[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
            );
        }

        console.log(
            `${hex(p)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" END");
    console.log("============================================================");
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 4154;
const CALLER_FUNC = 6491;
const TARGET_TABLE_INDEX = 0x72;

const CALL_OFFSETS = [
    0x00229661,
    0x00229675,
    0x00229689,
    0x0022969d,
    0x002296b1,
    0x002296c5,
    0x002296d9,
    0x002296ed,
    0x00229701,
    0x00229715,
    0x00229729,
    0x00229734,
    0x00229748,
    0x00229753,
    0x00229767,
    0x0022977b,
    0x002298ce,
    0x00229901,
    0x00229911,
    0x00229c52,
    0x00229c74,
    0x00229c96,
    0x00229cb3,
    0x00229ea0,
    0x00229eb1,
    0x00229ec2,
    0x00229ed3,
];

function readULEB(
    buf: Buffer,
    pos: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (pos < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF in ULEB");
}

function readSLEB(
    buf: Buffer,
    pos: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (pos < buf.length) {
        b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readU32(buf: Buffer, pos: number): number {
    return (
        buf[pos] |
        (buf[pos + 1] << 8) |
        (buf[pos + 2] << 16) |
        (buf[pos + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function opcodeName(op: number): string {
    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x00: "unreachable",
        0x01: "nop",
        0x02: "block",
        0x03: "loop",
        0x04: "if",
        0x05: "else",
        0x0b: "end",
        0x0c: "br",
        0x0d: "br_if",
        0x0e: "br_table",
        0x0f: "return",
        0x10: "call",
        0x11: "call_indirect",
        0x1a: "drop",
        0x1b: "select",

        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",

        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2a: "f32.load",
        0x2b: "f64.load",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",

        0x3f: "memory.size",
        0x40: "memory.grow",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",

        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",
        0x48: "i32.lt_s",
        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
        0x4c: "i32.le_s",
        0x4d: "i32.le_u",
        0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
        0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

        0x50: "i64.eqz",

        0x51: "i64.eq",
        0x52: "i64.ne",

        0x67: "i32.clz",
        0x68: "i32.ctz",
        0x69: "i32.popcnt",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",

        0x6d: "i32.div_s",
        0x6e: "i32.div_u",
        0x6f: "i32.rem_s",
        0x70: "i32.rem_u",

        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x73: "i32.xor",
        0x74: "i32.shl",
        0x75: "i32.shr_s",
        0x76: "i32.shr_u",
        0x77: "i32.rotl",
        0x78: "i32.rotr",

        0x79: "i64.clz",
        0x7a: "i64.ctz",

        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",

        0x7f: "i64.div_s",
        0x80: "i64.div_u",
        0x81: "i64.rem_s",
        0x82: "i64.rem_u",

        0x83: "i64.and",
        0x84: "i64.or",
        0x85: "i64.xor",
        0x86: "i64.shl",
        0x87: "i64.shr_s",
        0x88: "i64.shr_u",

        0x92: "f32.add",
        0x93: "f32.sub",
        0x94: "f32.mul",
        0x95: "f32.div",

        0xa0: "f64.add",
        0xa1: "f64.sub",
        0xa2: "f64.mul",
        0xa3: "f64.div",

        0xfc: "prefix.fc",
        0xfd: "prefix.fd",
        0xfe: "prefix.fe",
    };

    return names[op] ?? "op_" + op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0");
}

function findSections(buf: Buffer) {
    let p = 8;

    const sections: {
        id: number;
        payload: number;
        size: number;
        end: number;
    }[] = [];

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = r.next;

        const payload = p;
        const end = payload + r.value;

        sections.push({
            id,
            payload,
            size: r.value,
            end,
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

function parseCodeBodies(
    buf: Buffer,
    codeSection: {
        payload: number;
        size: number;
        end: number;
    }
) {
    let p = codeSection.payload;

    const countR = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = countR.next;

    const bodies: {
        func: number;
        bodyStart: number;
        bodyEnd: number;
        instrStart: number;
        instrEnd: number;
    }[] = [];

    const importedFunctions = 83;

    for (let i = 0; i < countR.value; i++) {
        const sizeR = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = sizeR.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = p + sizeR.value;

        const localR = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = localR.next;

        for (let j = 0; j < localR.value; j++) {
            const n = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = n.next;

            p += 1; // valtype
        }

        bodies.push({
            func: importedFunctions + i,
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            instrStart: p,
            instrEnd: bodyEnd,
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function dumpRaw(
    buf: Buffer,
    start: number,
    end: number
) {
    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
        const e = Math.min(p + 16, end);

        const bytes: string[] = [];

        for (let q = p; q < e; q++) {
            bytes.push(buf[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
        }

        console.log(
            `${hex(p)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
        );
    }
}

function decodeWindow(
    buf: Buffer,
    start: number,
    end: number
) {
    let p = start;

    while (p < end) {
        const at = p;
        const op = buf[p++];

        try {
            switch (op) {
                case 0x10: {
                    const r = readULEB(buf, p);
                    p = r.next;

                    console.log(
                        `${hex(at)}  call             func=${r.value}`
                    );
                    break;
                }

                case 0x11: {
                    const typeR = readULEB(buf, p);
                    p = typeR.next;

                    const tableR = readULEB(buf, p);
                    p = tableR.next;

                    console.log(
                        `${hex(at)}  call_indirect    type=${typeR.value} table=${hex(tableR.value)}`
                    );
                    break;
                }

                case 0x20: {
                    const r = readULEB(buf, p);
                    p = r.next;

                    console.log(
                        `${hex(at)}  local.get         ${r.value}`
                    );
                    break;
                }

                case 0x21: {
                    const r = readULEB(buf, p);
                    p = r.next;

                    console.log(
                        `${hex(at)}  local.set         ${r.value}`
                    );
                    break;
                }

                case 0x22: {
                    const r = readULEB(buf, p);
                    p = r.next;

                    console.log(
                        `${hex(at)}  local.tee         ${r.value}`
                    );
                    break;
                }

                case 0x23: {
                    const r = readULEB(buf, p);
                    p = r.next;

                    console.log(
                        `${hex(at)}  global.get        ${r.value}`
                    );
                    break;
                }

                case 0x24: {
                    const r = readULEB(buf, p);
                    p = r.next;

                    console.log(
                        `${hex(at)}  global.set        ${r.value}`
                    );
                    break;
                }

                case 0x28:
                case 0x29:
                case 0x2a:
                case 0x2b:
                case 0x36:
                case 0x37: {
                    const align = readULEB(buf, p);
                    p = align.next;

                    const offset = readULEB(buf, p);
                    p = offset.next;

                    console.log(
                        `${hex(at)}  ${opcodeName(op).padEnd(16)} align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`
                    );
                    break;
                }

                case 0x41: {
                    const r = readSLEB(buf, p);
                    p = r.next;

                    console.log(
                        `${hex(at)}  i32.const        ${r.value} (${hex(r.value >>> 0)})`
                    );
                    break;
                }

                case 0x42: {
                    const r = readSLEB(buf, p);
                    p = r.next;

                    console.log(
                        `${hex(at)}  i64.const        ${r.value}`
                    );
                    break;
                }

                case 0x0c:
                case 0x0d:
                case 0x02:
                case 0x03: {
                    const r = readULEB(buf, p);
                    p = r.next;

                    console.log(
                        `${hex(at)}  ${opcodeName(op).padEnd(16)} ${r.value}`
                    );
                    break;
                }

                case 0x0e: {
                    const n = readULEB(buf, p);
                    p = n.next;

                    const labels: number[] = [];

                    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
                        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
                        p = r.next;
                        labels.push(r.value);
                    }

                    const d = readULEB(buf, p);
                    p = d.next;

                    console.log(
                        `${hex(at)}  br_table         count=${n.value} default=${d.value}`
                    );
                    break;
                }

                default:
                    console.log(
                        `${hex(at)}  ${opcodeName(op)}`
                    );
                    break;
            }
        } catch {
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)}  ${opcodeName(op)}  [decode-stop]`
            );
            break;
        }
    }
}

export async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" FUNCTION 4154 / CALLER 6491 TRACE");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();

    const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log("WASM size :", buf.length);
    console.log();

    const sections = findSections(buf);

    const code = sections.find(s => s.id === 10);

    if (!code) {
        throw new Error("Code section not found");
    }

    console.log("Code section:");
    console.log("  payload :", hex(code.payload));
    console.log("  size    :", code.size);
    console.log("  end     :", hex(code.end));
    console.log();

    const bodies = parseCodeBodies(buf, code);

    console.log("Code bodies :", bodies.length);
    console.log();

    const target = bodies.find(x => x.func === TARGET_FUNC);
    const caller = bodies.find(x => x.func === CALLER_FUNC);

    if (!target) throw new Error(`Function ${TARGET_FUNC} not found`);
    if (!caller) throw new Error(`Function ${CALLER_FUNC} not found`);

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" TARGET FUNCTION 4154");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log("Function :", TARGET_FUNC);
    console.log("Body     :", `${hex(target.bodyStart)} .. ${hex(target.bodyEnd)}`);
    console.log("Instr    :", `${hex(target.instrStart)} .. ${hex(target.instrEnd)}`);
    console.log("Size     :", target.instrEnd - target.instrStart);
    console.log();

    console.log("RAW:");
    dumpRaw(
        buf,
        target.instrStart,
        target.instrEnd
    );

    console.log();
    console.log("DECODE:");

    decodeWindow(
        buf,
        target.instrStart,
        target.instrEnd
    );

    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" CALLER FUNCTION 6491");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log("Function :", CALLER_FUNC);
    console.log("Body     :", `${hex(caller.bodyStart)} .. ${hex(caller.bodyEnd)}`);
    console.log("Instr    :", `${hex(caller.instrStart)} .. ${hex(caller.instrEnd)}`);
    console.log("Size     :", caller.instrEnd - caller.instrStart);
    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" TARGET CALL SITES");
    console.log("============================================================");

    let found = 0;

    for (const callOffset of CALL_OFFSETS) {
        if (
            callOffset < caller.instrStart ||
            callOffset >= caller.instrEnd
        ) {
            console.log(
                `${hex(callOffset)}  OUTSIDE FUNCTION 6491`
            );
            continue;
        }

        found++;

        console.log();
        console.log(
            `--- call_indirect @ ${hex(callOffset)} ---`
        );

        const before = Math.max(
            caller.instrStart,
            callOffset - 40
        );

        const after = Math.min(
            caller.instrEnd,
            callOffset + 20
        );

        console.log(
            `Window: ${hex(before)} .. ${hex(after)}`
        );
        console.log();

        decodeWindow(
            buf,
            before,
            after
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SUMMARY");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log("Function 4154");
    console.log("  table index :", hex(TARGET_TABLE_INDEX));
    console.log();

    console.log("Function 6491");
    console.log("  listed call sites :", CALL_OFFSETS.length);
    console.log("  inside function   :", found);
    console.log();

    console.log("Expected relationship:");
    console.log(
        `  table[${hex(TARGET_TABLE_INDEX)}] -> function ${TARGET_FUNC}`
    );

    console.log();
    console.log("END");
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace4154Indirect.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u32leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("Invalid ULEB128");
    }

    return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos,
    };
}

function parseSections() {
    let pos = 8;

    const sections: Record<
        number,
        { start: number; end: number }
    > = {};

    while (pos < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[pos++];

        const s = u32leb(pos);
        pos = s.next;

        const start = pos;
        const end = pos + s.value;

        sections[id] = { start, end };
        pos = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

function readInstr(pos: number): {
    next: number;
    text: string;
    isCallIndirect: boolean;
} {
    const start = pos;
    const op = wasm[pos++];

    const result = (
        next: number,
        text: string,
        isCallIndirect = false
    ) => ({
        next,
        text,
        isCallIndirect,
    });

    // control
    if (op === 0x00)
        return result(pos, "unreachable");

    if (op === 0x01)
        return result(pos, "nop");

    if (op === 0x02) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        return result(pos, `block              ${b}`);
    }

    if (op === 0x03) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        return result(pos, `loop               ${b}`);
    }

    if (op === 0x04) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        return result(pos, `if                 ${b}`);
    }

    if (op === 0x05)
        return result(pos, "else");

    if (op === 0x0b)
        return result(pos, "end");

    if (op === 0x0c) {
        const x = u32leb(pos);
        return result(x.next, `br                 ${x.value}`);
    }

    if (op === 0x0d) {
        const x = u32leb(pos);
        return result(x.next, `br_if              ${x.value}`);
    }

    if (op === 0x0e) {
        const n = u32leb(pos);
        pos = n.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
            const x = u32leb(pos);
            pos = x.next;
        }

        const d = u32leb(pos);
        pos = d.next;

        return result(
            pos,
            `br_table            count=${n.value} default=${d.value}`
        );
    }

    if (op === 0x0f)
        return result(pos, "return");

    // calls
    if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = u32leb(pos);
        return result(x.next, `call               func=${x.value}`);
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
        const type = u32leb(pos);
        pos = type.next;

        const table = u32leb(pos);
        pos = table.next;

        return result(
            pos,
            `CALL_INDIRECT      type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`,
            true
        );
    }

    // param/local
    if (op === 0x20) {
        const x = u32leb(pos);
        return result(x.next, `local.get          ${x.value}`);
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const x = u32leb(pos);
        return result(x.next, `local.set          ${x.value}`);
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const x = u32leb(pos);
        return result(x.next, `local.tee          ${x.value}`);
    }

    // constants
    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = u32leb(pos);
        return result(x.next, `i32.const         ${x.value} (0x${x.value.toString(16)})`);
    }

    // memory
    if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
        const align = wasm[pos++];

        const off = u32leb(pos);
        pos = off.next;

        const names: Record<number, string> = {
            0x28: "i32.load",
            0x29: "i64.load",
            0x2a: "f32.load",
            0x2b: "f64.load",
            0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
            0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
            0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
            0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
            0x30: "i64.load8_s",
            0x31: "i64.load8_u",
            0x32: "i64.load16_s",
            0x33: "i64.load16_u",
            0x34: "i64.load32_s",
            0x35: "i64.load32_u",
            0x36: "i32.store",
            0x37: "i64.store",
            0x38: "f32.store",
            0x39: "f64.store",
            0x3a: "i32.store8",
            0x3b: "i32.store16",
            0x3c: "i64.store8",
            0x3d: "i64.store16",
            0x3e: "i64.store32",
        };

        return result(
            pos,
            `${names[op] ?? "memory"}        align=${align} offset=0x${off.value.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );
    }

    // memory.size / memory.grow
    if (op === 0x3f)
        return result(pos + 1, "memory.size");

    if (op === 0x40)
        return result(pos + 1, "memory.grow");

    // comparisons
    const comparisons: Record<number, string> = {
        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",
        0x48: "i32.lt_s",
        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
        0x4c: "i32.le_s",
        0x4d: "i32.le_u",
        0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
        0x4f: "i32.ge_u",
    };

    if (comparisons[op])
        return result(pos, comparisons[op]);

    // i32 numeric
    const numeric: Record<number, string> = {
        0x67: "i32.clz",
        0x68: "i32.ctz",
        0x69: "i32.popcnt",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",

        0x6d: "i32.div_s",
        0x6e: "i32.div_u",
        0x6f: "i32.rem_s",
        0x70: "i32.rem_u",

        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x73: "i32.xor",

        0x74: "i32.shl",
        0x75: "i32.shr_s",
        0x76: "i32.shr_u",
        0x77: "i32.rotl",
        0x78: "i32.rotr",
    };

    if (numeric[op])
        return result(pos, numeric[op]);

    // selected conversions
    const conversions: Record<number, string> = {
        0xa7: "i32.wrap_i64",
        0xac: "i64.extend_i32_s",
        0xad: "i64.extend_i32_u",
    };

    if (conversions[op])
        return result(pos, conversions[op]);

    throw new Error(
        `Unknown opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")} at 0x${start
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, "0")}`
    );
}

function decodeWindow(start: number, end: number) {

    console.log("");

        const at = pos;

        const ins = readInstr(pos);
        pos = ins.next;

        console.log(
            `0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${ins.text}`
        );

        if (ins.isCallIndirect) {
            indirectCount++;

            console.log(
                `             >>> REAL call_indirect #${indirectCount}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log("============================================================");
}

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGETS = [
  0x1901dc,
  0x1901e0,
  0x1901e4,
  0x1901e8,
  0x1901ec,
  0x1901f0,
  0x1901f4,
  0x1901f8,
  0x1901fc
];

const TARGET_SET = new Set(TARGETS);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE 4275 ADDRESS CONSTRUCTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET TABLE:");
for (const x of TARGETS) {
  console.log(
    "  0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
  );
}
