function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << (shift + 7));
      }

      return {
        value: result | 0,
        next: pos
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/*
 * Locate CODE section.
 */
let p = 8;
let codeStart = null;
let codeEnd = null;

while (p < buf.length) {

  const id = buf[p++];

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  if (!sizeInfo) break;

  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const payloadStart = p;
  const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sizeInfo.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = payloadStart;
    codeEnd = payloadEnd;
    break;
  }

  p = payloadEnd;
}

if (codeStart === null) {
  throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

let q = codeStart;

const countInfo = readULEB(q);
q = countInfo.next;

const functionCount = countInfo.value;

let exactHits = 0;
let smallOffsetHits = 0;

console.log("CODE FUNCTION COUNT :", functionCount);
console.log("");
console.log("EXACT ADDRESS HITS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let func = 0; func < functionCount; func++) {

  const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(q);
  q = bodySizeInfo.next;

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySizeInfo.value;

  let x = bodyStart;

  const localInfo = readULEB(x);
  x = localInfo.next;

  for (let g = 0; g < localInfo.value; g++) {
    const n = readULEB(x);
    if (!n) break;
    x = n.next;
    x++;
  }

  while (x < bodyEnd) {

    const opPos = x;
    const opcode = buf[x++];

    /*
     * i32.const
     */
    if (opcode === 0x41) {

      const r = readSLEB(x);
      if (!r) break;

      const value = r.value >>> 0;
      x = r.next;

      if (TARGET_SET.has(value)) {

        exactHits++;

        console.log("");
        console.log("MATCH");
        console.log("FUNC :", func);
        console.log("CODE :", hex(opPos));
        console.log("CONST:", hex(value));

        if (value === 0x1901ec) {
          console.log("      *** THIS IS 4275 ADDRESS ***");
        }
      }

      /*
       * Also track low offsets in the exact table range.
       */
      if (value >= 0x1dc && value <= 0x1fc) {

        smallOffsetHits++;

        console.log("");
        console.log("SMALL OFFSET");
        console.log("FUNC :", func);
        console.log("CODE :", hex(opPos));
        console.log("VALUE:", hex(value));

        const from = Math.max(bodyStart, opPos - 24);
        const to = Math.min(bodyEnd, x + 48);

        for (let z = from; z < to; z += 16) {

          const rowEnd = Math.min(to, z + 16);
          const bytes = [];

          for (let k = z; k < rowEnd; k++) {
            bytes.push(
              buf[k].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
            );
          }

          console.log(
            "  " + hex(z) + " : " + bytes.join(" ")
          );
        }
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * local.get/set/tee
     */
    if (
      opcode === 0x20 ||
      opcode === 0x21 ||
      opcode === 0x22
    ) {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      if (!r) break;
      x = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * call
     */
    if (opcode === 0x10) {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      if (!r) break;
      x = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * call_indirect
     */
    if (opcode === 0x11) {
      const a = readULEB(x);
      if (!a) break;
      x = a.next;

      const b = readULEB(x);
      if (!b) break;
      x = b.next;

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * br / br_if
     */
    if (opcode === 0x0c || opcode === 0x0d) {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      if (!r) break;
      x = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * br_table
     */
    if (opcode === 0x0e) {

      const n = readULEB(x);
      if (!n) break;

      x = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const r = readULEB(x);
        if (!r) break;
        x = r.next;
      }

      const def = readULEB(x);
      if (!def) break;

      x = def.next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * block / loop / if
     */
    if (
      opcode === 0x02 ||
      opcode === 0x03 ||
      opcode === 0x04
    ) {
      x++;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * memory operations
     */
    if (
      opcode >= 0x28 &&
      opcode <= 0x3e
    ) {

      const a = readULEB(x);
      if (!a) break;

      x = a.next;

      const b = readULEB(x);
      if (!b) break;

      x = b.next;

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * memory.size / memory.grow
     */
    if (opcode === 0x3f || opcode === 0x40) {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      if (!r) break;
      x = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * global.get/set
     */
    if (opcode === 0x23 || opcode === 0x24) {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      if (!r) break;
      x = r.next;
      continue;
    }
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;
const TARGET = 446;

function readU32(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) {
            return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
        }

        s += 7;
    }
}

function readS32(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) {
            if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40))
                v |= (~0 << (s + 7));

            return { value: v | 0, next: p };
        }

        s += 7;
    }
}

function skipBlockType(p) {
    const b = buf[p];

    // blocktype is either valtype byte or signed LEB type index
    if (
        b === 0x40 ||
        b === 0x7f ||
        b === 0x7e ||
        b === 0x7d ||
        b === 0x7c
    ) {
        return p + 1;
    }

    return readS32(p).next;
}

function skipInstruction(p) {

    const op = buf[p++];

    // control
    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
        return skipBlockType(p);
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
        return readU32(p).next;
    }

    if (op === 0x0e) {
        const n = readU32(p);
        p = n.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++)
            p = readU32(p).next;

        return readU32(p).next;
    }

    if (op === 0x0f || op === 0x1a || op === 0x1b) {
        return p;
    }

    // locals/globals
    if (
        op === 0x20 ||
        op === 0x21 ||
        op === 0x22 ||
        op === 0x23 ||
        op === 0x24
    ) {
        return readU32(p).next;
    }

    // calls
    if (op === 0x10) {
        return readU32(p).next;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
        const a = readU32(p);
        const b = readU32(a.next);
        return b.next;
    }

    // memory load/store
    if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
        const a = readU32(p);
        return readU32(a.next).next;
    }

    // memory.size / grow
    if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
        return p + 1;
    }

    // constants
    if (op === 0x41) {
        return readS32(p).next;
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        // signed i64 LEB
        while (true) {
            const b = buf[p++];
            if (!(b & 0x80))
                return p;
        }
    }

    if (op === 0x43)
        return p + 4;

    if (op === 0x44)
        return p + 8;

    // ref.null
    if (op === 0xd0) {
        return p + 1;
    }

    // ref.func
    if (op === 0xd2) {
        return readU32(p).next;
    }

    // bulk-memory / prefixed instructions
    if (op === 0xfc) {
        const sub = readU32(p);
        p = sub.next;

        // Most relevant bulk-memory forms have variable immediates.
        // Conservative fallback.
        return p;
    }

    // SIMD
    if (op === 0xfd) {
        const sub = readU32(p);
        return sub.next + 16;
    }

    // EH / misc prefixes
    if (op === 0xfb) {
        const sub = readU32(p);
        return sub.next;
    }

    return p;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse function section and code section.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;

let functionTypes = [];
let bodies = [];

while (p < buf.length) {

    const sectionId = buf[p++];

    const sz = readU32(p);
    p = sz.next;

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = p + sz.value;

    if (sectionId === 3) {

        const n = readU32(p);
        p = n.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

            const t = readU32(p);
            p = t.next;

            functionTypes.push(t.value);
        }
    }

    if (sectionId === 10) {

        const n = readU32(p);
        p = n.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

            const bodyStart = p;

            const bs = readU32(p);
            p = bs.next;

            const bodyEnd = p + bs.value;

            const locals = readU32(p);
            p = locals.next;

            let extra = 0;

            for (let j = 0; j < locals.value; j++) {

                const cnt = readU32(p);
                p = cnt.next;

                p++;
                extra += cnt.value;
            }

            bodies.push({
                bodyStart,
                instrStart: p,
                bodyEnd,
                extra
            });

            p = bodyEnd;
        }
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE CALLERS OF FUNC 446");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`FUNCTION : ${TARGET}`);
console.log(`BODY     : ${bodies[TARGET - IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS] ? "FOUND" : "NOT FOUND"}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let callers = [];

for (let bi = 0; bi < bodies.length; bi++) {

    const fn = bi + IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS;

    let q = bodies[bi].instrStart;

    while (q < bodies[bi].bodyEnd) {

        const op = buf[q];

        if (op === 0x10) {

            const r = readU32(q + 1);

            if (r.value === TARGET) {

                callers.push({
                    func: fn,
                    offset: q
                });
            }

            q = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        q = skipInstruction(q);
    }
}

console.log(`DIRECT CALLERS : ${callers.length}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const c of callers) {

    console.log(
        `FUNC ${c.func} -> CALL 446 @ 0x${c.offset.toString(16)}`
    );
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("CALLER CONTEXTS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const c of callers) {

    const f = bodies[c.func - IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS];

    const from = Math.max(
        f.instrStart,
        c.offset - 120
    );

    const to = Math.min(
        f.bodyEnd,
        c.offset + 30
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        `FUNC ${c.func} @ 0x${c.offset.toString(16)}`
    );

    let q = from;

    while (q < to) {

        const op = buf[q];

        let text = `0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} : ${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

        if (op === 0x20) {

            const r = readU32(q + 1);

            text += `  local.get ${r.value}`;
            q = r.next;

        } else if (op === 0x21) {

            const r = readU32(q + 1);

            text += `  local.set ${r.value}`;
            q = r.next;

        } else if (op === 0x22) {

            const r = readU32(q + 1);

            text += `  local.tee ${r.value}`;
            q = r.next;

        } else if (op === 0x41) {

            const r = readS32(q + 1);

            text += `  i32.const ${r.value}`;
            q = r.next;

        } else if (op === 0x10) {

            const r = readU32(q + 1);

            text += `  call ${r.value}`;
            q = r.next;

        } else if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x36) {

            const a = readU32(q + 1);
            const b = readU32(a.next);

            text += `  MEMORY op=0x${op.toString(16)} align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;

            q = b.next;

        } else if (op === 0x11) {

            const a = readU32(q + 1);
            const b = readU32(a.next);

            text += `  call_indirect type=${a.value} table=${b.value}`;

            q = b.next;

        } else {

            q++;
        }

        console.log(text);
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace446Expression.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const START = 0x000222c0;
const END   = 0x000225f2;

function uleb(pos) {
  let r = 0;
  let s = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    r += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    s += 7;
  }

  return { value: r >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let r = 0;
  let s = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[p++];
    r += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    r -= 2 ** s;
  }

  return { value: r | 0, next: p };
}

function decode() {
  let p = START;

  // locals
  const ld = uleb(p);
  p = ld.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < ld.value; i++) {
    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next + 1;
  }

  const out = [];

  while (p < END) {
    const at = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        kind:
          op === 0x20 ? "get" :
          op === 0x21 ? "set" :
          "tee",
        local: x.value
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = sleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        kind: "const",
        value: x.value
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x29 ||
        op === 0x2d || op === 0x2c) {

      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        kind: "load",
        offset: o.value
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6a ||
        op === 0x6b ||
        op === 0x6c ||
        op === 0x71 ||
        op === 0x72 ||
        op === 0x73 ||
        op === 0x74 ||
        op === 0x75 ||
        op === 0x76 ||
        op === 0x67 ||
        op === 0x1b) {

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        kind: "op"
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x04 ||
        op === 0x05 ||
        op === 0x0b ||
        op === 0x0c ||
        op === 0x0d) {

      if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        out.push({
          at,
          op,
          kind: "branch",
          depth: x.value
        });
      } else {
        out.push({
          at,
          op,
          kind:
            op === 0x04 ? "if" :
            op === 0x05 ? "else" :
            "end"
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      out.push({
        at,
        op,
        kind: "call",
        target: x.value
      });

      continue;
    }

    out.push({
      at,
      op,
      kind: "unknown"
    });
  }

  return out;
}

const ins = decode();

const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const TARGET = 1795;

function readU32(pos) {
  return wasm.readUInt32LE(pos);
}

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: result >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[p++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;
    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    result |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: result | 0, next: p };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate function section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let pos = 8;
let functionTypes = [];

while (pos < wasm.length) {
  const sectionStart = pos;
  const id = wasm[pos++];

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);
  const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
  pos = sizeInfo.next;

  if (id === 3) {
    const countInfo = readULEB(pos);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    pos = countInfo.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const t = readULEB(pos);
      functionTypes.push(t.value);
      pos = t.next;
    }
    break;
  }

  pos = sectionStart + 1 + (sizeInfo.next - (sectionStart + 1)) + sectionSize;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

pos = 8;

let codeStart = 0;
let codeCount = 0;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
  const sectionStart = pos;
  const id = wasm[pos++];

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);
  const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
  pos = sizeInfo.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = pos;

    const countInfo = readULEB(pos);
    codeCount = countInfo.value;
    break;
  }

  pos = sectionStart + 1 + (sizeInfo.next - (sectionStart + 1)) + sectionSize;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find target body
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = codeStart;
const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const bodyCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

let bodyStart = null;
let bodyEnd = null;

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {
  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + bodySize;

  const funcIndex = 83 + i;

  if (funcIndex === TARGET) {
    bodyStart = start;
    bodyEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (bodyStart === null) {
  throw new Error("Target function not found");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Decoder
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function decode(start, end) {
  let p = start;

  // locals
  const localDeclCountInfo = readULEB(p);
  const localDeclCount = localDeclCountInfo.value;
  p = localDeclCountInfo.next;

  const locals = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount; i++) {
    const nInfo = readULEB(p);
    const n = nInfo.value;
    p = nInfo.next;

    const type = wasm[p++];
    for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) locals.push(type);
  }

  const params = 5;
  const localTypes = Array(params).fill(0x7f).concat(locals);

  function localName(n) {
    return `local${n}`;
  }

  const ins = [];

  while (p < end) {
    const at = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    if (op === 0x0b) {
      ins.push({ at, text: "end" });
      break;
    }

    if (op === 0x20) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;
      ins.push({ at, text: `local.get ${localName(x.value)}` });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;
      ins.push({ at, text: `local.set ${localName(x.value)}` });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;
      ins.push({ at, text: `local.tee ${localName(x.value)}` });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = readSLEB(p);
      p = x.next;
      ins.push({ at, text: `i32.const ${x.value}` });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;
      ins.push({ at, text: `call ${x.value}` });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x28) {
      const align = readULEB(p);
      p = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(p);
      p = off.next;

      ins.push({
        at,
        text: `i32.load align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x2d) {
      const align = readULEB(p);
      p = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(p);
      p = off.next;

      ins.push({
        at,
        text: `i32.load8_u align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x36) {
      const align = readULEB(p);
      p = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(p);
      p = off.next;

      ins.push({
        at,
        text: `i32.store align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6a) {
      ins.push({ at, text: "i32.add" });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6b) {
      ins.push({ at, text: "i32.sub" });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x71) {
      ins.push({ at, text: "i32.and" });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x72) {
      ins.push({ at, text: "i32.or" });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x73) {
      ins.push({ at, text: "i32.xor" });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x74) {
      ins.push({ at, text: "i32.shl" });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x75) {
      ins.push({ at, text: "i32.shr_s" });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x76) {
      ins.push({ at, text: "i32.shr_u" });
      continue;
    }

    // generic one-byte opcode
    ins.push({ at, text: `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}` });
  }

  return ins;
}

const ins = decode(bodyStart, bodyEnd);

const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

function u32(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];

    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function i32(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];

    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        v |= (~0 << s);

      return { value: v | 0, next: p };
    }
  }
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const id = wasm[p++];

  const s = u32(p);
  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + s.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate exact code region around known caller
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const START = 0x0022ce40;
const END   = 0x0022ced0;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE INPUT OBJECT OF FUNC 446");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`RANGE : ${hx(START)} .. ${hx(END)}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Decode exact region
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = START;

const stack = [];
const locals = new Map();

function push(x) {
  stack.push(x);
}

function pop() {
  return stack.length
    ? stack.pop()
    : "<?>"; 
}

function localGet(n) {
  return locals.has(n)
    ? locals.get(n)
    : `local${n}`;
}

function print(s) {
  console.log(s);
}

while (p < END) {

  const off = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  // local.get
  if (op === 0x20) {

    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    const e = localGet(x.value);

    push(e);

    print(`${hx(off)} local.get ${x.value} -> ${e}`);
    continue;
  }

  // local.set
  if (op === 0x21) {

    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    const e = pop();

    locals.set(x.value, e);

    print(`${hx(off)} local.set ${x.value} = ${e}`);
    continue;
  }

  // local.tee
  if (op === 0x22) {

    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    const e = pop();

    locals.set(x.value, e);
    push(e);

    print(`${hx(off)} local.tee ${x.value} = ${e}`);
    continue;
  }

  // i32.const
  if (op === 0x41) {

    const x = i32(p);
    p = x.next;

    push(String(x.value));

    print(
      `${hx(off)} i32.const ${x.value}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // i32.load
  if (op === 0x28) {

    const a = u32(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = u32(p);
    p = o.next;

    const addr = pop();

    const e =
      o.value === 0
        ? `MEM[${addr}]`
        : `MEM[${addr}+${o.value}]`;

    push(e);

    print(
      `${hx(off)} i32.load offset=${o.value} : ${addr} -> ${e}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // i64.load
  if (op === 0x29) {

    const a = u32(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = u32(p);
    p = o.next;

    const addr = pop();

    const e =
      o.value === 0
        ? `MEM64[${addr}]`
        : `MEM64[${addr}+${o.value}]`;

    push(e);

    print(
      `${hx(off)} i64.load offset=${o.value}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // call
  if (op === 0x10) {

    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    print(`${hx(off)} CALL ${x.value}`);

    if (x.value === 446) {

      const arg = pop();

      push(`RET[446](${arg})`);

      print("");
      print("************************************************************");
      print("CALL 446 INPUT");
      print("************************************************************");
      print(`ARG0 = ${arg}`);
      print("Expected:");
      print("ARG0 = MEM[local4 + 40]");
      print("************************************************************");
      print("");
    }

    else if (x.value === 248) {

      const a3 = pop();
      const a2 = pop();
      const a1 = pop();

      push(`RET[248]`);

      print(
        `CALL 248 args = [${a1}, ${a2}, ${a3}]`
      );
    }

    continue;
  }

  // local stack-related control
  if (op === 0x45) {

    const a = pop();

    print(`${hx(off)} i32.eqz ${a}`);

    push(`(${a}==0)`);

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0d) {

    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    const cond = pop();

    print(
      `${hx(off)} br_if ${x.value} [${cond}]`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // i32.add
  if (op === 0x6a) {

    const b = pop();
    const a = pop();

    const e = `(${a}+${b})`;

    push(e);

    print(
      `${hx(off)} i32.add -> ${e}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  print(
    `${hx(off)} opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
  );
}

for (const [k, v] of locals) {

}

console.log("============================================================");

  console.log(`[${i}] ${x}`);
});

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET = 446;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 446 RETURN DATAFLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

function readULEB(p) {
    let v = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        v += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if (!(b & 0x80)) {
            return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    return null;
}

function readSLEB(p) {
    let v = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) {

            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                v |= (~0 << (shift + 7));
            }

            return {
                value: v | 0,
                next: p
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    return null;
}

function hx(v) {
    return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/*
 * Locate CODE section.
 */
let p = 8;

let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < buf.length) {

    const id = buf[p++];

    const s = readULEB(p);
    if (!s) break;

    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + s.value;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

let q = codeStart;

const count = readULEB(q);
q = count.next;

const functions = [];

for (let f = 0; f < count.value; f++) {

    const s = readULEB(q);
    q = s.next;

    const start = q;
    const end = start + s.value;

    functions.push({
        func: f,
        start,
        end
    });

    q = end;
}

const fn = functions[TARGET];

console.log("FUNC :", TARGET);
console.log("BODY :", hx(fn.start), "..", hx(fn.end));
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

/*
 * Decode FUNC 446.
 */
let x = fn.start;

const groups = readULEB(x);
x = groups.next;

let localCount = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < groups.value; i++) {

    const n = readULEB(x);
    x = n.next;

    const type = buf[x++];

    localCount += n.value;
}

console.log("EXTRA LOCALS :", localCount);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const ins = [];

while (x < fn.end) {

    const pos = x;
    const op = buf[x++];

    const item = {
        pos,
        op,
        text: ""
    };

    if (op === 0x20) {

        const r = readULEB(x);
        if (!r) break;

        x = r.next;

        item.local = r.value;
        item.text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x21) {

        const r = readULEB(x);
        if (!r) break;

        x = r.next;

        item.local = r.value;
        item.text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x22) {

        const r = readULEB(x);
        if (!r) break;

        x = r.next;

        item.local = r.value;
        item.text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x41) {

        const r = readSLEB(x);
        if (!r) break;

        x = r.next;

        item.value = r.value;

        item.text =
            `i32.const ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x10) {

        const r = readULEB(x);
        if (!r) break;

        x = r.next;

        item.target = r.value;

        item.text =
            `call ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x0f) {

        item.text = "return";
    }

    else if (op === 0x1a) {

        item.text = "drop";
    }

    else if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x35) {

        const a = readULEB(x);
        if (!a) break;

        x = a.next;

        const o = readULEB(x);
        if (!o) break;

        x = o.next;

        item.offset = o.value;
        item.text =
            `load offset=${o.value}`;
    }

    else if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {

        const a = readULEB(x);
        if (!a) break;

        x = a.next;

        const o = readULEB(x);
        if (!o) break;

        x = o.next;

        item.offset = o.value;
        item.text =
            `store offset=${o.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x6a) {
        item.text = "i32.add";
    }

    else if (op === 0x6b) {
        item.text = "i32.sub";
    }

    else if (op === 0x45) {
        item.text = "i32.eqz";
    }

    else if (op === 0x46) {
        item.text = "i32.eq";
    }

    else if (op === 0x47) {
        item.text = "i32.ne";
    }

    else if (op === 0x71) {
        item.text = "i32.and";
    }

    else if (op === 0x72) {
        item.text = "i32.or";
    }

    else if (op === 0x73) {
        item.text = "i32.xor";
    }

    else if (op === 0x74) {
        item.text = "i32.shl";
    }

    else if (op === 0x75) {
        item.text = "i32.shr_s";
    }

    else if (op === 0x76) {
        item.text = "i32.shr_u";
    }

    else if (op === 0x77) {
        item.text = "i32.rotl";
    }

    else if (op === 0x78) {
        item.text = "i32.rotr";
    }

    else if (op === 0x79) {
        item.text = "i64.extend_i32_s";
    }

    else if (op === 0x42) {

        const r = readSLEB(x);
        if (!r) break;

        x = r.next;

        item.value = r.value;
        item.text =
            `i64.const ${r.value}`;
    }

    else {
        item.text =
            `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
    }

    ins.push(item);
}

console.log("");

let returns = 0;

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace446ReturnedObject.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function uleb(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: result, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {

      if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        result |= (~0 << shift);

      return {
        value: result | 0,
        next: pos
      };
    }
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function decode(pos, end) {

  if (pos >= end)
    return null;

  const start = pos;
  const op = buf[pos++];

  const ins = {
    start,
    end: pos,
    op,
    text: `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`
  };

  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22
  ) {

    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    ins.end = a.next;
    ins.local = a.value;

    const name =
      op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
      op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
      "local.tee";

    ins.text = `${name} local=${a.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {

    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    ins.end = a.next;
    ins.callTarget = a.value;
    ins.text = `call ${a.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x2d
  ) {

    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.isLoad = true;

    const name =
      op === 0x28 ? "i32.load" :
      op === 0x29 ? "i64.load" :
      "i32.load8_u";

    ins.text =
      `${name} align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x36) {

    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.isStore = true;

    ins.text =
      `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {

    const a = sleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    ins.end = a.next;
    ins.value = a.value;
    ins.text = `i32.const ${a.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x6a) {
    ins.text = "i32.add";
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x6b) {
    ins.text = "i32.sub";
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x0b) {
    ins.text = "end";
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x05) {
    ins.text = "else";
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x04) {

    if (pos >= end)
      return null;

    pos++;
    ins.end = pos;
    ins.text = "if";

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03) {

    if (pos >= end)
      return null;

    pos++;
    ins.end = pos;
    ins.text =
      op === 0x02 ? "block" : "loop";

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {

    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    ins.end = a.next;

    ins.text =
      `${op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if"} ${a.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  return ins;
}

/*
 * Locate code section.
 */

let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < buf.length) {

  const sectionId = buf[p++];

  const s = uleb(p);
  if (!s)
    throw new Error("Bad section");

  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = start + s.value;

  if (sectionId === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
  throw new Error("Code section not found");

/*
 * Decode all function bodies.
 */

let q = codeStart;

const countInfo = uleb(q);
q = countInfo.next;

const bodyCount = countInfo.value;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE FUNC 446 RETURNED OBJECT");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("BODY COUNT:", bodyCount);
console.log("TARGET CALL: 446");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let totalCalls = 0;
let useful = 0;

for (let func = 0; func < bodyCount; func++) {

  const sizeInfo = uleb(q);
  if (!sizeInfo)
    break;

  const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
  const bodyStart = sizeInfo.next;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

  q = bodyEnd;

  let x = bodyStart;

  const localInfo = uleb(x);
  if (!localInfo)
    continue;

  x = localInfo.next;

  /*
   * Skip local declaration groups.
   */
  let localGroupCount = localInfo.value;

  for (let g = 0; g < localGroupCount; g++) {

    const n = uleb(x);
    if (!n) {
      x = bodyEnd;
      break;
    }

    x = n.next;

    if (x >= bodyEnd)
      break;

    x++;
  }

  const ins = [];

  while (x < bodyEnd) {

    const z = decode(x, bodyEnd);

    if (!z)
      break;

    ins.push(z);
    x = z.end;
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {

    if (ins[i].callTarget !== 446)
      continue;

    totalCalls++;

    /*
     * We only care when call 446 is immediately captured.
     */
    if (
      i + 1 >= ins.length ||
      ins[i + 1].text.indexOf("local.tee") !== 0
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    const returnedLocal = ins[i + 1].local;

    const hits = [];

    /*
     * Scan forward until the returned local is overwritten.
     */
    for (
      let j = i + 2;
      j < Math.min(ins.length, i + 80);
      j++
    ) {

      if (
        (ins[j].text === `local.set local=${returnedLocal}`) ||
        (ins[j].text === `local.tee local=${returnedLocal}`)
      ) {
        break;
      }

      if (
        ins[j].isLoad &&
        [0, 4, 36, 128].includes(ins[j].offset)
      ) {

        /*
         * Check whether immediately before the load
         * there is local.get returnedLocal.
         */
        const prev = ins[j - 1];

        if (
          prev &&
          prev.text === `local.get local=${returnedLocal}`
        ) {

          hits.push({
            index: j,
            offset: ins[j].offset
          });
        }
      }

      /*
       * Detect direct call 248 using returned local.
       */
      if (
        ins[j].callTarget === 248 &&
        j >= 1
      ) {

        const from = Math.max(i + 2, j - 8);

        for (let k = from; k < j; k++) {

          if (
            ins[k].text ===
            `local.get local=${returnedLocal}`
          ) {

            hits.push({
              index: j,
              offset: "CALL_248"
            });

            break;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    if (hits.length === 0)
      continue;

    useful++;

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("FUNC", func);
    console.log(
      "CALL 446 @",
      hex(ins[i].start)
    );
    console.log(
      "RETURN CAPTURED IN LOCAL",
      returnedLocal
    );
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log("");
    console.log("CALL 446:");
    console.log(
      " ",
      hex(ins[i].start),
      ins[i].text
    );
    console.log(
      " ",
      hex(ins[i + 1].start),
      ins[i + 1].text
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("CONSUMERS:");

    for (const h of hits) {

      const j = h.index;

      console.log("");
      console.log(
        " ",
        h.offset === "CALL_248"
          ? "FUNC 248"
          : `LOAD +${h.offset}`
      );

      const from = Math.max(i, j - 8);

      for (let k = from; k <= j; k++) {

        console.log(
          "   ",
          hex(ins[k].start),
          ins[k].text
        );
      }
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TOTAL CALL 446 :", totalCalls);
console.log("USEFUL RETURNS  :", useful);
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\trace446Type2Dispatch.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readU32(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;
    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) {
            return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
        }
        s += 7;
    }
}

function readS32(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;
    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) {
            if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40))
                v |= (~0 << (s + 7));
            return { value: v | 0, next: p };
        }

        s += 7;
    }
}

function readVecU32(p) {
    const c = readU32(p);
    p = c.next;

    const arr = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
        const r = readU32(p);
        arr.push(r.value);
        p = r.next;
    }

    return {
        values: arr,
        next: p
    };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find function bodies.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let functionBodies = [];
let functionTypes = [];

while (p < buf.length) {

    const sectionId = buf[p++];

    const size = readU32(p);
    p = size.next;

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = p + size.value;

    if (sectionId === 3) {

        const r = readVecU32(p);

        functionTypes = r.values;
    }

    if (sectionId === 10) {

        const count = readU32(p);
        p = count.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

            const bodyStart = p;

            const bs = readU32(p);
            p = bs.next;

            const bodyEnd = p + bs.value;

            const localDecl = readU32(p);
            p = localDecl.next;

            let extraLocals = 0;

            for (let j = 0; j < localDecl.value; j++) {

                const n = readU32(p);
                p = n.next;

                const type = buf[p++];
                extraLocals += n.value;
            }

            functionBodies.push({
                bodyIndex: i,
                start: bodyStart,
                instrStart: p,
                end: bodyEnd,
                extraLocals
            });

            p = bodyEnd;
        }
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// ELEMENT SECTION
// Build TABLE[index] -> FUNC.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = 8;

let table = [];

while (p < buf.length) {

    const sectionId = buf[p++];

    const size = readU32(p);
    p = size.next;

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = p + size.value;

    if (sectionId === 9) {

        const segCount = readU32(p);
        p = segCount.next;

        for (let s = 0; s < segCount.value; s++) {

            const flags = buf[p++];

            if (flags === 0) {

                // offset expression
                const opcode = buf[p++];

                if (opcode !== 0x41) {
                    throw new Error("Unexpected offset opcode");
                }

                const off = readS32(p);
                p = off.next;

                if (buf[p++] !== 0x0b) {
                    throw new Error("Missing end");
                }

                const count = readU32(p);
                p = count.next;

                const start = off.value;

                for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

                    const f = readU32(p);
                    p = f.next;

                    table[start + i] = f.value;
                }
            }

            else {
                console.log(
                    "Unhandled element flags:",
                    flags
                );
            }
        }
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNC 446
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;
const FUNC = 446;

const bodyIndex = FUNC - IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS;
const f = functionBodies[bodyIndex];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 446 TYPE-2 DISPATCH");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`FUNCTION      : ${FUNC}`);
console.log(`BODY INDEX    : ${bodyIndex}`);
console.log(
    `BODY          : 0x${f.start.toString(16)} .. 0x${f.end.toString(16)}`
);
console.log(
    `INSTR         : 0x${f.instrStart.toString(16)} .. 0x${f.end.toString(16)}`
);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const start = 0x1d95d;
const end   = 0x1d984;

console.log("RAW INSTRUCTION BYTES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let x = start; x <= end; x++) {

    console.log(
        `0x${x.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} : ${buf[x]
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2,"0")}`
    );
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Decode only the exact known region.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("SEMANTIC CHAIN");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

console.log(`
param0
  |
  +-- [0] ----> OBJECT
  |                |
  |                +-- [24] ---> TYPE-2 TABLE INDEX
  |
  +-- [4] ----> CURRENT VALUE
                   |
                   +------------+
                                |
                                v
                        call_indirect TYPE 2
                                |
                                v
                              RESULT
                                |
                                v
                         *(param0 + 4)
`);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("TABLE SEARCH");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const matches = [];

for (let i = 0; i < table.length; i++) {

    if (table[i] === FUNC) {
        matches.push(i);
    }
}

console.log(
    `TABLE ENTRIES -> FUNC ${FUNC} : ${matches.length}`
);

if (matches.length) {

    for (const i of matches) {

        console.log(
            `TABLE[${i}] -> FUNC ${FUNC}`
        );
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Show local functions with TYPE 2 indirect calls.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("TYPE-2 INDIRECT CALL SITES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const type2Sites = [];

for (let fi = 0; fi < functionBodies.length; fi++) {

    const fn = fi + IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS;

    let q = functionBodies[fi].instrStart;

    while (q < functionBodies[fi].end) {

        const op = buf[q];

        if (op === 0x11) {

            const a = readU32(q + 1);
            const b = readU32(a.next);

            if (a.value === 2) {

                type2Sites.push({
                    func: fn,
                    offset: q,
                    table: b.value
                });
            }

            q = b.next;
            continue;
        }

        // Minimal operand skipping sufficient for this scan.
        if (op === 0x41) {
            q = readS32(q + 1).next;
            continue;
        }

        if (
            op === 0x20 ||
            op === 0x21 ||
            op === 0x22 ||
            op === 0x23 ||
            op === 0x24 ||
            op === 0x10 ||
            op === 0x0c ||
            op === 0x0d
        ) {
            q = readU32(q + 1).next;
            continue;
        }

        if (
            op >= 0x28 &&
            op <= 0x3e
        ) {
            const a = readU32(q + 1);
            const b = readU32(a.next);
            q = b.next;
            continue;
        }

        q++;
    }
}

console.log(
    `TYPE-2 SITES FOUND : ${type2Sites.length}`
);

for (const s of type2Sites) {

    console.log(
        `FUNC ${s.func} @ 0x${s.offset.toString(16)}` +
        `  call_indirect type=2 table=${s.table}`
    );
}

console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\trace4788Stores.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const TARGET = 4788;
const TARGET_HEX = 0x12b4;

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      return { value: v >>> 0, end: p };
    }

    s += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) {
        v |= (-1 << s);
      }

      return { value: v | 0, end: p };
    }
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("TRACE 4788 STORES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET DEC :", TARGET);
console.log("TARGET HEX :", hex(TARGET_HEX));
console.log();

let constHits = [];
let storeHits = [];

//
// Find every i32.const 4788
//
for (let p = 0; p < b.length - 2; p++) {

  if (b[p] !== 0x41)
    continue;

  const r = sleb(p + 1);

  if (!r)
    continue;

  if ((r.value >>> 0) === TARGET) {
    constHits.push({
      pos: p,
      end: r.end
    });
  }
}

console.log("CONST 4788 HITS :", constHits.length);
console.log();

for (const h of constHits) {

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log("CONST 4788 @", hex(h.pos));
  console.log("NEXT BYTES:");

  const end = Math.min(b.length, h.end + 48);

  for (let p = h.pos; p < end; p++) {
    console.log(
      " ",
      hex(p),
      b[p].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
    );
  }

  //
  // Search forward for nearby stores
  //
  const limit = Math.min(b.length, h.end + 120);

  for (let p = h.end; p < limit; p++) {

    if (b[p] !== 0x36)
      continue;

    const a = uleb(p + 1);
    if (!a)
      continue;

    const o = uleb(a.end);
    if (!o)
      continue;

    storeHits.push({
      constPos: h.pos,
      storePos: p,
      offset: o.value
    });
  }
}

console.log();
console.log("NEARBY STORE CANDIDATES :", storeHits.length);
console.log();

for (const h of storeHits) {

  console.log(
    "CONST @", hex(h.constPos),
    "STORE @", hex(h.storePos),
    "offset=", h.offset
  );
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace4815Callers.ts

import { readFileSync } from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 4815;

type Instr = {
    offset: number;
    op: number;
    name: string;
    args?: any;
};

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, p: number) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: result >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readSLEB(buf: Buffer, p: number, bits = 32) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = buf[p++];
        result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < bits && (byte & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return { value: result | 0, next: p };
}

function opcodeName(op: number) {
    const m: Record<number, string> = {
        0x0b: "end",
        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",
        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
        0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
        0x2f: "i32.load16_u",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",
        0x3a: "i32.store8",
        0x3b: "i32.store16",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x10: "call",
        0x11: "call_indirect",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",
        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x73: "i32.xor",
        0x74: "i32.shl",
        0x75: "i32.shr_s",
        0x76: "i32.shr_u",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",
        0x48: "i32.lt_s",
        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
        0x4c: "i32.le_s",
        0x4d: "i32.le_u",
        0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
        0x4f: "i32.ge_u",
    };

    return m[op] ?? `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function decodeBody(buf: Buffer, start: number, end: number): Instr[] {
    const out: Instr[] = [];
    let p = start;

    while (p < end) {
        const offset = p;
        const op = buf[p++];

        let args: any = undefined;

        if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
            const r = readULEB(buf, p);
            args = { local: r.value };
            p = r.next;
        }

        else if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
            const r = readULEB(buf, p);
            args = { global: r.value };
            p = r.next;
        }

        else if (
            op === 0x28 ||
            op === 0x29 ||
            op === 0x2c ||
            op === 0x2d ||
            op === 0x2e ||
            op === 0x2f ||
            op === 0x36 ||
            op === 0x37 ||
            op === 0x3a ||
            op === 0x3b
        ) {
            const a = readULEB(buf, p);
            const b = readULEB(buf, a.next);

            args = {
                align: a.value,
                offset: b.value,
            };

            p = b.next;
        }

        else if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = readSLEB(buf, p, 32);
            args = { value: r.value };
            p = r.next;
        }

        else if (op === 0x42) {
            const r = readSLEB(buf, p, 64);
            args = { value: r.value };
            p = r.next;
        }

        else if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = readULEB(buf, p);
            args = { func: r.value };
            p = r.next;
        }

        else if (op === 0x11) {
            const a = readULEB(buf, p);
            const b = readULEB(buf, a.next);

            args = {
                type: a.value,
                table: b.value,
            };

            p = b.next;
        }

        out.push({
            offset,
            op,
            name: opcodeName(op),
            args,
        });

        if (op === 0x0b) break;
    }

    return out;
}

function readFunctions(buf: Buffer) {
    let p = 8;
    let codeStart = -1;
    let codeSize = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const s = readULEB(buf, p);
        const size = s.value;
        p = s.next;

        const payload = p;

        if (id === 10) {
            codeStart = payload;
            codeSize = size;
            break;
        }

        p += size;
    }

    if (codeStart < 0) {
        throw new Error("Code section not found");
    }

    const end = codeStart + codeSize;

    let q = codeStart;

    const countInfo = readULEB(buf, q);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    q = countInfo.next;

    const bodies: {
        func: number;
        bodyStart: number;
        bodyEnd: number;
        instrStart: number;
        instrEnd: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const sizeInfo = readULEB(buf, q);
        const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
        q = sizeInfo.next;

        const bodyStart = q;
        const bodyEnd = q + bodySize;

        let x = bodyStart;

        const localInfo = readULEB(buf, x);
        const localDeclCount = localInfo.value;
        x = localInfo.next;

        for (let j = 0; j < localDeclCount; j++) {
            const n = readULEB(buf, x);
            x = n.next;

            // valtype
            x++;
        }

        bodies.push({
            func: 83 + i,
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            instrStart: x,
            instrEnd: bodyEnd,
        });

        q = bodyEnd;
    }

    if (q !== end) {
        throw new Error(
            `Code section parse mismatch: ${q.toString(16)} != ${end.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    return bodies;
}

function fmt(i: Instr) {
    let s =
        `0x${i.offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
        i.name;

    if (i.args) {
        if (i.name === "local.get" ||
            i.name === "local.set" ||
            i.name === "local.tee") {
            s += ` ${i.args.local}`;
        }
        else if (i.name === "call") {
            s += ` ${i.args.func}`;
        }
        else if (i.name === "i32.const") {
            s += ` ${i.args.value}`;
        }
        else if (i.name === "i64.const") {
            s += ` ${i.args.value}`;
        }
        else if (i.name === "i32.load" ||
                 i.name === "i64.load" ||
                 i.name === "i32.load8_s" ||
                 i.name === "i32.load8_u" ||
                 i.name === "i32.load16_s" ||
                 i.name === "i32.load16_u" ||
                 i.name === "i32.store" ||
                 i.name === "i64.store" ||
                 i.name === "i32.store8" ||
                 i.name === "i32.store16") {
            s += ` align=${i.args.align} offset=${i.args.offset}`;
        }
        else if (i.name === "call_indirect") {
            s += ` type=${i.args.type} table=${i.args.table}`;
        }
    }

    return s;
}

export async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" CALLERS OF FUNCTION 4815");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("");

    const wasm = readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

    console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length}`);
    console.log(`Target    : Function ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
    console.log("");

    const bodies = readFunctions(wasm);

    console.log(`Functions decoded : ${bodies.length}`);
    console.log("");

    let callers = 0;

    for (const body of bodies) {
        const instr = decodeBody(
            wasm,
            body.instrStart,
            body.instrEnd
        );

        for (let i = 0; i < instr.length; i++) {
            const x = instr[i];

            if (
                x.name === "call" &&
                x.args?.func === TARGET_FUNC
            ) {
                callers++;

                console.log("============================================================");
                console.log(`CALLER #${callers}`);
                console.log("============================================================");

                console.log(`Caller Function : ${body.func}`);
                console.log(
                    `CALL offset     : 0x${x.offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
                );

                console.log("");
                console.log("CONTEXT:");

                const from = Math.max(0, i - 8);
                const to = Math.min(instr.length, i + 4);

                for (let j = from; j < to; j++) {
                    console.log(
                        (j === i ? "  >>> " : "      ") +
                        fmt(instr[j])
                    );
                }

                console.log("");
                console.log("ARGUMENT #2 CANDIDATE FLOW:");

                const before = instr.slice(Math.max(0, i - 8), i);

                for (const b of before) {
                    if (
                        b.name === "local.get" ||
                        b.name === "local.set" ||
                        b.name === "local.tee" ||
                        b.name === "i32.const" ||
                        b.name === "i32.load" ||
                        b.name === "call" ||
                        b.name === "global.get" ||
                        b.name === "i32.add" ||
                        b.name === "i32.sub"
                    ) {
                        console.log("  " + fmt(b));
                    }
                }

                /*
                 * For Function 4815 signature we already know:
                 *
                 * param0 -> 4815.local0
                 * param1 -> 4815.local1
                 * param2 -> 4815.local2
                 * param3 -> 4815.local3
                 * param4 -> 4815.local4
                 * param5 -> 4815.local5
                 *
                 * The six local.get instructions immediately before
                 * call 4815 are therefore the most important evidence.
                 */

                console.log("");
                console.log("STACK-LEVEL VIEW:");

                const stackArgs = instr.slice(Math.max(0, i - 6), i);

                if (stackArgs.length === 6) {
                    for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) {
                        console.log(
                            `  arg${k} <- ${fmt(stackArgs[k])}`
                        );
                    }
                } else {
                    console.log(
                        "  WARNING: fewer than 6 immediate instructions."
                    );
                }

                console.log("");
            }
        }
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SUMMARY");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log(`Direct callers of 4815 : ${callers}`);
    console.log("");

    if (callers === 0) {
        console.log("No direct caller of Function 4815 found.");
    } else {
        console.log(
            "NEXT: reconstruct argument #2 for each caller."
        );
    }
}

FILE: scripts\trace4815DispatchDataflow.ts
