import * as fs from "fs";
import * as path from "path";

const WASM = path.resolve(
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const TARGET_FUNC = 4815;
const TARGET_TABLE = 7969;

type Instr = {
    off: number;
    op: number;
    text: string;
    imm?: number;
    memOffset?: number;
};

function readU32(buf: Buffer, p: number): [number, number] {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return [result >>> 0, p];
        }

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function readS32(buf: Buffer, p: number): [number, number] {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        b = buf[p++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                result |= (~0 << shift);
            }
            return [result | 0, p];
        }

        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function readS64(buf: Buffer, p: number): [bigint, number] {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        result |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 64n && (b & 0x40)) {
                result |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
            }
            return [result, p];
        }

        shift += 7n;
        if (shift > 70n) throw new Error("SLEB64 overflow");
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function readName(buf: Buffer, p: number): [string, number] {
    const [n, q] = readU32(buf, p);
    const end = q + n;
    return [buf.subarray(q, end).toString("utf8"), end];
}

function memarg(buf: Buffer, p: number): [number, number, number] {
    const [align, p1] = readU32(buf, p);
    const [offset, p2] = readU32(buf, p1);
    return [align, offset, p2];
}

function decodeInstr(buf: Buffer, start: number): Instr {
    let p = start;
    const op = buf[p++];

    switch (op) {
        case 0x00: return { off: start, op, text: "unreachable" };
        case 0x01: return { off: start, op, text: "nop" };
        case 0x02: {
            const [bt, q] = readS32(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `block ${bt}`, imm: bt };
        }
        case 0x03: {
            const [bt, q] = readS32(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `loop ${bt}`, imm: bt };
        }
        case 0x04: {
            const [bt, q] = readS32(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `if ${bt}`, imm: bt };
        }
        case 0x05: return { off: start, op, text: "else" };
        case 0x0b: return { off: start, op, text: "end" };
        case 0x0c: {
            const [x] = readU32(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `br ${x}`, imm: x };
        }
        case 0x0d: {
            const [x] = readU32(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `br_if ${x}`, imm: x };
        }
        case 0x0e: {
            const [n, p1] = readU32(buf, p);
            p = p1;
            const labels: number[] = [];
            for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                const [x, q] = readU32(buf, p);
                labels.push(x);
                p = q;
            }
            const [d] = readU32(buf, p);
            return {
                off: start,
                op,
                text: `br_table [${labels.join(",")}] default=${d}`,
            };
        }
        case 0x0f: return { off: start, op, text: "return" };

        case 0x10: {
            const [x] = readU32(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `call ${x}`, imm: x };
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const [typeidx, p1] = readU32(buf, p);
            const [tableidx] = readU32(buf, p1);
            return {
                off: start,
                op,
                text: `call_indirect type=${typeidx} table=${tableidx}`,
                imm: typeidx,
            };
        }

        case 0x1a: return { off: start, op, text: "drop" };
        case 0x1b: return { off: start, op, text: "select" };

        case 0x20: {
            const [x] = readU32(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `local.get ${x}`, imm: x };
        }
        case 0x21: {
            const [x] = readU32(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `local.set ${x}`, imm: x };
        }
        case 0x22: {
            const [x] = readU32(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `local.tee ${x}`, imm: x };
        }
        case 0x23: {
            const [x] = readU32(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `global.get ${x}`, imm: x };
        }
        case 0x24: {
            const [x] = readU32(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `global.set ${x}`, imm: x };
        }

        case 0x28: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i32.load align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }
        case 0x29: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i64.load align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }
        case 0x2a: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `f32.load align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }
        case 0x2b: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `f64.load align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }
        case 0x2c: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i32.load8_s align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }
        case 0x2d: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i32.load8_u align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }
        case 0x2e: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i32.load16_s align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }
        case 0x2f: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i32.load16_u align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }

        case 0x30: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i64.load8_s align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }
        case 0x31: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i64.load8_u align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }
        case 0x32: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i64.load16_s align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }
        case 0x33: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i64.load16_u align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }
        case 0x34: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i64.load32_s align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }
        case 0x35: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i64.load32_u align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }

        case 0x36: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i32.store align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }
        case 0x37: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i64.store align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }
        case 0x38: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `f32.store align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }
        case 0x39: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `f64.store align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }

        case 0x3a: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i32.store8 align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }
        case 0x3b: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i32.store16 align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }
        case 0x3c: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i64.store8 align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }
        case 0x3d: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i64.store16 align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }
        case 0x3e: {
            const [a, o] = memarg(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i64.store32 align=${a} offset=${o}`, memOffset: o };
        }

        case 0x3f: {
            const [x] = readU32(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `memory.size ${x}` };
        }
        case 0x40: {
            const [x] = readU32(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `memory.grow ${x}` };
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const [x] = readS32(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i32.const ${x}`, imm: x };
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const [x] = readS64(buf, p);
            return { off: start, op, text: `i64.const ${x}` };
        }

        case 0x43:
            return { off: start, op, text: "f32.const" };

        case 0x44:
            return { off: start, op, text: "f64.const" };

        default:
            return {
                off: start,
                op,
                text: `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
            };
    }
}

function locateCodeSection(wasm: Buffer) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];

        const [size, q] = readU32(wasm, p);
        const payload = q;
        const end = payload + size;

        if (id === 10) {
            return { start: payload, end };
        }

        p = end;
    }

    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function parseCodeBodies(wasm: Buffer) {
    const sec = locateCodeSection(wasm);
    let p = sec.start;

    const [count, q] = readU32(wasm, p);
    p = q;

    const bodies: {
        func: number;
        bodyStart: number;
        bodyEnd: number;
        codeStart: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const [bodySize, p1] = readU32(wasm, p);
        p = p1;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

        // local declarations
        const [localGroups, p2] = readU32(wasm, p);
        p = p2;

        for (let g = 0; g < localGroups; g++) {
            const [, p3] = readU32(wasm, p); // count
            p = p3;
            p++; // value type
        }

        const codeStart = p;

        bodies.push({
            func: 83 + i,
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            codeStart,
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function decodeBody(
    wasm: Buffer,
    body: {
        func: number;
        bodyStart: number;
        bodyEnd: number;
        codeStart: number;
    }
): Instr[] {
    const out: Instr[] = [];
    let p = body.codeStart;

    while (p < body.bodyEnd) {
        const old = p;

        try {
            const ins = decodeInstr(wasm, p);

            // determine immediate size by looking for known operand structure
            const op = wasm[p];

            if (
                op === 0x20 ||
                op === 0x21 ||
                op === 0x22 ||
                op === 0x23 ||
                op === 0x24 ||
                op === 0x10 ||
                op === 0x0c ||
                op === 0x0d ||
                op === 0x41
            ) {
                const [, q] = op === 0x41
                    ? readS32(wasm, p + 1)
                    : readU32(wasm, p + 1);

                p = q;
            } else if (op === 0x11) {
                const [, q1] = readU32(wasm, p + 1);
                const [, q2] = readU32(wasm, q1);
                p = q2;
            } else if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x29 || op === 0x2a ||
                       op === 0x2b || op === 0x2c || op === 0x2d ||
                       op === 0x2e || op === 0x2f || op === 0x30 ||
                       op === 0x31 || op === 0x32 || op === 0x33 ||
                       op === 0x34 || op === 0x35 || op === 0x36 ||
                       op === 0x37 || op === 0x38 || op === 0x39 ||
                       op === 0x3a || op === 0x3b || op === 0x3c ||
                       op === 0x3d || op === 0x3e) {
                const [, , q] = memarg(wasm, p + 1);
                p = q;
            } else if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
                const [, q] = readS32(wasm, p + 1);
                p = q;
            } else if (op === 0x0e) {
                const [n, q1] = readU32(wasm, p + 1);
                p = q1;

                for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                    const [, q2] = readU32(wasm, p);
                    p = q2;
                }

                const [, q3] = readU32(wasm, p);
                p = q3;
            } else if (op === 0x42) {
                const [, q] = readS64(wasm, p + 1);
                p = q;
            } else if (op === 0x43) {
                p += 5;
            } else if (op === 0x44) {
                p += 9;
            } else if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
                const [, q] = readU32(wasm, p + 1);
                p = q;
            } else {
                p++;
            }

            out.push(ins);
        } catch {
            p = old + 1;
            out.push({
                off: old,
                op: wasm[old],
                text: `UNKNOWN_BYTE 0x${wasm[old].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
            });
        }
    }

    return out;
}

async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" FUNCTION 4815 DISPATCH DATAFLOW");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================\n");

    const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log(`WASM size        : ${wasm.length}`);
    console.log(`Target function  : ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
    console.log(`Target table idx : ${TARGET_TABLE}\n`);

    const bodies = parseCodeBodies(wasm);

    console.log(`Functions decoded : ${bodies.length}\n`);

    const targets = [
        0x00019129,
        0x0001a778,
        0x0001d9a8,
        0x00028167,
        0x0002cde8,
        0x0002d2e6,
        0x0003d0c6,
        0x0003d196,
        0x0004b0e2,
        0x00057fa7,
        0x00063e9c,
        0x0007069a,
        0x00074f92,
        0x000b69fc,
        0x000ba2bb,
        0x000eafa2,
        0x000fdeb0,
        0x0010dd09,
        0x00117886,
        0x00132b85,
        0x00148a4f,
        0x00148c46,
        0x001c7ec8,
        0x0022d677,
        0x002307bf,
        0x002395eb,
        0x00239630,
        0x0023d880,
        0x0024aee4,
        0x00256356,
        0x002563b3,
        0x0025d154,
        0x002b4a88,
    ];

    console.log("Candidate dispatches : " + targets.length);
    console.log("");

    for (const target of targets) {
        const body = bodies.find(
            b => target >= b.codeStart && target < b.bodyEnd
        );

        if (!body) {
            console.log(`TARGET ${hex(target)} : body not found`);
            continue;
        }

        const ins = decodeBody(wasm, body);

        const idx = ins.findIndex(x => x.off === target);

        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log(`DISPATCH @ ${hex(target)}`);
        console.log(`Function : ${body.func}`);
        console.log(`Body     : ${hex(body.codeStart)} .. ${hex(body.bodyEnd)}`);
        console.log("============================================================");

        if (idx < 0) {
            console.log("Instruction not decoded at exact offset.");
            console.log("");
            continue;
        }

        const start = Math.max(0, idx - 14);
        const end = Math.min(ins.length, idx + 2);

        for (let i = start; i < end; i++) {
            const x = ins[i];

            const marker = x.off === target ? ">>>" : "   ";

            console.log(
                `${marker} ${hex(x.off)} ${x.text}`
            );
        }

        console.log("\nSTACK/OBJECT HINT:");

        const before = ins.slice(start, idx);

        const loads = before.filter(
            x => x.text.startsWith("i32.load")
        );

        const locals = before.filter(
            x =>
                x.text.startsWith("local.get") ||
                x.text.startsWith("local.set") ||
                x.text.startsWith("local.tee")
        );

        const consts = before.filter(
            x => x.text.startsWith("i32.const")
        );

        if (loads.length) {
            console.log(
                "  i32.load chain : " +
                loads.map(x =>
                    `${hex(x.off)}[off=${x.memOffset}]`
                ).join(" -> ")
            );
        } else {
            console.log("  i32.load chain : none");
        }

        if (locals.length) {
            console.log(
                "  local flow     : " +
                locals.map(x => `${hex(x.off)} ${x.text}`).join(" | ")
            );
        }

        if (consts.length) {
            console.log(
                "  constants      : " +
                consts.map(x => `${hex(x.off)} ${x.text}`).join(" | ")
            );
        }

        console.log("");
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" FINAL");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("");
    console.log(`Target mapping: table[${TARGET_TABLE}] = Function ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
    console.log("");
    console.log("این گزارش فقط مسیر تولید index را بررسی می‌کند.");
    console.log("هیچ تراکنش، اتصال به شبکه یا تغییر WASM انجام نمی‌شود.");
}

export { run };

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace4815Local2.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 4815;
const TARGET_LOCAL = 2;
const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

type Instr = {
    offset: number;
    opcode: number;
    text: string;
    local?: number;
    target?: number;
};

function readU32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos,
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readS32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                value |= ~0 << shift;
            }

            return {
                value: value | 0,
                next: pos,
            };
        }
    }
}

function readU64Leb(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value,
                next: pos,
            };
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }
}

function skipMemArg(pos: number): number {
    const a = readU32Leb(pos);
    return readU32Leb(a.next).next;
}

function decodeInstruction(pos: number): {
    instr: Instr;
    next: number;
} {
    const start = pos;
    const op = wasm[pos++];

    switch (op) {
        case 0x00:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "unreachable",
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x01:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "nop",
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x02:
        case 0x03:
        case 0x04:
            pos++;

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text:
                        op === 0x02
                            ? "block"
                            : op === 0x03
                              ? "loop"
                              : "if",
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x05:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "else",
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x0b:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "end",
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x0c: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `br ${x.value}`,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `br_if ${x.value}`,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x0e: {
            const n = readU32Leb(pos);
            pos = n.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
                pos = readU32Leb(pos).next;
            }

            pos = readU32Leb(pos).next;

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "br_table",
                },
                next: pos,
            };
        }

        case 0x0f:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "return",
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x10: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `call ${x.value}`,
                    target: x.value,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const t = readU32Leb(pos);
            const table = readU32Leb(t.next);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text:
                        `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`,
                },
                next: table.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x20: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
                    local: x.value,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
                    local: x.value,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
                    local: x.value,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x23: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `global.get ${x.value}`,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x24: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `global.set ${x.value}`,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x28:
        case 0x29:
        case 0x2a:
        case 0x2b:
        case 0x2c:
        case 0x2d:
        case 0x2e:
        case 0x2f:
        case 0x30:
        case 0x31:
        case 0x32:
        case 0x33:
        case 0x34:
        case 0x35:
        case 0x36:
        case 0x37:
        case 0x38:
        case 0x39:
        case 0x3a:
        case 0x3b:
        case 0x3c:
        case 0x3d:
        case 0x3e:
            pos = skipMemArg(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `memory-op 0x${op.toString(16)}`,
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x41: {
            const x = readS32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const x = readU64Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x43:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "f32.const",
                },
                next: pos + 4,
            };

        case 0x44:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "f64.const",
                },
                next: pos + 8,
            };

        default:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text:
                        `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
                },
                next: pos,
            };
    }
}

function findCodeSection() {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];

        const s = readU32Leb(p);
        p = s.next;

        if (id === 10) {
            return {
                start: p,
                size: s.value,
            };
        }

        p += s.value;
    }

    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function getBodies() {
    const sec = findCodeSection();

    let p = sec.start;

    const count = readU32Leb(p);
    p = count.next;

    const bodies = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const size = readU32Leb(p);
        p = size.next;

        bodies.push({
            funcIndex: IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + i,
            start: p,
            end: p + size.value,
        });

        p += size.value;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function decodeBody(start: number, end: number): Instr[] {
    let p = start;

    const locals = readU32Leb(p);
    p = locals.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < locals.value; i++) {
        const count = readU32Leb(p);
        p = count.next;

        p++;
    }

    const result: Instr[] = [];

    while (p < end) {
        const d = decodeInstruction(p);

        if (d.next <= p) {
            throw new Error(
                `Decoder stalled at 0x${p.toString(16)}`,
            );
        }

        result.push(d.instr);
        p = d.next;
    }

    return result;
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

export async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" BACKTRACE FUNCTION 4815 LOCAL 2");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();

    console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length}`);
    console.log(`Function  : ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
    console.log(`Target    : local ${TARGET_LOCAL}`);
    console.log();

    const bodies = getBodies();

    const body = bodies.find(
        x => x.funcIndex === TARGET_FUNC,
    );

    if (!body) {
        throw new Error(
            `Function ${TARGET_FUNC} not found`,
        );
    }

    const ins = decodeBody(
        body.start,
        body.end,
    );

    console.log(
        `Range : ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end - 1)}`,
    );

    console.log(
        `Instructions : ${ins.length}`,
    );

    console.log();

    const defs: {
        index: number;
        type: string;
        offset: number;
    }[] = [];

    const uses: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
        const x = ins[i];

        if (
            (x.opcode === 0x21 ||
                x.opcode === 0x22) &&
            x.local === TARGET_LOCAL
        ) {
            defs.push({
                index: i,
                type:
                    x.opcode === 0x21
                        ? "local.set"
                        : "local.tee",
                offset: x.offset,
            });
        }

        if (
            x.opcode === 0x20 &&
            x.local === TARGET_LOCAL
        ) {
            uses.push(i);
        }
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" LOCAL 2");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();

    console.log(`Definitions : ${defs.length}`);
    console.log(`Uses        : ${uses.length}`);
    console.log();

    for (const d of defs) {
        console.log(
            `${d.type} @ ${hex(d.offset)}`,
        );
    }

    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" DEFINITIONS OF LOCAL 2");
    console.log("============================================================");

    if (defs.length === 0) {
        console.log();
        console.log(
            "NO local.set/local.tee for local2.",
        );
        console.log(
            "Therefore local2 is a function parameter.",
        );
    }

    for (const d of defs) {
        const from = Math.max(
            0,
            d.index - 20,
        );

        const to = Math.min(
            ins.length,
            d.index + 4,
        );

        console.log();

        for (let i = from; i < to; i++) {
            console.log(
                `  ${hex(ins[i].offset)} ${ins[i].text}`,
            );
        }
    }

    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" USES OF LOCAL 2");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (const idx of uses) {
        console.log();

        console.log(
            `LOCAL2 USE @ ${hex(ins[idx].offset)}`,
        );

        const from = Math.max(
            0,
            idx - 12,
        );

        const to = Math.min(
            ins.length,
            idx + 12,
        );

        for (let i = from; i < to; i++) {
            console.log(
                `  ${hex(ins[i].offset)} ${ins[i].text}`,
            );
        }
    }

    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" PARAMETER FLOW");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();

    if (defs.length === 0) {
        console.log(
            "4815.local2 is never reassigned.",
        );

        console.log(
            "Therefore every use of local2 refers directly",
        );

        console.log(
            "to parameter #2 of Function 4815.",
        );
    }

    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" CALLS RECEIVING LOCAL 2");
    console.log("============================================================");

    let callCount = 0;
    let indirectCount = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
        const x = ins[i];

        if (
            x.opcode !== 0x10 &&
            x.opcode !== 0x11
        ) {
            continue;
        }

        const from = Math.max(
            0,
            i - 12,
        );

        let found = false;

        for (let j = from; j < i; j++) {
            if (
                ins[j].opcode === 0x20 &&
                ins[j].local === TARGET_LOCAL
            ) {
                found = true;
            }
        }

        if (!found) continue;

        if (x.opcode === 0x10) {
            callCount++;
        } else {
            indirectCount++;
        }

        console.log();

        console.log(
            `CALL @ ${hex(x.offset)} : ${x.text}`,
        );

        for (
            let j = from;
            j <= i;
            j++
        ) {
            console.log(
                `  ${hex(ins[j].offset)} ${ins[j].text}`,
            );
        }
    }

    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SUMMARY");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();

    console.log(
        `local2 definitions : ${defs.length}`,
    );

    console.log(
        `local2 uses        : ${uses.length}`,
    );

    console.log(
        `calls near local2  : ${callCount}`,
    );

    console.log(
        `indirect near it   : ${indirectCount}`,
    );

    console.log();

    if (defs.length === 0) {
        console.log(
            "RESULT: 4815.local2 = parameter #2.",
        );

        console.log(
            "Therefore the pointer/value written as 114 by",
        );

        console.log(
            "Function 2774 originates outside Function 4815.",
        );

        console.log();

        console.log(
            "NEXT TARGET: find direct callers of Function 4815",
        );

        console.log(
            "and reconstruct their argument #2.",
        );
    }

    console.log();
}

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return [v >>> 0, p];
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;
let code = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const [n, q] = u(p);
  p = q;

  if (id === 10) {
    code = p;
    break;
  }

  p += n;
}

p = code;

const [count, q0] = u(p);
p = q0;

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

  const [size, q] = u(p);
  p = q;

  const func = 83 + i;
  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  for (let x = start; x < end - 1; x++) {

    if (b[x] !== 0x10) continue;

    const [target, q2] = u(x + 1);

    if (target !== 656) continue;

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================");
    console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
    console.log(`CALL656 @ 0x${x.toString(16)}`);
    console.log("--------------------------------------------");

    let z = Math.max(start, x - 100);

    while (z < x) {

      const op = b[z];
      let s = "";
      let next = z + 1;

      if (op === 0x20) {
        const [v,q] = u(z+1);
        s = `local.get L${v}`;
        next=q;
      }
      else if (op === 0x21) {
        const [v,q] = u(z+1);
        s = `local.set L${v}`;
        next=q;
      }
      else if (op === 0x22) {
        const [v,q] = u(z+1);
        s = `local.tee L${v}`;
        next=q;
      }
      else if (op === 0x41) {
        const [v,q] = u(z+1);
        s = `i32.const ${v} (0x${v.toString(16)})`;
        next=q;
      }
      else if (op === 0x28) {
        const [a,q1] = u(z+1);
        const [off,q2] = u(q1);
        s = `i32.load offset=${off}`;
        next=q2;
      }
      else if (op === 0x36) {
        const [a,q1] = u(z+1);
        const [off,q2] = u(q1);
        s = `i32.store offset=${off}`;
        next=q2;
      }
      else if (op === 0x6a) s = "i32.add";
      else if (op === 0x6b) s = "i32.sub";
      else if (op === 0x6c) s = "i32.mul";
      else if (op === 0x71) s = "i32.and";
      else if (op === 0x72) s = "i32.or";
      else if (op === 0x74) s = "i32.shl";
      else if (op === 0x76) s = "i32.shr_u";
      else if (op === 0x45) s = "i32.eqz";
      else if (op === 0x46) s = "i32.eq";
      else if (op === 0x47) s = "i32.ne";
      else if (op === 0x4a) s = "i32.lt_s";
      else if (op === 0x4b) s = "i32.lt_u";
      else if (op === 0x4e) s = "i32.gt_u";
      else if (op === 0x4f) s = "i32.le_u";
      else {
        z++;
        continue;
      }

      console.log(`0x${z.toString(16)} ${s}`);
      z=next;
    }

    console.log(`>>> CALL 656`);
  }

  p=end;
}

console.log("");

FILE: scripts\trace656FourArgs.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return [v >>> 0, p];
    s += 7;
  }
}

function s32(v) {
  return v >>> 0;
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16);
}

function readInstr(pos) {
  const op = b[pos];

  if (op === 0x20) {
    const [x, q] = u(pos + 1);
    return { text: `local.get L${x}`, q, kind: "value", value: `L${x}` };
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const [x, q] = u(pos + 1);
    return {
      text: `i32.const ${x} (${hex(x)})`,
      q,
      kind: "value",
      value: x
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x21) {
    const [x, q] = u(pos + 1);
    return { text: `local.set L${x}`, q, kind: "other" };
  }

  if (op === 0x22) {
    const [x, q] = u(pos + 1);
    return { text: `local.tee L${x}`, q, kind: "value", value: `L${x}` };
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const [x, q] = u(pos + 1);
    return { text: `call ${x}`, q, kind: "call", target: x };
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const [type, q1] = u(pos + 1);
    const [table, q2] = u(q1);
    return {
      text: `call_indirect type=${type} table=${table}`,
      q: q2,
      kind: "call_indirect",
      type,
      table
    };
  }

  return {
    text: `opcode=0x${op.toString(16)}`,
    q: pos + 1,
    kind: "other"
  };
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;
let funcBodies = [];

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const [size, q] = u(p);
  p = q;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  if (id === 3) {
    const [count, q0] = u(p);
    p = q0;

    // Function section is only needed to align body numbers.
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const [x, q1] = u(p);
      p = q1;
    }
  }

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    codeEnd = end;

    const [count, q0] = u(p);
    p = q0;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const bodyStart = p;

      const [bodySize, q1] = u(p);
      p = q1;

      const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

      funcBodies.push({
        func: 83 + i,
        start: p,
        end: bodyEnd
      });

      p = bodyEnd;
    }
  }

  p = end;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 656 FOUR-ARG CALL TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

for (const f of funcBodies) {

  let p = f.start;

  const [localGroups, q0] = u(p);
  p = q0;

  // Skip local declarations.
  for (let i = 0; i < localGroups; i++) {
    const [, q1] = u(p);
    p = q1;
    p++;
  }

  const instr = [];

  while (p < f.end) {
    const x = readInstr(p);
    instr.push({
      pos: p,
      ...x
    });
    p = x.q;
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < instr.length; i++) {

    const x = instr[i];

    if (x.kind !== "call" || x.target !== 656)
      continue;

    console.log(`FUNC ${f.func}`);
    console.log(`CALL656 @ ${hex(x.pos)}`);

    // Find the four immediately preceding stack-producing values.
    const vals = [];

    for (let j = i - 1; j >= 0 && vals.length < 4; j--) {

      const y = instr[j];

      if (y.kind === "value") {
        vals.unshift(y.value);
      }
    }

    console.log(
      `ARG0 = ${vals[0] ?? "UNKNOWN"}`
    );
    console.log(
      `ARG1 = ${vals[1] ?? "UNKNOWN"}`
    );
    console.log(
      `ARG2 = ${vals[2] ?? "UNKNOWN"}`
    );
    console.log(
      `ARG3 = ${vals[3] ?? "UNKNOWN"}`
    );

    console.log("--------------------------------------------");

    // Show local instruction window around CALL656.
    const from = Math.max(0, i - 8);
    const to = Math.min(instr.length, i + 2);

    for (let k = from; k < to; k++) {
      console.log(
        `${hex(instr[k].pos)}  ${instr[k].text}`
      );
    }

    console.log("");
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace656InternalChain.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 656;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readU32LE(pos: number) {
    return wasm.readUInt32LE(pos);
}

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;
        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

function findSection(id: number) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const sectionId = wasm[p++];
        const s = readULEB(p);
        const size = s.value;
        p = s.next;

        if (sectionId === id) {
            return {
                start: p,
                end: p + size
            };
        }

        p += size;
    }

    throw new Error(`SECTION ${id} NOT FOUND`);
}

function parseTypeSection() {
    const sec = findSection(1);
    let p = sec.start;

    const c = readULEB(p);
    const count = c.value;
    p = c.next;

    const types: any[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const form = wasm[p++];

        if (form !== 0x60) {
            throw new Error(`Unexpected type form 0x${form.toString(16)}`);
        }

        const pc = readULEB(p);
        const paramCount = pc.value;
        p = pc.next;

        const params: number[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < paramCount; j++) {
            params.push(wasm[p++]);
        }

        const rc = readULEB(p);
        const resultCount = rc.value;
        p = rc.next;

        const results: number[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++) {
            results.push(wasm[p++]);
        }

        types.push({ params, results });
    }

    return types;
}

function parseFunctionSection() {
    const sec = findSection(3);
    let p = sec.start;

    const c = readULEB(p);
    const count = c.value;
    p = c.next;

    const typeIndices: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const x = readULEB(p);
        typeIndices.push(x.value);
        p = x.next;
    }

    return typeIndices;
}

function parseCodeBodies() {
    const sec = findSection(10);
    let p = sec.start;

    const c = readULEB(p);
    const count = c.value;
    p = c.next;

    const bodies: any[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const bs = readULEB(p);
        const size = bs.value;
        p = bs.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = p + size;

        const localDeclCount = readULEB(p);
        p = localDeclCount.next;

        const locals: number[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < localDeclCount.value; j++) {
            const n = readULEB(p);
            p = n.next;

            const type = wasm[p++];

            for (let k = 0; k < n.value; k++) {
                locals.push(type);
            }
        }

        const codeStart = p;

        bodies.push({
            ordinal: i,
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            codeStart,
            locals
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function funcGlobalFromDefinedOrd(ord: number) {
    return 83 + ord;
}

function getFunctionBody(globalFunc: number, bodies: any[]) {
    const definedOrd = globalFunc - 83;

    if (definedOrd < 0 || definedOrd >= bodies.length) {
        throw new Error(`Function ${globalFunc} is not a defined function`);
    }

    return bodies[definedOrd];
}

function decodeBytes(start: number, end: number) {
    let p = start;

    while (p < end) {
        const at = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        let text = `0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

        switch (op) {
            case 0x0b:
                text += "  end";
                break;

            case 0x0f:
                text += "  return";
                break;

            case 0x10: {
                const x = readULEB(p);
                p = x.next;
                text += `  call ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x11: {
                const t = readULEB(p);
                p = t.next;

                const table = readULEB(p);
                p = table.next;

                text += `  call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x20: {
                const x = readULEB(p);
                p = x.next;
                text += `  local.get ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x21: {
                const x = readULEB(p);
                p = x.next;
                text += `  local.set ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x22: {
                const x = readULEB(p);
                p = x.next;
                text += `  local.tee ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x41: {
                const x = readSLEB(p);
                p = x.next;
                text += `  i32.const ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x42: {
                const x = readSLEB(p);
                p = x.next;
                text += `  i64.const ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x28:
            case 0x36: {
                const align = readULEB(p);
                p = align.next;

                const off = readULEB(p);
                p = off.next;

                text += `  ${op === 0x28 ? "i32.load" : "i32.store"} align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x04:
                text += "  if";
                break;

            case 0x05:
                text += "  else";
                break;

            case 0x02:
                text += "  block";
                break;

            case 0x03:
                text += "  loop";
                break;

            default:
                break;
        }

        console.log(text);
    }
}

const types = parseTypeSection();
const functionTypes = parseFunctionSection();
const bodies = parseCodeBodies();

const body = getFunctionBody(TARGET_FUNC, bodies);
const typeIndex = functionTypes[TARGET_FUNC - 83];
const type = types[typeIndex];

console.log("TRACE INTERNAL CHAIN OF FUNC 656");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC = ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log(`DEFINED ORD = ${TARGET_FUNC - 83}`);
console.log(`TYPE INDEX  = ${typeIndex}`);
console.log(`PARAMS      = [${type.params.join(", ")}]`);
console.log(`RESULTS     = [${type.results.join(", ")}]`);
console.log(`BODY        = 0x${body.bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${body.bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
console.log(`CODE        = 0x${body.codeStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${body.bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);

console.log("");
console.log("FUNC 656 RAW CODE");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

decodeBytes(body.codeStart, body.bodyEnd);

console.log("");
console.log("IMPORTANT INDIRECT CALLS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = body.codeStart;

while (p < body.bodyEnd) {
    const at = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    if (op === 0x11) {
        const t = readULEB(p);
        p = t.next;

        const table = readULEB(p);
        p = table.next;

        console.log(
            `call_indirect @ 0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
            `TYPE=${t.value} TABLE=${table.value}`
        );

        if (t.value < types.length) {
            const tt = types[t.value];

            console.log(
                `  TYPE PARAMS = [${tt.params.join(", ")}] ` +
                `RESULTS = [${tt.results.join(", ")}]`
            );
        }

        continue;
    }

    switch (op) {
        case 0x10: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x20:
        case 0x21:
        case 0x22: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x41:
        case 0x42: {
            const x = readSLEB(p);
            p = x.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x28:
        case 0x36: {
            const a = readULEB(p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = readULEB(p);
            p = o.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x02:
        case 0x03:
        case 0x04: {
            const b = wasm[p++];
            if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
                void b;
            }
            break;
        }

        default:
            break;
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace656Stack.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return [v >>> 0, p];
    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16);
}

let p = 8;
let code = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const [n, q] = u(p);
  p = q;

  if (id === 10) {
    code = p;
    break;
  }

  p += n;
}

p = code;

const [count, q0] = u(p);
p = q0;

console.log("FUNC656 CALL STACK TRACE");
console.log("============================================");

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

  const [size, q] = u(p);
  p = q;

  const func = 83 + i;
  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  let stack = [];

  for (let x = start; x < end; ) {

    const op = b[x];
    const at = x;

    // local.get
    if (op === 0x20) {
      const [v, q] = u(x + 1);
      stack.push(`L${v}`);
      x = q;
      continue;
    }

    // i32.const
    if (op === 0x41) {
      const [v, q] = u(x + 1);
      stack.push(`${v}(${hex(v)})`);
      x = q;
      continue;
    }

    // local.set
    if (op === 0x21) {
      const [v, q] = u(x + 1);
      const value = stack.pop() ?? "?";
      // Keep symbolic knowledge only conceptually.
      x = q;
      continue;
    }

    // local.tee
    if (op === 0x22) {
      const [v, q] = u(x + 1);
      const value = stack.length ? stack[stack.length - 1] : "?";
      x = q;
      continue;
    }

    // i32.load
    if (op === 0x28) {
      const [align, q1] = u(x + 1);
      const [off, q2] = u(q1);

      const base = stack.pop() ?? "?";
      stack.push(`MEM[${base}+${off}]`);

      x = q2;
      continue;
    }

    // i32.add
    if (op === 0x6a) {
      const b1 = stack.pop() ?? "?";
      const a1 = stack.pop() ?? "?";
      stack.push(`(${a1}+${b1})`);
      x++;
      continue;
    }

    // i32.sub
    if (op === 0x6b) {
      const b1 = stack.pop() ?? "?";
      const a1 = stack.pop() ?? "?";
      stack.push(`(${a1}-${b1})`);
      x++;
      continue;
    }

    // i32.mul
    if (op === 0x6c) {
      const b1 = stack.pop() ?? "?";
      const a1 = stack.pop() ?? "?";
      stack.push(`(${a1}*${b1})`);
      x++;
      continue;
    }

    // i32.and
    if (op === 0x71) {
      const b1 = stack.pop() ?? "?";
      const a1 = stack.pop() ?? "?";
      stack.push(`(${a1}&${b1})`);
      x++;
      continue;
    }

    // i32.or
    if (op === 0x72) {
      const b1 = stack.pop() ?? "?";
      const a1 = stack.pop() ?? "?";
      stack.push(`(${a1}|${b1})`);
      x++;
      continue;
    }

    // i32.shl
    if (op === 0x74) {
      const b1 = stack.pop() ?? "?";
      const a1 = stack.pop() ?? "?";
      stack.push(`(${a1}<<${b1})`);
      x++;
      continue;
    }

    // i32.shr_u
    if (op === 0x76) {
      const b1 = stack.pop() ?? "?";
      const a1 = stack.pop() ?? "?";
      stack.push(`(${a1}>>>${b1})`);
      x++;
      continue;
    }

    // CALL
    if (op === 0x10) {
      const [target, q] = u(x + 1);

      if (target === 656) {

        const a1 = stack.length >= 2
          ? stack[stack.length - 2]
          : "?";

        const a2 = stack.length >= 1
          ? stack[stack.length - 1]
          : "?";

        console.log("");
        console.log("--------------------------------------------");
        console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
        console.log(`CALL656 @ ${hex(at)}`);
        console.log(`ARG0 = ${a1}`);
        console.log(`ARG1 = ${a2}`);
        console.log("--------------------------------------------");
      }

      x = q;
      continue;
    }

    // Unknown opcode:
    // reset symbolic stack conservatively because
    // we don't know its stack effect.
    stack = [];
    x++;
  }

  p = end;
}

console.log("");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace656State.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v=0,s=0;
  while(true){
    const x=b[p++];
    v+=(x&127)*2**s;
    if(!(x&128)) return [v>>>0,p];
    s+=7;
  }
}

let p=8, code=0;

while(p<b.length){
  const id=b[p++];
  const [n,q]=u(p); p=q;
  if(id===10){ code=p; break; }
  p+=n;
}

p=code;
const [count,q0]=u(p); p=q0;

for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
  const [size,q]=u(p); p=q;

  const func=83+i;
  const start=p, end=p+size;

  if(func!==656){
    p=end;
    continue;
  }

  console.log(`FUNC 656 BODY 0x${start.toString(16)} .. 0x${end.toString(16)}`);
  console.log(`SIZE ${size}`);
  console.log("");

  for(let x=start;x<end;x++){

    let s="";

    if(b[x]===0x41){
      const [v,q]=u(x+1);
      s=`i32.const ${v} (0x${v.toString(16)})`;
      x=q-1;
    }
    else if(b[x]===0x20){
      const [v,q]=u(x+1);
      s=`local.get L${v}`;
      x=q-1;
    }
    else if(b[x]===0x21){
      const [v,q]=u(x+1);
      s=`local.set L${v}`;
      x=q-1;
    }
    else if(b[x]===0x22){
      const [v,q]=u(x+1);
      s=`local.tee L${v}`;
      x=q-1;
    }
    else if(b[x]===0x24){
      const [v,q]=u(x+1);
      s=`global.set G${v}`;
      x=q-1;
    }
    else if(b[x]===0x23){
      const [v,q]=u(x+1);
      s=`global.get G${v}`;
      x=q-1;
    }
    else if(b[x]===0x28){
      const [align,q1]=u(x+1);
      const [off,q2]=u(q1);
      s=`i32.load offset=${off}`;
      x=q2-1;
    }
    else if(b[x]===0x36){
      const [align,q1]=u(x+1);
      const [off,q2]=u(q1);
      s=`i32.store offset=${off}`;
      x=q2-1;
    }
    else if(b[x]===0x10){
      const [v,q]=u(x+1);
      s=`CALL ${v}`;
      x=q-1;
    }
    else if(b[x]===0x11){
      const [v,q1]=u(x+1);
      const [t,q2]=u(q1);
      s=`CALL_INDIRECT type=${v} table=${t}`;
      x=q2-1;
    }

    if(s) console.log(`0x${x.toString(16)} ${s}`);
  }

  break;
}

FILE: scripts\trace656To122.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return [v >>> 0, p];
    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16);
}

function instr(p) {
  const op = b[p];

  if (op === 0x20) {
    const [x,q] = u(p+1);
    return { q, kind:"push", text:`local.get L${x}`, value:`L${x}` };
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const [x,q] = u(p+1);
    return { q, kind:"push", text:`i32.const ${x} (${hex(x)})`, value:`${x}` };
  }

  if (op === 0x22) {
    const [x,q] = u(p+1);
    return { q, kind:"push", text:`local.tee L${x}`, value:`L${x}` };
  }

  if (op === 0x21) {
    const [x,q] = u(p+1);
    return { q, kind:"other", text:`local.set L${x}` };
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const [x,q] = u(p+1);
    return { q, kind:"call", target:x, text:`CALL ${x}` };
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const [t,q1] = u(p+1);
    const [tab,q2] = u(q1);
    return {
      q:q2,
      kind:"indirect",
      type:t,
      table:tab,
      text:`CALL_INDIRECT type=${t} table=${tab}`
    };
  }

  return {
    q:p+1,
    kind:"other",
    text:`opcode=0x${op.toString(16)}`
  };
}

// Locate code section
let p = 8;
let body656 = null;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const [size,q] = u(p);
  p = q;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  if (id === 10) {
    const [count,q0] = u(p);
    p = q0;

    for (let i=0;i<count;i++) {
      const [bs,q1] = u(p);
      p = q1;

      const be = p + bs;
      const func = 83 + i;

      if (func === 656) {
        body656 = {start:p,end:be};
      }

      p = be;
    }
  }

  p = end;
}

if (!body656)
  throw new Error("FUNC656 not found");

p = body656.start;

const [groups,q0] = u(p);
p = q0;

// Skip local declarations
for (let i=0;i<groups;i++) {
  const [,q1] = u(p);
  p = q1;
  p++;
}

const list = [];

while (p < body656.end) {
  const x = instr(p);
  list.push({pos:p,...x});
  p = x.q;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 656 -> CALL 122 STACK TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

for (let i=0;i<list.length;i++) {

  if (list[i].kind === "call" &&
      list[i].target === 122) {

    console.log(`CALL 122 @ ${hex(list[i].pos)}`);
    console.log("--------------------------------------------");

    const stack = [];

    for (let j=0;j<i;j++) {

      const x = list[j];

      if (x.kind === "push") {
        stack.push(x.value);
      }

      // Simple stack effect for CALL 122:
      // (i32,i32,i32) -> i32
      if (x.kind === "call" && x.target === 122) {
        stack.splice(-3,3);
        stack.push("RESULT122");
      }

      // Known indirect call:
      // type 4 = four args -> result
      if (x.kind === "indirect" && x.type === 4) {
        stack.splice(-4,4);
        stack.push("INDIRECT_RESULT");
      }

      // local.set consumes one stack item
      if (x.text?.startsWith("local.set")) {
        stack.pop();
      }

      // local.tee leaves result on stack
    }

    console.log("STACK BEFORE CALL122:");
    console.log(stack.slice(-10).join(" , "));
    console.log("");

    console.log("LOCAL/PUSH SEQUENCE:");
    for (let j=Math.max(0,i-12);j<i;j++) {
      console.log(
        `${hex(list[j].pos)}  ${list[j].text}`
      );
    }

    console.log("");
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\trace656Type4Index.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const IMPORTS = 83;

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(v: number) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function section(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sid = wasm[p++];
    const z = uleb(p);
    p = z.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + z.value;

    if (sid === id) return { start, end };

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`section ${id} not found`);
}

/* ================= TYPE SECTION ================= */

const ts = section(1);

let p = ts.start;
const tc = uleb(p);
p = tc.next;

const types: { params: number[]; results: number[] }[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < tc.value; i++) {
  const form = wasm[p++];

  if (form !== 0x60)
    throw new Error(`bad type form @ ${hex(p - 1)}`);

  const pc = uleb(p);
  p = pc.next;

  const params: number[] = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++)
    params.push(wasm[p++]);

  const rc = uleb(p);
  p = rc.next;

  const results: number[] = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++)
    results.push(wasm[p++]);

  types.push({ params, results });
}

/* ================= FUNCTION SECTION ================= */

const fsx = section(3);

p = fsx.start;

const fc = uleb(p);
p = fc.next;

const funcTypes: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < fc.value; i++) {
  const x = uleb(p);
  funcTypes.push(x.value);
  p = x.next;
}

/* ================= CODE SECTION ================= */

const cs = section(10);

p = cs.start;

const cc = uleb(p);
p = cc.next;

const bodies = new Map<number, {
  start: number;
  end: number;
  type: number;
}>();

for (let i = 0; i < cc.value; i++) {
  const z = uleb(p);
  p = z.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = start + z.value;

  bodies.set(IMPORTS + i, {
    start,
    end,
    type: funcTypes[i]
  });

  p = end;
}

/* ================= FUNC 656 ================= */

const func = 656;
const body = bodies.get(func)!;
const type = types[body.type];

console.log("TRACE FUNC 656 TYPE-4 INDEX");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`FUNC        = ${func}`);
console.log(`TYPE        = ${body.type}`);
console.log(`PARAMS      = ${type.params.length}`);
console.log(`RESULTS     = ${type.results.length}`);
console.log(`BODY        = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end - 1)}`);
console.log("============================================================");

/*
 * Symbolic locals.
 */

const locals: string[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < type.params.length; i++)
  locals.push(`PARAM(local${i})`);

/*
 * Decode local declarations.
 */

p = body.start;

const ldc = uleb(p);
p = ldc.next;

for (let i = 0; i < ldc.value; i++) {
  const n = uleb(p);
  p = n.next;

  const vt = wasm[p++];

  for (let j = 0; j < n.value; j++)
    locals.push(`LOCAL(${locals.length})`);
}

const stack: string[] = [];

function push(v: string) {
  stack.push(v);
}

function pop() {
  return stack.length
    ? stack.pop()!
    : "STACK_UNDERFLOW";
}

function dumpStack() {
  console.log(
    "STACK = [" + stack.join(", ") + "]"
  );
}

while (p < body.end) {

  const opPos = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  switch (op) {

    case 0x41: { // i32.const
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      push(`CONST(${x.value})`);

      break;
    }

    case 0x20: { // local.get
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      push(locals[x.value] ?? `LOCAL(${x.value})`);

      break;
    }

    case 0x21: { // local.set
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const v = pop();
      locals[x.value] = v;

      console.log(
        `${hex(opPos)} local.set ${x.value} <- ${v}`
      );

      break;
    }

    case 0x22: { // local.tee
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const v = pop();

      locals[x.value] = v;
      push(v);

      console.log(
        `${hex(opPos)} local.tee ${x.value} <- ${v}`
      );

      break;
    }

    case 0x28: { // i32.load
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      const addr = pop();

      push(
        `LOAD(${addr}+${hex(o.value)})`
      );

      break;
    }

    case 0x45: { // i32.eqz
      const v = pop();

      push(`EQZ(${v})`);

      break;
    }

    case 0x47: { // i32.ne
      const b = pop();
      const a = pop();

      push(`NE(${a},${b})`);

      break;
    }

    case 0x0d: { // br_if
      const depth = uleb(p);
      p = depth.next;

      const cond = pop();

      console.log(
        `${hex(opPos)} br_if ${depth.value} condition=${cond}`
      );

      break;
    }

    case 0x04: { // if
      const cond = pop();

      console.log();
      console.log(
        `${hex(opPos)} IF condition=${cond}`
      );

      dumpStack();

      break;
    }

    case 0x11: { // call_indirect

      const ti = uleb(p);
      p = ti.next;

      const table = uleb(p);
      p = table.next;

      const target = types[ti.value];

      console.log();
      console.log(
        "************************************************************"
      );

      console.log(
        `CALL_INDIRECT @ ${hex(opPos)}`
      );

      console.log(`TYPE  = ${ti.value}`);
      console.log(`TABLE = ${table.value}`);

      console.log(
        `PARAMS = ${target.params.length}`
      );

      console.log(
        `RESULTS = ${target.results.length}`
      );

      const args: string[] = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < target.params.length; i++)
        args.unshift(pop());

      const index = pop();

      console.log("ARGS:");

      args.forEach((x, i) => {
        console.log(`  ARG${i} = ${x}`);
      });

      console.log(
        `INDEX = ${index}`
      );

      console.log(
        `INDEX SOURCE = ${
          index.startsWith("PARAM")
            ? "FUNCTION PARAMETER"
            : index.startsWith("CALL_RESULT")
            ? "PREVIOUS INDIRECT CALL"
            : index
        }`
      );

      if (target.results.length > 0) {

        for (let i = 0; i < target.results.length; i++) {

          const r =
            `CALL_RESULT(type=${ti.value})`;

          push(r);

          console.log(
            `RESULT${i} = ${r}`
          );
        }
      }

      dumpStack();

      console.log(
        "************************************************************"
      );

      break;
    }

    case 0x10: { // direct call

      const fi = uleb(p);
      p = fi.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(opPos)} CALL direct FUNC ${fi.value}`
      );

      break;
    }

    case 0x0f: { // return

      console.log();
      console.log(
        `${hex(opPos)} RETURN`
      );

      dumpStack();

      break;
    }

    default:
      break;
  }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL LOCALS");
console.log("============================================================");

locals.forEach((v, i) => {
  console.log(`local${i} = ${v}`);
});

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTION");
console.log("============================================================");
