FILE: scripts\trace6618_6619Chain.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const path =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(path);

const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGETS = new Set([6618, 6619]);

function u32(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];

        v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

        if (!(x & 0x80))
            return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

        s += 7;
    }
}

function s32(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];

        v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

        if (!(x & 0x80)) {
            if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
                v |= (~0 << (s + 7));

            return { value: v | 0, next: p };
        }

        s += 7;
    }
}

function skipBlockType(p) {

    const x = b[p];

    if (
        x === 0x40 ||
        x === 0x7f ||
        x === 0x7e ||
        x === 0x7d ||
        x === 0x7c
    )
        return p + 1;

    return s32(p).next;
}

function skip(p) {

    const op = b[p++];

    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04)
        return skipBlockType(p);

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d)
        return u32(p).next;

    if (op === 0x0e) {

        const n = u32(p);
        p = n.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++)
            p = u32(p).next;

        return u32(p).next;
    }

    if (op === 0x10)
        return u32(p).next;

    if (op === 0x11) {

        const a = u32(p);
        const c = u32(a.next);

        return c.next;
    }

    if (op >= 0x20 && op <= 0x24)
        return u32(p).next;

    if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {

        const a = u32(p);
        const c = u32(a.next);

        return c.next;
    }

    if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40)
        return p + 1;

    if (op === 0x41)
        return s32(p).next;

    if (op === 0x42) {

        while (true) {

            const x = b[p++];

            if (!(x & 0x80))
                return p;
        }
    }

    if (op === 0x43)
        return p + 4;

    if (op === 0x44)
        return p + 8;

    return p;
}

function decode(p) {

    const op = b[p];

    if (op === 0x20) {

        const r = u32(p + 1);

        return {
            text: `local.get ${r.value}`,
            next: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {

        const r = u32(p + 1);

        return {
            text: `local.set ${r.value}`,
            next: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {

        const r = u32(p + 1);

        return {
            text: `local.tee ${r.value}`,
            next: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {

        const r = s32(p + 1);

        return {
            text: `i32.const ${r.value}`,
            next: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {

        const r = u32(p + 1);

        return {
            text: `CALL ${r.value}`,
            next: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x36) {

        const a = u32(p + 1);
        const c = u32(a.next);

        return {
            text:
                `MEMORY 0x${op.toString(16)}` +
                ` align=${a.value}` +
                ` offset=${c.value}`,
            next: c.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {

        const a = u32(p + 1);
        const c = u32(a.next);

        return {
            text:
                `CALL_INDIRECT type=${a.value}` +
                ` table=${c.value}`,
            next: c.next
        };
    }

    return {
        text: `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`,
        next: p + 1
    };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse code section.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
const bodies = new Map();

while (p < b.length) {

    const section = b[p++];

    const sz = u32(p);
    p = sz.next;

    const end = p + sz.value;

    if (section === 10) {

        const count = u32(p);
        p = count.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

            const bodyStart = p;

            const bs = u32(p);
            p = bs.next;

            const bodyEnd = p + bs.value;

            const locals = u32(p);
            p = locals.next;

            for (let j = 0; j < locals.value; j++) {

                const n = u32(p);
                p = n.next + 1;
            }

            bodies.set(IMPORTED + i, {
                start: bodyStart,
                instr: p,
                end: bodyEnd
            });

            p = bodyEnd;
        }
    }

    p = end;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Trace exact CALL 446 -> CALL 248 chains.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 6618 / 6619 : 446 -> 248 CHAIN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const fn of TARGETS) {

    const f = bodies.get(fn);

    if (!f) {
        console.log(`FUNC ${fn} NOT FOUND`);
        continue;
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(`============================================================`);
    console.log(`FUNC ${fn}`);
    console.log(`BODY 0x${f.start.toString(16)} .. 0x${f.end.toString(16)}`);
    console.log(`============================================================`);

    let q = f.instr;
    const instructions = [];

    while (q < f.end) {

        const d = decode(q);

        instructions.push({
            pos: q,
            text: d.text,
            next: d.next
        });

        q = d.next;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {

        const ins = instructions[i];

        if (
            ins.text === "CALL 446" ||
            ins.text === "CALL 248"
        ) {

            console.log("");
            console.log(
                `>>> ${ins.text} @ 0x${ins.pos.toString(16)}`
            );

            console.log("--------------------------------------------");

            const from = Math.max(0, i - 12);
            const to = Math.min(
                instructions.length,
                i + 8
            );

            for (let j = from; j < to; j++) {

                const x = instructions[j];

                const mark =
                    x.pos === ins.pos ? " <===" : "";

                console.log(
                    `0x${x.pos.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  ` +
                    `${x.text}${mark}`
                );
            }
        }
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace6991Family.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 6991;

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function findCode() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const r = uleb(p);
    const size = r.value;
    p = r.next;

    if (id === 10)
      return { start: p, end: p + size };

    p += size;
  }

  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function getBody(target) {
  const sec = findCode();

  let p = sec.start;

  const c = uleb(p);
  p = c.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    const size = r.value;
    p = r.next;

    const func = 83 + i;

    if (func === target) {
      return {
        func,
        start: p,
        end: p + size
      };
    }

    p += size;
  }

  throw new Error(`FUNC ${target} not found`);
}

function decode(pos) {
  const op = wasm[pos];

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = uleb(pos + 1);
    return {
      text: `i32.const ${r.value} (0x${r.value.toString(16)})`,
      next: r.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x20) {
    const r = uleb(pos + 1);
    return {
      text: `local.get ${r.value}`,
      next: r.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x21) {
    const r = uleb(pos + 1);
    return {
      text: `local.set ${r.value}`,
      next: r.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x22) {
    const r = uleb(pos + 1);
    return {
      text: `local.tee ${r.value}`,
      next: r.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x28) {
    const a = uleb(pos + 1);
    const o = uleb(a.next);

    return {
      text: `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`,
      next: o.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x36) {
    const a = uleb(pos + 1);
    const o = uleb(a.next);

    return {
      text: `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`,
      next: o.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x3a) {
    const a = uleb(pos + 1);
    const o = uleb(a.next);

    return {
      text: `i32.store8 align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`,
      next: o.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = uleb(pos + 1);

    return {
      text: `call ${r.value}`,
      next: r.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const t = uleb(pos + 1);
    const table = uleb(t.next);

    return {
      text: `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`,
      next: table.next
    };
  }

  const names = {
    0x00: "unreachable",
    0x0b: "end",
    0x0f: "return",
    0x1a: "drop",
    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.lt_u",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x6c: "i32.mul",
    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor"
  };

  return {
    text: names[op] || `UNKNOWN opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
    next: pos + 1
  };
}

const body = getBody(TARGET);

console.log("FUNC 6991 FAMILY TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("========================================================================");
console.log(
  `BODY : 0x${body.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${body.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
);
console.log(`SIZE : ${body.end - body.start}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = body.start;

while (p < body.end) {
  const pos = p;
  const ins = decode(p);
  p = ins.next;

  /*
   * Print the entire function because we need
   * control-flow context around 0x195348.
   */
  console.log(
    `0x${pos.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${ins.text}`
  );
}

console.log("========================================================================");
console.log("DONE");

FILE: scripts\trace6993_6995To3317.js

const fs = require("fs");

const file = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const targetFuncs = new Set([6993, 6995]);

const buf = fs.readFileSync(file);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// ULEB128
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function readULEB(pos) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Read WASM sections
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let pos = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeSize = 0;

while (pos < buf.length) {
    const sectionId = buf[pos++];

    const sz = readULEB(pos);
    const sectionSize = sz.value;
    pos = sz.next;

    const payloadStart = pos;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = payloadStart;
        codeSize = sectionSize;
        break;
    }

    pos = payloadEnd;
}

if (!codeStart) {
    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Function body map
// Imported functions = 83
// ------------------------------------------------------------
const IMPORTED = 83;

let p = codeStart;

const bodyCountInfo = readULEB(p);
const bodyCount = bodyCountInfo.value;
p = bodyCountInfo.next;

const bodies = [];

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodyCount; bodyIndex++) {

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
    const bodySizePos = p;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    const funcIndex = IMPORTED + bodyIndex;

    bodies.push({
        funcIndex,
        bodyStart,
        bodyEnd,
        bodySize,
        bodySizePos
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
}

function getBody(funcIndex) {
    return bodies.find(x => x.funcIndex === funcIndex);
}

function hex(n, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function dumpAround(at, radius = 100) {
    const start = Math.max(0, at - radius);
    const end = Math.min(buf.length, at + radius);

    let out = [];

    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
        const rowEnd = Math.min(end, p + 16);

        let bytes = [];

        for (let q = p; q < rowEnd; q++) {
            bytes.push(buf[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
        }

        out.push(
            hex(p) + "  " +
            bytes.join(" ")
        );
    }

    return out.join("\n");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find CALL opcode 0x10 with target 3317
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function findCallsTo3317(body) {

    const hits = [];

    for (let p = body.bodyStart; p < body.bodyEnd; p++) {

        if (buf[p] !== 0x10) continue;

        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        if (r.value === 3317) {
            hits.push({
                addr: p,
                next: r.next
            });
        }
    }

    return hits;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Main
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE FUNC 6993 / 6995 -> FUNC 3317");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const f of [6993, 6995]) {

    const body = getBody(f);

    if (!body) {
        console.log(`\nFUNC ${f}: BODY NOT FOUND`);
        continue;
    }

    console.log("\n------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(`FUNC ${f}`);
    console.log(`BODY : ${hex(body.bodyStart)} .. ${hex(body.bodyEnd)}`);
    console.log(`SIZE : ${body.bodySize}`);

    const hits = findCallsTo3317(body);

    for (const hit of hits) {

        console.log("\n============================================================");
        console.log(`CALL 3317 @ ${hex(hit.addr)}`);
        console.log("============================================================");

        const start = Math.max(body.bodyStart, hit.addr - 120);
        const end = Math.min(body.bodyEnd, hit.next + 80);

        console.log("BYTES AROUND CALL:");
        console.log(dumpAround(hit.addr, 120));

        console.log("\nCALL BYTES:");

        for (let p = hit.addr; p < hit.next; p++) {
            console.log(
                `${hex(p)}  ${buf[p].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
            );
        }

        console.log("\nAFTER CALL:");

        for (let p = hit.next; p < Math.min(body.bodyEnd, hit.next + 40); p++) {
            console.log(
                `${hex(p)}  ${buf[p].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

    functions: 0,

    lines.push(String(s));

function instructionText(ins) {
    return ins?.text ?? "";
}

function formatInstruction(ins, index) {
    const offset = Number.isFinite(ins?.start)
        ? `0x${ins.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        : "??????????";

    return `${String(index).padStart(5)} ${offset} ${instructionText(ins)}`;
}

function hasTargetConstant(ins) {
    const t = instructionText(ins);
    return t.includes(`i32.const ${TARGET} `) ||
        t === `i32.const ${TARGET}` ||
        t.includes(`i32.const ${TARGET} (`);
}

function hasStore(ins) {
    return /\bi32\.store(?:8|16)?\b|\bi64\.store\b|\bf32\.store\b|\bf64\.store\b/.test(
        instructionText(ins)
    );
}

function printContext(func, instructions, index, radius = 7, label = "") {
    const from = Math.max(0, index - radius);
    const to = Math.min(instructions.length - 1, index + radius);

    log(`\nFUNC ${func} | ${label} | instruction ${index}`);
    for (let j = from; j <= to; j++) {
        const mark = j === index ? " >>> " : "     ";
        log(mark + formatInstruction(instructions[j], j));
    }
}

    stats.functions++;

    let result;
    try {
        result = decodeFunction(func);
        stats.decoded++;
    } catch {
        stats.errors++;
        continue;
    }

    const instructions = result?.instructions;
    if (!Array.isArray(instructions)) continue;

    for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
        const text = instructionText(instructions[i]);

        if (hasTargetConstant(instructions[i])) {
            stats.targetReferences++;

            const end = Math.min(instructions.length - 1, i + 8);
            let storeIndex = -1;

            for (let j = i + 1; j <= end; j++) {
                if (hasStore(instructions[j])) {
                    storeIndex = j;
                    break;
                }
            }

            const label = storeIndex >= 0
                ? `TARGET CONSTANT; STORE CANDIDATE AT +${storeIndex - i}`
                : "TARGET CONSTANT; NO STORE IN NEXT 8 INSTRUCTIONS";

            if (storeIndex >= 0) stats.likelyWrites++;

            printContext(func, instructions, i, 7, label);
        }

        if (text.includes("i32.const 4769") ||
            text.includes("i32.const 4768") ||
            text.includes("i32.const 4770")) {
            stats.constant4769++;
            printContext(func, instructions, i, 5, "DISPATCH SLOT CONSTANT");
        }
    }
}

log("SUMMARY");
for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(stats)) {
    log(`${key}: ${value}`);
}

log("- A target constant followed by a store is only a candidate write.");
log("- Confirm the store's stack address and value before declaring a write.");
log("- A read from 0x256aac must be linked to the value written there.");
log("- Slot 4769 identifies function 7006 only if it is the actual table index.");

import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

const decoderPath = path.resolve("scripts/disassembleAddressUsers.mjs");
const outputPath = path.resolve("scripts/trace7006DispatchStores_output.txt");

const NEAR_RANGE = 0x4000;
const MAX_REPORTS = 250;

    bodies: bodies.size,
    decoded: 0,
    errors: 0,
    stores: 0,
    storesNearAddressConstants: 0,
    storesNearTargetReferences: 0,
    exactTargetConstants: 0,
    reports: 0
};

const output = [];
const reported = new Set();

function log(s = "") {
    output.push(String(s));
}

function txt(ins) {
    return ins?.text ?? "";
}

function addr(ins) {
    return Number.isFinite(ins?.start)
        ? `0x${ins.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        : "??????????";
}

function isStore(ins) {
    return /\b(?:i32|i64|f32|f64)\.store(?:8|16)?\b/.test(txt(ins));
}

function parseI32Const(s) {
    const m = s.match(/\bi32\.const\s+(-?\d+)(?:\s+\(0x[0-9a-f]+\))?/i);
    if (!m) return null;
    return Number(m[1]) >>> 0;
}

function format(func, ins, i) {
    return `FUNC ${func} | #${i} | ${addr(ins)} | ${txt(ins)}`;
}

function report(func, ins, instructions, i, reason) {
    const key = `${func}:${ins.start}:${reason}`;
    if (reported.has(key) || stats.reports >= MAX_REPORTS) return;
    reported.add(key);
    stats.reports++;

    log("\n" + "-".repeat(78));
    log(`${reason}`);
    log(format(func, ins, i));

    const from = Math.max(0, i - 12);
    const to = Math.min(instructions.length - 1, i + 5);

    for (let j = from; j <= to; j++) {
        log(`${j === i ? " >>> " : "     "}${format(func, instructions[j], j)}`);
    }
}

log("ROOSTER WASM — DISPATCH CELL STORE SCAN");
log("READ ONLY / OFFLINE / NO WASM EXECUTION");
log(`TARGET: 0x${TARGET.toString(16)} (${TARGET})`);
log(`CONTEXT WINDOW: ${WINDOW} instructions`);
log(`NEARBY ADDRESS RANGE: +/- 0x${NEAR_RANGE.toString(16)}`);
log("All matches are candidates, not confirmed writes.");
log("=".repeat(78));

for (const [func] of bodies) {
    let result;
    try {
        result = decodeFunction(func);
        stats.decoded++;
    } catch {
        stats.errors++;
        continue;
    }

    const instructions = result?.instructions;
    if (!Array.isArray(instructions)) continue;

    for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
        const current = instructions[i];
        const currentText = txt(current);

        if (currentText.includes(`i32.const ${TARGET} `) ||
            currentText === `i32.const ${TARGET}` ||
            currentText.includes(`i32.const ${TARGET} (`)) {
            stats.exactTargetConstants++;
        }

        if (!isStore(current)) continue;
        stats.stores++;

        const from = Math.max(0, i - WINDOW);
        let exact = false;
        let nearby = false;
        let targetRef = false;
        const foundConstants = [];

        for (let j = from; j < i; j++) {
            const value = parseI32Const(txt(instructions[j]));
            if (value === null) continue;

            if (value === TARGET) {
                exact = true;
                targetRef = true;
                foundConstants.push(`${addr(instructions[j])}=0x${value.toString(16)}`);
            } else if (Math.abs(value - TARGET) <= NEAR_RANGE) {
                nearby = true;
                foundConstants.push(`${addr(instructions[j])}=0x${value.toString(16)}`);
            }
        }

        // Also catch a target-address constant immediately preceding a store,
        // even if other instructions intervene between them.
        if (exact) {
            stats.storesNearTargetReferences++;
            report(
                func,
                current,
                instructions,
                i,
                `STORE WITH EXACT TARGET ADDRESS IN PRIOR ${WINDOW} INSTRUCTIONS`
            );
            log(`Address constants in window: ${foundConstants.join(", ")}`);
        } else if (nearby) {
            stats.storesNearAddressConstants++;
            report(
                func,
                current,
                instructions,
                i,
                `STORE WITH NEARBY ADDRESS CONSTANT IN PRIOR ${WINDOW} INSTRUCTIONS`
            );
            log(`Nearby constants: ${foundConstants.join(", ")}`);
        }
    }
}

log("\n" + "=".repeat(78));
log("SUMMARY");
for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(stats)) {
    log(`${key}: ${value}`);
}
log("\nImportant:");
log("- Exact-address proximity does not prove the store writes to 0x256aac.");
log("- A nearby constant may be unrelated data or another structure field.");
log("- Zero matches does not rule out pointer-based or data-segment initialization.");
log("- Next, inspect candidate store stack operands and WASM data/global initialization.");

console.log(`Nearby-address candidates: ${stats.storesNearAddressConstants}`);
console.log(`Reports capped at: ${MAX_REPORTS}`);

FILE: scripts\trace7006IndexSources.mjs

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import os from "node:os";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

const root = process.cwd();
const scriptsDir = path.join(root, "scripts");
const decoderPath = path.join(scriptsDir, "disassembleAddressUsers.mjs");
const wasmPath = path.join(root, "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const outputPath = path.join(scriptsDir, "trace7006IndexSources_output.txt");

const MARKER = "console.log('\\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');";

const TARGET_CALLERS = [98, 203, 2616, 3837];

const SEARCH_TERMS = [
    "2452140",
    "0x256aac",
    "1837864",
    "0x1c0b28",
    "4769",
    "call_indirect type=15 table=0",
];

function heading(title) {
    return [
        "",
        "=".repeat(76),
        title,
        "=".repeat(76),
    ];
}

function renderInstruction(instruction) {
    if (typeof instruction === "string") return instruction;

    try {
        return JSON.stringify(instruction);
    } catch {
        return String(instruction);
    }
}

function instructionOffset(instruction) {
    if (!instruction || typeof instruction !== "object") return null;

    for (const key of ["offset", "start", "address", "pc"]) {
        const value = instruction[key];

        if (typeof value === "number") {
            return `0x${value.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`;
        }
    }

    return null;
}

function formatInstruction(instruction, index) {
    const offset = instructionOffset(instruction);
    const rendered = renderInstruction(instruction);

    return `${String(index).padStart(5, " ")} ${offset ?? "????????"}  ${rendered}`;
}

async function main() {
    if (!fs.existsSync(decoderPath)) {
        throw new Error(`Decoder not found: ${decoderPath}`);
    }

    if (!fs.existsSync(wasmPath)) {
        throw new Error(`WASM file not found: ${wasmPath}`);
    }

    const source = fs.readFileSync(decoderPath, "utf8");
    const markerIndex = source.indexOf(MARKER);

    if (markerIndex < 0) {
        throw new Error(
            "Could not find decoder bootstrap marker. Original decoder remains unchanged."
        );
    }

    // Use the decoder's existing initialization and function definitions.
    // Export the existing internal variables only in a temporary module.
    const bootstrap =
        source.slice(0, markerIndex) +
        "\nexport { bodies, decodeFunction };\n";

    const tempPath = path.join(
        os.tmpdir(),
        `rooster_decoder_export_${process.pid}.mjs`
    );

    let decoder;

    try {
        fs.writeFileSync(tempPath, bootstrap, "utf8");
        decoder = await import(pathToFileURL(tempPath).href);
    } finally {
        try {
            fs.unlinkSync(tempPath);
        } catch {
            // Temporary file cleanup is best-effort.
        }
    }

    const { bodies, decodeFunction } = decoder;

    if (!(bodies instanceof Map) || typeof decodeFunction !== "function") {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected decoder interface: bodies Map=${bodies instanceof Map}, ` +
            `decodeFunction=${typeof decodeFunction}`
        );
    }

    const output = [
        "ROOSTER WASM — FUNCTION 7006 INDEX-SOURCE TRACE",
        "READ ONLY / OFFLINE / NO WASM EXECUTION",
        `WASM SIZE: ${fs.statSync(wasmPath).size}`,
        `FUNCTION BODIES: ${bodies.size}`,
        `KNOWN CALLERS: ${TARGET_CALLERS.join(", ")}`,
        "",
        "Search targets:",
        ...SEARCH_TERMS.map(term => `  ${term}`),
    ];

    // First, inspect the four known call sites.
    for (const funcIndex of TARGET_CALLERS) {
        output.push(...heading(`KNOWN CALLER FUNCTION ${funcIndex}`));

        try {
            const decoded = decodeFunction(funcIndex);
            const instructions = decoded.instructions;

            if (!Array.isArray(instructions)) {
                output.push("Unexpected instruction representation.");
                output.push(JSON.stringify(decoded, null, 2).slice(0, 12000));
                continue;
            }

            output.push(`BODY: ${JSON.stringify(decoded.body)}`);
            output.push(`INSTRUCTION COUNT: ${instructions.length}`);

            const hits = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
                const line = renderInstruction(instructions[i]);

                if (
                    line.includes("call_indirect") ||
                    SEARCH_TERMS.some(term => line.includes(term))
                ) {
                    hits.push(i);
                }
            }

            const selected = new Set();

            for (const hit of hits) {
                for (
                    let i = Math.max(0, hit - 8);
                    i <= Math.min(instructions.length - 1, hit + 8);
                    i++
                ) {
                    selected.add(i);
                }
            }

            if (selected.size === 0) {
                output.push("No matching instruction text found.");
            } else {
                for (const i of [...selected].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {
                    output.push(formatInstruction(instructions[i], i));
                }
            }
        } catch (error) {
            output.push(`DECODE ERROR: ${error.stack ?? error}`);
        }
    }

    // Then scan all successfully decoded functions for the target constants.
    output.push(...heading("GLOBAL CONSTANT / ADDRESS REFERENCE SCAN"));

    let decodedCount = 0;
    let decodeErrors = 0;
    let hitFunctionCount = 0;
    const hitFunctions = [];

    for (const funcIndex of bodies.keys()) {
        let decoded;

        try {
            decoded = decodeFunction(funcIndex);
            decodedCount++;
        } catch {
            decodeErrors++;
            continue;
        }

        const instructions = decoded.instructions;

        if (!Array.isArray(instructions)) continue;

        const hits = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
            const line = renderInstruction(instructions[i]);

            if (SEARCH_TERMS.some(term => line.includes(term))) {
                hits.push(i);
            }
        }

        if (hits.length === 0) continue;

        hitFunctionCount++;
        hitFunctions.push(funcIndex);

        output.push(
            ...heading(
                `FUNCTION ${funcIndex} — ${hits.length} MATCHING INSTRUCTION(S)`
            )
        );

        const selected = new Set();

        for (const hit of hits) {
            for (
                let i = Math.max(0, hit - 5);
                i <= Math.min(instructions.length - 1, hit + 7);
                i++
            ) {
                selected.add(i);
            }
        }

        for (const i of [...selected].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {
            output.push(formatInstruction(instructions[i], i));
        }
    }

    output.push(...heading("SUMMARY"));

    output.push(`FUNCTION BODIES IN MAP: ${bodies.size}`);
    output.push(`FUNCTIONS DECODED SUCCESSFULLY: ${decodedCount}`);
    output.push(`DECODE ERRORS: ${decodeErrors}`);
    output.push(`FUNCTIONS WITH SEARCH HITS: ${hitFunctionCount}`);
    output.push(`HIT FUNCTION INDICES: ${hitFunctions.join(", ") || "(none)"}`);
    output.push("");
    output.push("IMPORTANT:");
    output.push("- A matching constant alone does not prove index provenance.");
    output.push("- Verify local/stack flow leading to call_indirect.");
    output.push("- Type 15 matches the signature () -> (), not a specific table slot.");
    output.push("- No WASM execution or transaction was performed.");

    fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, output.join("\n"), "utf8");

    console.log(output.slice(0, 150).join("\n"));
    console.log("");
    console.log(`Full output saved to: ${outputPath}`);
    console.log(`Functions with search hits: ${hitFunctionCount}`);
    console.log(`Successful decodes: ${decodedCount}`);
    console.log(`Decode errors: ${decodeErrors}`);
}

main().catch(error => {
    console.error(error.stack ?? error);
    process.exitCode = 1;
});

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace7006MemoryDestinations.mjs

import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

const sourcePath = path.resolve("scripts/disassembleAddressUsers.mjs");
const source = fs.readFileSync(sourcePath, "utf8");
const marker = "console.log('\\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');";
const markerIndex = source.indexOf(marker);

if (markerIndex < 0) {
    throw new Error("Decoder bootstrap marker not found.");
}

const tempPath = path.join(
    os.tmpdir(),
    `rooster_memory_dest_${process.pid}.mjs`
);

fs.writeFileSync(
    tempPath,
    source.slice(0, markerIndex) +
        "\nexport { bodies, decodeFunction };\n",
    "utf8"
);

let decoder;
try {
    decoder = await import(pathToFileURL(tempPath).href);
} finally {
    try { fs.unlinkSync(tempPath); } catch {}
}

const { bodies, decodeFunction } = decoder;

const TARGET = 0x256aac;
const RANGE = 0x1000;
const MAX_REPORTS = 180;
const output = [];

const counts = {
    functions: bodies.size,
    decoded: 0,
    errors: 0,
    bulkOps: 0,
    knownBulkDestinations: 0,
    possibleBulkTargetHits: 0,
    knownStores: 0,
    possibleStoreTargetHits: 0,
    reports: 0
};

const hex = n =>
    "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");

function log(s = "") {
    output.push(String(s));
}

function unknown() {
    return { kind: "unknown", text: "?" };
}

function constant(n) {
    return { kind: "const", value: n | 0, text: String(n | 0) };
}

function expression(text) {
    return { kind: "expr", text };
}

function add(a, b) {
    if (a.kind === "const" && b.kind === "const") {
        return constant((a.value + b.value) | 0);
    }
    return expression(`(${a.text}+${b.text})`);
}

function sub(a, b) {
    if (a.kind === "const" && b.kind === "const") {
        return constant((a.value - b.value) | 0);
    }
    return expression(`(${a.text}-${b.text})`);
}

function mul(a, b) {
    if (a.kind === "const" && b.kind === "const") {
        return constant(Math.imul(a.value, b.value));
    }
    return expression(`(${a.text}*${b.text})`);
}

function shl(a, b) {
    if (a.kind === "const" && b.kind === "const") {
        return constant(a.value << (b.value & 31));
    }
    return expression(`(${a.text}<<${b.text})`);
}

function mentionsTarget(v) {
    if (!v) return false;

    if (v.kind === "const") {
        const n = v.value >>> 0;
        return Math.abs(n - TARGET) <= RANGE;
    }

    return /256aac|2452140/i.test(v.text || "");
}

function knownNearTarget(v) {
    if (!v || v.kind !== "const") return false;
    return Math.abs((v.value >>> 0) - TARGET) <= RANGE;
}

function parseConst(text) {
    const m = text.match(/^i32\.const\s+(-?\d+)/);
    return m ? Number(m[1]) | 0 : null;
}

function parseIndex(text, op) {
    const m = text.match(new RegExp("^" + op.replace(".", "\\.") + "\\s+(\\d+)"));
    return m ? Number(m[1]) : null;
}

function memOffset(text) {
    const m = text.match(/\boffset=(\d+)/);
    return m ? Number(m[1]) : 0;
}

function formatValue(v) {
    if (!v) return "?";
    if (v.kind === "const") {
        return `${v.text} (${hex(v.value)})`;
    }
    return v.text;
}

function nearContext(instructions, i, before = 8, after = 3) {
    const from = Math.max(0, i - before);
    const to = Math.min(instructions.length - 1, i + after);
    const lines = [];

    for (let j = from; j <= to; j++) {
        const ins = instructions[j];
        lines.push(
            `${j === i ? " >>> " : "     "}#${j} ${hex(ins.start)} ${ins.text}`
        );
    }
    return lines;
}

function scanFunction(func, instructions) {
    const stack = [];
    const locals = new Map();

    const pop = () => stack.length ? stack.pop() : unknown();
    const push = v => stack.push(v || unknown());

    function report(kind, ins, i, destination, extra = "") {
        counts.reports++;
        if (counts.reports > MAX_REPORTS) return;

        log("\n" + "-".repeat(78));
        log(`${kind} | FUNC ${func} | ${hex(ins.start)}`);
        log(`DESTINATION: ${formatValue(destination)}`);
        if (extra) log(extra);
        log("Static heuristic only; not proof of runtime reachability.");

        for (const line of nearContext(instructions, i)) log(line);
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
        const ins = instructions[i];
        const t = ins.text;

        const c = parseConst(t);
        if (c !== null) {
            push(constant(c));
            continue;
        }

        let index;

        if ((index = parseIndex(t, "local.get")) !== null) {
            push(locals.get(index) || expression(`local${index}?`));
            continue;
        }

        if ((index = parseIndex(t, "local.set")) !== null) {
            locals.set(index, pop());
            continue;
        }

        if ((index = parseIndex(t, "local.tee")) !== null) {
            const v = stack.length ? stack[stack.length - 1] : unknown();
            locals.set(index, v);
            continue;
        }

        if (t.startsWith("global.get")) {
            push(expression(`global(${t.split(/\s+/)[1]})`));
            continue;
        }

        if (t.startsWith("global.set")) {
            pop();
            continue;
        }

        if (t.startsWith("i32.add")) {
            const b = pop(), a = pop();
            push(add(a, b));
            continue;
        }

        if (t.startsWith("i32.sub")) {
            const b = pop(), a = pop();
            push(sub(a, b));
            continue;
        }

        if (t.startsWith("i32.mul")) {
            const b = pop(), a = pop();
            push(mul(a, b));
            continue;
        }

        if (t.startsWith("i32.shl")) {
            const b = pop(), a = pop();
            push(shl(a, b));
            continue;
        }

        if (t.startsWith("i32.and") ||
            t.startsWith("i32.or") ||
            t.startsWith("i32.xor") ||
            t.startsWith("i32.shr") ||
            t.startsWith("i32.rot")) {
            pop();
            pop();
            push(unknown());
            continue;
        }

        if (/^i32\.load\b/.test(t) ||
            /^i64\.load\b/.test(t) ||
            /^f32\.load\b/.test(t) ||
            /^f64\.load\b/.test(t)) {
            pop();
            push(unknown());
            continue;
        }

        if (/^(i32|i64|f32|f64)\.store/.test(t)) {
            const value = pop();
            const address = pop();
            const offset = memOffset(t);
            const destination = add(address, constant(offset));

            counts.knownStores++;

            if (mentionsTarget(destination)) {
                counts.possibleStoreTargetHits++;
                report(
                    "STORE DESTINATION CANDIDATE",
                    ins, i, destination,
                    `Stored value (abstract): ${formatValue(value)}`
                );
            }
            continue;
        }

        if (/memory\.copy\b/.test(t)) {
            const length = pop();
            const src = pop();
            const dst = pop();

            counts.bulkOps++;

            if (dst.kind === "const") counts.knownBulkDestinations++;

            if (mentionsTarget(dst)) {
                counts.possibleBulkTargetHits++;
                report(
                    "MEMORY.COPY DESTINATION CANDIDATE",
                    ins, i, dst,
                    `SOURCE: ${formatValue(src)} | LENGTH: ${formatValue(length)}`
                );
            }
            continue;
        }

        if (/memory\.fill\b/.test(t)) {
            const length = pop();
            const fillValue = pop();
            const dst = pop();

            counts.bulkOps++;

            if (dst.kind === "const") counts.knownBulkDestinations++;

            if (mentionsTarget(dst)) {
                counts.possibleBulkTargetHits++;
                report(
                    "MEMORY.FILL DESTINATION CANDIDATE",
                    ins, i, dst,
                    `FILL VALUE: ${formatValue(fillValue)} | LENGTH: ${formatValue(length)}`
                );
            }
            continue;
        }

        if (/^call_indirect\b/.test(t) || /^call\b/.test(t)) {
            // Signatures are not modeled here; discard stack state conservatively.
            stack.length = 0;
            continue;
        }

        if (/^(block|loop|if|else|end|br|br_if|br_table|return|unreachable)\b/.test(t)) {
            // Control flow is not modeled. Avoid carrying stack values across edges.
            stack.length = 0;
            continue;
        }

        if (/^(i32|i64|f32|f64)\.(eq|ne|lt|gt|le|ge|eqz|clz|ctz|popcnt|div|rem)\b/.test(t)) {
            pop();
            if (!/eqz|clz|ctz|popcnt/.test(t)) pop();
            push(unknown());
            continue;
        }

        if (/^(i32|i64)\.(wrap|extend|trunc|reinterpret|extend8|extend16|extend32)/.test(t)) {
            pop();
            push(unknown());
            continue;
        }

        if (/^drop\b/.test(t)) {
            pop();
            continue;
        }

        if (/^select\b/.test(t)) {
            pop();
            const b = pop(), a = pop();
            push(a.kind === b.kind && a.text === b.text ? a : unknown());
            continue;
        }

        if (/^memory\.size\b/.test(t)) {
            push(unknown());
            continue;
        }

        if (/^memory\.grow\b/.test(t)) {
            pop();
            push(unknown());
            continue;
        }

        // Instructions whose stack effect is not modeled invalidate this linear trace.
        if (/^(i64\.const|f32\.const|f64\.const)\b/.test(t)) {
            push(unknown());
            continue;
        }

        if (/^(nop|0xfc\.)/.test(t)) {
            continue;
        }

        // Unrecognized instruction: clear stack to avoid inventing provenance.
        stack.length = 0;
    }
}

log("ROOSTER WASM — TARGET MEMORY DESTINATION TRACE");
log("READ ONLY / OFFLINE / NO WASM EXECUTION");
log(`TARGET CELL: ${hex(TARGET)}`);
log(`NEAR RANGE: +/- ${hex(RANGE)}`);
log("Linear abstract interpretation; candidates can be missed.");
log("No WASM is executed and no files are modified.");
log("=".repeat(78));

for (const [func] of bodies) {
    try {
        const result = decodeFunction(func);
        counts.decoded++;
        if (Array.isArray(result.instructions)) {
            scanFunction(func, result.instructions);
        }
    } catch {
        counts.errors++;
    }
}

log("\n" + "=".repeat(78));
log("SUMMARY");
for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(counts)) log(`${k}: ${v}`);
log("Important: zero hits does not prove the target is never written.");
log("Local/global state, indirect pointers and control flow are only partially modeled.");

const outPath = path.resolve("scripts/trace7006MemoryDestinations_output.txt");
fs.writeFileSync(outPath, output.join("\n") + "\n", "utf8");

console.log(output.slice(0, 100).join("\n"));
console.log(`\nFull report: ${outPath}`);

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: result >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  do {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;
  } while (b & 0x80);

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    result |= (~0 << shift);

  return { value: result | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/*
 * Locate sections
 */
let p = 8;

let importSection;
let functionSection;
let codeSection;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const id = wasm[p++];

  const s = readULEB(p);

  const start = s.next;
  const end = start + s.value;

  if (id === 2)
    importSection = { start, end };

  if (id === 3)
    functionSection = { start, end };

  if (id === 10)
    codeSection = { start, end };

  p = end;
}

/*
 * Imported function count
 */
p = importSection.start;

let x = readULEB(p);
const importCount = x.value;
p = x.next;

let importedFunctions = 0;

function skipName(pos) {
  const n = readULEB(pos);
  return n.next + n.value;
}

for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {

  p = skipName(p);
  p = skipName(p);

  const kind = wasm[p++];

  if (kind === 0) {
    p = readULEB(p).next;
    importedFunctions++;
  }
  else if (kind === 1) {
    p++;
    const flags = wasm[p++];
    p = readULEB(p).next;
    if (flags & 1)
      p = readULEB(p).next;
  }
  else if (kind === 2) {
    const flags = readULEB(p);
    p = flags.next;
    p = readULEB(p).next;
    if (flags.value & 1)
      p = readULEB(p).next;
  }
  else if (kind === 3) {
    p += 2;
  }
}

/*
 * Function section
 */
p = functionSection.start;

const fc = readULEB(p);
const definedCount = fc.value;
p = fc.next;

const types = [];

for (let i = 0; i < definedCount; i++) {
  const t = readULEB(p);
  types.push(t.value);
  p = t.next;
}

/*
 * Code section
 */
p = codeSection.start;

const bc = readULEB(p);
const bodyCount = bc.value;
p = bc.next;

let targetBody = null;

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {

  const bs = readULEB(p);

  const size = bs.value;
  const start = bs.next;
  const end = start + size;

  const func = importedFunctions + i;

  if (func === 7484) {
    targetBody = { start, end, func };
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (!targetBody) {
  throw new Error("FUNC 7484 NOT FOUND");
}

/*
 * Decode a focused subset
 */
function decode(pos) {

  const start = pos;
  const op = wasm[pos++];

  if (op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22) {

    const x = readULEB(pos);

    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      local: x.value,
      text:
        op === 0x20
          ? `local.get ${x.value}`
          : op === 0x21
          ? `local.set ${x.value}`
          : `local.tee ${x.value}`
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {

    const x = readSLEB(pos);

    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      value: x.value,
      text: `i32.const ${x.value}`
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x28 ||
      op === 0x29 ||
      op === 0x2a ||
      op === 0x2b ||
      op === 0x2c ||
      op === 0x2d ||
      op === 0x2e ||
      op === 0x2f ||
      op === 0x30 ||
      op === 0x31 ||
      op === 0x32 ||
      op === 0x33 ||
      op === 0x34 ||
      op === 0x35) {

    const a = readULEB(pos);
    const o = readULEB(a.next);

    return {
      start,
      end: o.next,
      op,
      offset: o.value,
      text:
        `load opcode=0x${op.toString(16)} ` +
        `align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
    };
  }

  if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {

    const a = readULEB(pos);
    const o = readULEB(a.next);

    return {
      start,
      end: o.next,
      op,
      offset: o.value,
      text:
        `store opcode=0x${op.toString(16)} ` +
        `align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {

    const x = readULEB(pos);

    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      func: x.value,
      text: `call ${x.value}`
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {

    const t = readULEB(pos);
    const table = readULEB(t.next);

    return {
      start,
      end: table.next,
      op,
      type: t.value,
      text:
        `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`
    };
  }

  return {
    start,
    end: pos,
    op,
    text:
      `opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
  };
}

/*
 * Parse locals
 */
p = targetBody.start;

const localGroups = readULEB(p);
p = localGroups.next;

for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
  p = readULEB(p).next;
  p++;
}

/*
 * Decode entire function
 */
const code = [];

while (p < targetBody.end) {

  const ins = decode(p);

  code.push(ins);

  p = ins.end;

  if (ins.op === 0x0b)
    break;
}

 * Output focused events
 */

import fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

// Exact known region of Function 8620
const START = 0x002c9a00;
const END   = 0x002c9b00;

const TARGET = 1837608; // 0x001c0a28

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function uleb(buf: Buffer, p: number): {
    value: number;
    next: number;
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: p
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    return {
        value: 0,
        next: p
    };
}

function decodeOne(
    buf: Buffer,
    p: number
): {
    next: number;
    text: string;
} {

    const op = buf[p++];

    switch (op) {

        case 0x20: {
            const r = uleb(buf, p);
            return {
                next: r.next,
                text: `local.get ${r.value}`
            };
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const r = uleb(buf, p);
            return {
                next: r.next,
                text: `local.set ${r.value}`
            };
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const r = uleb(buf, p);
            return {
                next: r.next,
                text: `local.tee ${r.value}`
            };
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const r = uleb(buf, p);

            return {
                next: r.next,
                text:
                    `i32.const ${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`
            };
        }

        case 0x28: {
            const a = uleb(buf, p);
            const o = uleb(buf, a.next);

            return {
                next: o.next,
                text:
                    `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
            };
        }

        case 0x36: {
            const a = uleb(buf, p);
            const o = uleb(buf, a.next);

            return {
                next: o.next,
                text:
                    `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
            };
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const r = uleb(buf, p);

            return {
                next: r.next,
                text: `call ${r.value}`
            };
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const t = uleb(buf, p);
            const table = uleb(buf, t.next);

            return {
                next: table.next,
                text:
                    `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`
            };
        }

        case 0x6a:
            return { next: p, text: "i32.add" };

        case 0x6b:
            return { next: p, text: "i32.sub" };

        case 0x0b:
            return { next: p, text: "end" };

        case 0x02:
            return { next: p, text: "block" };

        case 0x03:
            return { next: p, text: "loop" };

        case 0x04:
            return { next: p, text: "if" };

        case 0x05:
            return { next: p, text: "else" };

        case 0x0c: {
            const r = uleb(buf, p);
            return {
                next: r.next,
                text: `br ${r.value}`
            };
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const r = uleb(buf, p);
            return {
                next: r.next,
                text: `br_if ${r.value}`
            };
        }

        default:
            return {
                next: p,
                text:
                    "opcode=0x" +
                    op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
            };
    }
}

export async function run() {

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" FUNCTION 8620 LOCAL0 TRACE");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();

    console.log("Function :", 8620);
    console.log("Range    :", hex(START), "..", hex(END));
    console.log("Target   :", TARGET, "(", hex(TARGET), ")");
    console.log();

    // IMPORTANT:
    // Do NOT load the complete WASM into memory.
    // Read only the 256-byte function window.

    const fd = fs.openSync(WASM_PATH, "r");

    const length = END - START;
    const buf = Buffer.alloc(length);

    fs.readSync(
        fd,
        buf,
        0,
        length,
        START
    );

    fs.closeSync(fd);

    console.log("Window bytes :", length);
    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" DECODE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    let p = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {

        const fileOffset = START + p;

        const decoded = decodeOne(buf, p);

        if (decoded.next <= p) {
            p++;
        } else {
            p = decoded.next;
        }

        console.log(
            hex(fileOffset),
            decoded.text
        );
    }

    console.log();

    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // Exact known writer location
    // ---------------------------------------------------------

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" TARGET WRITER CHECK");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const targetBytes = [
        TARGET & 0xff,
        (TARGET >>> 8) & 0xff,
        (TARGET >>> 16) & 0xff,
        (TARGET >>> 24) & 0xff
    ];

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i <= buf.length - 4;
        i++
    ) {

        if (
            buf[i] !== targetBytes[0] ||
            buf[i + 1] !== targetBytes[1] ||
            buf[i + 2] !== targetBytes[2] ||
            buf[i + 3] !== targetBytes[3]
        ) {
            continue;
        }

        console.log(
            "Raw target bytes at",
            hex(START + i)
        );
    }

    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" END");
    console.log("============================================================");
}

const fs = require("fs");

const FILE =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const IMPORTS = 83;
const TARGET = 8927;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

function uleb(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            break;

        s += 7;
    }

    return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sleb(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0, b;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
        s += 7;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            break;
    }

    if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        v |= (~0 << s);

    return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function findCode() {

    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {

        const type = wasm[p++];

        const z = uleb(p);
        p = z.next;

        if (type === 10)
            return {
                start: p,
                end: p + z.value
            };

        p += z.value;
    }

    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

function decode(start, end) {

    let p = start;

    const groups = uleb(p);
    p = groups.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < groups.value; i++) {
        const n = uleb(p);
        p = n.next;
        p++;
    }

    const ins = [];

    while (p < end) {

        const addr = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        let text =
            `opcode_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

        switch (op) {

            case 0x20: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `local.get ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x21: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `local.set ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x22: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `local.tee ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x41: {
                const x = sleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `i32.const ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x28: {
                const a = uleb(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = uleb(p);
                p = o.next;

                text =
                    `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x2d: {
                const a = uleb(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = uleb(p);
                p = o.next;

                text =
                    `i32.load8_u align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x36: {
                const a = uleb(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = uleb(p);
                p = o.next;

                text =
                    `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x10: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `call ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x11: {
                const t = uleb(p);
                p = t.next;

                const tab = uleb(p);
                p = tab.next;

                text =
                    `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x45:
                text = "i32.eqz";
                break;

            case 0x46:
                text = "i32.eq";
                break;

            case 0x47:
                text = "i32.ne";
                break;

            case 0x6a:
                text = "i32.add";
                break;

            case 0x6b:
                text = "i32.sub";
                break;

            case 0x1a:
                text = "drop";
                break;

            case 0x04:
                text = "if";
                break;

            case 0x05:
                text = "else";
                break;

            case 0x0b:
                text = "end";
                break;

            case 0x0c: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `br ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x0d: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `br_if ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x23: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `global.get ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x24: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `global.set ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }
        }

        ins.push({ addr, text });
    }

    return ins;
}

function getTargetBody() {

    const sec = findCode();

    let p = sec.start;

    const count = uleb(p);
    p = count.next;

    for (let body = 0; body < count.value; body++) {

        const z = uleb(p);
        p = z.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + z.value;

        const func = IMPORTS + body;

        if (func === TARGET) {

            return {
                body,
                start,
                end
            };
        }

        p = end;
    }

    throw new Error("TARGET NOT FOUND");
}

function hx(x) {
    return "0x" +
        x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

const b = getTargetBody();
const ins = decode(b.start, b.end);

console.log("============================================================");

for (const x of ins)
    console.log(`${hx(x.addr)} ${x.text}`);

console.log("");

for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {

    if (

    ) {

        console.log("");

        );

        const from = Math.max(0, i - 2);

        for (let j = from; j < to; j++) {

            const mark =
                j === i ? ">>" : "  ";

            console.log(

            );
        }
    }
}

    }

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = new Set([
    1613172,
    1613200,
    1613248,
    1613268,
    1613316,
    1613344,
]);

const CONTEXT_BEFORE = 12;
const CONTEXT_AFTER = 20;

function readULEB(buf, p) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: result >>> 0, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function readSLEB(buf, p) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        b = buf[p++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                result |= (~0 << shift);
            }
            return { value: result | 0, next: p };
        }
    }

    throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
}

function u32(buf, p) {
    return (
        buf[p] |
        (buf[p + 1] << 8) |
        (buf[p + 2] << 16) |
        (buf[p + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function hex(n, width = 8) {
    return n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function opcodeName(op) {
    const names = {
        0x0b: "end",
        0x0c: "br",
        0x0d: "br_if",
        0x0e: "br_table",
        0x0f: "return",
        0x10: "call",
        0x11: "call_indirect",

        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",

        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",
        0x48: "i32.lt_s",
        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
        0x4c: "i32.le_s",
        0x4d: "i32.le_u",
        0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
        0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",

        0x1b: "select"
    };

    return names[op] || `opcode_0x${hex(op, 2)}`;
}

/*
 * Conservative instruction decoder.
 * We only need enough decoding to identify i32.const and
 * show nearby instruction boundaries.
 */
function decodeInstruction(buf, p, end) {
    const start = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = readSLEB(buf, p);
        return {
            start,
            end: r.next,
            text: `i32.const ${r.value}`,
            constValue: r.value >>> 0,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        const r = readSLEB(buf, p);
        return {
            start,
            end: r.next,
            text: `i64.const ${r.value}`,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        return {
            start,
            end: r.next,
            text: `call func=${r.value}`,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        const table = buf[r.next];
        return {
            start,
            end: r.next + 2,
            text: `call_indirect type=${r.value} table=${table}`,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        return {
            start,
            end: r.next,
            text: `${opcodeName(op)} local=${r.value}`,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
        const r = readULEB(buf, p);
        return {
            start,
            end: r.next,
            text: `${opcodeName(op)} global=${r.value}`,
        };
    }

    if (
        op === 0x28 ||
        op === 0x29 ||
        op === 0x2c ||
        op === 0x2d ||
        op === 0x36 ||
        op === 0x37
    ) {
        const align = readULEB(buf, p);
        const offset = readULEB(buf, align.next);

        return {
            start,
            end: offset.next,
            text: `${opcodeName(op)} align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`,
        };
    }

    if (
        op === 0x45 ||
        op === 0x46 ||
        op === 0x47 ||
        op === 0x48 ||
        op === 0x49 ||
        op === 0x4a ||
        op === 0x4b ||
        op === 0x4c ||
        op === 0x4d ||
        op === 0x4e ||
        op === 0x4f ||
        op === 0x6a ||
        op === 0x6b ||
        op === 0x6c ||
        op === 0x1b ||
        op === 0x0b ||
        op === 0x0f
    ) {
        return {
            start,
            end: p,
            text: opcodeName(op),
        };
    }

    /*
     * Unknown opcode:
     * advance one byte conservatively.
     */
    return {
        start,
        end: p,
        text: opcodeName(op),
    };
}

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE 8952 STATIC CONSTANT USES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("TARGET CONSTANTS :", [...TARGETS].join(", "));
console.log();

let totalHits = 0;
let decodedFunctions = 0;
let decodeFailures = 0;

/*
 * Locate code section.
 */
let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = p + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = payloadStart;
        codeEnd = payloadEnd;
        break;
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

console.log(
    `CODE SECTION : 0x${hex(codeStart)} .. 0x${hex(codeEnd)}`
);
console.log();

p = codeStart;

const bodyCountInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
const bodyCount = bodyCountInfo.value;
p = bodyCountInfo.next;

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodyCount; bodyIndex++) {
    const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
    p = bodySizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

    /*
     * Skip local declarations.
     */
    const localDeclInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const localDeclCount = localDeclInfo.value;
    p = localDeclInfo.next;

    let localDecodeOK = true;

    try {
        for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount; i++) {
            const n = readULEB(wasm, p);
            p = n.next;

            /*
             * valtype byte
             */
            p += 1;
        }
    } catch {
        localDecodeOK = false;
    }

    if (!localDecodeOK || p > bodyEnd) {
        decodeFailures++;
        p = bodyEnd;
        continue;
    }

    const instructions = [];

    try {
        while (p < bodyEnd) {
            const ins = decodeInstruction(wasm, p, bodyEnd);
            instructions.push(ins);
            p = ins.end;

            if (ins.text === "end" && p >= bodyEnd) {
                break;
            }
        }

        decodedFunctions++;
    } catch {
        decodeFailures++;
        p = bodyEnd;
        continue;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
        const ins = instructions[i];

        if (
            ins.constValue !== undefined &&
            TARGETS.has(ins.constValue)
        ) {
            totalHits++;

            console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
            console.log(
                `HIT #${totalHits}  FUNC BODY=${bodyIndex}`
            );
            console.log(
                `CONSTANT : ${ins.constValue} (0x${hex(ins.constValue)})`
            );
            console.log(
                `ADDRESS  : 0x${hex(ins.start)}`
            );
            console.log();

            console.log("CONTEXT");
            console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

            const from = Math.max(0, i - CONTEXT_BEFORE);
            const to = Math.min(
                instructions.length,
                i + CONTEXT_AFTER + 1
            );

            for (let j = from; j < to; j++) {
                const x = instructions[j];

                console.log(
                    `${j === i ? ">>" : "  "} ` +
                    `0x${hex(x.start)}  ${x.text}`
                );
            }

            console.log();
        }
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CONSTANT HITS :", totalHits);
console.log("DECODED BODIES :", decodedFunctions);
console.log("DECODER FAILURES :", decodeFailures);
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");

FILE: scripts\trace91.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const TARGET = 91;

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value, next: p };
}

function findCodeSection() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];
    const r = readULEB(p);
    const size = r.value;
    p = r.next;

    if (id === 10) {
      return { start: p, end: p + size };
    }

    p += size;
  }

  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function parseFunctionBodies() {
  const sec = findCodeSection();
  let p = sec.start;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  const bodies = [];

  // 83 imported functions
  const IMPORTED = 83;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    const bodySize = r.value;
    p = r.next;

    bodies.push({
      func: IMPORTED + i,
      start: p,
      end: p + bodySize
    });

    p += bodySize;
  }

  return bodies;
}

function readInstr(pos) {
  const op = wasm[pos];

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = readULEB(pos + 1);
    return {
      text: `i32.const ${r.value} (0x${r.value.toString(16)})`,
      next: r.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x20) {
    const r = readULEB(pos + 1);
    return {
      text: `local.get ${r.value}`,
      next: r.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x21) {
    const r = readULEB(pos + 1);
    return {
      text: `local.set ${r.value}`,
      next: r.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x22) {
    const r = readULEB(pos + 1);
    return {
      text: `local.tee ${r.value}`,
      next: r.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x28) {
    const a = readULEB(pos + 1);
    const o = readULEB(a.next);

    return {
      text: `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`,
      next: o.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x36) {
    const a = readULEB(pos + 1);
    const o = readULEB(a.next);

    return {
      text: `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`,
      next: o.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = readULEB(pos + 1);

    return {
      text: `call ${r.value}`,
      next: r.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const t = readULEB(pos + 1);
    const table = readULEB(t.next);

    return {
      text: `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`,
      next: table.next
    };
  }

  const names = {
    0x00: "unreachable",
    0x0b: "end",
    0x0f: "return",
    0x1a: "drop",
    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.lt_u",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor"
  };

  return {
    text: names[op] || `UNKNOWN opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
    next: pos + 1
  };
}

const bodies = parseFunctionBodies();
const body = bodies.find(x => x.func === TARGET);

if (!body) {
  throw new Error(`FUNC ${TARGET} not found`);
}

console.log("FUNC 91 TARGET TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("========================================================================");
console.log(
  `BODY : 0x${body.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${body.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
);
console.log(`SIZE : ${body.end - body.start}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = body.start;

const localGroups = readULEB(p);
p = localGroups.next;

console.log(`LOCAL GROUPS : ${localGroups.value}`);

for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
  const n = readULEB(p);
  p = n.next;

  const type = wasm[p++];

  console.log(
    `LOCALS GROUP ${i}: count=${n.value} type=0x${type.toString(16)}`
  );
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------------------");

while (p < body.end) {
  const pos = p;
  const ins = readInstr(p);
  p = ins.next;

  console.log(
    `0x${pos.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${ins.text}`
  );
}

console.log("========================================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\trace91_656.ts
