import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = new Set([91, 656]);
const TABLE_TARGETS = new Set([4789, 4790]);
const ADDR_TARGETS = new Set([0x195350, 0x195354]);

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

function readSection(id: number) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const sectionId = wasm[p++];

        const s = readULEB(p);
        p = s.next;

        if (sectionId === id) {
            return {
                start: p,
                end: p + s.value
            };
        }

        p += s.value;
    }

    throw new Error(`SECTION ${id} NOT FOUND`);
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readBytes(start: number, end: number) {
    return Array.from(wasm.subarray(start, end))
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ");
}

// ============================================================
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ============================================================

const funcSec = readSection(3);

let fp = funcSec.start;
const funcCountInfo = readULEB(fp);
fp = funcCountInfo.next;

const typeIndices: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < funcCountInfo.value; i++) {
    const x = readULEB(fp);
    fp = x.next;
    typeIndices.push(x.value);
}

const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

// ============================================================
// CODE SECTION
// ============================================================

const codeSec = readSection(10);

let cp = codeSec.start;
const bodyCountInfo = readULEB(cp);
cp = bodyCountInfo.next;

interface FuncBody {
    globalFunc: number;
    ordinal: number;
    typeIndex: number;
    bodyStart: number;
    bodyEnd: number;
    instrStart: number;
    instrEnd: number;
}

const bodies: FuncBody[] = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < bodyCountInfo.value; ordinal++) {

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(cp);
    cp = sizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = cp;
    const bodyEnd = cp + sizeInfo.value;

    let p = bodyStart;

    const localDecls = readULEB(p);
    p = localDecls.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localDecls.value; i++) {
        const n = readULEB(p);
        p = n.next;

        // value type
        p++;
    }

    const globalFunc = IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + ordinal;

    bodies.push({
        globalFunc,
        ordinal,
        typeIndex: typeIndices[ordinal] ?? -1,
        bodyStart,
        bodyEnd,
        instrStart: p,
        instrEnd: bodyEnd
    });

    cp = bodyEnd;
}

// ============================================================
// TYPE SECTION
// ============================================================

const typeSec = readSection(1);

let tp = typeSec.start;
const typeCountInfo = readULEB(tp);
tp = typeCountInfo.next;

interface FuncType {
    params: number[];
    results: number[];
}

const types: FuncType[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < typeCountInfo.value; i++) {

    const form = wasm[tp++];

    if (form !== 0x60) {
        throw new Error(`Unexpected type form at ${hex(tp - 1)}`);
    }

    const pc = readULEB(tp);
    tp = pc.next;

    const params: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++) {
        params.push(wasm[tp++]);
    }

    const rc = readULEB(tp);
    tp = rc.next;

    const results: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++) {
        results.push(wasm[tp++]);
    }

    types.push({ params, results });
}

// ============================================================
// TABLE / ELEMENT INFORMATION
// ============================================================

const tableSec = readSection(4);

let tb = tableSec.start;
const tableCount = readULEB(tb);
tb = tableCount.next;

console.log("TRACE FUNC91 + FUNC656");

    if (!body) {
        console.log(`FUNC ${f}: NOT FOUND`);
        continue;
    }

    const t = types[body.typeIndex];

// ============================================================
// SCAN FUNCTION BODIES
// ============================================================

interface DirectCall {
    caller: number;
    target: number;
    offset: number;
}

interface IndirectCall {
    caller: number;
    offset: number;
    typeIndex: number;
}

interface AddressRef {
    caller: number;
    offset: number;
    address: number;
}

interface ConstRef {
    caller: number;
    offset: number;
    value: number;
}

const directCalls: DirectCall[] = [];
const indirectCalls: IndirectCall[] = [];
const addressRefs: AddressRef[] = [];
const selectorRefs: ConstRef[] = [];

for (const body of bodies) {

    let p = body.instrStart;

    while (p < body.instrEnd) {

        const opOffset = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        // i32.const
        if (op === 0x41) {
            const x = readSLEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            const v = x.value >>> 0;

            if (ADDR_TARGETS.has(v)) {
                addressRefs.push({
                    caller: body.globalFunc,
                    offset: opOffset,
                    address: v
                });
            }

            if (TABLE_TARGETS.has(v)) {
                selectorRefs.push({
                    caller: body.globalFunc,
                    offset: opOffset,
                    value: v
                });
            }

            continue;
        }

        // call
        if (op === 0x10) {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            if (TARGETS.has(x.value)) {
                directCalls.push({
                    caller: body.globalFunc,
                    target: x.value,
                    offset: opOffset
                });
            }

            continue;
        }

        // call_indirect
        if (op === 0x11) {

            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            const table = wasm[p++];

            indirectCalls.push({
                caller: body.globalFunc,
                offset: opOffset,
                typeIndex: x.value
            });

            continue;
        }

        // Memory load/store immediates
        if (
            op === 0x28 || // i32.load
            op === 0x29 || // i64.load
            op === 0x2a || // f32.load
            op === 0x2b || // f64.load
            op === 0x2c || // i32.load8_s
            op === 0x2d || // i32.load8_u
            op === 0x2e || // i32.load16_s
            op === 0x2f || // i32.load16_u
            op === 0x30 || // i64.load8_s
            op === 0x31 || // i64.load8_u
            op === 0x32 || // i64.load16_s
            op === 0x33 || // i64.load16_u
            op === 0x34 || // i64.load32_s
            op === 0x35 || // i64.load32_u
            op === 0x36 || // i32.store
            op === 0x37 || // i64.store
            op === 0x38 || // f32.store
            op === 0x39 || // f64.store
            op === 0x3a || // i32.store8
            op === 0x3b || // i32.store16
            op === 0x3c || // i64.store8
            op === 0x3d || // i64.store16
            op === 0x3e    // i64.store32
        ) {
            const align = readULEB(p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(p);
            p = off.next;

            continue;
        }

        // Most opcodes have no immediate.
        // Handle variable-length/simple immediates that matter here.

        if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22 ||
            op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0e) {
            const n = readULEB(p);
            p = n.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < n.value + 1; i++) {
                const x = readULEB(p);
                p = x.next;
            }

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 ||
            op === 0x04 || op === 0x06) {
            p++; // block type
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
            p++;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x42) {
            const x = readSLEB(p);
            p = x.next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x43) {
            p += 4;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x44) {
            p += 8;
            continue;
        }
    }
}

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const TARGET = 9368;
const IMPORTED = 83;

function uleb(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;
    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[p++];
        v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };
        s += 7;
    }
}

function getCodeSection() {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < bytes.length) {
        const id = bytes[p++];
        const z = uleb(p);
        const start = z.next;
        const end = start + z.value;

        if (id === 10) return { start, end };
        p = end;
    }

    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

function readInstr(p) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = bytes[p++];

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `local.get ${x.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `local.set ${x.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `local.tee ${x.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `i32.const ${x.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x28) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);
        return [o.next, `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x36) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);
        return [o.next, `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x45) return [p, "i32.eqz"];
    if (op === 0x46) return [p, "i32.eq"];
    if (op === 0x47) return [p, "i32.ne"];

    if (op === 0x6b) return [p, "i32.sub"];

    if (op === 0x0d) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `br_if ${x.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x0c) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `br ${x.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        return [x.next, `call ${x.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x04) return [p, "if"];
    if (op === 0x05) return [p, "else"];
    if (op === 0x40) return [p, "end-block"];
    if (op === 0x0b) return [p, "end"];

    return [p, `opcode_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`];
}

const code = getCodeSection();

let p = code.start;
const count = uleb(p);
p = count.next;

let body = null;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const z = uleb(p);
    const start = z.next;
    const end = start + z.value;
    const global = IMPORTED + i;

    if (global === TARGET) {
        body = { start, end };
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (!body) throw new Error("FUNC 9368 NOT FOUND");

let q = body.start;

const groups = uleb(q);
q = groups.next;

for (let i = 0; i < groups.value; i++) {
    const n = uleb(q);
    q = n.next + 1;
}

const ins = [];

while (q < body.end) {
    const start = q;
    const [next, text] = readInstr(q);

    ins.push({
        addr: start,
        text
    });

    q = next;

    if (text === "end") break;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE FUNC 9368 LOCAL2");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log("BODY :", "0x" + body.start.toString(16),
            "..", "0x" + body.end.toString(16));
console.log("INSTRUCTIONS :", ins.length);
console.log("");

for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {

    if (ins[i].text === "local.get 2" ||
        ins[i].text === "local.set 2" ||
        ins[i].text === "local.tee 2") {

        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log("LOCAL2 USE/DEF @ 0x" + ins[i].addr.toString(16));
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        const from = Math.max(0, i - 10);
        const to = Math.min(ins.length - 1, i + 8);

        for (let j = from; j <= to; j++) {
            console.log(
                j === i ? ">>" : "  ",
                "0x" + ins[j].addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
                ins[j].text
            );
        }

        console.log("");
    }
}

console.log("FINAL");

console.log("FUNC 9368 : FOUND");
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET = 9368;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;
    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[p++];
        v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
        s += 7;
    }
    return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function section(p) {
    const id = bytes[p++];
    const z = uleb(p);
    return { id, start: z.next, end: z.next + z.value };
}

function findCode() {
    let p = 8;
    while (p < bytes.length) {
        const s = section(p);
        if (s.id === 10) return s;
        p = s.end;
    }
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

function instr(p) {
    const start = p;
    const op = bytes[p++];

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const r = uleb(p);
        return [r.next, `local.get ${r.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const r = uleb(p);
        return [r.next, `local.set ${r.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const r = uleb(p);
        return [r.next, `local.tee ${r.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = uleb(p);
        return [r.next, `i32.const ${r.value} (0x${r.value.toString(16)})`];
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        const r = uleb(p);
        return [r.next, `i64.const ${r.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x6a) return [p, "i32.add"];
    if (op === 0x6b) return [p, "i32.sub"];
    if (op === 0x6c) return [p, "i32.mul"];

    if (op === 0x28) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);
        return [o.next, `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x29) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);
        return [o.next, `i64.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x2d) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);
        return [o.next, `i32.load8_u align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x36) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);
        return [o.next, `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x37) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        const o = uleb(a.next);
        return [o.next, `i64.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x46) return [p, "i32.eq"];
    if (op === 0x47) return [p, "i32.ne"];
    if (op === 0x48) return [p, "i32.lt_s"];
    if (op === 0x49) return [p, "i32.lt_u"];

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const r = uleb(p);
        return [r.next, `call ${r.value}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
        const r = uleb(p);
        const table = bytes[r.next];
        return [r.next + 1, `call_indirect type=${r.value} table=${table}`];
    }

    if (op === 0x1b) return [p, "select"];
    if (op === 0x0b) return [p, "end"];

    return [p, `opcode_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`];
}

const code = findCode();

let p = code.start;
const count = uleb(p);
p = count.next;

let body = null;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const z = uleb(p);
    const start = z.next;
    const end = start + z.value;

    const globalFunc = 83 + i;

    if (globalFunc === TARGET) {
        body = { start, end, size: z.value };
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (!body) throw new Error("TARGET FUNCTION NOT FOUND");

let q = body.start;

const localGroups = uleb(q);
q = localGroups.next;

for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
    const n = uleb(q);
    q = n.next + 1;
}

const ins = [];

while (q < body.end) {
    const addr = q;
    const [next, text] = instr(q);

    ins.push({ addr, text });
    q = next;

    if (text === "end") break;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE FUNC 9368 OFFSET+4 VALUE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log("BODY :", "0x" + body.start.toString(16),
            "..", "0x" + body.end.toString(16));
console.log("SIZE :", body.size);
console.log("");

const hits = [];

for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
    const t = ins[i].text;

    if (
        (t.startsWith("i32.store") || t.startsWith("i64.store")) &&
        /offset=4$/.test(t)
    ) {
        hits.push(i);
    }
}

console.log("OFFSET+4 STORE COUNT :", hits.length);
console.log("");

for (let h = 0; h < hits.length; h++) {
    const i = hits[h];

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(`HIT #${h + 1}`);
    console.log("ADDRESS :", "0x" + ins[i].addr.toString(16));
    console.log("STORE   :", ins[i].text);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    const from = Math.max(0, i - 12);
    const to = Math.min(ins.length - 1, i + 4);

    for (let j = from; j <= to; j++) {
        console.log(
            j === i ? ">>" : "  ",
            "0x" + ins[j].addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
            ins[j].text
        );
    }

    console.log("");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC :", TARGET);
console.log("STORE+4 HITS :", hits.length);
console.log("DECODE :", q === body.end ? "EXACT" : "CHECK");
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceActualIndex4154.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 114;

function uleb(b: Buffer, p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 128))
      return { value: v, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }

  throw Error("ULEB");
}

function sleb(b: Buffer, p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;
  let x = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 127) << s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(x & 128))
      break;
  }

  if (s < 32 && (x & 64))
    v |= -1 << s;

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function skipName(b: Buffer, p: number) {
  const n = uleb(b, p);
  return n.next + n.value;
}

function sections(b: Buffer): any {
  let p = 8;
  const r: any = {};

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const s = uleb(b, p);
    p = s.next;

    r[id] = {
      start: p,
      size: s.value
    };

    p += s.value;
  }

  return r;
}

function importedFuncs(b: Buffer, sec: any) {
  let p = sec.start;

  const n = uleb(b, p);
  p = n.next;

  let count = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    p = skipName(b, p);
    p = skipName(b, p);

    const kind = b[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      p = uleb(b, p).next;
      count++;
    }
    else if (kind === 1 || kind === 2) {
      const f = uleb(b, p);
      p = f.next;
      p = uleb(b, p).next;

      if (f.value & 1)
        p = uleb(b, p).next;
    }
    else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }

  return count;
}

function getBodies(b: Buffer, sec: any, imported: number) {
  let p = sec.start;

  const n = uleb(b, p);
  p = n.next;

  const out: any[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    const s = uleb(b, p);
    p = s.next;

    out.push({
      func: imported + i,
      start: p,
      end: p + s.value,
      bytes: b.subarray(p, p + s.value)
    });

    p += s.value;
  }

  return out;
}

type I = {
  off: number;
  op: number;
  imm: number[];
  text: string;
};

function decode(b: Buffer, base: number): I[] {
  const a: I[] = [];

  let p = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const off = base + p;
    const op = b[p++];

    const imm: number[] = [];
    let text = `op=0x${op.toString(16)}`;

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = sleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;

      imm.push(r.value);
      text = `i32.const ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;

      imm.push(r.value);

      text =
        op === 0x20 ? `local.get ${r.value}` :
        op === 0x21 ? `local.set ${r.value}` :
        `local.tee ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;

      imm.push(r.value);

      text =
        op === 0x23
          ? `global.get ${r.value}`
          : `global.set ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x10) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;

      imm.push(r.value);
      text = `call ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x11) {
      const t = uleb(b, p);
      p = t.next;

      const tab = uleb(b, p);
      p = tab.next;

      imm.push(t.value, tab.value);

      text =
        `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;
    }

    else if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
      const al = uleb(b, p);
      p = al.next;

      const of = uleb(b, p);
      p = of.next;

      imm.push(al.value, of.value);

      text = `load offset=${of.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x6a)
      text = "i32.add";

    else if (op === 0x6b)
      text = "i32.sub";

    else if (op === 0x6c)
      text = "i32.mul";

    else if (op === 0x1a)
      text = "drop";

    else if (op === 0x0f)
      text = "return";

    else if (op === 0x0b)
      text = "end";

    else if (op === 0x05)
      text = "else";

    else if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
      if (p < b.length)
        p++;

      text =
        op === 0x02 ? "block" :
        op === 0x03 ? "loop" :
        "if";
    }

    else if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;

      imm.push(r.value);

      text =
        op === 0x0c
          ? `br ${r.value}`
          : `br_if ${r.value}`;
    }

    /*
     * Important:
     * For unknown opcodes we still retain the instruction.
     * This script is intentionally conservative.
     */

    a.push({
      off,
      op,
      imm,
      text
    });
  }

  return a;
}

function showWindow(a: I[], i: number) {
  console.log();

  for (
    let j = Math.max(0, i - 14);
    j <= Math.min(a.length - 1, i + 3);
    j++
  ) {
    console.log(
      `${j === i ? " >>>" : "    "} ` +
      `0x${a[j].off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
      a[j].text
    );
  }
}

function describeSource(a: I[], i: number) {
  const p = i - 1;

  if (p < 0)
    return "NONE";

  const x = a[p];

  if (x.op === 0x41)
    return `CONST(${x.imm[0]})`;

  if (x.op === 0x20)
    return `LOCAL(${x.imm[0]})`;

  if (x.op === 0x23)
    return `GLOBAL(${x.imm[0]})`;

  if (x.op >= 0x28 && x.op <= 0x3e)
    return `LOAD(+${x.imm[1]})`;

  if (x.op === 0x6a)
    return "ADD";

  if (x.op === 0x6b)
    return "SUB";

  if (x.op === 0x6c)
    return "MUL";

  if (x.op === 0x10)
    return `CALL(${x.imm[0]})`;

  return x.text;
}

export async function run() {
  const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);
  const sec = sections(wasm);

  const imported = importedFuncs(wasm, sec[2]);
  const bodies = getBodies(wasm, sec[10], imported);

  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(" ACTUAL INDIRECT INDEX SOURCE -> 114");
  console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length}`);
  console.log(`Target slot : ${TARGET}`);
  console.log();

  let sites = 0;
  let const114 = 0;
  let constOther = 0;
  let load = 0;
  let local = 0;
  let global = 0;
  let call = 0;
  let arithmetic = 0;
  let unresolved = 0;

  for (const body of bodies) {
    let a: I[];

    try {
      a = decode(body.bytes, body.start);
    }
    catch {
      continue;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
      const c = a[i];

      if (
        c.op !== 0x11 ||
        c.imm[0] !== 0 ||
        c.imm[1] !== 0
      )
        continue;

      sites++;

      const src = describeSource(a, i);

      if (src === `CONST(${TARGET})`) {
        const114++;

        console.log(
          `FOUND 114: func=${body.func} ` +
          `call=0x${c.off.toString(16)}`
        );

        showWindow(a, i);
        continue;
      }

      if (src.startsWith("CONST(")) {
        constOther++;

        if (constOther <= 30) {
          console.log(
            `CONST OTHER: func=${body.func} ` +
            `value=${src} call=0x${c.off.toString(16)}`
          );
        }

        continue;
      }

      if (src.startsWith("LOAD(")) {
        load++;

        /*
         * Look one more level backward.
         */
        const p1 = i - 1;
        const p2 = i - 2;

        if (
          p2 >= 0 &&
          a[p2].op >= 0x28 &&
          a[p2].op <= 0x3e
        ) {
          if (load <= 100) {
            console.log(
              `LOAD CHAIN: func=${body.func} ` +
              `call=0x${c.off.toString(16)} ` +
              `outer=+${a[p1].imm[1]} ` +
              `inner=+${a[p2].imm[1]}`
            );
          }
        }

        continue;
      }

      if (src.startsWith("LOCAL(")) {
        local++;
        continue;
      }

      if (src.startsWith("GLOBAL(")) {
        global++;
        continue;
      }

      if (src.startsWith("CALL(")) {
        call++;
        continue;
      }

      if (
        src === "ADD" ||
        src === "SUB" ||
        src === "MUL"
      ) {
        arithmetic++;
        continue;
      }

      unresolved++;
    }
  }

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(" SUMMARY");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`Type-0/table-0 sites : ${sites}`);
  console.log(`CONST(114)           : ${const114}`);
  console.log(`Other constants      : ${constOther}`);
  console.log(`Memory loads         : ${load}`);
  console.log(`Local-derived        : ${local}`);
  console.log(`Global-derived       : ${global}`);
  console.log(`Call-derived         : ${call}`);
  console.log(`Arithmetic-derived   : ${arithmetic}`);
  console.log(`Unresolved           : ${unresolved}`);
  console.log();
  console.log(
    "NEXT: trace the base address of LOAD-derived indices."
  );
}

import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const BASE = 0x195348;

function sleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
        v -= 2 ** s;

      return { value: v | 0, end: p };
    }
  }

  return null;
}

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, end: p };

    s += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let hits = [];

for (let p = 0; p < b.length - 5; p++) {

  if (b[p] !== 0x41)
    continue;

  const r = sleb(p + 1);

  if (!r)
    continue;

  if ((r.value >>> 0) !== BASE)
    continue;

  hits.push({
    pos: p,
    end: r.end
  });
}

console.log("ALL S0 USES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("S0 :", hex(BASE));
console.log("HITS:", hits.length);
console.log();

for (const h of hits) {

  console.log("############################################################");
  console.log("S0 CONST @", hex(h.pos));
  console.log("############################################################");

  const start = Math.max(0, h.pos - 20);
  const end = Math.min(b.length, h.end + 100);

  for (let p = start; p < end; p++) {

    let name = "";

    if (b[p] === 0x10) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);
      if (r)
        name = `CALL ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (b[p] === 0x11) {
      const t = uleb(p + 1);
      if (t)
        name = `CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value}`;
    }

    else if (b[p] === 0x28) {
      name = "i32.load";
    }

    else if (b[p] === 0x36) {
      const a = uleb(p + 1);

      if (a) {
        const o = uleb(a.end);
        if (o)
          name = `i32.store offset=${o.value}`;
      }
    }

    else if (b[p] === 0x21) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);
      if (r)
        name = `local.set L${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (b[p] === 0x22) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);
      if (r)
        name = `local.tee L${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (b[p] === 0x20) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);
      if (r)
        name = `local.get L${r.value}`;
    }

    console.log(
      " ",
      hex(p),
      b[p].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"),
      name
    );
  }

  console.log();
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

FILE: scripts\traceBackwardTargetRecords.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = [
    { index: 2053, addr: 0x00189dec, fn: 8952 },
    { index: 2101, addr: 0x00189f9c, fn: 8927 },
    { index: 2612, addr: 0x0018a8b0, fn: 8637 },
];

const SEG412_START = 0x0018744c;
const SEG412_END   = 0x0018ab8d;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u32(p: number): number {
    if (p < 0 || p + 4 > bytes.length) return 0;
    return (
        bytes[p] |
        (bytes[p + 1] << 8) |
        (bytes[p + 2] << 16) |
        (bytes[p + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function in412(v: number): boolean {
    return v >= SEG412_START && v <= SEG412_END;
}

function isLow(v: number): boolean {
    return v < 0x00100000;
}

function isPtr(v: number): boolean {
    return in412(v) || (v >= 0x00100000 && v < 0x00200000);
}

function scanDataPointers(target: number, radius = 0x120) {
    const lo = Math.max(SEG412_START, target - radius);
    const hi = Math.min(SEG412_END, target + radius);

    console.log(`\nTARGET ${hex(target)}`);
    console.log(`WINDOW ${hex(lo)} .. ${hex(hi)}`);

    const hits: Array<{
        src: number;
        value: number;
        delta: number;
    }> = [];

    for (let addr = SEG412_START; addr <= SEG412_END - 4; addr += 4) {
        const file = findMemoryAddress(addr);
        if (file < 0) continue;

        const v = u32(file);

        if (v >= lo && v <= hi) {
            hits.push({
                src: addr,
                value: v,
                delta: v - target,
            });
        }
    }

    for (const h of hits) {
        console.log(
            `${hex(h.src)} -> ${hex(h.value)}  delta=${h.delta >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${h.delta}`
        );
    }

    console.log(`POINTERS INTO WINDOW = ${hits.length}`);

    return hits;
}

/*
 * Build a minimal memory-address -> file-offset map for data segments.
 * We parse the WASM data section directly.
 */
function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (pos < bytes.length) {
        const b = bytes[pos++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function findSection(id: number): { payload: number; end: number } {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < bytes.length) {
        const sid = bytes[p++];
        const sz = readULEB(p);
        p = sz.next;

        const payload = p;
        const end = payload + sz.value;

        if (sid === id) {
            return { payload, end };
        }

        p = end;
    }

    throw new Error(`Section ${id} not found`);
}

type Segment = {
    index: number;
    memoryStart: number;
    fileStart: number;
    size: number;
    fileEnd: number;
};

const dataSegments: Segment[] = [];

function parseDataSegments() {
    const sec = findSection(11);

    let p = sec.payload;

    const count = readULEB(p);
    p = count.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const flags = readULEB(p);
        p = flags.next;

        let memoryStart = 0;

        if (flags.value === 0) {
            if (bytes[p++] !== 0x41) {
                throw new Error("Expected i32.const in data offset");
            }

            const off = readULEB(p);
            p = off.next;

            memoryStart = off.value;

            if (bytes[p++] !== 0x0b) {
                throw new Error("Expected end opcode in data offset");
            }
        } else if (flags.value === 2) {
            const memidx = readULEB(p);
            p = memidx.next;

            if (bytes[p++] !== 0x41) {
                throw new Error("Expected i32.const in active data offset");
            }

            const off = readULEB(p);
            p = off.next;

            memoryStart = off.value;

            if (bytes[p++] !== 0x0b) {
                throw new Error("Expected end opcode in data offset");
            }
        } else {
            if (flags.value === 1) {
                const len = readULEB(p);
                p = len.next;
                p += len.value;
                continue;
            }

            throw new Error(`Unsupported data segment flags=${flags.value}`);
        }

        const len = readULEB(p);
        p = len.next;

        const fileStart = p;

        dataSegments.push({
            index: i,
            memoryStart,
            fileStart,
            size: len.value,
            fileEnd: fileStart + len.value,
        });

        p += len.value;
    }
}

function findMemoryAddress(addr: number): number {
    for (const s of dataSegments) {
        if (
            addr >= s.memoryStart &&
            addr < s.memoryStart + s.size
        ) {
            return s.fileStart + (addr - s.memoryStart);
        }
    }

    return -1;
}

function inspectWord(addr: number): {
    value: number;
    file: number;
} | null {
    const file = findMemoryAddress(addr);

    if (file < 0 || file + 4 > bytes.length) {
        return null;
    }

    return {
        value: u32(file),
        file,
    };
}

/*
 * Trace a bounded pointer graph inside data memory.
 *
 * We deliberately do NOT treat arbitrary integers as pointers.
 * Only values inside known data-memory ranges are followed.
 */
function tracePointerGraph(root: number, depthMax = 4) {
    console.log(`\nROOT GRAPH ${hex(root)}`);

    const seen = new Set<number>();

    function walk(addr: number, depth: number, path: string[]) {
        if (depth > depthMax) return;
        if (seen.has(addr)) return;

        seen.add(addr);

        const info = inspectWord(addr);

        if (!info) {
            console.log(
                `${" ".repeat(depth * 2)}depth ${depth}: ${hex(addr)} -> [not mapped]`
            );
            return;
        }

        const v = info.value;

        console.log(
            `${" ".repeat(depth * 2)}depth ${depth}: ` +
            `${hex(addr)} -> ${v} (${hex(v)})` +
            `${in412(v) ? " -> SEG412" : ""}`
        );

        if (!isPtr(v)) return;

        walk(v, depth + 1, [...path, hex(addr)]);
    }

    walk(root, 0, []);
}

/*
 * Inspect the exact record and identify all pointer-like fields.
 */
function inspectRecord(addr: number, radius = 0x80) {
    const start = Math.max(SEG412_START, addr - radius);
    const end = Math.min(SEG412_END, addr + radius);

    console.log(`\nRECORD ${hex(addr)}`);
    console.log(`RANGE  ${hex(start)} .. ${hex(end)}`);

    for (let p = start; p <= end - 4; p += 4) {
        const info = inspectWord(p);
        if (!info) continue;

        const v = info.value;

        if (p === addr) {
            console.log(
                `${hex(p)} : ${v} ${hex(v)} <<< TARGET`
            );
            continue;
        }

        if (in412(v)) {
            console.log(
                `${hex(p)} : ${v} ${hex(v)} -> SEG412`
            );
        } else if (isLow(v)) {
            console.log(
                `${hex(p)} : ${v} ${hex(v)} -> LOW`
            );
        }
    }
}

/*
 * Search for raw occurrences of target addresses and nearby addresses
 * in the entire WASM. This is intentionally independent of code/data
 * classification and is used only as a cross-check.
 */
function rawAddressSearch(target: number) {
    console.log(`\nRAW ADDRESS SEARCH ${hex(target)}`);

    let count = 0;

    for (let p = 0; p <= bytes.length - 4; p++) {
        if (u32(p) === target) {
            console.log(`file ${hex(p)} -> ${hex(target)}`);
            count++;
        }
    }

    console.log(`TOTAL RAW ADDRESS HITS = ${count}`);
}

/*
 * Search for 32-bit values in the target neighborhood.
 */
function rawNeighborhoodSearch(target: number, radius = 0x100) {
    console.log(`\nRAW NEIGHBORHOOD ADDRESS SEARCH ${hex(target)}`);

    const lo = target - radius;
    const hi = target + radius;

    let count = 0;

    for (let p = 0; p <= bytes.length - 4; p++) {
        const v = u32(p);

        if (v >= lo && v <= hi && in412(v)) {
            console.log(
                `file ${hex(p)} -> ${hex(v)}`
            );
            count++;
        }
    }

    console.log(`TOTAL NEIGHBORHOOD HITS = ${count}`);
}

/*
 * Code-section discovery.
 */
function getCodeSection() {
    return findSection(10);
}

function scanCodeForI32Const(value: number) {
    const sec = getCodeSection();

    console.log(
        `\nCODE CONST SEARCH ${value} ${hex(value)}`
    );

    let hits = 0;

    for (let p = sec.payload; p < sec.end - 5; p++) {
        if (bytes[p] !== 0x41) continue;

        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        if (r.value === value) {
            console.log(`file ${hex(p)} -> i32.const ${hex(value)}`);
            hits++;
        }
    }

    console.log(`TOTAL CODE CONST HITS = ${hits}`);
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" BACKWARD TARGET RECORD TRACE");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size = ${bytes.length}`);

parseDataSegments();

console.log(`Data segments = ${dataSegments.length}`);

const seg412 = dataSegments.find(
    s =>
        s.memoryStart === SEG412_START &&
        SEG412_START + s.size - 1 >= SEG412_END
);

if (seg412) {
    console.log(
        `Segment 412 confirmed: index=${seg412.index} ` +
        `memory=${hex(seg412.memoryStart)}..${hex(seg412.memoryStart + seg412.size - 1)}`
    );
} else {
    console.log("WARNING: exact Segment 412 mapping not found by start address.");
}

for (const t of TARGETS) {
    console.log("\n============================================================");
    console.log(` TARGET ${t.index} -> Function ${t.fn}`);
    console.log(` ADDRESS ${hex(t.addr)}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    const info = inspectWord(t.addr);

    if (info) {
        console.log(
            `TARGET VALUE = ${info.value} ${hex(info.value)}`
        );
    } else {
        console.log("TARGET ADDRESS NOT MAPPED");
    }

    inspectRecord(t.addr, 0x80);

    scanDataPointers(t.addr, 0x120);

    tracePointerGraph(t.addr, 3);

    rawAddressSearch(t.addr);

    rawNeighborhoodSearch(t.addr, 0x80);
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log(" CODE-SIDE TARGET ADDRESS CROSS-CHECK");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const t of TARGETS) {
    scanCodeForI32Const(t.addr);
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log(" STATIC DISPATCH-BASE CROSS-CHECK");
console.log("============================================================");

    1634268,
    1634408,
    1634128,
    1633988,
    1633928,
    1634104,
    1617084,
];

for (const base of STATIC_BASES) {
    const mapped = inspectWord(base);

    console.log(
        `BASE ${base} ${hex(base)} -> ` +
        (mapped
            ? `${mapped.value} ${hex(mapped.value)}`
            : "[not mapped]")
    );

    if (mapped && isPtr(mapped.value)) {
        console.log(
            `  POINTER -> ${hex(mapped.value)}`
        );
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceBulkCallerArgs.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = new Map<number, string>([
    [480, "memory.fill"],
    [1907, "memory.copy"],
]);

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function hex(n: number, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos,
            };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: pos,
    };
}

function readBlockType(pos: number) {
    const b = wasm[pos];

    if (b === 0x40)
        return { next: pos + 1 };

    if (
        b === 0x7f ||
        b === 0x7e ||
        b === 0x7d ||
        b === 0x7c
    ) {
        return { next: pos + 1 };
    }

    return readSLEB(pos);
}

function parseCodeSection() {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];

        const s = readULEB(p);
        p = s.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + s.value;

        if (id !== 10) {
            p = end;
            continue;
        }

        const count = readULEB(p);
        p = count.next;

        const bodies: {
            ordinal: number;
            start: number;
            end: number;
            codeStart: number;
        }[] = [];

        for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
            const bodySize = readULEB(p);
            p = bodySize.next;

            const bodyStart = p;
            const bodyEnd = p + bodySize.value;

            const localCount = readULEB(p);
            p = localCount.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < localCount.value; i++) {
                const n = readULEB(p);
                p = n.next;
                p++;
            }

            bodies.push({
                ordinal,
                start: bodyStart,
                end: bodyEnd,
                codeStart: p,
            });

            p = bodyEnd;
        }

        return {
            start,
            end,
            bodies,
        };
    }

    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

type Val =
    | { kind: "const"; value: number }
    | { kind: "local"; index: number }
    | { kind: "global"; index: number }
    | { kind: "unknown" };

function describe(v: Val) {
    if (v.kind === "const")
        return `CONST(${hex(v.value)})`;

    if (v.kind === "local")
        return `LOCAL(${v.index})`;

    if (v.kind === "global")
        return `GLOBAL(${v.index})`;

    return "UNKNOWN";
}

const code = parseCodeSection();

console.log("TRACE BULK CALLER ARGUMENTS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`CODE SECTION = ${hex(code.start)} .. ${hex(code.end)}`);
console.log(`CODE BODIES  = ${code.bodies.length}`);
console.log("");
console.log("TARGETS:");
console.log("  FUNC480  = memory.fill");
console.log("  FUNC1907 = memory.copy");
console.log("============================================================");

let calls = 0;

for (const body of code.bodies) {
    let p = body.codeStart;

    const stack: Val[] = [];

    function pop(): Val {
        if (stack.length === 0)
            return { kind: "unknown" };

        return stack.pop()!;
    }

    function push(v: Val) {
        stack.push(v);
    }

    while (p < body.end) {
        const pc = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        if (op === 0x0b)
            break;

        if (op === 0x01)
            continue;

        if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
            p = readBlockType(p).next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x05)
            continue;

        if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
            p = readULEB(p).next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0e) {
            const n = readULEB(p);
            p = n.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++)
                p = readULEB(p).next;

            p = readULEB(p).next;

            pop();
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0f)
            continue;

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // LOCAL
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x20) {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            push({
                kind: "local",
                index: r.value,
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x21) {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            pop();
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x22) {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            const v = pop();
            push(v);

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // GLOBAL
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x23) {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            push({
                kind: "global",
                index: r.value,
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x24) {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            pop();
            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // CONST
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = readSLEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            push({
                kind: "const",
                value: r.value >>> 0,
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x42) {
            const r = readSLEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            push({
                kind: "unknown",
            });

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // CALL
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            if (TARGETS.has(r.value)) {
                calls++;

                const name = TARGETS.get(r.value)!;

                console.log("");
                console.log("============================================================");
                console.log("BULK CALL");
                console.log("============================================================");
                console.log(`CALLER FUNC = ${body.ordinal}`);
                console.log(`CALL PC     = ${hex(pc)}`);
                console.log(`TARGET FUNC = ${r.value}`);
                console.log(`TARGET      = ${name}`);
                console.log("");
                console.log("STACK BEFORE CALL:");
                
                const start = Math.max(0, stack.length - 8);

                for (let i = start; i < stack.length; i++) {
                    console.log(
                        `  [${i - start}] ${describe(stack[i])}`
                    );
                }

                console.log("");
                console.log("IMPORTANT:");
                console.log("Arguments are shown symbolically.");
                console.log("No WASM execution.");
                console.log("============================================================");
            }

            /*
             * Do not clear stack here.
             * We want to see the exact caller-side argument
             * provenance immediately before the bulk call.
             */
            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // MEMORY LOAD
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (
            op >= 0x28 &&
            op <= 0x35
        ) {
            p = readULEB(p).next;
            p = readULEB(p).next;

            pop();

            push({
                kind: "unknown",
            });

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // MEMORY STORE
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (
            op >= 0x36 &&
            op <= 0x3e
        ) {
            p = readULEB(p).next;
            p = readULEB(p).next;

            pop();
            pop();

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // MEMORY.SIZE / GROW
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
            p++;
            push({
                kind: "unknown",
            });
            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // BINARY NUMERIC OPS
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        const binaryOps = new Set([
            0x46, 0x47, 0x48, 0x49, 0x4a, 0x4b,
            0x4c, 0x4d, 0x4e, 0x4f, 0x50, 0x51,
            0x6a, 0x6b, 0x6c, 0x6d, 0x6e, 0x6f,
            0x70, 0x71, 0x72, 0x73, 0x74, 0x75,
            0x76, 0x77, 0x78, 0x79, 0x7a,
            0x7b, 0x7c, 0x7d, 0x7e,
        ]);

        if (binaryOps.has(op)) {
            pop();
            pop();

            push({
                kind: "unknown",
            });

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // UNARY
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (
            op === 0x45 ||
            op === 0x67 ||
            op === 0x68 ||
            op === 0x69
        ) {
            pop();

            push({
                kind: "unknown",
            });

            continue;
        }

        // Unknown instruction:
        // leave stack untouched.
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BULK CALLS FOUND = ${calls}`);
console.log("");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceBulkDestinationProvenance.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_START = 0x19534c;
const TARGET_END   = 0x19535c;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function hex(n: number, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = bytes[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        shift += 7;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value -= 2 ** shift;

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function getCodeSection() {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < bytes.length) {
        const id = bytes[p++];

        const s = readULEB(p);
        const start = s.next;
        const end = start + s.value;

        if (id === 10)
            return { start, end };

        p = end;
    }

    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

const code = getCodeSection();

interface Body {
    func: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
    codeStart: number;
}

const bodies: Body[] = [];

let p = code.start;

const fc = readULEB(p);
p = fc.next;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < fc.value; ordinal++) {
    const bs = readULEB(p);
    const bodyStart = bs.next;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bs.value;

    let q = bodyStart;

    const locals = readULEB(q);
    q = locals.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < locals.value; i++) {
        const count = readULEB(q);
        q = count.next;
        q++;
    }

    bodies.push({
        func: ordinal + 83,
        start: bodyStart,
        end: bodyEnd,
        codeStart: q
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
}

const f480 = bodies.find(x => x.func === 480);

if (!f480)
    throw new Error("FUNC480 NOT FOUND");

interface Ins {
    pc: number;
    op: number;
    text: string;
    imm?: number;
}

const instructions: Ins[] = [];

function decode(pc: number): Ins {
    const op = bytes[pc];

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const r = readULEB(pc + 1);
        return {
            pc,
            op,
            text: `local.get ${r.value}`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const r = readULEB(pc + 1);
        return {
            pc,
            op,
            text: `local.set ${r.value}`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const r = readULEB(pc + 1);
        return {
            pc,
            op,
            text: `local.tee ${r.value}`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = readSLEB(pc + 1);

        return {
            pc,
            op,
            text: `i32.const ${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x6a)
        return { pc, op, text: "i32.add" };

    if (op === 0x6b)
        return { pc, op, text: "i32.sub" };

    if (op === 0x28) {
        const r = readULEB(pc + 2);

        return {
            pc,
            op,
            text: `i32.load offset=${hex(r.value)}`
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x36) {
        const r = readULEB(pc + 2);

        return {
            pc,
            op,
            text: `i32.store offset=${hex(r.value)}`
        };
    }

    if (op === 0xfc) {
        const r = readULEB(pc + 1);

        if (r.value === 10)
            return { pc, op, text: "memory.copy" };

        if (r.value === 11)
            return { pc, op, text: "memory.fill" };

        return { pc, op, text: `0xfc/${r.value}` };
    }

    // Control-flow / simple one-byte instructions.
    return {
        pc,
        op,
        text: `opcode ${hex(op, 2)}`
    };
}

function nextPC(pc: number) {
    const op = bytes[pc];

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
        return readULEB(pc + 1).next;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        return readSLEB(pc + 1).next;
    }

    if (
        op === 0x28 ||
        op === 0x29 ||
        op === 0x2a ||
        op === 0x2b ||
        op === 0x36 ||
        op === 0x37 ||
        op === 0x38 ||
        op === 0x39
    ) {
        return readULEB(pc + 2).next;
    }

    if (op === 0xfc) {
        return readULEB(pc + 1).next;
    }

    return pc + 1;
}

let q = f480.codeStart;

while (q < f480.end) {
    const ins = decode(q);
    instructions.push(ins);

    const n = nextPC(q);

    if (n <= q)
        q++;
    else
        q = n;
}

function isTarget(v: number) {
    return v >= TARGET_START && v < TARGET_END;
}

function showBackward(local: number, fromIndex: number, depth = 0, seen = new Set<string>()) {
    if (depth > 12)
        return ["MAX_DEPTH"];

    const key = `${local}@${fromIndex}`;

    if (seen.has(key))
        return ["LOOP"];

    seen.add(key);

    const lines: string[] = [];

    for (let i = fromIndex; i >= 0; i--) {
        const x = instructions[i];

        if (
            (x.text === `local.set ${local}` ||
             x.text === `local.tee ${local}`)
        ) {
            lines.push(
                `${" ".repeat(depth * 2)}${hex(x.pc)} ${x.text}`
            );

            const producerIndex = i - 1;

            if (producerIndex >= 0) {
                const prod = instructions[producerIndex];

                lines.push(
                    `${" ".repeat(depth * 2)}  <- ${hex(prod.pc)} ${prod.text}`
                );

                if (
                    prod.text.startsWith("i32.const")
                ) {
                    const v = prod.imm!;

                    if (isTarget(v)) {
                        lines.push(
                            `${" ".repeat(depth * 2)}  >>> TARGET CONSTANT ${hex(v)}`
                        );
                    }
                }

                if (
                    prod.text === "local.get " + local
                ) {
                    lines.push(
                        `${" ".repeat(depth * 2)}  SELF-REFERENCE`
                    );
                }
            }

            break;
        }
    }

    return lines.length ? lines : ["NO LOCAL DEFINITION FOUND"];
}

console.log("TRACE BULK DESTINATION PROVENANCE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`FUNC 480 BODY = ${hex(f480.start)} .. ${hex(f480.end)}`);
console.log(`TARGET RANGE = ${hex(TARGET_START)} .. ${hex(TARGET_END - 1)}`);

let bulkCount = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
    const ins = instructions[i];

    if (
        ins.text !== "memory.fill" &&
        ins.text !== "memory.copy"
    )
        continue;

    bulkCount++;

    console.log("");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(`BULK #${bulkCount}`);
    console.log(`PC        = ${hex(ins.pc)}`);
    console.log(`OPERATION = ${ins.text}`);

    /*
     * Immediately preceding stack producers.
     *
     * For this diagnostic we intentionally only identify
     * direct local/constant destination producers.
     */
    const before = instructions.slice(
        Math.max(0, i - 8),
        i
    );

    const gets = before.filter(
        x => x.text.startsWith("local.get ")
    );

    const consts = before.filter(
        x => x.text.startsWith("i32.const ")
    );

    console.log("RECENT:");

    for (const x of before)
        console.log(`${hex(x.pc)} ${x.text}`);

    if (gets.length === 0) {
        console.log("DESTINATION LOCAL = NONE");
        continue;
    }

    /*
     * For fill/copy the first stack operand is destination.
     * The nearest local.get before the bulk op is normally the
     * destination in these compiler-generated patterns.
     */
    const dstGet = gets[0];
    const local = dstGet.imm!;

    console.log(`DESTINATION LOCAL = ${local}`);

    const provenance = showBackward(
        local,
        i - 1
    );

    for (const line of provenance)
        console.log(line);
}

import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const START = 0x961f0;
const END   = 0x96270;

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { v: v >>> 0, p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;
  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v |= (-1 << s);
  return { v: v | 0, p };
}

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  if (op >= 0x20 && op <= 0x24) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    const names: Record<number,string> = {
      0x20: "local.get",
      0x21: "local.set",
      0x22: "local.tee",
      0x23: "global.get",
      0x24: "global.set"
    };

    console.log(`${hex(at)} ${names[op]} ${r.v}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = sleb(p);
    p = r.p;
    console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.const ${r.v}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x42) {
    const r = sleb(p);
    p = r.p;
    console.log(`${hex(at)} i64.const ${r.v}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;
    console.log(`${hex(at)} CALL ${r.v}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
    const a = uleb(p);
    p = a.p;

    const o = uleb(p);
    p = o.p;

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} ${op >= 0x36 ? "STORE" : "LOAD"} ` +
      `op=0x${op.toString(16)} align=${a.v} offset=${o.v}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x6a || op === 0x6b ||
      op === 0x71 || op === 0x72 ||
      op === 0x73 || op === 0x74 ||
      op === 0x75 || op === 0x76 ||
      op === 0x7c || op === 0x7d ||
      op === 0x7e || op === 0x7f ||
      op === 0x80 || op === 0x81 ||
      op === 0x82 || op === 0x83 ||
      op === 0x84 || op === 0x85 ||
      op === 0x86 || op === 0x87 ||
      op === 0x88) {
    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0b || op === 0x05 ||
      op === 0x1a) {
    console.log(`${hex(at)} control/drop 0x${op.toString(16)}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;
    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} ${op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if"} ${r.v}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  console.log(
    `${hex(at)} UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
  );
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 2094;
const TARGET_CALL = 185;

const START = 0x95e67;
const END = 0x96da8;
const CONTEXT = 24;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u8(p: number): number {
  return wasm[p];
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = u8(p++);
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value, next: p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number, bits = 32): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = u8(p++);
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (shift < bits && (b & 0x40)) {
    value -= 2 ** shift;
  }

  return { value, next: p };
}

function hex(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function bytes(a: number, b: number): string {
  return Array.from(wasm.slice(a, b))
    .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join(" ");
}

function decode(pos: number): {
  next: number;
  text: string;
} {
  const op = u8(pos++);

  switch (op) {
    case 0x00: return { next: pos, text: "unreachable" };
    case 0x01: return { next: pos, text: "nop" };
    case 0x05: return { next: pos, text: "else" };
    case 0x0b: return { next: pos, text: "end" };
    case 0x0f: return { next: pos, text: "return" };

    case 0x02:
    case 0x03:
    case 0x04: {
      const x = readSLEB(pos, 33);
      const name =
        op === 0x02 ? "block" :
        op === 0x03 ? "loop" : "if";
      return { next: x.next, text: `${name} ${x.value}` };
    }

    case 0x0c:
    case 0x0d: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `${op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if"} ${x.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x0e: {
      const n = readULEB(pos);
      pos = n.next;

      const labels: number[] = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const x = readULEB(pos);
        pos = x.next;
        labels.push(x.value);
      }

      const d = readULEB(pos);
      pos = d.next;

      return {
        next: pos,
        text: `br_table [${labels.join(",")}] default=${d.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { next: x.next, text: `call ${x.value}` };
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      pos = x.next;

      const t = readULEB(pos);
      pos = t.next;

      return {
        next: pos,
        text: `call_indirect type=${x.value} table=${t.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x1a: return { next: pos, text: "drop" };
    case 0x1b: return { next: pos, text: "select" };

    case 0x20:
    case 0x21:
    case 0x22: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);

      const n =
        op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
        op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
        "local.tee";

      return { next: x.next, text: `${n} ${x.value}` };
    }

    case 0x23:
    case 0x24: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);

      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `${op === 0x23 ? "global.get" : "global.set"} ${x.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x28:
    case 0x29:
    case 0x2a:
    case 0x2b:
    case 0x2c:
    case 0x2d:
    case 0x2e:
    case 0x2f:
    case 0x30:
    case 0x31:
    case 0x32:
    case 0x33:
    case 0x34:
    case 0x35:
    case 0x36:
    case 0x37:
    case 0x38:
    case 0x39:
    case 0x3a:
    case 0x3b:
    case 0x3c:
    case 0x3d:
    case 0x3e: {
      const align = readULEB(pos);
      pos = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(pos);
      pos = off.next;

      const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2a: "f32.load",
        0x2b: "f64.load",
        0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
        0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
        0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
        0x30: "i64.load8_s",
        0x31: "i64.load8_u",
        0x32: "i64.load16_s",
        0x33: "i64.load16_u",
        0x34: "i64.load32_s",
        0x35: "i64.load32_u",
        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",
        0x38: "f32.store",
        0x39: "f64.store",
        0x3a: "i32.store8",
        0x3b: "i32.store16",
        0x3c: "i64.store8",
        0x3d: "i64.store16",
        0x3e: "i64.store32",
      };

      return {
        next: pos,
        text:
          `${names[op]} ` +
          `align=${align.value} offset=${off.value} (${hex(off.value)})`,
      };
    }

    case 0x3f:
    case 0x40: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `${op === 0x3f ? "memory.size" : "memory.grow"} ${x.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = readSLEB(pos, 32);
      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const x = readSLEB(pos, 64);
      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x45: return { next: pos, text: "i32.eqz" };
    case 0x46: return { next: pos, text: "i32.eq" };
    case 0x47: return { next: pos, text: "i32.ne" };
    case 0x48: return { next: pos, text: "i32.lt_s" };
    case 0x49: return { next: pos, text: "i32.lt_u" };
    case 0x4a: return { next: pos, text: "i32.gt_s" };
    case 0x4b: return { next: pos, text: "i32.gt_u" };
    case 0x4c: return { next: pos, text: "i32.le_s" };
    case 0x4d: return { next: pos, text: "i32.le_u" };
    case 0x4e: return { next: pos, text: "i32.ge_s" };
    case 0x4f: return { next: pos, text: "i32.ge_u" };

    case 0x50: return { next: pos, text: "i64.eqz" };
    case 0x51: return { next: pos, text: "i64.eq" };
    case 0x52: return { next: pos, text: "i64.ne" };
    case 0x53: return { next: pos, text: "i64.lt_s" };
    case 0x54: return { next: pos, text: "i64.lt_u" };
    case 0x55: return { next: pos, text: "i64.gt_s" };
    case 0x56: return { next: pos, text: "i64.gt_u" };
    case 0x57: return { next: pos, text: "i64.le_s" };
    case 0x58: return { next: pos, text: "i64.le_u" };
    case 0x59: return { next: pos, text: "i64.ge_s" };
    case 0x5a: return { next: pos, text: "i64.ge_u" };

    case 0x6a: return { next: pos, text: "i32.add" };
    case 0x6b: return { next: pos, text: "i32.sub" };
    case 0x6c: return { next: pos, text: "i32.mul" };
    case 0x6d: return { next: pos, text: "i32.div_s" };
    case 0x6e: return { next: pos, text: "i32.div_u" };
    case 0x71: return { next: pos, text: "i32.and" };
    case 0x72: return { next: pos, text: "i32.or" };
    case 0x73: return { next: pos, text: "i32.xor" };
    case 0x74: return { next: pos, text: "i32.shl" };
    case 0x75: return { next: pos, text: "i32.shr_s" };
    case 0x76: return { next: pos, text: "i32.shr_u" };

    case 0x7c: return { next: pos, text: "i64.add" };
    case 0x7d: return { next: pos, text: "i64.sub" };
    case 0x7e: return { next: pos, text: "i64.mul" };
    case 0x7f: return { next: pos, text: "i64.div_s" };
    case 0x80: return { next: pos, text: "i64.div_u" };
    case 0x81: return { next: pos, text: "i64.rem_s" };
    case 0x82: return { next: pos, text: "i64.rem_u" };
    case 0x83: return { next: pos, text: "i64.and" };
    case 0x84: return { next: pos, text: "i64.or" };
    case 0x85: return { next: pos, text: "i64.xor" };
    case 0x86: return { next: pos, text: "i64.shl" };
    case 0x87: return { next: pos, text: "i64.shr_s" };
    case 0x88: return { next: pos, text: "i64.shr_u" };

    case 0xad:
      return { next: pos, text: "i64.extend_i32_u" };

    case 0xac:
      return { next: pos, text: "i64.extend_i32_s" };

    default:
      return {
        next: pos,
        text: `UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
      };
  }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Find Function 2094 body
 * ------------------------------------------------------------ */

function parseSections() {
  const out: any[] = [];
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const start = p;
    const id = u8(p++);

    const sz = readULEB(p);
    p = sz.next;

    out.push({
      id,
      start,
      payloadStart: p,
      payloadEnd: p + sz.value,
      size: sz.value,
    });

    p += sz.value;
  }

  return out;
}

const sections = parseSections();

const importSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 2);
let importedFunctions = 0;

if (importSec) {
  let p = importSec.payloadStart;
  const n = readULEB(p);
  p = n.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    const ml = readULEB(p);
    p = ml.next + ml.value;

    const fl = readULEB(p);
    p = fl.next + fl.value;

    const kind = u8(p++);

    if (kind === 0) {
      const t = readULEB(p);
      p = t.next;
      importedFunctions++;
    } else if (kind === 1) {
      p++;
      const flags = readULEB(p);
      p = flags.next;
      const min = readULEB(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags.value & 1) {
        const max = readULEB(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 2) {
      const flags = readULEB(p);
      p = flags.next;
      const min = readULEB(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags.value & 1) {
        const max = readULEB(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }
}

const codeSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!codeSec) throw new Error("Code section missing");

let p = codeSec.payloadStart;

const bodyCount = readULEB(p);
p = bodyCount.next;

let targetStart = 0;
let targetEnd = 0;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < bodyCount.value; ordinal++) {
  const sz = readULEB(p);
  p = sz.next;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = p + sz.value;

  const funcIndex = importedFunctions + ordinal;

  if (funcIndex === TARGET_FUNC) {
    targetStart = bodyStart;
    targetEnd = bodyEnd;
    break;
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

if (!targetStart) {
  throw new Error("Function 2094 body not found");
}

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';

const root = 'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin';
const sourcePath = path.join(root, 'scripts', 'disassembleAddressUsers.mjs');
const wasmPath = path.join(root, 'emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm');
const target = 2034;

if (!fs.existsSync(sourcePath) || !fs.existsSync(wasmPath)) {
    throw new Error('Source script or WASM file not found');
}

const source = fs.readFileSync(sourcePath, 'utf8');
const marker = "console.log('\\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');";
const cut = source.indexOf(marker);

if (cut < 0) {
    throw new Error('Could not find the expected decoder marker');
}

let prefix = source.slice(0, cut);

function replaceExactlyOnce(input, search, replacement, label) {
    const first = input.indexOf(search);

    if (first < 0 || input.indexOf(search, first + search.length) >= 0) {
        throw new Error(`Expected exactly one replacement point: ${label}`);
    }

    return input.slice(0, first) +
        replacement +
        input.slice(first + search.length);
}

// Add the missing standard numeric opcodes.

FILE: scripts\traceCall2057Args.mjs
