import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 2057;
const CALL_OFFSETS = [
  0x00100fff,
  0x001015ac,
  0x00101759
];

function readULEB(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let n = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    n++;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, len: n };

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35)
      return null;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/*
 * Parse code section and locate function bodies.
 */
function parseCodeSection() {

  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {

    const sectionId = b[p++];

    const sr = readULEB(p);
    if (!sr) break;

    const sectionSize = sr.value;
    const sectionStart = p + sr.len;
    const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 10) {

      let q = sectionStart;

      const cr = readULEB(q);
      if (!cr) return [];

      const count = cr.value;
      q += cr.len;

      const funcs = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

        const br = readULEB(q);
        if (!br) break;

        const bodySize = br.value;
        q += br.len;

        const bodyStart = q;
        const bodyEnd = q + bodySize;

        funcs.push({
          func: i + 100, // corrected below using imported count
          start: bodyStart,
          end: bodyEnd
        });

        q = bodyEnd;
      }

      return funcs;
    }

    p = sectionEnd;
  }

  return [];
}

/*
 * Get imported function count.
 */
function getImportedFunctionCount() {

  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {

    const sectionId = b[p++];

    const sr = readULEB(p);
    if (!sr) break;

    const sectionSize = sr.value;
    const sectionStart = p + sr.len;
    const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 2) {

      let q = sectionStart;

      const r = readULEB(q);
      if (!r) return 0;

      const count = r.value;
      q += r.len;

      let funcs = 0;

      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

        const ml = readULEB(q);
        q += ml.len;

        const fl = readULEB(q);
        q += fl.len;

        const kl = readULEB(q);
        q += kl.len;

        // import kind:
        // 0=function
        // 1=table
        // 2=memory
        // 3=global

        if (kl.value === 0) {

          const typeIndex = readULEB(q);
          q += typeIndex.len;

          funcs++;
        }
        else if (kl.value === 1) {

          const elemType = b[q++];

          const flags = readULEB(q);
          q += flags.len;

          if (flags.value & 1) {
            const a = readULEB(q);
            q += a.len;
          }

          const initial = readULEB(q);
          q += initial.len;

        }
        else if (kl.value === 2) {

          const flags = readULEB(q);
          q += flags.len;

          const initial = readULEB(q);
          q += initial.len;

          if (flags.value & 1) {
            const maximum = readULEB(q);
            q += maximum.len;
          }

        }
        else if (kl.value === 3) {

          q += 2;
        }
      }

      return funcs;
    }

    p = sectionEnd;
  }

  return 0;
}

const importedCount = getImportedFunctionCount();
const bodies = parseCodeSection();

for (const x of bodies)
  x.func += importedCount;

function findFunction(offset) {
  return bodies.find(x => offset >= x.start && offset < x.end);
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("FUNC 2057 CALL ARGUMENT TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`TARGET FUNC : ${TARGET}`);
console.log(`IMPORTS     : ${importedCount}`);
console.log("");

for (const callOffset of CALL_OFFSETS) {

  const fn = findFunction(callOffset);

  console.log("-".repeat(72));
  console.log(`CALL @ ${hex(callOffset)}`);

  if (!fn) {
    console.log("CALLER FUNC : UNKNOWN");
    continue;
  }

  console.log(`CALLER FUNC : ${fn.func}`);
  console.log(
    `BODY        : ${hex(fn.start)} .. ${hex(fn.end)}`
  );

  /*
   * Show the 80 bytes immediately before the call.
   */
  const start = Math.max(fn.start, callOffset - 80);
  const end = Math.min(fn.end, callOffset + 8);

  console.log("");
  console.log("RAW CONTEXT:");

  for (let p = start; p < end; ) {

    const at = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    let text = `0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  ` +
               `${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`;

    if (op === 0x20) {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (r) {
        text += `  local.get ${r.value}`;
        p += r.len;
      }
    }
    else if (op === 0x21) {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (r) {
        text += `  local.set ${r.value}`;
        p += r.len;
      }
    }
    else if (op === 0x22) {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (r) {
        text += `  local.tee ${r.value}`;
        p += r.len;
      }
    }
    else if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (r) {
        text += `  i32.const ${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`;
        p += r.len;
      }
    }
    else if (op === 0x10) {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (r) {
        text += `  call ${r.value}`;
        p += r.len;
      }
    }
    else if (op === 0x28) {
      if (p + 1 < b.length) {
        const align = b[p++];
        const r = readULEB(p);
        if (r) {
          text += `  i32.load align=${align} offset=${r.value}`;
          p += r.len;
        }
      }
    }
    else if (op === 0x36) {
      if (p + 1 < b.length) {
        const align = b[p++];
        const r = readULEB(p);
        if (r) {
          text += `  i32.store align=${align} offset=${r.value}`;
          p += r.len;
        }
      }
    }
    else if (op === 0x6a) {
      text += "  i32.add";
    }
    else if (op === 0x6b) {
      text += "  i32.sub";
    }
    else if (op === 0x74) {
      text += "  i32.shl";
    }
    else if (op === 0x76) {
      text += "  i32.shr_u";
    }
    else if (op === 0x45) {
      text += "  i32.eqz";
    }
    else if (op === 0x0d) {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (r) {
        text += `  br_if ${r.value}`;
        p += r.len;
      }
    }
    else if (op === 0x0c) {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      if (r) {
        text += `  br ${r.value}`;
        p += r.len;
      }
    }
    else if (op === 0x0b) {
      text += "  end";
    }
    else if (op === 0x02) {
      text += `  block 0x${b[p].toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`;
      p++;
    }
    else if (op === 0x04) {
      text += `  if 0x${b[p].toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`;
      p++;
    }
    else if (op === 0x05) {
      text += "  else";
    }
    else {
      text += `  opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`;
    }

    console.log(text);
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("DONE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceCall205ProducersV2.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 2094;

const STORES = [
    0x9670d,
    0x967c8,
    0x96883,
    0x96baf,
];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 2094 CALL 205 PRODUCER TRACE V2");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

const bytes = new Uint8Array(fs.readFileSync(WASM));

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; end: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, end: p };
}

function sleb32(pos: number): { value: number; end: number } {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = bytes[p++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= ~0 << shift;
    }

    return {
        value: result,
        end: p,
    };
}

function readSections() {
    let p = 8;

    let codeStart = 0;
    let codeEnd = 0;

    while (p < bytes.length) {
        const id = bytes[p++];

        const s = uleb(p);
        p = s.end;

        const payload = p;
        const end = p + s.value;

        if (id === 10) {
            codeStart = payload;
            codeEnd = end;
        }

        p = end;
    }

    return { codeStart, codeEnd };
}

function importedFunctions(): number {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < bytes.length) {
        const id = bytes[p++];

        const s = uleb(p);
        p = s.end;

        const end = p + s.value;

        if (id !== 2) {
            p = end;
            continue;
        }

        let q = p;

        const count = uleb(q);
        q = count.end;

        let n = 0;

        for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

            const ml = uleb(q);
            q = ml.end + ml.value;

            const nl = uleb(q);
            q = nl.end + nl.value;

            const kind = bytes[q++];

            if (kind === 0) {
                const t = uleb(q);
                q = t.end;
                n++;
            } else if (kind === 1) {
                q += 4;
            } else if (kind === 2) {
                q += 1;
            } else if (kind === 3) {
                q += 2;
            }
        }

        return n;
    }

    return 0;
}

function getBody(func: number) {

    const sec = readSections();

    const imported = importedFunctions();

    const bodyIndex = func - imported;

    let p = sec.codeStart;

    const count = uleb(p);
    p = count.end;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

        const s = uleb(p);

        const bodyStart = s.end;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + s.value;

        if (i === bodyIndex) {
            return {
                bodyStart,
                bodyEnd,
                imported,
            };
        }

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    throw new Error("Function body not found");
}

function decode(pc: number) {

    const op = bytes[pc];

    let p = pc + 1;

    switch (op) {

        case 0x20: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            return {
                text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            return {
                text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            return {
                text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const x = sleb32(p);
            return {
                text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x42: {
            while (bytes[p++] & 0x80) {}

            return {
                text: "i64.const",
                end: p,
            };
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const x = uleb(p);

            return {
                text: `call ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x28:
        case 0x29:
        case 0x2a:
        case 0x2b:
        case 0x36:
        case 0x37:
        case 0x38:
        case 0x39: {

            const a = uleb(p);
            const o = uleb(a.end);

            return {
                text:
                    `memory-op 0x${op.toString(16)} ` +
                    `offset=${o.value}`,
                end: o.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x6a:
            return { text: "i32.add", end: p };

        case 0x6b:
            return { text: "i32.sub", end: p };

        case 0x0b:
            return { text: "end", end: p };

        case 0x0c: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            return {
                text: `br ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            return {
                text: `br_if ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x02:
        case 0x03:
        case 0x04: {

            const bt = bytes[p++];

            const name =
                op === 0x02 ? "block" :
                op === 0x03 ? "loop" :
                "if";

            return {
                text:
                    `${name} blocktype=0x${bt.toString(16)}`,
                end: p,
            };
        }

        default:
            return {
                text:
                    `op_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
                end: p,
            };
    }
}

);

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 2094;

const TARGETS = [
    0x9670d,
    0x967c8,
    0x96883,
    0x96baf,
];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 2094 PRODUCER ADDRESS TRACE V3");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

const bytes = new Uint8Array(fs.readFileSync(WASM));

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; end: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, end: p };
}

function sleb32(pos: number): { value: number; end: number } {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = bytes[p++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 0;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= ~0 << shift;
    }

    return {
        value: result,
        end: p,
    };
}

function sections() {

    let p = 8;

    let codeStart = 0;
    let codeEnd = 0;

    while (p < bytes.length) {

        const id = bytes[p++];

        const s = uleb(p);
        p = s.end;

        const end = p + s.value;

        if (id === 10) {
            codeStart = p;
            codeEnd = end;
        }

        p = end;
    }

    return { codeStart, codeEnd };
}

function importedFunctions() {

    let p = 8;

    while (p < bytes.length) {

        const id = bytes[p++];

        const s = uleb(p);
        p = s.end;

        const end = p + s.value;

        if (id !== 2) {
            p = end;
            continue;
        }

        let q = p;

        const c = uleb(q);
        q = c.end;

        let n = 0;

        for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {

            const ml = uleb(q);
            q = ml.end + ml.value;

            const nl = uleb(q);
            q = nl.end + nl.value;

            const kind = bytes[q++];

            if (kind === 0) {
                const t = uleb(q);
                q = t.end;
                n++;
            }
            else if (kind === 1) {
                q += 4;
            }
            else if (kind === 2) {
                q += 1;
            }
            else if (kind === 3) {
                q += 2;
            }
        }

        return n;
    }

    return 0;
}

function getBody(func: number) {

    const sec = sections();

    const imported = importedFunctions();

    const index = func - imported;

    let p = sec.codeStart;

    const count = uleb(p);
    p = count.end;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

        const s = uleb(p);

        const start = s.end;
        const end = start + s.value;

        if (i === index) {

            return {
                start,
                end,
                imported,
            };
        }

        p = end;
    }

    throw new Error("Function body not found");
}

function decode(pc: number) {

    const op = bytes[pc];

    let p = pc + 1;

    switch (op) {

        case 0x20: {
            const x = uleb(p);

            return {
                text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const x = uleb(p);

            return {
                text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const x = uleb(p);

            return {
                text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const x = sleb32(p);

            return {
                text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x42: {

            while (bytes[p++] & 0x80) {}

            return {
                text: "i64.const",
                end: p,
            };
        }

        case 0x6a:
            return {
                text: "i32.add",
                end: p,
            };

        case 0x6b:
            return {
                text: "i32.sub",
                end: p,
            };

        case 0x74:
            return {
                text: "i32.shl",
                end: p,
            };

        case 0x46:
            return {
                text: "i32.eq",
                end: p,
            };

        case 0x1b:
            return {
                text: "select",
                end: p,
            };

        case 0x7c:
            return {
                text: "i64.add",
                end: p,
            };

        case 0x7d:
            return {
                text: "i64.sub",
                end: p,
            };

        case 0x29: {
            const a = uleb(p);
            const o = uleb(a.end);

            return {
                text: `i64.load offset=${o.value}`,
                end: o.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x37: {
            const a = uleb(p);
            const o = uleb(a.end);

            return {
                text: `i64.store offset=${o.value}`,
                end: o.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const x = uleb(p);

            return {
                text: `call ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x03: {

            const bt = bytes[p++];

            return {
                text: `loop blocktype=0x${bt.toString(16)}`,
                end: p,
            };
        }

        case 0x02: {

            const bt = bytes[p++];

            return {
                text: `block blocktype=0x${bt.toString(16)}`,
                end: p,
            };
        }

        case 0x04: {

            const bt = bytes[p++];

            return {
                text: `if blocktype=0x${bt.toString(16)}`,
                end: p,
            };
        }

        case 0x0b:
            return {
                text: "end",
                end: p,
            };

        case 0x0c: {
            const x = uleb(p);

            return {
                text: `br ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const x = uleb(p);

            return {
                text: `br_if ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        default:
            return {
                text:
                    `op_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
                end: p,
            };
    }
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x012200;
const END   = 0x01295b;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function sleb(pos: number, bits: number): {
    value: bigint;
    next: number;
} {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (
                shift + 7n < BigInt(bits) &&
                (b & 0x40)
            ) {
                value |=
                    (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
            }

            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }

    throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
}

function memarg(pos: number) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    const o = uleb(a.next);

    return {
        align: a.value,
        offset: o.value,
        next: o.next
    };
}

interface Ins {
    start: number;
    end: number;
    op: number;
    text: string;
    kind: string;
    local?: number;
    offset?: number;
    call?: number;
}

function decode(pos: number): Ins {
    const start = pos;
    const op = buf[pos++];

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const x = uleb(pos);
        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
            kind: "local.get",
            local: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const x = uleb(pos);
        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
            kind: "local.set",
            local: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const x = uleb(pos);
        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
            kind: "local.tee",
            local: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = sleb(pos, 32);
        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
            kind: "i32.const"
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        const x = sleb(pos, 64);
        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
            kind: "i64.const"
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = uleb(pos);
        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `call ${x.value}`,
            kind: "call",
            call: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x29 || op === 0x36 || op === 0x37) {
        const m = memarg(pos);

        let name = `op=0x${op.toString(16)}`;

        if (op === 0x28)
            name = "i32.load";

        if (op === 0x29)
            name = "i64.load";

        if (op === 0x36)
            name = "i32.store";

        if (op === 0x37)
            name = "i64.store";

        return {
            start,
            end: m.next,
            op,
            text: `${name} offset=${m.offset}`,
            kind: name,
            offset: m.offset
        };
    }

    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",

        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",

        0x83: "i64.and",
        0x84: "i64.or",
        0x86: "i64.shl",
        0x88: "i64.shr_u",

        0x50: "i64.eqz",
        0x54: "i64.lt_u",
        0x55: "i64.gt_u",
        0x56: "i64.le_u",

        0xad: "i64.extend32_s",
        0xac: "i64.extend_i32_u"
    };

    return {
        start,
        end: pos,
        op,
        text:
            names[op] ??
            `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
        kind: "other"
    };
}

const ins: Ins[] = [];

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
    const x = decode(p);
    ins.push(x);
    p = x.end;
}

if (p !== END) {
    throw new Error(
        `Decode mismatch: ${p.toString(16)} != ${END.toString(16)}`
    );
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" +
        n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Address symbolic expressions
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

type Expr = string;

const stack: Expr[] = [];
const locals = new Map<number, Expr>();

function push(x: Expr) {
    stack.push(x);
}

function pop(): Expr {
    if (!stack.length)
        return "STACK_UNDERFLOW";

    return stack.pop()!;
}

function pop2(): [Expr, Expr] {
    const b = pop();
    const a = pop();
    return [a, b];
}

function normalizeAddress(x: Expr): Expr {
    /*
     * Convert common equivalent forms into one canonical form.
     */
    x = x.replace(/\s+/g, "");

    x = x.replace(
        /^\(local2-(-?\d+)\)$/,
        "local2+$1"
    );

    x = x.replace(
        /^\(local2\+(-?\d+)\)$/,
        "local2+$1"
    );

    return x;
}

function addressOffset(x: Expr): number | null {
    x = normalizeAddress(x);

    if (x === "local2")
        return 0;

    const m =
        /^local2\+(-?\d+)$/.exec(x);

    if (!m)
        return null;

    return Number(m[1]);
}

function addAddress(
    a: Expr,
    b: Expr,
    subtract = false
): Expr {
    /*
     * local2 +/- constant
     */
    const ao = addressOffset(a);
    const bo = addressOffset(b);

    if (ao !== null) {
        const n = Number(b);

        if (Number.isFinite(n)) {
            return `local2+${ao + (subtract ? -n : n)}`;
        }
    }

    if (bo !== null) {
        const n = Number(a);

        if (Number.isFinite(n)) {
            return `local2+${bo + (subtract ? n : n)}`;
        }
    }

    return subtract
        ? `(${a}-${b})`
        : `(${a}+${b})`;
}

    " READ ONLY - OFFLINE"

    "============================================================"
);

console.log(`Range     : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log(`Decoded   : ${ins.length}`);

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Important known address events
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

interface Event {
    at: number;
    type: string;
    address: string;
    offset: number | null;
    detail: string;
}

const events: Event[] = [];

function event(
    at: number,
    type: string,
    address: string,
    detail: string
) {
    events.push({
        at,
        type,
        address: normalizeAddress(address),
        offset: addressOffset(address),
        detail
    });
}

/*
 * We are particularly interested in:
 *
 *   CALL #26 destination
 *   loads/stores at +432 / +440
 *   all addresses around the second half
 */
let call26Dest = "";
let call26At = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
    const x = ins[i];

    switch (x.kind) {
        case "local.get": {
            push(
                locals.get(x.local!) ??
                `local${x.local!}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case "local.set": {
            const v = pop();
            locals.set(x.local!, v);
            break;
        }

        case "local.tee": {
            const v =
                stack.length
                    ? stack[stack.length - 1]
                    : "STACK_UNDERFLOW";

            locals.set(x.local!, v);
            break;
        }

        case "i32.const": {
            const value =
                x.text.substring("i32.const ".length);

            push(value);
            break;
        }

        case "i64.const": {
            const value =
                x.text.substring("i64.const ".length);

            push(`i64:${value}`);
            break;
        }

        case "i32.load": {
            const [base] = [pop()];

            push(
                `LOAD32(${normalizeAddress(base)}+${x.offset ?? 0})`
            );

            break;
        }

        case "i64.load": {
            const base = pop();

            const addr =
                addressOffset(base) !== null
                    ? `local2+${(addressOffset(base) ?? 0) + (x.offset ?? 0)}`
                    : `(${base}+${x.offset ?? 0})`;

            event(
                x.start,
                "LOAD",
                addr,
                x.text
            );

            push(
                `MEM64[${normalizeAddress(addr)}]`
            );

            break;
        }

        case "i32.store": {
            const value = pop();
            const base = pop();

            const addr =
                addressOffset(base) !== null
                    ? `local2+${(addressOffset(base) ?? 0) + (x.offset ?? 0)}`
                    : `(${base}+${x.offset ?? 0})`;

            event(
                x.start,
                "STORE32",
                addr,
                `value=${value}`
            );

            break;
        }

        case "i64.store": {
            const value = pop();
            const base = pop();

            const addr =
                addressOffset(base) !== null
                    ? `local2+${(addressOffset(base) ?? 0) + (x.offset ?? 0)}`
                    : `(${base}+${x.offset ?? 0})`;

            event(
                x.start,
                "STORE",
                addr,
                `value=${value}`
            );

            break;
        }

        case "call": {
            if (x.call !== 87)
                break;

            /*
             * Function 87:
             *
             * destination, A, zero, B, zero
             */
            const zero2 = pop();
            const B = pop();
            const zero1 = pop();
            const A = pop();
            const destination = pop();

            if (x.start === 0x0126b4) {
                call26At = x.start;
                call26Dest =
                    normalizeAddress(destination);

                console.log(
                    "\n============================================================"
                );

                console.log(
                    "CALL #26 DESTINATION PROVENANCE"
                );

                console.log(
                    "============================================================"
                );

                console.log(
                    `CALL @ ${hex(x.start)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `destination expression = ${destination}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `normalized             = ${call26Dest}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `resolved offset        = ${
                        addressOffset(call26Dest)
                    }`
                );

                console.log(
                    `A = ${A}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `B = ${B}`
                );
            }

            /*
             * Function 87 returns nothing.
             */
            break;
        }

        case "other": {
            if (
                x.text === "i32.add" ||
                x.text === "i32.sub"
            ) {
                const [a, b] = pop2();

                /*
                 * i32 arithmetic is used for addresses.
                 */
                if (x.text === "i32.add") {
                    const ao = addressOffset(a);

                    if (
                        ao !== null &&
                        /^-?\d+$/.test(b)
                    ) {
                        push(
                            `local2+${ao + Number(b)}`
                        );
                    } else {
                        push(
                            `(${a}+${b})`
                        );
                    }
                } else {
                    const ao = addressOffset(a);

                    if (
                        ao !== null &&
                        /^-?\d+$/.test(b)
                    ) {
                        push(
                            `local2+${ao - Number(b)}`
                        );
                    } else {
                        push(
                            `(${a}-${b})`
                        );
                    }
                }

                break;
            }

            if (
                x.text === "i64.add" ||
                x.text === "i64.sub" ||
                x.text === "i64.mul" ||
                x.text === "i64.and" ||
                x.text === "i64.or" ||
                x.text === "i64.shl" ||
                x.text === "i64.shr_u"
            ) {
                const [a, b] = pop2();

                push(
                    `(${a} ${x.text.replace("i64.", "")} ${b})`
                );

                break;
            }

            if (
                x.text === "i64.lt_u" ||
                x.text === "i64.gt_u" ||
                x.text === "i64.le_u"
            ) {
                const [a, b] = pop2();

                push(
                    `(${a} ${x.text.replace("i64.", "")} ${b})`
                );

                break;
            }

            if (
                x.text === "i64.extend32_s" ||
                x.text === "i64.extend_i32_u"
            ) {
                const a = pop();

                push(
                    `${x.text}(${a})`
                );

                break;
            }

            if (x.text === "i64.eqz") {
                const a = pop();

                push(
                    `eqz(${a})`
                );

                break;
            }

            /*
             * Unknown opcode:
             *
             * We deliberately don't fabricate stack effects.
             */
            break;
        }
    }
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Final alias report
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log(
    "\n============================================================"
);
console.log(
    " CALL #26 ALIAS RESULT"
);
console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

console.log(
    `CALL #26 @ ${hex(call26At)}`
);

console.log(
    `Destination = ${call26Dest}`
);

console.log(
    `Offset      = ${addressOffset(call26Dest)}`
);

console.log(
    "\nRelevant memory events:"
);

for (const e of events) {
    const off = e.offset;

    if (
        off === 432 ||
        off === 440 ||
        off === 0 ||
        off === 8 ||
        off === 48 ||
        off === 56 ||
        off === 448 ||
        off === 456
    ) {
        console.log(
            `${hex(e.at)}  ${e.type.padEnd(7)} ` +
            `${e.address.padEnd(18)} ` +
            `${e.detail}`
        );
    }
}

 */
console.log(
    "\n============================================================"

    " SLOT COMPARISON"
);
console.log(

const known = [
    0,

for (const k of known) {

        console.log(

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const START = 0x01226d;
const END   = 0x01295b;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) return { value, next: pos };
        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error(`ULEB overflow @ 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
}

function readSLEB(pos: number, bits: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        result |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift + 7n < BigInt(bits) && (b & 0x40)) {
                result |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
            }
            return { value: result, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }

    throw new Error(`SLEB overflow @ 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
}

function memarg(pos: number) {
    const a = readULEB(pos);
    const o = readULEB(a.next);

    return {
        align: a.value,
        offset: o.value,
        next: o.next
    };
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

interface Ins {
    start: number;
    end: number;
    op: number;
    text: string;
    kind: string;
    local?: number;
    offset?: number;
    call?: number;
}

function decode(pos: number): Ins {
    const start = pos;
    const op = buf[pos++];

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const x = readULEB(pos);
        return {
            start, end: x.next, op,
            text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
            kind: "get",
            local: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const x = readULEB(pos);
        return {
            start, end: x.next, op,
            text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
            kind: "set",
            local: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const x = readULEB(pos);
        return {
            start, end: x.next, op,
            text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
            kind: "tee",
            local: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = readSLEB(pos, 32);
        return {
            start, end: x.next, op,
            text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
            kind: "const"
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        const x = readSLEB(pos, 64);
        return {
            start, end: x.next, op,
            text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
            kind: "const"
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = readULEB(pos);
        return {
            start, end: x.next, op,
            text: `call ${x.value}`,
            kind: "call",
            call: x.value
        };
    }

    if (
        op === 0x29 ||
        op === 0x2f ||
        op === 0x37
    ) {
        const m = memarg(pos);

        const name =
            op === 0x29 ? "i64.load" :
            op === 0x2f ? "i64.load32_u" :
            "i64.store";

        return {
            start,
            end: m.next,
            op,
            text: `${name} align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`,
            kind: name,
            offset: m.offset
        };
    }

    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x0b: "end",
        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",
        0x80: "i64.div_u",
        0x83: "i64.and",
        0x84: "i64.or",
        0x86: "i64.shl",
        0x88: "i64.shr_u",
        0x54: "i64.lt_u",
        0x55: "i64.ne",
        0x56: "i64.gt_u",
        0x57: "i64.le_u",
        0x50: "i64.eqz",
        0x7f: "i64.extend_i32_u",
        0xad: "i64.extend32_s"
    };

    return {
        start,
        end: pos,
        op,
        text: names[op] ?? `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
        kind: "other"
    };
}

const ins: Ins[] = [];

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
    const x = decode(p);
    ins.push(x);
    p = x.end;
}

if (p !== END) {
    throw new Error(
        `Decode ended @ ${hex(p)}, expected ${hex(END)}`
    );
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 122 CALL #26 DATAFLOW TRACE v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Decoded   : ${ins.length}`);

const call26Index = ins.findIndex(
    x => x.start === 0x0126b4 &&
         x.kind === "call" &&
         x.call === 87
);

if (call26Index < 0) {
    throw new Error("CALL #26 @ 0x126b4 not found");
}

console.log("\n============================================================");

console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`CALL #26 @ ${hex(ins[call26Index].start)}`);
console.log("Function : 87");
console.log("DEST     : L2 + 432");
console.log("LOW      : L2 + 432");
console.log("HIGH     : L2 + 440");
console.log("INPUT A  : local13");
console.log("INPUT B  : local29");

console.log(" BACKWARD WINDOW");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (
    let i = Math.max(0, call26Index - 45);
    i < call26Index;
    i++
) {
    console.log(
        `${hex(ins[i].start)}  ${ins[i].text}`
    );
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log(" FORWARD WINDOW");
console.log("============================================================");

for (

    i < Math.min(ins.length, call26Index + 180);
    i++
) {
    const x = ins[i];

    console.log(
        `${hex(x.start)}  ${x.text}`
    );
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log(" LOCAL13 WRITES");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let i = 0; i < call26Index; i++) {
    const x = ins[i];

    if (
        (x.kind === "set" || x.kind === "tee") &&
        x.local === 13
    ) {
        console.log(
            `WRITE local13 @ ${hex(x.start)} : ${x.text}`
        );

        const idx = i;

        for (
            let j = Math.max(0, idx - 6);
            j < Math.min(ins.length, idx + 3);
            j++
        ) {
            console.log(

            );
        }

        console.log();
    }
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log(" LOCAL29 WRITES");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let i = 0; i < call26Index; i++) {
    const x = ins[i];

    if (
        (x.kind === "set" || x.kind === "tee") &&
        x.local === 29
    ) {
        console.log(
            `WRITE local29 @ ${hex(x.start)} : ${x.text}`
        );

        for (
            let j = Math.max(0, i - 6);
            j < Math.min(ins.length, i + 3);
            j++
        ) {
            console.log(
                `  ${hex(ins[j].start)} ${ins[j].text}`
            );
        }

        console.log();
    }
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log(" DIRECT SLOT REFERENCES AFTER CALL #26");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let i = call26Index + 1; i < ins.length; i++) {
    const x = ins[i];

    if (
        (x.kind === "i64.load" || x.kind === "i64.store") &&
        (x.offset === 432 || x.offset === 440)
    ) {
        console.log(

        );
    }
}

console.log("\n============================================================");

for (const targetLocal of [13, 29]) {
    console.log(`\nlocal.${targetLocal}:`);

    for (let i = call26Index + 1; i < ins.length; i++) {
        const x = ins[i];

        if (
            (x.kind === "get" ||
             x.kind === "set" ||
             x.kind === "tee") &&
            x.local === targetLocal
        ) {
            console.log(

        }
    }

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceCall26MemoryConsumerV2.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

/*
 * Function 122
 *
 * Function body starts at:
 *   0x1226d
 *
 * Local declarations:
 *   0x1226d  02        -> 2 local-declaration groups
 *   0x1226e  03 7f     -> 3 x i32
 *   0x12270  25 7e     -> 37 x i64
 *
 * Therefore the first REAL instruction is:
 *   0x12272
 *
 * Function end:
 *   0x1295c
 */

const START = 0x00012272;
const END   = 0x0001295c;

const CALL26 = 0x000126b4;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

function hex(n: number, width = 8): string {
    return n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(`ULEB too long at 0x${hex(pos)}`);
        }
    }

    throw new Error(`EOF in ULEB at 0x${hex(pos)}`);
}

function sleb(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let p = pos;
    let byte = 0;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        byte = wasm[p++];

        result |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7n;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            if ((byte & 0x40) !== 0 && shift < 64n) {
                result |= (-1n) << shift;
            }

            return { value: result, next: p };
        }

        if (shift >= 70n) {
            throw new Error(`SLEB too long at 0x${hex(pos)}`);
        }
    }

    throw new Error(`EOF in SLEB at 0x${hex(pos)}`);
}

function memarg(pos: number): {
    align: number;
    offset: number;
    next: number;
} {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    const o = uleb(a.next);

    return {
        align: a.value,
        offset: o.value,
        next: o.next
    };
}

function decodeInstruction(pos: number): {
    op: number;
    next: number;
    name: string;
    immediate?: number | bigint;
    memOffset?: number;
    localIndex?: number;
    globalIndex?: number;
    functionIndex?: number;
} {
    const op = wasm[pos];

    if (op === undefined) {
        throw new Error(`EOF at 0x${hex(pos)}`);
    }

    let p = pos + 1;

    const simple: Record<number, string> = {
        0x00: "unreachable",
        0x01: "nop",

        0x05: "else",
        0x0b: "end",
        0x0f: "return",

        0x1a: "drop",
        0x1b: "select",

        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",

        0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
        0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
        0x2f: "i32.load16_u",

        0x30: "i64.load8_s",
        0x31: "i64.load8_u",
        0x32: "i64.load16_s",
        0x33: "i64.load16_u",
        0x34: "i64.load32_s",
        0x35: "i64.load32_u",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",

        0x3a: "i32.store8",
        0x3b: "i32.store16",
        0x3e: "i64.store32",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",
        0x48: "i32.lt_s",
        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
        0x4c: "i32.le_s",
        0x4d: "i32.le_u",
        0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
        0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

        0x50: "i64.eqz",
        0x51: "i64.eq",
        0x52: "i64.ne",
        0x53: "i64.lt_s",
        0x54: "i64.lt_u",
        0x55: "i64.gt_s",
        0x56: "i64.gt_u",
        0x57: "i64.le_s",
        0x58: "i64.le_u",
        0x59: "i64.ge_s",
        0x5a: "i64.ge_u",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",
        0x6d: "i32.div_s",
        0x6e: "i32.div_u",

        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x73: "i32.xor",
        0x74: "i32.shr_s",
        0x75: "i32.shr_u",

        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",
        0x7f: "i64.div_s",
        0x80: "i64.div_u",
        0x81: "i64.rem_s",
        0x82: "i64.rem_u",
        0x83: "i64.and",
        0x84: "i64.or",
        0x85: "i64.xor",
        0x86: "i64.shl",
        0x87: "i64.shr_s",
        0x88: "i64.shr_u",
        0x89: "i64.rotl",
        0x8a: "i64.rotr",

        0xad: "i64.extend_i32_u",
        0xac: "i64.extend_i32_s"
    };

    /*
     * block / loop / if
     */
    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
        const x = sleb(p);

        return {
            op,
            next: x.next,
            name:
                op === 0x02
                    ? "block"
                    : op === 0x03
                        ? "loop"
                        : "if",
            immediate: x.value
        };
    }

    /*
     * br / br_if
     */
    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
        const x = uleb(p);

        return {
            op,
            next: x.next,
            name: op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if",
            immediate: x.value
        };
    }

    /*
     * br_table
     */
    if (op === 0x0e) {
        const count = uleb(p);

        let q = count.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;
        }

        const def = uleb(q);

        return {
            op,
            next: def.next,
            name: "br_table",
            immediate: def.value
        };
    }

    /*
     * call
     */
    if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = uleb(p);

        return {
            op,
            next: x.next,
            name: "call",
            functionIndex: x.value
        };
    }

    /*
     * local.get / local.set / local.tee
     */
    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
        const x = uleb(p);

        return {
            op,
            next: x.next,
            name:
                op === 0x20
                    ? "local.get"
                    : op === 0x21
                        ? "local.set"
                        : "local.tee",
            localIndex: x.value
        };
    }

    /*
     * global.get / global.set
     */
    if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
        const x = uleb(p);

        return {
            op,
            next: x.next,
            name: op === 0x23 ? "global.get" : "global.set",
            globalIndex: x.value
        };
    }

    /*
     * Memory instructions
     */
    if (
        op >= 0x28 &&
        op <= 0x3e
    ) {
        const m = memarg(p);

        return {
            op,
            next: m.next,
            name: simple[op] ?? `opcode 0x${hex(op, 2)}`,
            memOffset: m.offset
        };
    }

    /*
     * i32.const
     */
    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = sleb(p);

        return {
            op,
            next: x.next,
            name: "i32.const",
            immediate: x.value
        };
    }

    /*
     * i64.const
     */
    if (op === 0x42) {
        const x = sleb(p);

        return {
            op,
            next: x.next,
            name: "i64.const",
            immediate: x.value
        };
    }

    /*
     * Everything else in the verified Function 122 stream
     * must be one of the simple opcodes above.
     */
    if (simple[op]) {
        return {
            op,
            next: p,
            name: simple[op]
        };
    }

    throw new Error(
        `UNKNOWN OPCODE 0x${hex(op, 2)} at 0x${hex(pos)}`
    );
}

interface Instr {
    addr: number;
    next: number;
    op: number;
    name: string;
    local?: number;
    global?: number;
    functionIndex?: number;
    memOffset?: number;
    immediate?: number | bigint;
}

const instructions: Instr[] = [];

let pos = START;

while (pos < END) {
    const d = decodeInstruction(pos);

    instructions.push({
        addr: pos,
        next: d.next,
        op: d.op,
        name: d.name,
        local: d.localIndex,
        global: d.globalIndex,
        functionIndex: d.functionIndex,
        memOffset: d.memOffset,
        immediate: d.immediate
    });

    pos = d.next;
}

if (pos !== END) {
    throw new Error(
        `Decoder end mismatch: 0x${hex(pos)} != 0x${hex(END)}`
    );
}

 */

FILE: scripts\traceCall26PointerFlowV1.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x012200;
const END   = 0x01295b;
const CALL26 = 0x0126b4;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function sleb(pos: number, bits: number) {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (
                shift + 7n < BigInt(bits) &&
                (b & 0x40)
            ) {
                value |=
                    (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
            }

            return {
                value,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }

    throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
}

function memarg(pos: number) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    const b = uleb(a.next);

    return {
        align: a.value,
        offset: b.value,
        next: b.next
    };
}

interface Ins {
    start: number;
    end: number;
    op: number;
    text: string;
    kind: string;
    local?: number;
    value?: number | bigint;
    offset?: number;
    global?: number;
}

function decode(pos: number): Ins {
    const start = pos;
    const op = buf[pos++];

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const x = uleb(pos);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
            kind: "local.get",
            local: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const x = uleb(pos);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
            kind: "local.set",
            local: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const x = uleb(pos);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
            kind: "local.tee",
            local: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x23) {
        const x = uleb(pos);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `global.get ${x.value}`,
            kind: "global.get",
            global: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x24) {
        const x = uleb(pos);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `global.set ${x.value}`,
            kind: "global.set",
            global: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = sleb(pos, 32);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
            kind: "i32.const",
            value: Number(x.value)
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        const x = sleb(pos, 64);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
            kind: "i64.const",
            value: x.value
        };
    }

    if (
        op === 0x28 ||
        op === 0x29 ||
        op === 0x2a ||
        op === 0x2b ||
        op === 0x36 ||
        op === 0x37 ||
        op === 0x38 ||
        op === 0x39
    ) {
        const m = memarg(pos);

        const names: Record<number, string> = {
            0x28: "i32.load",
            0x29: "i64.load",
            0x2a: "f32.load",
            0x2b: "f64.load",
            0x36: "i32.store",
            0x37: "i64.store",
            0x38: "f32.store",
            0x39: "f64.store"
        };

        return {
            start,
            end: m.next,
            op,
            text:
                `${names[op]} offset=${m.offset}`,
            kind: names[op],
            offset: m.offset
        };
    }

    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",

        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",

        0x54: "i64.lt_u",
        0x56: "i64.gt_u",

        0x57: "i64.le_u",
        0x58: "i64.ge_u",

        0x10: "call",
        0x0b: "end"
    };

    return {
        start,
        end: pos,
        op,
        text:
            names[op] ??
            `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
        kind: names[op] ?? "other"
    };
}

function H(n: number) {
    return "0x" +
        n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

const ins: Ins[] = [];

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
    const x = decode(p);
    ins.push(x);
    p = x.end;
}

if (p !== END) {
    throw new Error(
        `Decode mismatch: ${H(p)} != ${H(END)}`
    );
}

const call26 =
    ins.findIndex(
        x => x.start === CALL26
    );

if (call26 < 0)
    throw new Error("CALL #26 not found");

console.log(
    "============================================================"
);
console.log(
    " CALL #26 POINTER FLOW v1"
);
console.log(
    " READ ONLY - OFFLINE"
);
console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Decoded   : ${ins.length}`);
console.log(`CALL #26  : ${H(CALL26)}`);

console.log(
    "\nCALL #26:"
);

for (
    let i = call26 - 8;
    i <= call26 + 3;
    i++
) {
    if (i < 0 || i >= ins.length)
        continue;

    console.log(
        `  ${H(ins[i].start)}  ${ins[i].text}`
    );
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * All locals written after CALL #26
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

interface LocalWrite {
    index: number;
    local: number;
    ins: Ins;
}

const writes: LocalWrite[] = [];

for (
    let i = call26 + 1;
    i < ins.length;
    i++
) {
    const x = ins[i];

    if (
        (x.kind === "local.set" ||
         x.kind === "local.tee") &&
        x.local !== undefined
    ) {
        writes.push({
            index: i,
            local: x.local,
            ins: x
        });
    }
}

console.log(
    "\n============================================================"
);
console.log(
    " LOCALS WRITTEN AFTER CALL #26"
);
console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

for (const w of writes) {
    console.log(
        `${H(w.ins.start)}  ` +
        `${w.ins.kind} ${w.local}`
    );
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * For every local written after CALL #26,
 * find all subsequent reads.
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log(
    "\n============================================================"
);
console.log(
    " LOCAL READ-BACK FLOW"
);
console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

const interesting = new Set<number>();

for (const w of writes) {
    let reads = 0;

    for (
        let i = w.index + 1;
        i < ins.length;
        i++
    ) {
        const x = ins[i];

        if (
            x.kind === "local.get" &&
            x.local === w.local
        ) {
            if (reads === 0) {
                console.log(
                    `\nLOCAL ${w.local} written @ ${H(w.ins.start)}`
                );
            }

            console.log(
                `  read @ ${H(x.start)}`
            );

            interesting.add(w.local);
            reads++;
        }
    }
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Specifically trace locals that occur near memory accesses.
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log(
    "\n============================================================"
);
console.log(
    " LOCAL -> MEMORY ACCESS PROXIMITY"
);
console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

for (const local of interesting) {

    for (let i = call26 + 1; i < ins.length; i++) {

        const x = ins[i];

        if (
            x.kind !== "local.get" ||
            x.local !== local
        )
            continue;

        const to =
            Math.min(ins.length, i + 6);

        const nearby =
            ins.slice(i, to);

        const memory =
            nearby.find(y =>
                y.kind === "i64.load" ||
                y.kind === "i32.load" ||
                y.kind === "i64.store" ||
                y.kind === "i32.store"
            );

        if (!memory)
            continue;

        console.log(
            `\nLOCAL ${local} @ ${H(x.start)}`
        );

        for (const y of nearby) {
            console.log(
                `  ${H(y.start)}  ${y.text}`
            );
        }
    }
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Every local.get 2 followed by i32 arithmetic
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log(
    "\n============================================================"
);
console.log(
    " LOCAL2 ADDRESS BUILDERS AFTER CALL #26"
);
console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

for (
    let i = call26 + 1;
    i < ins.length - 3;
    i++
) {
    const a = ins[i];
    const b = ins[i + 1];
    const c = ins[i + 2];

    if (
        a.kind === "local.get" &&
        a.local === 2 &&
        b.kind === "i32.const" &&
        (
            c.text === "i32.add" ||
            c.text === "i32.sub"
        )
    ) {
        console.log(
            `${H(a.start)}  local2 ` +
            `${c.text === "i32.add" ? "+" : "-"} ` +
            `${b.value}`
        );

        for (
            let j = i;
            j < Math.min(ins.length, i + 5);
            j++
        ) {
            console.log(
                `  ${H(ins[j].start)} ` +
                `${ins[j].text}`
            );
        }
    }
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Search exact constants that can represent:
 *
 * global0 - 576
 * global0 - 568
 *
 * and their LEB byte representation.
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log(
    "\n============================================================"
);
console.log(
    " RAW CONSTANT PATTERN SEARCH"
);
console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

const targets = [-576, -568, -64, -56, -512];

for (const n of targets) {

    const needle: number[] = [];

    let v = n | 0;

    while (true) {
        let byte = v & 0x7f;

        v >>= 7;

        const sign =
            (byte & 0x40) !== 0;

        const done =
            (v === 0 && !sign) ||
            (v === -1 && sign);

        if (!done)
            byte |= 0x80;

        needle.push(byte);

        if (done)
            break;
    }

    const hits: number[] = [];

    outer:
    for (
        let i = CALL26;
        i <= END - needle.length;
        i++
    ) {
        for (
            let j = 0;
            j < needle.length;
            j++
        ) {
            if (
                buf[i + j] !== needle[j]
            ) {
                continue outer;
            }
        }

        hits.push(i);
    }

    console.log(
        `${n}: bytes ` +
        needle
            .map(
                x =>
                    x.toString(16)
                        .padStart(2, "0")
            )
            .join(" ") +
        `  hits=${hits.length}`
    );

    for (const h of hits) {
        console.log(
            `  ${H(h)}`
        );
    }
}

console.log(
    "\n============================================================"
);
console.log(
    " DONE - NO NETWORK / NO TRANSACTION"
);
console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

FILE: scripts\traceCall26StackDataflowV1.ts
