import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x012600;
const END   = 0x01295b;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error(`ULEB overflow @ 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
}

function readSLEB(pos: number, bits: number): {
    value: bigint;
    next: number;
} {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        result |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (
                shift + 7n < BigInt(bits) &&
                (b & 0x40)
            ) {
                result |=
                    (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
            }

            return {
                value: result,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }

    throw new Error(`SLEB overflow @ 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
}

function memarg(pos: number) {
    const a = readULEB(pos);
    const o = readULEB(a.next);

    return {
        align: a.value,
        offset: o.value,
        next: o.next
    };
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" +
        n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

interface Ins {
    start: number;
    end: number;
    op: number;
    text: string;
    kind: string;
    local?: number;
    offset?: number;
    call?: number;
}

function decode(pos: number): Ins {
    const start = pos;
    const op = buf[pos++];

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const x = readULEB(pos);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
            kind: "get",
            local: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const x = readULEB(pos);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
            kind: "set",
            local: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const x = readULEB(pos);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
            kind: "tee",
            local: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = readSLEB(pos, 32);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
            kind: "const"
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        const x = readSLEB(pos, 64);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
            kind: "const"
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = readULEB(pos);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `call ${x.value}`,
            kind: "call",
            call: x.value
        };
    }

    if (
        op === 0x29 ||
        op === 0x2f ||
        op === 0x37
    ) {
        const m = memarg(pos);

        let name = "memory-op";

        if (op === 0x29)
            name = "i64.load";

        if (op === 0x2f)
            name = "i64.load32_u";

        if (op === 0x37)
            name = "i64.store";

        return {
            start,
            end: m.next,
            op,
            text:
                `${name} align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`,
            kind: name,
            offset: m.offset
        };
    }

    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x0b: "end",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",

        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",

        0x80: "i64.div_u",

        0x83: "i64.and",
        0x84: "i64.or",

        0x86: "i64.shl",
        0x88: "i64.shr_u",

        0x54: "i64.lt_u",
        0x55: "i64.ne",
        0x56: "i64.gt_u",
        0x57: "i64.le_u",

        0x50: "i64.eqz",

        0x7f: "i64.extend_i32_u",
        0xad: "i64.extend32_s"
    };

    return {
        start,
        end: pos,
        op,
        text:
            names[op] ??
            `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
        kind: "other"
    };
}

const ins: Ins[] = [];

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
    const x = decode(p);
    ins.push(x);
    p = x.end;
}

if (p !== END) {
    throw new Error(
        `Decode ended @ ${hex(p)}, expected ${hex(END)}`
    );
}

console.log(
    "============================================================"
);
console.log(
    " FUNCTION 122 CALL #26 STACK + MEMORY DATAFLOW v1"
);
console.log(
    " READ ONLY - OFFLINE"
);
console.log(
    "============================================================"
);
console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log(`Decoded   : ${ins.length}`);

/*
 * Symbolic stack.
 *
 * We only need the scalar operations occurring in this region.
 */
type Expr = string;

const stack: Expr[] = [];
const locals = new Map<number, Expr>();

/*
 * Scratch memory symbols.
 *
 * key = L2 + offset
 */
const memory = new Map<number, Expr>();

/*
 * Track writes and reads of the four critical slots.
 */
interface WriteInfo {
    addr: number;
    ins: number;
    expr: string;
    call87?: number;
}

interface ReadInfo {
    addr: number;
    ins: number;
    expr: string;
}

const writes = new Map<number, WriteInfo[]>();
const reads = new Map<number, ReadInfo[]>();

function push(x: Expr) {
    stack.push(x);
}

function pop(): Expr {
    if (stack.length === 0) {
        return "STACK_UNDERFLOW";
    }

    return stack.pop()!;
}

function popN(n: number): Expr[] {
    const out: Expr[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        out.push(pop());
    }

    return out.reverse();
}

function recordWrite(
    addr: number,
    insIndex: number,
    expr: string,
    call87?: number
) {
    if (!writes.has(addr)) {
        writes.set(addr, []);
    }

    writes.get(addr)!.push({
        addr,
        ins: insIndex,
        expr,
        call87
    });

    memory.set(addr, expr);
}

function recordRead(
    addr: number,
    insIndex: number,
    expr: string
) {
    if (!reads.has(addr)) {
        reads.set(addr, []);
    }

    reads.get(addr)!.push({
        addr,
        ins: insIndex,
        expr
    });
}

/*
 * The exact CALL87 destination mapping already established.
 */
const call87Dest: Record<number, number> = {
    0x012675: 48,
    0x012685: 448,
    0x012695: 400,
    0x0126a5: 288,
    0x0126b4: 432,
    0x0126c4: 176,
    0x0126d4: 352,
    0x0126e4: 112,
    0x0126f4: 240
};

const call87Number: Record<number, number> = {
    0x012675: 22,
    0x012685: 23,
    0x012695: 24,
    0x0126a5: 25,
    0x0126b4: 26,
    0x0126c4: 27,
    0x0126d4: 28,
    0x0126e4: 29,
    0x0126f4: 30
};

console.log(
    "\n============================================================"
);
console.log(
    " SYMBOLIC EXECUTION"
);
console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

let call26Seen = false;
let call26LowExpr = "";
let call26HighExpr = "";

for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
    const x = ins[i];

    switch (x.kind) {
        case "get": {
            const value =
                locals.get(x.local!) ??
                `local${x.local!}`;

            push(value);
            break;
        }

        case "set": {
            const value = pop();

            locals.set(
                x.local!,
                value
            );

            break;
        }

        case "tee": {
            const value = stack.length
                ? stack[stack.length - 1]
                : "STACK_UNDERFLOW";

            locals.set(
                x.local!,
                value
            );

            break;
        }

        case "const": {
            /*
             * The text is sufficient for symbolic purposes.
             */
            push(x.text);
            break;
        }

        case "i64.load": {
            const base = pop();

            let expr: Expr;

            /*
             * The known scratch pattern is:
             *
             * local.get 2
             * i64.load offset=N
             *
             */
            if (
                base === "local2" &&
                x.offset !== undefined
            ) {
                const old =
                    memory.get(x.offset);

                expr =
                    old ??
                    `MEM64[L2+${x.offset}]`;

                recordRead(
                    x.offset,
                    i,
                    expr
                );
            } else {
                expr =
                    `LOAD64(${base}+${x.offset ?? 0})`;
            }

            push(expr);
            break;
        }

        case "i64.load32_u": {
            const base = pop();

            push(
                `LOAD32_U(${base}+${x.offset ?? 0})`
            );

            break;
        }

        case "i64.store": {
            const value = pop();
            const base = pop();

            /*
             * Only resolve L2-relative stores.
             */
            if (
                base === "local2" &&
                x.offset !== undefined
            ) {
                recordWrite(
                    x.offset,
                    i,
                    value
                );
            }

            break;
        }

        case "call": {
            if (x.call !== 87) {
                /*
                 * Unknown call:
                 * do not invent return values.
                 *
                 * For this region the known relevant
                 * call is 87.
                 */
                break;
            }

            /*
             * Function 87 has five i64/i32-level
             * stack arguments in this calling sequence:
             *
             * destination
             * A
             * 0
             * B
             * 0
             */
            const args = popN(5);

            const destination = args[0];
            const A = args[1];
            const B = args[3];

            const dest =
                call87Dest[x.start];

            const no =
                call87Number[x.start];

            if (dest === undefined) {
                console.log(
                    `CALL87 @ ${hex(x.start)} ` +
                    `dest=${destination}`
                );
                break;
            }

            const low =
                `CALL87#${no}.LOW(${A} × ${B})`;

            const high =
                `CALL87#${no}.HIGH(${A} × ${B})`;

            recordWrite(
                dest,
                i,
                low,
                no
            );

            recordWrite(
                dest + 8,
                i,
                high,
                no
            );

            if (no === 26) {
                call26Seen = true;
                call26LowExpr = low;
                call26HighExpr = high;

                console.log(
                    "\n*** CALL #26 SYMBOLIC RESULT ***"
                );

                console.log(
                    `@ ${hex(x.start)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `A    = ${A}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `B    = ${B}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `LOW  = ${low}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `HIGH = ${high}`
                );

                console.log(
                    "***********************************\n"
                );
            }

            break;
        }

        case "other": {
            /*
             * Handle the binary i64 operations needed here.
             */
            if (
                x.text === "i64.add" ||
                x.text === "i64.sub" ||
                x.text === "i64.mul" ||
                x.text === "i64.and" ||
                x.text === "i64.or" ||
                x.text === "i64.shl" ||
                x.text === "i64.shr_u"
            ) {
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();

                push(
                    `(${a} ${x.text.replace("i64.", "")} ${b})`
                );

                break;
            }

            if (
                x.text === "i64.lt_u" ||
                x.text === "i64.gt_u" ||
                x.text === "i64.le_u" ||
                x.text === "i64.ne"
            ) {
                const b = pop();
                const a = pop();

                push(
                    `(${a} ${x.text.replace("i64.", "")} ${b})`
                );

                break;
            }

            if (
                x.text === "i64.extend32_s" ||
                x.text === "i64.extend_i32_u" ||
                x.text === "i64.eqz"
            ) {
                const a = pop();

                push(
                    `${x.text}(${a})`
                );

                break;
            }

            /*
             * Unknown one-byte opcode:
             *
             * We intentionally do not invent stack
             * behavior. This protects the analysis
             * from false provenance.
             */
            break;
        }
    }

    /*
     * Print all critical activity around CALL #26.
     */
    if (
        x.start >= 0x0126b4 &&
        x.start <= 0x01295b &&
        (
            x.kind === "i64.load" ||
            x.kind === "i64.store" ||
            x.kind === "call" ||
            x.kind === "tee"
        )
    ) {
        if (
            x.kind === "i64.load" &&
            (
                x.offset === 432 ||
                x.offset === 440
            )
        ) {
            console.log(
                `CRITICAL LOAD @ ${hex(x.start)} ` +
                `offset=${x.offset}`
            );
        }

        if (
            x.kind === "i64.store" &&
            (
                x.offset === 432 ||
                x.offset === 440
            )
        ) {
            console.log(
                `CRITICAL STORE @ ${hex(x.start)} ` +
                `offset=${x.offset}`
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log(
    "\n============================================================"
);
console.log(
    " CALL #26 FINAL MEMORY LIFETIME"
);
console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

if (!call26Seen) {
    console.log("CALL #26 was not symbolically recognized.");
} else {
    console.log(
        `LOW  = ${call26LowExpr}`
    );

    console.log(
        `HIGH = ${call26HighExpr}`
    );

    for (const slot of [432, 440]) {
        console.log(`\nL2 + ${slot}`);

        const ws =
            writes.get(slot) ?? [];

        const rs =
            reads.get(slot) ?? [];

        console.log(
            `  WRITES : ${ws.length}`
        );

        for (const w of ws) {
            console.log(
                `    @ ${hex(ins[w.ins].start)} ` +
                `${w.expr}` +
                (w.call87
                    ? ` [CALL87 #${w.call87}]`
                    : "")
            );
        }

        console.log(
            `  READS  : ${rs.length}`
        );

        for (const r of rs) {
            console.log(
                `    @ ${hex(ins[r.ins].start)} ` +
                `${r.expr}`
            );
        }

        if (rs.length === 0) {
            console.log(
                "  STATUS : NO READ IN ANALYZED REGION"
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log(
    "\n============================================================"
);
console.log(
    " CALL #26 PROVENANCE SUMMARY"
);
console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

console.log(
    "CALL #26:"
);

console.log(
    "  destination = L2 + 432"
);

console.log(
    "  LOW         = L2 + 432"
);

console.log(
    "  HIGH        = L2 + 440"
);

console.log(
    "  direct LOAD after CALL #26 = NONE"
);

console.log(
    "\nIMPORTANT:"
);

console.log(
    "If L2+432 and L2+440 have zero reads after CALL #26,"
);

console.log(

    "linear analyzed region (a dead/spill result), unless"

console.log(

);

import * as nodefs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = nodefs.readFileSync(file);

const START = 0x1f00d0;
const END   = 0x1f00d7;

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { v, p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CALL 2827 STACK TRACE v36");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = START;

const stack: string[] = [];

while (p < END) {

  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  if (op >= 0x20 && op <= 0x24) {

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    const names: Record<number,string> = {
      0x20: "local.get",
      0x21: "local.set",
      0x22: "local.tee",
      0x23: "global.get",
      0x24: "global.set"
    };

    if (op === 0x20) {
      const value = `L${r.v}`;
      stack.push(value);

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} local.get ${r.v}    PUSH ${value}`
      );
    } else {
      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} ${names[op]} ${r.v}`
      );
    }

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    const value = `CONST(${r.v})`;
    stack.push(value);

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} i32.const ${r.v}    PUSH ${value}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x42) {

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    const value = `CONST64(${r.v})`;
    stack.push(value);

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} i64.const ${r.v}    PUSH ${value}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x28) {

    const a = uleb(p);
    p = a.p;

    const o = uleb(p);
    p = o.p;

    const base = stack.pop() ?? "?";
    const value = `MEM[${base}+${o.v}]`;

    stack.push(value);

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} i32.load offset=${o.v}    PUSH ${value}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x6a || op === 0x6b) {

    const rhs = stack.pop() ?? "?";
    const lhs = stack.pop() ?? "?";

    const value =
      op === 0x6a
        ? `(${lhs}+${rhs})`
        : `(${lhs}-${rhs})`;

    stack.push(value);

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} ${op === 0x6a ? "i32.add" : "i32.sub"}    PUSH ${value}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    if (r.v === 2827) {

      console.log();
      console.log("============================================================");
      console.log("CALL 2827");
      console.log("============================================================");

      console.log(`Stack depth before CALL : ${stack.length}`);

      for (let i = stack.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        console.log(
          `arg[${stack.length - 1 - i}] = ${stack[i]}`
        );
      }

      console.log("============================================================");
    }

    continue;
  }

  console.log(
    `${hex(at)} op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
  );
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END V36");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\traceCall446Stack.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x22d200;
const END = 0x22d263;

function readULEB(): number {
    let v = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];
        v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        if (!(x & 0x80)) return v;
        shift += 7;
    }
}

function loadName(local: number) {
    return `L${local}`;
}

let p = START;

const stack: string[] = [];

function push(v: string) {
    stack.push(v);
}

function pop(): string {
    return stack.length ? stack.pop()! : "<EMPTY>";
}

function dump(addr: number, text: string) {
    console.log(
        `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${text}`
    );
    console.log(
        `             STACK = ${stack.length ? stack.join(" | ") : "<empty>"}`
    );
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 6618 -> CALL 446 EXACT STACK TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

while (p < END) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    switch (op) {

case 0x02: {
    const blockType = b[p++];
    dump(addr, `block type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`);
    break;
}

case 0x03: {
    const blockType = b[p++];
    dump(addr, `loop type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`);
    break;
}

case 0x04: {
    const blockType = b[p++];
    dump(addr, `if type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`);
    break;
}

        case 0x20: {
            const l = readULEB();
            push(loadName(l));
            dump(addr, `local.get ${l}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const l = readULEB();
            const v = pop();
            dump(addr, `local.set ${l} <- ${v}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const l = readULEB();
            const v = pop();
            push(v);
            push(loadName(l));
            dump(addr, `local.tee ${l} <- ${v}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const v = readULEB();
            push(`CONST(${v})`);
            dump(addr, `i32.const ${v}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x28: {
            const align = readULEB();
            const off = readULEB();
            const base = pop();
            push(`MEM[${base}+${off}]`);
            dump(addr, `i32.load align=${align} offset=${off}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x36: {
            const align = readULEB();
            const off = readULEB();
            const value = pop();
            const base = pop();
            dump(
                addr,
                `i32.store align=${align} offset=${off} VALUE=${value} BASE=${base}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x6a: {
            const b2 = pop();
            const a2 = pop();
            push(`(${a2}+${b2})`);
            dump(addr, "i32.add");
            break;
        }

        case 0x45: {
            const v = pop();
            push(`eqz(${v})`);
            dump(addr, "i32.eqz");
            break;
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const fn = readULEB();

            if (fn === 446) {
                console.log("");
                console.log("############################################################");
                console.log("CALL 446 FOUND @ 0x" + addr.toString(16));
                console.log("############################################################");

                console.log(
                    "STACK BEFORE CALL =",
                    stack.length ? stack.join(" | ") : "<empty>"
                );

                console.log("TOP 3 ARGUMENTS:");
                console.log("ARG0 =", stack.length >= 3 ? stack[stack.length - 3] : "<EMPTY>");
                console.log("ARG1 =", stack.length >= 2 ? stack[stack.length - 2] : "<EMPTY>");
                console.log("ARG2 =", stack.length >= 1 ? stack[stack.length - 1] : "<EMPTY>");

                console.log("============================================================");
                process.exit(0);
            }

            /*
             * For calls whose signature/result we are not resolving,
             * preserve a symbolic result rather than pretending
             * that the call consumed a known number of operands.
             */
            push(`CALL${fn}(...)`);
            dump(addr, `call ${fn}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const depth = readULEB();
            dump(addr, `br_if ${depth}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x0b:
            dump(addr, "end");
            break;

        default:
            console.log("");
            console.log("STOP: UNKNOWN/UNSUPPORTED OPCODE");
            console.log(
                "ADDRESS : 0x" + addr.toString(16)
            );
            console.log(
                "OPCODE  : 0x" + op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
            );
            console.log(
                "STACK   :",
                stack.length ? stack.join(" | ") : "<empty>"
            );
            process.exit(0);
    }
}

FILE: scripts\traceCall87ConsumersV1.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u32(pos: number): number {
    return wasm.readUInt32LE(pos);
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(`ULEB overflow @ 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
        }
    }

    throw new Error(`EOF in ULEB @ 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
}

function hex(x: number): string {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function s32(x: number): number {
    return x | 0;
}

function opcodeName(op: number): string {
    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x0b: "end",
        0x0f: "return",

        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x37: "i64.store",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",

        0x4f: "i64.eqz",
        0x50: "i64.eq",
        0x51: "i64.ne",
        0x52: "i64.lt_s",
        0x53: "i64.lt_u",
        0x54: "i64.gt_s",
        0x55: "i64.gt_u",
        0x56: "i64.le_s",
        0x57: "i64.le_u",
        0x58: "i64.ge_s",
        0x59: "i64.ge_u",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",

        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",
        0x7f: "i64.div_s",
        0x80: "i64.div_u",
        0x81: "i64.rem_s",
        0x82: "i64.rem_u",
        0x83: "i64.and",
        0x84: "i64.or",
        0x85: "i64.xor",
        0x86: "i64.shl",
        0x87: "i64.shr_s",
        0x88: "i64.shr_u",
        0x89: "i64.rotl",
        0x8a: "i64.rotr",

        0xa7: "i32.wrap_i64",
        0xad: "i64.extend32_s",

        0x10: "call",
    };

    return names[op] ?? `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

interface Insn {
    pos: number;
    end: number;
    op: number;
    name: string;
    text: string;
    loadOffset?: number;
    callIndex?: number;
}

function decodeOne(pos: number): Insn {
    const start = pos;
    const op = wasm[pos++];

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
        const r = readULEB(pos);
        return {
            pos: start,
            end: r.next,
            op,
            name: opcodeName(op),
            text: `${opcodeName(op)} ${r.value}`,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = readULEB(pos);
        return {
            pos: start,
            end: r.next,
            op,
            name: opcodeName(op),
            text: `${opcodeName(op)} ${s32(r.value)}`,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        const r = readULEB(pos);
        return {
            pos: start,
            end: r.next,
            op,
            name: opcodeName(op),
            text: `${opcodeName(op)} ${r.value}`,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const r = readULEB(pos);
        return {
            pos: start,
            end: r.next,
            op,
            name: opcodeName(op),
            text: `call ${r.value}`,
            callIndex: r.value,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x29) {
        const align = readULEB(pos);
        const off = readULEB(align.next);

        return {
            pos: start,
            end: off.next,
            op,
            name: opcodeName(op),
            text: `i64.load align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`,
            loadOffset: off.value,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x37) {
        const align = readULEB(pos);
        const off = readULEB(align.next);

        return {
            pos: start,
            end: off.next,
            op,
            name: opcodeName(op),
            text: `i64.store align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`,
        };
    }

    return {
        pos: start,
        end: pos,
        op,
        name: opcodeName(op),
        text: opcodeName(op),
    };
}

const START = 0x1226d;
const END = 0x1295b;

const call87 = [

    { n: 30, pos: 0x126f4, dest: 240 },

const CALL87_BY_POS = new Map(call87.map(x => [x.pos, x]));

const TARGETS = [
    0x126fa,
    0x1283f,
    0x124c6,
    0x124f9,
    0x12701,
    0x12846,

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 122 -> CALL 87 CONSUMER TRACE v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log("");

/*
 * Decode the complete function using correct instruction lengths.
 */
const insns: Insn[] = [];

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
    try {
        const ins = decodeOne(p);
        insns.push(ins);

        if (ins.end <= p) {
            console.log(`DECODER STALL @ ${hex(p)}`);
            break;
        }

        p = ins.end;
    } catch (e) {
        console.log(`DECODE ERROR @ ${hex(p)} : ${String(e)}`);
        break;
    }
}

console.log(`Decoded instructions : ${insns.length}`);
console.log("");

/*
 * Map instruction positions.
 */
const byPos = new Map<number, Insn>();

for (const x of insns) {
    byPos.set(x.pos, x);
}

/*
 * Print a bounded window around a target.
 */
function printWindow(target: number, before = 12, after = 24) {
    const idx = insns.findIndex(x => x.pos === target);

    if (idx < 0) {
        console.log(`TARGET ${hex(target)} NOT FOUND`);
        return;
    }

    const lo = Math.max(0, idx - before);
    const hi = Math.min(insns.length, idx + after + 1);

    console.log(`------------------------------------------------------------`);
    console.log(`TARGET @ ${hex(target)}`);
    console.log(`------------------------------------------------------------`);

    for (let i = lo; i < hi; i++) {
        const x = insns[i];
        const marker = x.pos === target ? " <== TARGET" : "";
        const c87 = CALL87_BY_POS.get(x.pos);

        if (c87) {
            console.log(
                `${hex(x.pos)}  ${x.text}  [CALL87 #${c87.n}, DEST=L2+${c87.dest}]${marker}`
            );
        } else {
            console.log(`${hex(x.pos)}  ${x.text}${marker}`);
        }
    }

    console.log("");
}

/*
 * Find nearest preceding CALL87 for a given memory slot.
 */
function producerForLoad(loadPos: number, offset: number) {
    let producer: { n: number; pos: number; dest: number } | null = null;

    for (const c of call87) {
        if (c.pos >= loadPos) continue;

        if (c.dest === offset || c.dest + 8 === offset) {
            if (!producer || c.pos > producer.pos) {
                producer = c;
            }
        }
    }

    return producer;
}

/*
 * Determine whether a LOAD is direct L2+offset.
 *
 * Expected:
 *   local.get 2
 *   i64.load offset=X
 */
function isDirectL2(loadPos: number): boolean {
    const idx = insns.findIndex(x => x.pos === loadPos);

    if (idx <= 0) return false;

    const prev = insns[idx - 1];

    return prev.op === 0x20 && prev.text === "local.get 2";
}

/*
 * Detailed target analysis.
 */
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" TARGET CONSUMER WINDOWS");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

for (const t of TARGETS) {
    printWindow(t, 10, 28);

    const ins = byPos.get(t);

    if (!ins || ins.op !== 0x29) {
        console.log(`Not an i64.load: ${hex(t)}`);
        console.log("");
        continue;
    }

    const off = ins.loadOffset!;

    console.log(`LOAD ADDRESS : L2 + ${off}`);
    console.log(`DIRECT L2    : ${isDirectL2(t) ? "YES" : "NO"}`);

    const prod = producerForLoad(t, off);

    if (prod) {
        const half =
            off === prod.dest ? "LOW" :
            off === prod.dest + 8 ? "HIGH" :
            "UNKNOWN";

        console.log(
            `NEAREST CALL87 PRODUCER : CALL #${prod.n} @ ${hex(prod.pos)} -> ${half}`
        );
    } else {
        console.log(`NEAREST CALL87 PRODUCER : NONE`);
    }

    console.log("");
}

/*
 * Print every direct L2 load and the nearest producer.
 */
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" ALL DIRECT L2 LOADS -> NEAREST CALL87 PRODUCER");
console.log("============================================================");

let directCount = 0;

for (const ins of insns) {
    if (ins.op !== 0x29) continue;

    const off = ins.loadOffset!;

    if (!isDirectL2(ins.pos)) continue;

    directCount++;

    const prod = producerForLoad(ins.pos, off);

    if (prod) {
        const half =
            off === prod.dest ? "LOW" :
            off === prod.dest + 8 ? "HIGH" :
            "?";

        console.log(
            `${hex(ins.pos)}  L2+${off}` +
            ` <- CALL #${prod.n} ${half} @ ${hex(prod.pos)}`
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            `${hex(ins.pos)}  L2+${off} <- NO PRECEDING CALL87`
        );
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log(`Direct L2 loads : ${directCount}`);
console.log("");

/*
 * Explicit overwrite analysis for L2+432.
 */
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" OVERWRITE ANALYSIS : L2 + 432");
console.log("============================================================");

const slot432 = call87.filter(x => x.dest === 432);

console.log("");

for (const c of slot432) {
    console.log(
        `CALL #${c.n} @ ${hex(c.pos)} writes:` +
        ` L2+432 LOW, L2+440 HIGH`
    );
}

console.log("");

for (const ins of insns) {
    if (ins.op !== 0x29) continue;

    const off = ins.loadOffset!;

    if (off !== 432 && off !== 440) continue;

    const prod = producerForLoad(ins.pos, off);

    console.log(
        `LOAD @ ${hex(ins.pos)} L2+${off}` +
        ` -> ${prod ? `CALL #${prod.n} @ ${hex(prod.pos)}` : "NONE"}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceCall87ConsumersV2.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);
const U8 = new Uint8Array(wasm);

function hex(n: number, w = 8): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (pos < U8.length) {
        const b = U8[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35)
            throw new Error(`ULEB too large at ${hex(pos - 1)}`);
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF in ULEB");
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let b = 0;

    while (pos < U8.length) {
        b = U8[pos++];
        result |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if ((b & 0x40) !== 0)
                result |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);

            return { value: result, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7n;

        if (shift > 70n)
            throw new Error(`SLEB too large at ${hex(pos - 1)}`);
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF in SLEB");
}

function readMemArg(pos: number): {
    align: number;
    offset: number;
    next: number;
} {
    const a = readULEB(pos);
    const o = readULEB(a.next);

    return {
        align: a.value,
        offset: o.value,
        next: o.next
    };
}

type Instr = {
    pos: number;
    op: number;
    end: number;
    text: string;
    kind?: string;
    offset?: number;
    local?: number;
    target?: number;
};

function decodeOne(pos: number): Instr {
    const start = pos;
    const op = U8[pos++];

    const simple: Record<number, string> = {
        0x00: "unreachable",
        0x01: "nop",
        0x05: "else",
        0x0b: "end",
        0x0c: "br",
        0x0d: "br_if",

        0x1a: "drop",
        0x1b: "select",

        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",

        0x50: "i64.eqz",
        0x51: "i64.eq",
        0x52: "i64.ne",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",

        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",
        0x7f: "i64.div_s",
        0x80: "i64.div_u",
        0x81: "i64.rem_s",
        0x82: "i64.rem_u",
        0x83: "i64.and",
        0x84: "i64.or",
        0x85: "i64.xor",
        0x86: "i64.shl",
        0x87: "i64.shr_s",
        0x88: "i64.shr_u",
        0x89: "i64.rotl",
        0x8a: "i64.rotr",

        0xa7: "i64.extend_i32_s"
    };

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const r = readULEB(pos);
        return {
            pos: start,
            op,
            end: r.next,
            text: `call ${r.value}`,
            target: r.value,
            kind: "call"
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x02) {
        const r = readSLEB(pos);
        return {
            pos: start,
            op,
            end: r.next,
            text: `block ${r.value}`
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x03) {
        const r = readSLEB(pos);
        return {
            pos: start,
            op,
            end: r.next,
            text: `loop ${r.value}`
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x04) {
        const r = readSLEB(pos);
        return {
            pos: start,
            op,
            end: r.next,
            text: `if ${r.value}`
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x0e) {
        const r = readULEB(pos);
        let p = r.next;

        const labels: number[] = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < r.value; i++) {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            labels.push(x.value);
            p = x.next;
        }

        const d = readULEB(p);

        return {
            pos: start,
            op,
            end: d.next,
            text: `br_table [${labels.join(",")}] default=${d.value}`
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x0f) {
        return {
            pos: start,
            op,
            end: pos,
            text: "return"
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
        const a = readULEB(pos);
        const b = readULEB(a.next);

        return {
            pos: start,
            op,
            end: b.next,
            text: `call_indirect type=${a.value} table=${b.value}`,
            kind: "call_indirect"
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
        const r = readULEB(pos);
        const name = op === 0x3f ? "memory.size" : "memory.grow";

        return {
            pos: start,
            op,
            end: r.next,
            text: `${name} ${r.value}`
        };
    }

    /*
     * i64.load
     */
    if (op === 0x29) {
        const m = readMemArg(pos);

        return {
            pos: start,
            op,
            end: m.next,
            text: `i64.load align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`,
            kind: "i64.load",
            offset: m.offset
        };
    }

    /*
     * i64.store
     */
    if (op === 0x37) {
        const m = readMemArg(pos);

        return {
            pos: start,
            op,
            end: m.next,
            text: `i64.store align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`,
            kind: "i64.store",
            offset: m.offset
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = readSLEB(pos);

        return {
            pos: start,
            op,
            end: r.next,
            text: `i32.const ${r.value}`
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        const r = readSLEB(pos);

        return {
            pos: start,
            op,
            end: r.next,
            text: `i64.const ${r.value}`
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
        const r = readULEB(pos);
        const n =
            op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
            op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
            "local.tee";

        return {
            pos: start,
            op,
            end: r.next,
            text: `${n} ${r.value}`,
            kind: n,
            local: r.value
        };
    }

    const name = simple[op];

    if (name) {
        return {
            pos: start,
            op,
            end: pos,
            text: name
        };
    }

    /*
     * SIMD / bulk / reference / miscellaneous unknown instruction.
     * Keep the byte visible instead of treating it as an instruction
     * with an invented length.
     */
    return {
        pos: start,
        op,
        end: pos,
        text: `UNKNOWN(0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")})`
    };
}

function decodeWindow(start: number, end: number): Instr[] {
    const out: Instr[] = [];
    let p = start;

    while (p < end) {
        try {
            const ins = decodeOne(p);
            out.push(ins);

            if (ins.end <= p)
                break;

            p = ins.end;
        } catch {
            out.push({
                pos: p,
                op: U8[p],
                end: p + 1,
                text: `DECODE_ERROR(0x${U8[p].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")})`
            });

            p++;
        }
    }

    return out;
}

    0x0125ad, 0x0125ba, 0x0125c3, 0x0125d1,

    0x012835, 0x01283f, 0x012846, 0x0128a8,
    0x0128b5
];

 * Known L2-relative offsets of the loads.

const storeOffsets: Record<number, number> = {
    0x0129c: 0,
    0x012589: 8,

    0x012789: 16,

    0x012950: 0
};

function symbolicForOffset(offset: number, callNo: number): string {
    return `C${callNo}_${offset % 16 === 0 ? "LOW" : "HIGH"}`;
}

function findCallProducer(pc: number, offset: number): {
    callNo: number;
    side: string;
} | null {
    let best: { callNo: number; side: string; pc: number } | null = null;

    for (const c of calls) {
        if (c.pc >= pc)
            continue;

        if (offset === c.dest) {
            if (!best || c.pc > best.pc)
                best = { callNo: c.n, side: "LOW", pc: c.pc };
        }

        if (offset === c.dest + 8) {
            if (!best || c.pc > best.pc)
                best = { callNo: c.n, side: "HIGH", pc: c.pc };
        }
    }

    return best ? {
        callNo: best.callNo,
        side: best.side
    } : null;
}

function printBytes(center: number, radius = 48) {
    const a = Math.max(FUNC_START, center - radius);
    const b = Math.min(FUNC_END, center + radius);

    console.log(`\nRAW WINDOW ${hex(a)} .. ${hex(b)}`);

    for (let p = a; p < b;) {
        const ins = decodeOne(p);

        const bytes = Array.from(U8.slice(p, Math.min(ins.end, b)))
            .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
            .join(" ");

        console.log(
            `${hex(p)}  ${bytes.padEnd(28)} ${ins.text}`
        );

        if (ins.end <= p)
            break;

        p = ins.end;
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 122 -> CALL 87 CONSUMER DATAFLOW v2");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${U8.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Function  : 122`);
console.log(`Range     : ${hex(FUNC_START)} .. ${hex(FUNC_END)}`);

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log(" PHASE 1 - VALIDATE LOAD INSTRUCTIONS");
console.log("============================================================");

let validLoads = 0;

for (const pc of loads) {
    if (U8[pc] !== 0x29) {
        console.log(
            `LOAD CHECK FAIL @ ${hex(pc)} byte=0x${U8[pc]
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(2, "0")}`
        );
        continue;
    }

    validLoads++;
}

console.log(`Valid i64.load sites : ${validLoads}/${loads.length}`);

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log(" PHASE 2 - CHRONOLOGICAL CALL87 CONSUMERS");
console.log("============================================================");

const validConsumers: {
    pc: number;
    offset: number;
    callNo: number;
    side: string;
}[] = [];

for (const pc of loads) {
    const offset = loadOffsets[pc];

    if (offset === undefined)
        continue;

    const producer = findCallProducer(pc, offset);

    if (!producer)
        continue;

    /*
     * A load is only a valid consumer if the producer is the
     * most recent CALL87 producer of this exact 8-byte slot.
     */
    const overwritten = calls.some(c =>
        c.n > producer.callNo &&
        c.pc < pc &&
        (c.dest === offset || c.dest + 8 === offset)
    );

    if (overwritten)
        continue;

    validConsumers.push({
        pc,
        offset,
        callNo: producer.callNo,
        side: producer.side
    });
}

for (const c of validConsumers) {
    console.log(
        `C${c.callNo}_${c.side.padEnd(4)}  ` +
        `LOAD @ ${hex(c.pc)}  ` +
        `L2+${c.offset}`
    );
}

console.log(`\nVALID TEMPORAL CONSUMERS : ${validConsumers.length}`);

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log(" PHASE 3 - EXPRESSION WINDOWS");
console.log("============================================================");

/*
 * For each valid consumer print a compact decoded window.
 *
 * We deliberately do NOT attempt fake stack reconstruction here.
 * The purpose is to expose the actual instruction neighborhood
 * around the consumer so the next pass can perform exact symbolic
 * stack tracking.
 */

for (const c of validConsumers) {
    console.log("\n------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(
        `CALL #${c.callNo} ${c.side} -> LOAD @ ${hex(c.pc)}`
    );
    console.log(
        `SOURCE SLOT : L2 + ${c.offset}`
    );

    printBytes(c.pc, 36);
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log(" PHASE 4 - SECOND STAGE / CALL #26");
console.log("============================================================");

const call26 = calls.find(x => x.n === 26)!;

console.log(`CALL #26 @ ${hex(call26.pc)}`);
console.log(`DEST = L2 + ${call26.dest}`);
console.log(`LOW  = L2 + ${call26.dest}`);
console.log(`HIGH = L2 + ${call26.dest + 8}`);

console.log("\nInstructions immediately after CALL #26:");

{
    let p = call26.pc + 2;

    for (let i = 0; i < 28 && p < FUNC_END; i++) {
        const ins = decodeOne(p);

        const bytes = Array.from(U8.slice(p, ins.end))
            .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
            .join(" ");

        console.log(
            `${hex(p)}  ${bytes.padEnd(24)} ${ins.text}`
        );

        if (ins.end <= p)
            break;

        p = ins.end;
    }
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log(" PHASE 5 - ALL LOADS AFTER CALL #26");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const pc of loads) {
    if (pc <= call26.pc)
        continue;

    const offset = loadOffsets[pc];

    if (offset === undefined)
        continue;

    console.log(
        `LOAD @ ${hex(pc)}  L2+${offset}`
    );
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log(" SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`CALL87 sites              : ${calls.length}`);
console.log(`Listed i64.load sites     : ${loads.length}`);
console.log(`Temporal valid consumers  : ${validConsumers.length}`);

console.log("\nIMPORTANT:");
console.log("1. Loads before a CALL87 producer are NOT consumers of that call.");
console.log("2. A consumer is invalid if a later CALL87 overwrites the same slot.");
console.log("3. This pass exposes real instruction windows; it does not invent");
console.log("   semantic variable names.");
console.log("4. CALL #26 is treated separately because its 432/440 outputs have");
console.log("   no later direct i64.load in the supplied load set.");
console.log("5. NO TRANSACTION / NO NETWORK ACCESS");

console.log("\nDONE");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

function hex(n: number, width = 6) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

/*
 * Function 122 exact CALL 87 sites.
 *
 * DEST = L2 + offset
 */
const CALLS = [
  [0x0122c4, 432],
  [0x0122dc, 0],
  [0x0122f6, 384],
  [0x012310, 320],
  [0x01232c, 96],
  [0x012346, 208],
  [0x012358, 16],
  [0x01236b, 224],
  [0x01237b, 80],
  [0x01238b, 192],
  [0x01239b, 464],
  [0x0123ae, 336],
  [0x0123be, 304],
  [0x0123ce, 144],
  [0x0123de, 416],
  [0x0123f0, 32],
  [0x012403, 272],
  [0x012416, 160],
  [0x01242b, 368],
  [0x012440, 128],
  [0x012455, 256],

  [0x0126b4, 432],
  [0x0126c4, 176],
  [0x0126d4, 352],
  [0x0126e4, 112],
  [0x0126f4, 240],
] as const;

/*
 * Known Function 122 memory accesses.
 *
 * These were already identified from the correct instruction decoder.
 *
 * We only use accesses whose base is known/expected to be L2.
 * The purpose here is provenance candidate discovery, not full execution.
 */
const LOADS = [
  [0x012293, 32],
  [0x012298, 0],
  [0x0122b4, 24],
  [0x0122bd, 0],
  [0x0122ca, 504],
  [0x0122d4, 480],
  [0x0122e6, 56],
  [0x0122ef, 32],
  [0x012300, 16],
  [0x012309, 8],
  [0x01231a, 496],
  [0x012324, 488],
  [0x012336, 48],

  [0x01245b, 368],

  [0x012478, 256],

  [0x0124a8, 80],
  [0x0124af, 16],
  [0x0124b7, 0],
  [0x0124be, 320],
  [0x0124c6, 432],
  [0x0124d2, 96],

  [0x01270c, 288],
  [0x012714, 352],
  [0x012720, 240],
  [0x012729, 176],
  [0x01273e, 264],
  [0x01274a, 40],
  [0x012751, 376],
  [0x012759, 280],
  [0x012792, 400],
  [0x01279d, 112],
  [0x0127aa, 64],
  [0x0127be, 168],
  [0x0127cc, 136],

  [0x012811, 184],
  [0x01281d, 248],
  [0x01282f, 296],
  [0x012835, 360],
  [0x01283f, 56],
  [0x012846, 456],

  [0x01289d, 72],
  [0x0128a8, 120],
  [0x0128b5, 408],

/*
 * Known stores in Function 122.
 *
 * A Function 87 call writes:
 *
 *   DEST + 0 = LOW
 *   DEST + 8 = HIGH
 *
 * We compare those offsets against later stores too, because an
 * intervening store can invalidate a direct provenance chain.
 */
const STORES = [
  [0x01229c, 0],
  [0x012589, 8],
  [0x012665, 40],
  [0x012789, 16],
  [0x0127fe, 48],
  [0x01288a, 24],
  [0x0128fb, 56],
  [0x01293b, 32],
  [0x012950, 0],
] as const;

/*
 * Confirm all CALL 87 bytes first.
 */
function verifyCall87(pos: number): boolean {
  return wasm[pos] === 0x10 && wasm[pos + 1] === 0x57;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 122 -> CALL 87 MEMORY PROVENANCE v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log();

/*
 * Sort all events by file position.
 */
type Event =
  | {
      kind: "CALL";
      pos: number;
      dest: number;
      callIndex: number;
    }
  | {
      kind: "LOAD";
      pos: number;
      offset: number;
    }
  | {
      kind: "STORE";
      pos: number;
      offset: number;
    };

const events: Event[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < CALLS.length; i++) {
  const [pos, dest] = CALLS[i];

  if (verifyCall87(pos)) {
    events.push({
      kind: "CALL",
      pos,
      dest,
      callIndex: i + 1,
    });
  }
}

for (const [pos, offset] of LOADS) {
  events.push({
    kind: "LOAD",
    pos,
    offset,
  });
}

for (const [pos, offset] of STORES) {
  events.push({
    kind: "STORE",
    pos,
    offset,
  });
}

events.sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);

/*
 * For every CALL:
 *
 *   LOW  address = DEST
 *   HIGH address = DEST + 8
 *
 * Search forward for loads matching those addresses.
 *
 * We stop at:
 *   1. another CALL writing the same address
 *   2. a STORE to that address
 *
 * This gives a conservative direct-provenance candidate.
 */
let totalCandidates = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < CALLS.length; i++) {
  const [callPos, dest] = CALLS[i];

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`CALL #${i + 1} @ ${hex(callPos)}`);
  console.log(`DEST       = L2 + ${dest}`);
  console.log(`LOW WRITE  = L2 + ${dest}`);
  console.log(`HIGH WRITE = L2 + ${dest + 8}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  if (!verifyCall87(callPos)) {
    console.log("CALL87 BYTE CHECK: FAIL");
    console.log();
    continue;
  }

  console.log("CALL87 BYTE CHECK: OK");
  console.log();

  const targets = [
    {
      name: "LOW",
      offset: dest,
    },
    {
      name: "HIGH",
      offset: dest + 8,
    },
  ];

  for (const target of targets) {
    let found = 0;
    let invalidated = false;

    for (const ev of events) {
      if (ev.pos <= callPos) continue;

      /*
       * Another CALL with same output address overwrites the value.
       */
      if (
        ev.kind === "CALL" &&
        (ev.dest === target.offset ||
          ev.dest + 8 === target.offset)
      ) {
        console.log(
          `${target.name.padEnd(5)} -> overwritten by CALL #${ev.callIndex} @ ${hex(ev.pos)}`
        );
        break;
      }

      /*
       * A direct store to the same L2 offset breaks the simple chain.
       */
      if (
        ev.kind === "STORE" &&
        ev.offset === target.offset
      ) {
        console.log(
          `${target.name.padEnd(5)} -> INVALIDATED by STORE @ ${hex(ev.pos)}`
        );
        invalidated = true;
        break;
      }

      /*
       * Direct load candidate.
       */
      if (
        ev.kind === "LOAD" &&
        ev.offset === target.offset
      ) {
        console.log(
          `${target.name.padEnd(5)} -> LOAD @ ${hex(ev.pos)}  [L2+${target.offset}]`
        );

        found++;
        totalCandidates++;

        /*
         * Show up to three direct load candidates.
         */
        if (found >= 3) break;
      }
    }

    if (found === 0 && !invalidated) {
      console.log(
        `${target.name.padEnd(5)} -> NO DIRECT LOAD FOUND`
      );
    }
  }

  console.log();
}

/*
 * Compact reverse index:
 *
 * L2 offset -> producing CALL(s) -> later loads
 */
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" REVERSE INDEX");
console.log("============================================================");

const interestingOffsets = Array.from(
  new Set(
    CALLS.flatMap(([_, dest]) => [
      dest,
      dest + 8,
    ])
  )
).sort((a, b) => a - b);

for (const offset of interestingOffsets) {
  const producers = CALLS
    .map(([pos, dest], index) => ({
      index: index + 1,
      pos,
      dest,
    }))
    .filter(
      x => x.dest === offset || x.dest + 8 === offset
    );

  const consumers = LOADS
    .filter(([pos, loadOffset]) => loadOffset === offset);

  console.log();
  console.log(`L2 + ${offset}`);

  if (producers.length === 0) {
    console.log("  producers : none");
  } else {
    for (const p of producers) {
      const part = p.dest === offset ? "LOW" : "HIGH";

      console.log(
        `  producer  : CALL #${p.index} @ ${hex(p.pos)} -> ${part}`
      );
    }
  }

  if (consumers.length === 0) {
    console.log("  consumers : none");
  } else {
    for (const [pos] of consumers) {
      console.log(
        `  consumer  : LOAD @ ${hex(pos)}`
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`CALL87 sites checked : ${CALLS.length}`);
console.log(`Direct load candidates: ${totalCandidates}`);
console.log();
console.log("IMPORTANT:");
console.log("These are STATIC provenance candidates.");
console.log("They do NOT yet prove semantic variable names.");
console.log("Next step is expression/dataflow tracing at each consumer.");
console.log();
console.log("DONE - NO TRANSACTION / NO NETWORK ACCESS");

/*
 * Known stores in Function 122.
 */
const STORES = [
  [0x01229c, 0],
  [0x012589, 8],
  [0x012665, 40],
  [0x012789, 16],
  [0x0127fe, 48],
  [0x01288a, 24],
  [0x0128fb, 56],
  [0x01293b, 32],
  [0x012950, 0],
] as const;

function verifyCall87(pos: number): boolean {
  return wasm[pos] === 0x10 && wasm[pos + 1] === 0x57;
}

/*
 * Verify that a load really has:
 *
 *   local.get 2
 *   i64.load ...
 *
 * immediately before it.
 *
 * For the current Function 122 stack pattern this is the
 * critical proof that the memory base is L2.
 *
 * Encoding:
 *
 *   20 02       local.get 2
 *   29 ...      i64.load
 */
function verifyL2Base(loadPos: number): {
  valid: boolean;
  reason: string;
} {
  if (wasm[loadPos] !== 0x29) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      reason: `opcode=0x${(wasm[loadPos] ?? 0)
        .toString(16)
        .padStart(2, "0")} not i64.load`,
    };
  }

  if (wasm[loadPos - 2] !== 0x20 ||
      wasm[loadPos - 1] !== 0x02) {
    return {
      valid: false,
      reason:
        `preceding bytes=${hex(wasm[loadPos - 2])} ` +
        `${hex(wasm[loadPos - 1])}, expected 0x20 0x02`,
    };
  }

  return {
    valid: true,
    reason: "local.get 2 immediately precedes i64.load",
  };
}

/*
 * Read the exact i64.load immediate.
 *
 * We expect:
 *
 *   29 align offset
 */
function decodeLoad(loadPos: number) {
  if (wasm[loadPos] !== 0x29)
    return null;

  let p = loadPos + 1;

  function readULEB(): number {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
      const b = wasm[p++];

      value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

      if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
        break;

      shift += 7;
    }

    return value;
  }

  const align = readULEB();
  const offset = readULEB();

  return {
    align,
    offset,
    end: p,
  };
}

/*
 * Build events.
 */
type Event =
  | {
      kind: "CALL";
      pos: number;
      dest: number;
      callIndex: number;
    }
  | {
      kind: "LOAD";
      pos: number;
      offset: number;
    }
  | {
      kind: "STORE";
      pos: number;
      offset: number;
    };

const events: Event[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < CALLS.length; i++) {
  const [pos, dest] = CALLS[i];

  if (verifyCall87(pos)) {
    events.push({
      kind: "CALL",
      pos,
      dest,
      callIndex: i + 1,
    });
  }
}

for (const [pos, offset] of LOADS) {
  events.push({
    kind: "LOAD",
    pos,
    offset,
  });
}

for (const [pos, offset] of STORES) {
  events.push({
    kind: "STORE",
    pos,
    offset,
  });
}

events.sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);

console.log("============================================================");

for (const [pos, offset] of LOADS) {

  if (result.valid) {

let candidateCount = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < CALLS.length; i++) {
  const [callPos, dest] = CALLS[i];

  if (!verifyCall87(callPos))
    continue;

  console.log();
  console.log(
    `CALL #${i + 1} @ ${hex(callPos)}  DEST=L2+${dest}`
  );

  for (const part of [
    { name: "LOW", offset: dest },
    { name: "HIGH", offset: dest + 8 },
  ]) {

    let found = false;

    for (const [loadPos, loadOffset] of LOADS) {

      if (loadPos <= callPos)
        continue;

      if (loadOffset !== part.offset)
        continue;

      const base = verifyL2Base(loadPos);

      if (!base.valid)
        continue;

      /*
       * Check for an intervening CALL that overwrites this slot.
       */
      let overwritten = false;

      for (const [otherPos, otherDest] of CALLS) {

        if (otherPos <= callPos)
          continue;

        if (otherPos >= loadPos)
          continue;

        if (
          otherDest === part.offset ||
          otherDest + 8 === part.offset
        ) {
          overwritten = true;
          break;
        }
      }

      /*
       * Check for an intervening store.
       */
      let stored = false;

      for (const [storePos, storeOffset] of STORES) {

        if (storePos <= callPos)
          continue;

        if (storePos >= loadPos)
          continue;

        if (storeOffset === part.offset) {
          stored = true;
          break;
        }
      }

      if (overwritten || stored)
        continue;

      console.log(
        `  ${part.name.padEnd(5)} -> ` +
        `LOAD @ ${hex(loadPos)} ` +
        `= MEM64[L2+${part.offset}]`
      );

      candidateCount++;
      found = true;

      break;
    }

    if (!found) {
      console.log(
        `  ${part.name.padEnd(5)} -> NO DIRECT VERIFIED LOAD`
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log();

console.log("============================================================");

const offsets = Array.from(
  new Set(
    CALLS.flatMap(([_, dest]) => [
      dest,
      dest + 8,
    ])
  )
).sort((a, b) => a - b);

for (const offset of offsets) {

  console.log();
  console.log(`L2 + ${offset}`);

  const producers = CALLS
    .map(([pos, dest], index) => ({
      index: index + 1,
      pos,
      dest,
    }))
    .filter(
      x => x.dest === offset || x.dest + 8 === offset
    );

  const consumers = LOADS
    .filter(([_, loadOffset]) => loadOffset === offset)
    .filter(([pos]) => verifyL2Base(pos).valid);

  for (const p of producers) {
    const half = p.dest === offset ? "LOW" : "HIGH";

    console.log(
      `  producer : CALL #${p.index} @ ${hex(p.pos)} -> ${half}`
    );
  }

  for (const [pos] of consumers) {
    console.log(
      `  consumer : LOAD @ ${hex(pos)} -> MEM64[L2+${offset}]`
    );
  }

  if (producers.length === 0)
    console.log("  producer : none");

  if (consumers.length === 0)
    console.log("  consumer : none");
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x01226d;
const END   = 0x01295b;

const TARGET_SLOTS = [48, 56, 432, 440];

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        if (pos >= buf.length) throw new Error(`ULEB EOF @ 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);

        const b = buf[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error(`ULEB overflow @ 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
}

function readSLEB(pos: number, bits: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        if (pos >= buf.length) throw new Error(`SLEB EOF @ 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);

        const b = buf[pos++];
        result |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift + 7n < BigInt(bits) && (b & 0x40)) {
                result |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
            }
            return { value: result, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }

    throw new Error(`SLEB overflow @ 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
}

function readMemArg(pos: number): {
    align: number;
    offset: number;
    next: number;
} {
    const a = readULEB(pos);
    const o = readULEB(a.next);

    return {
        align: a.value,
        offset: o.value,
        next: o.next,
    };
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function decode(pos: number): {
    start: number;
    end: number;
    text: string;
    kind: string;
    callIndex?: number;
    localIndex?: number;
    memOffset?: number;
} {
    const start = pos;
    const op = buf[pos++];

    // local.get
    if (op === 0x20) {
        const x = readULEB(pos);
        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
            kind: "local.get",
            localIndex: x.value,
        };
    }

    // local.set
    if (op === 0x21) {
        const x = readULEB(pos);
        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
            kind: "local.set",
            localIndex: x.value,
        };
    }

    // local.tee
    if (op === 0x22) {
        const x = readULEB(pos);
        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
            kind: "local.tee",
            localIndex: x.value,
        };
    }

    // i32.const
    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = readSLEB(pos, 32);
        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
            kind: "other",
        };
    }

    // i64.const
    if (op === 0x42) {
        const x = readSLEB(pos, 64);
        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
            kind: "other",
        };
    }

    // call
    if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = readULEB(pos);
        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            text: `call ${x.value}`,
            kind: "call",
            callIndex: x.value,
        };
    }

    // i64 loads/stores
    if (
        op === 0x29 || // i64.load
        op === 0x2f || // i64.load32_u
        op === 0x37    // i64.store
    ) {
        const m = readMemArg(pos);

        let name = "memory-op";

        if (op === 0x29) name = "i64.load";
        if (op === 0x2f) name = "i64.load32_u";
        if (op === 0x37) name = "i64.store";

        return {
            start,
            end: m.next,
            text: `${name} align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`,
            kind: name,
            memOffset: m.offset,
        };
    }

    // Other single-byte instructions
    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x0b: "end",
        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",
        0x80: "i64.div_u",
        0x83: "i64.and",
        0x84: "i64.or",
        0x86: "i64.shl",
        0x88: "i64.shr_u",
        0x54: "i64.lt_u",
        0x56: "i64.gt_u",
        0x57: "i64.le_u",
        0x55: "i64.ne",
        0x50: "i64.eqz",
        0x7f: "i64.extend_i32_u",
        0xad: "i64.extend32_s",
    };

    return {
        start,
        end: pos,
        text: names[op] ?? `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
        kind: "other",
    };
}

interface Instr {
    start: number;
    end: number;
    text: string;
    kind: string;
    callIndex?: number;
    localIndex?: number;
    memOffset?: number;
}

const instructions: Instr[] = [];

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
    const ins = decode(p);
    instructions.push(ins);
    p = ins.end;
}

if (p !== END) {
    throw new Error(
        `Decoder ended at ${hex(p)}, expected ${hex(END)}`
    );
}

            for (const a of after) {
                console.log(
                    `      ${hex(a.start)} ${a.text}`
                );
            }
        }
    }

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 113;

function readUleb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(v: number, w = 8) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function findSection(idWanted: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = readUleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === idWanted)
      return { start, end };

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error("section not found");
}

/* imports */

const imp = findSection(2);

let importedFunctions = 0;

{
  let p = imp.start;

  const c = readUleb(p);
  p = c.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
    const ml = readUleb(p);
    p = ml.next + ml.value;

    const nl = readUleb(p);
    p = nl.next + nl.value;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      const t = readUleb(p);
      p = t.next;
      importedFunctions++;
    } else if (kind === 1) {
      p++;
      const f = readUleb(p);
      p = f.next;

      const a = readUleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      if (f.value & 1) {
        const b = readUleb(p);
        p = b.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 2) {
      const f = readUleb(p);
      p = f.next;

      const a = readUleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      if (f.value & 1) {
        const b = readUleb(p);
        p = b.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }
}

/* code */

const codeSec = findSection(10);

const bodies = new Map<number, { start: number; end: number }>();

{
  let p = codeSec.start;

  const c = readUleb(p);
  p = c.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
    const s = readUleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    bodies.set(importedFunctions + i, { start, end });

    p = end;
  }
}

console.log("CALLERS OF FUNC 113");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let hits = 0;

for (const [func, body] of bodies) {
  for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

    if (wasm[p] !== 0x10)
      continue;

    const x = readUleb(p + 1);

    if (x.value !== TARGET)
      continue;

    console.log("");
    console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
    console.log(`CALL @ ${hex(p)}`);
    console.log(`BODY = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);

    const from = Math.max(body.start, p - 24);
    const to = Math.min(body.end, x.next + 24);

    console.log("CONTEXT:");

    for (let q = from; q < to; q++) {
      console.log(
        `${hex(q)}  ${wasm[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
      );
    }

    hits++;
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(pos:number) {
  let v=0, shift=0, p=pos;
  while(p<wasm.length) {
    const b=wasm[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if(!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }
  return {value:v>>>0,next:p};
}

let p=8;
let imported=0;

while(p<wasm.length) {
  const id=wasm[p++];
  const s=uleb(p); p=s.next;
  const end=p+s.value;

  if(id===2) {
    let q=p;
    const n=uleb(q); q=n.next;

    for(let i=0;i<n.value;i++) {
      const ml=uleb(q); q=ml.next+ml.value;
      const nl=uleb(q); q=nl.next+nl.value;
      const kind=wasm[q++];

      if(kind===0) {
        q=uleb(q).next;
        imported++;
      } else if(kind===1) {
        q++;
        q=uleb(q).next;
        q=uleb(q).next;
      } else if(kind===2) {
        q=uleb(q).next;
        q=uleb(q).next;
      } else if(kind===3) {
        q+=2;
      }
    }
  }

  if(id===10) {
    let q=p;
    const n=uleb(q); q=n.next;

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("STATIC ARGUMENT TRACE -> FUNC 113");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for(let i=0;i<n.value;i++) {
      const bs=uleb(q); q=bs.next;
      const st=q, en=q+bs.value;
      const fn=imported+i;

      for(let z=st;z<en;) {
        const pos=z;
        const op=wasm[z++];

        if(op===0x10) {
          const x=uleb(z);
          z=x.next;

          if(x.value===113) {
            console.log("");
            console.log(`FUNC ${fn}  CALL 113 @ 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);

            // Show preceding 12 decoded instructions.
            let r=st;
            const ins:any[]=[];

            while(r<pos) {
              const ip=r;
              const rop=wasm[r++];

              let imm:any=null;

              if(
                rop===0x20 || rop===0x21 || rop===0x22 ||
                rop===0x10 || rop===0x41 || rop===0x42
              ) {
                const a=uleb(r);
                imm=a.value;
                r=a.next;
              }
              else if(rop>=0x28 && rop<=0x3e) {
                const a=uleb(r); r=a.next;
                const o=uleb(r); r=o.next;
                imm=`mem offset=${o.value}`;
              }
              else if(rop===0x11) {
                const a=uleb(r); r=a.next;
                const b=uleb(r); r=b.next;
                imm=`indirect type=${a.value}`;
              }

              ins.push({ip,op:rop,imm});
            }

            const tail=ins.slice(-12);

            for(const a of tail) {
              console.log(
                `  0x${a.ip.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ` +
                `${a.op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}` +
                (a.imm===null ? "" : ` ${a.imm}`)
              );
            }
          }
        }
        else if(
          op===0x20 || op===0x21 || op===0x22 ||
          op===0x10 || op===0x0c || op===0x0d ||
          op===0x41 || op===0x42
        ) {
          z=uleb(z).next;
        }
        else if(op>=0x28 && op<=0x3e) {
          z=uleb(z).next;
          z=uleb(z).next;
        }
        else if(op===0x11) {
          z=uleb(z).next;
          z=uleb(z).next;
        }
      }

      q=en;
    }

    break;
  }

  p=end;
}
