import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const START = 0x0e1147;
const END   = 0x0e1308;

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { v: v >>> 0, p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;
  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v |= (-1 << s);
  return { v: v | 0, p };
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  if (op >= 0x20 && op <= 0x24) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    const names: Record<number,string> = {
      0x20: "local.get",
      0x21: "local.set",
      0x22: "local.tee",
      0x23: "global.get",
      0x24: "global.set"
    };

    console.log(`${hex(at)} ${names[op]} ${r.v}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = sleb(p);
    p = r.p;
    console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.const ${r.v}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x42) {
    const r = sleb(p);
    p = r.p;
    console.log(`${hex(at)} i64.const ${r.v}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    console.log(`${hex(at)} CALL ${r.v}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
    const a = uleb(p);
    p = a.p;

    const o = uleb(p);
    p = o.p;

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} ${op >= 0x36 ? "STORE" : "LOAD"} ` +
      `op=0x${op.toString(16)} align=${a.v} offset=${o.v}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0xfc) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    console.log(`${hex(at)} 0xfc subop=${r.v}`);

    if (r.v === 8 || r.v === 10) {
      let x = uleb(p);
      p = x.p;

      x = uleb(p);
      p = x.p;
    }

    continue;
  }

  if (
    op === 0x0b || op === 0x05 || op === 0x1a ||
    op === 0x6a || op === 0x6b ||
    op === 0x71 || op === 0x72 ||
    op === 0x73 || op === 0x74 ||
    op === 0x75 || op === 0x76 ||
    op === 0x7c || op === 0x7d ||
    op === 0x7e || op === 0x7f ||
    op === 0x80 || op === 0x81 ||
    op === 0x82 || op === 0x83 ||
    op === 0x84 || op === 0x85 ||
    op === 0x86 || op === 0x87 ||
    op === 0x88
  ) {
    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} ${op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if"} ${r.v}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    if (b[p] === 0x40) {
      p++;
      console.log(`${hex(at)} block/loop/if 0x40`);
    } else {
      const r = sleb(p);
      p = r.p;
      console.log(`${hex(at)} blocktype=${r.v}`);
    }

    continue;
  }

  console.log(
    `${hex(at)} UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
  );
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END V31");
console.log("============================================================");

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { v: v >>> 0, p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;

  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      break;
  }

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (-1 << s);

  return { v: v | 0, p };
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceCallers1533_2034.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = new Set([1533, 2034]);

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readI32Const(pos: number): {
  value: number;
  next: number;
} {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: pos,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readString(pos: number): {
  value: string;
  next: number;
} {
  const r = readULEB(pos);
  const end = r.next + r.value;

  return {
    value: wasm.subarray(r.next, end).toString("utf8"),
    next: end,
  };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8): string {
  return (
    "0x" +
    (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0")
  );
}

function readMemArg(pos: number): {
  align: number;
  offset: number;
  next: number;
} {
  const a = readULEB(pos);
  const o = readULEB(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next,
  };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FIND SECTIONS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const sections: {
  id: number;
  payloadStart: number;
  end: number;
}[] = [];

let p = 8;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];

  const size = readULEB(p);
  p = size.next;

  sections.push({
    id,
    payloadStart: p,
    end: p + size.value,
  });

  p += size.value;
}

const importSec = sections.find((s) => s.id === 2);
const funcSec = sections.find((s) => s.id === 3);
const codeSec = sections.find((s) => s.id === 10);

if (!funcSec || !codeSec) {
  throw new Error("FUNCTION/CODE section not found");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// IMPORT COUNT
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let importedCount = 0;

if (importSec) {
  p = importSec.payloadStart;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    p = readString(p).next;
    p = readString(p).next;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    if (kind === 0x00) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      importedCount++;
    } else if (kind === 0x01) {
      p++;
      const flags = wasm[p++];
      p = readULEB(p).next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        p = readULEB(p).next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 0x02) {
      const flags = wasm[p++];

      p = readULEB(p).next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        p = readULEB(p).next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 0x03) {
      p += 2;
    } else if (kind === 0x04) {
      p++;
      p = readULEB(p).next;
    } else {
      throw new Error(
        `Unknown import kind 0x${kind.toString(16)}`
      );
    }
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION TYPES
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const functionTypes: number[] = [];

p = funcSec.payloadStart;

{
  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;

    functionTypes.push(r.value);
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE BODIES
// ------------------------------------------------------------

interface Body {
  globalFunc: number;
  ordinal: number;
  typeIndex: number;
  bodyStart: number;
  bodyEnd: number;
  codeStart: number;
  codeEnd: number;
}

const bodies: Body[] = [];

p = codeSec.payloadStart;

{
  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
    const size = readULEB(p);
    p = size.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + size.value;

    const localDecls = readULEB(p);
    p = localDecls.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localDecls.value; i++) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      p++;
    }

    const codeStart = p;

    bodies.push({
      globalFunc: importedCount + ordinal,
      ordinal,
      typeIndex: functionTypes[ordinal],
      bodyStart,
      bodyEnd,
      codeStart,
      codeEnd: bodyEnd,
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SIMPLE INSTRUCTION DECODER
// ------------------------------------------------------------

interface Instr {
  addr: number;
  op: number;
  text: string;
  next: number;
}

function decode(pos: number): Instr {
  const addr = pos;
  const op = wasm[pos++];

  let text = `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

  switch (op) {
    case 0x02: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `block type=${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x03: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `loop type=${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x04: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `if type=${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x05:
      text = "else";
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      text = "end";
      break;

    case 0x0c: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `br ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `br_if ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0f:
      text = "return";
      break;

    case 0x10: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `call func=${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const t = readULEB(pos);
      pos = t.next;

      const table = readULEB(pos);
      pos = table.next;

      text =
        `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`;

      break;
    }

    case 0x1a:
      text = "drop";
      break;

    case 0x20: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `global.get ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const r = readULEB(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `global.set ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const m = readMemArg(pos);
      pos = m.next;
      text =
        `i32.load align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x2c: {
      const m = readMemArg(pos);
      pos = m.next;
      text =
        `i32.load8_s align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x2d: {
      const m = readMemArg(pos);
      pos = m.next;
      text =
        `i32.load8_u align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const m = readMemArg(pos);
      pos = m.next;
      text =
        `i32.store align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x3a: {
      const m = readMemArg(pos);
      pos = m.next;
      text =
        `i32.store8 align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const r = readI32Const(pos);
      pos = r.next;
      text = `i32.const ${hex(r.value)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x45:
      text = "i32.eqz";
      break;

    case 0x46:
      text = "i32.eq";
      break;

    case 0x47:
      text = "i32.ne";
      break;

    case 0x48:
      text = "i32.lt_s";
      break;

    case 0x49:
      text = "i32.lt_u";
      break;

    case 0x4a:
      text = "i32.gt_s";
      break;

    case 0x4b:
      text = "i32.gt_u";
      break;

    case 0x4c:
      text = "i32.le_s";
      break;

    case 0x4d:
      text = "i32.le_u";
      break;

    case 0x4e:
      text = "i32.ge_s";
      break;

    case 0x4f:
      text = "i32.ge_u";
      break;

    case 0x6a:
      text = "i32.add";
      break;

    case 0x6b:
      text = "i32.sub";
      break;

    case 0x6c:
      text = "i32.mul";
      break;

    case 0x71:
      text = "i32.and";
      break;

    case 0x72:
      text = "i32.or";
      break;

    case 0x73:
      text = "i32.xor";
      break;

    case 0x74:
      text = "i32.shl";
      break;

    case 0x75:
      text = "i32.shr_s";
      break;

    case 0x76:
      text = "i32.shr_u";
      break;
  }

  return {
    addr,
    op,
    text,
    next: pos,
  };
}

  index: number;
  instructions: Instr[];
  target: number;
}[] = [];

  const instructions: Instr[] = [];

  let q = body.codeStart;

  while (q < body.codeEnd) {
    const ins = decode(q);

    instructions.push(ins);

    if (ins.next <= q) {
      break;
    }

    q = ins.next;
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
    const ins = instructions[i];

    if (!ins.text.startsWith("call func=")) {
      continue;
    }

    const target = Number(
      ins.text.substring("call func=".length)
    );

    if (!TARGETS.has(target)) {
      continue;
    }

    hits.push({
      body,
      index: i,
      instructions,
      target,
    });
  }
}

      i === hit.index ? "CALL " : "      ";

    );
  }

FILE: scripts\traceCallers248.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 248;

const TARGET_CALLERS = new Set([
  1795,
  2717,
  3015,
  4725
]);

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// ULEB / SLEB
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];

    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: result >>> 0,
        next: pos
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];

    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      break;
    }
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    result |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return {
    value: result | 0,
    next: pos
  };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Block type
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function skipBlockType(pos) {
  const b = wasm[pos];

  if (
    b === 0x40 ||
    b === 0x7f ||
    b === 0x7e ||
    b === 0x7d ||
    b === 0x7c ||
    b === 0x7b ||
    b === 0x70 ||
    b === 0x6f
  ) {
    return pos + 1;
  }

  return readSLEB(pos).next;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Memory argument
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function readMemArg(pos) {
  const align = readULEB(pos);
  const offset = readULEB(align.next);

  return {
    align: align.value,
    offset: offset.value,
    next: offset.next
  };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Decode selected function
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function decodeFunction(body) {
  const instructions = [];

  let p = body.start;

  while (p < body.end) {
    const opPos = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    try {
      let text = "";

      switch (op) {

        case 0x00:
          text = "unreachable";
          break;

        case 0x01:
          text = "nop";
          break;

        case 0x02:
          p = skipBlockType(p);
          text = "block";
          break;

        case 0x03:
          p = skipBlockType(p);
          text = "loop";
          break;

        case 0x04:
          p = skipBlockType(p);
          text = "if";
          break;

        case 0x05:
          text = "else";
          break;

        case 0x0b:
          text = "end";
          break;

        case 0x0c: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text = `br ${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x0d: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text = `br_if ${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x0e: {
          const n = readULEB(p);
          p = n.next;

          const labels = [];

          for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;
            labels.push(x.value);
          }

          const def = readULEB(p);
          p = def.next;

          text =
            `br_table [${labels.join(",")}] default=${def.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
          text = "return";
          break;

        case 0x10: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          if (r.value === TARGET) {
            text = `call ${r.value} <<< TARGET`;
          } else {
            text = `call ${r.value}`;
          }

          break;
        }

        case 0x11: {
          const type = readULEB(p);
          p = type.next;

          const table = readULEB(p);
          p = table.next;

          text =
            `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x1a:
          text = "drop";
          break;

        case 0x1b:
          text = "select";
          break;

        case 0x20: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x23: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text = `global.get ${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x24: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text = `global.set ${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x25: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text = `table.get ${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x26: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text = `table.set ${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        // Loads
        case 0x28:
        case 0x29:
        case 0x2a:
        case 0x2b:
        case 0x2c:
        case 0x2d:
        case 0x2e:
        case 0x2f:
        case 0x30:
        case 0x31:
        case 0x32:
        case 0x33:
        case 0x34:
        case 0x35:

        // Stores
        case 0x36:
        case 0x37:
        case 0x38:
        case 0x39:
        case 0x3a:
        case 0x3b:
        case 0x3c:
        case 0x3d:
        case 0x3e: {

          const mem = readMemArg(p);
          p = mem.next;

          const names = {
            0x28: "i32.load",
            0x29: "i64.load",
            0x2a: "f32.load",
            0x2b: "f64.load",
            0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
            0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
            0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
            0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
            0x30: "i64.load8_s",
            0x31: "i64.load8_u",
            0x32: "i64.load16_s",
            0x33: "i64.load16_u",
            0x34: "i64.load32_s",
            0x35: "i64.load32_u",
            0x36: "i32.store",
            0x37: "i64.store",
            0x38: "f32.store",
            0x39: "f64.store",
            0x3a: "i32.store8",
            0x3b: "i32.store16",
            0x3c: "i64.store8",
            0x3d: "i64.store16",
            0x3e: "i64.store32"
          };

          text =
            `${names[op]} align=${mem.align} offset=${mem.offset}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x3f: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text = `memory.size ${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x40: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text = `memory.grow ${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
          const r = readSLEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `i32.const ${r.value}`;

          if (r.value === 4275) {
            text += " <<< 4275";
          }

          break;
        }

        case 0x42: {
          const r = readSLEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text = `i64.const ${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x43:
          p += 4;
          text = "f32.const";
          break;

        case 0x44:
          p += 8;
          text = "f64.const";
          break;

        // Numeric / conversion / comparison opcodes.
        default:

          if (
            (op >= 0x45 && op <= 0xc4) ||
            (op >= 0xc5 && op <= 0xd1)
          ) {
            text =
              `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
            break;
          }

          text =
            `UNKNOWN opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

          instructions.push({
            pos: opPos,
            text,
            unknown: true
          });

          return instructions;
      }

      instructions.push({
        pos: opPos,
        text,
        unknown: false
      });

    } catch (e) {

      instructions.push({
        pos: opPos,
        text: `DECODE ERROR: ${e.message}`,
        unknown: true
      });

      return instructions;
    }
  }

  return instructions;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let pos = 8;
let bodies = [];

while (pos < wasm.length) {

  const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

  const secSize = readULEB(pos);
  const size = secSize.value;
  pos = secSize.next;

  const secStart = pos;
  const secEnd = secStart + size;

  if (sectionId === 10) {

    const count = readULEB(pos);
    pos = count.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

      const bodySize = readULEB(pos);
      pos = bodySize.next;

      const bodyStart = pos;
      const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize.value;

      bodies.push({
        index: i,
        start: bodyStart,
        end: bodyEnd
      });

      pos = bodyEnd;
    }

    break;
  }

  pos = secEnd;
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceCallersToFunc106.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGET = 106;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function findCode() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    if (id === 10) {
      return { start: p, end: p + s.value };
    }

    p += s.value;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

function skipBlockType(p) {
  const x = b[p];

  if (
    x === 0x40 ||
    x === 0x7f ||
    x === 0x7e ||
    x === 0x7d ||
    x === 0x7c ||
    x === 0x7b
  ) return p + 1;

  return uleb(p).next;
}

function decode(start, end) {
  let p = start;
  const out = [];

  while (p < end) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    const x = { addr, op, name: `0x${op.toString(16)}` };

    switch (op) {
      case 0x02:
        x.name = "BLOCK";
        p = skipBlockType(p);
        break;

      case 0x03:
        x.name = "LOOP";
        p = skipBlockType(p);
        break;

      case 0x04:
        x.name = "IF";
        p = skipBlockType(p);
        break;

      case 0x05:
        x.name = "ELSE";
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        x.name = "END";
        break;

      case 0x0c: {
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.name = "BR";
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.name = "BR_IF";
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0f:
        x.name = "RETURN";
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.name = "CALL";
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x11: {
        const t = uleb(p);
        p = t.next;

        const tab = uleb(p);
        p = tab.next;

        x.name = "CALL_INDIRECT";
        x.typeidx = t.value;
        x.tableidx = tab.value;
        break;
      }

      case 0x20: {
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.name = "LOCAL.GET";
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.name = "LOCAL.SET";
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.name = "LOCAL.TEE";
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.name = "GLOBAL.GET";
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.name = "GLOBAL.SET";
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x28:
      case 0x29:
      case 0x2a:
      case 0x2b:
      case 0x2c:
      case 0x2d:
      case 0x2e:
      case 0x2f:
      case 0x30:
      case 0x31:
      case 0x32:
      case 0x33:
      case 0x34:
      case 0x35:
      case 0x36:
      case 0x37:
      case 0x38:
      case 0x39:
      case 0x3a:
      case 0x3b:
      case 0x3c:
      case 0x3d:
      case 0x3e:
      case 0x3f:
      case 0x40: {
        const a = uleb(p);
        p = a.next;

        const o = uleb(p);
        p = o.next;

        x.name = op <= 0x35 ? "LOAD" : "STORE";
        x.align = a.value;
        x.offset = o.value;
        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        const q = uleb(p);
        x.name = "I32.CONST";
        x.value = q.value;
        p = q.next;
        break;
      }

      default:
        break;
    }

    out.push(x);
  }

  return out;
}

function getFunctions() {
  const code = findCode();

  let p = code.start;
  const c = uleb(p);
  p = c.next;

  const result = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;
    const index = IMPORTED + i;

    result.push({
      index,
      start,
      end,
      size: s.value,
      ins: decode(start, end)
    });

    p = end;
  }

  return result;
}

const funcs = getFunctions();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CALLERS OF FUNC 106");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let total = 0;

for (const fn of funcs) {
  for (let i = 0; i < fn.ins.length; i++) {

      console.log(line);

    console.log("");

      const z = fn.ins[j];

      if (
        z.name === "LOCAL.GET" ||
        z.name === "LOCAL.TEE" ||
        z.name === "LOCAL.SET" ||
        z.name === "I32.CONST" ||

        }

        console.log(line);

  }
}

FILE: scripts\traceCallGraphTo87.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const ROOTS = [
  724, 91,
  85, 84, 1546, 86,
  34, 2213, 11,
  107, 98, 199, 1316, 90, 294, 83, 139, 106, 108
];

const MAX_DEPTH = 3;

function readU32Leb(buf: Buffer, pos: number) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
}

function loadBodies() {
  let p = 8;
  let codeStart = -1;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];
    const s = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
    p = s.next;

    if (id === 10) {
      codeStart = p;
      break;
    }

    p += s.value;
  }

  if (codeStart < 0)
    throw new Error("Code section not found");

  let q = codeStart;
  const count = readU32Leb(wasm, q);
  q = count.next;

  const bodies = new Map<number, Buffer>();

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const s = readU32Leb(wasm, q);
    const size = s.value;
    q = s.next;

    bodies.set(i, wasm.subarray(q, q + size));
    q += size;
  }

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = loadBodies();

function getCalls(func: number): number[] {
  const body = bodies.get(func);
  if (!body) return [];

  const calls: number[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i++) {
    if (body[i] !== 0x10) continue;

    const r = readU32Leb(body, i + 1);
    calls.push(r.value);

    i = r.next - 1;
  }

  return calls;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CALL GRAPH TO FUNC 87");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const visited = new Set<string>();

function walk(func: number, depth: number, path: number[]) {

  const key = `${func}:${depth}`;

  if (visited.has(key))
    return;

  visited.add(key);

  const calls = getCalls(func);

  if (calls.includes(87)) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("############################################################");
    console.log(">>> FOUND FUNC 87 <<<");
    console.log(
      "PATH : " +
      [...path, func, 87].map(x => `FUNC ${x}`).join(" -> ")
    );
    console.log("############################################################");
    return;
  }

  if (depth >= MAX_DEPTH)
    return;

  for (const target of calls) {
    if (target === 87)
      continue;

    walk(target, depth + 1, [...path, func]);
  }
}

for (const root of ROOTS) {
  walk(root, 0, []);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceCallIndirectIndex.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const CODE_START = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_END   = 0x00324ef5;

const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;
const TARGET_TYPE = 4;
const TARGET_SLOT = 7697;

const CANDIDATES = [
  0x002bacae,
  0x002bbfa9,
  0x002bf7e6,
  0x002ced71,
  0x0030211a,
  0x0030229c,
];

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { v, p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function sleb32(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let x = 0;

  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];

    v |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << shift);

  return { v, p };
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

type TypeInfo = {
  params: number;
  results: number;
};

const types: TypeInfo[] = [];
const functionTypes: number[] = [];

function parseSections() {

  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {

    const id = b[p++];

    const sizeR = uleb(p);
    p = sizeR.p;

    const end = p + sizeR.v;

    /*
       Type section
    */
    if (id === 1) {

      const countR = uleb(p);
      p = countR.p;

      for (let i = 0; i < countR.v; i++) {

        const form = b[p++];

        if (form !== 0x60) {
          throw new Error(
            `Unexpected type form ${hex(form)}`
          );
        }

        const paramsR = uleb(p);
        p = paramsR.p;

        const params = paramsR.v;

        p += params;

        const resultsR = uleb(p);
        p = resultsR.p;

        const results = resultsR.v;

        p += results;

        types.push({
          params,
          results,
        });
      }
    }

    /*
       Function section
       Each defined function stores its type index.
    */
    if (id === 3) {

      const countR = uleb(p);
      p = countR.p;

      for (let i = 0; i < countR.v; i++) {

        const typeR = uleb(p);
        p = typeR.p;

        functionTypes.push(typeR.v);
      }
    }

    p = end;
  }
}

parseSections();

function signature(globalFunction: number): TypeInfo | null {

  /*
     Imported function types are not present in
     functionTypes, but defined functions are.

     For this analysis all interesting calls are
     defined functions. If unavailable, return null.
  */

  if (globalFunction < IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS)
    return null;

  const ordinal =
    globalFunction - IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS;

  const typeIndex = functionTypes[ordinal];

  if (typeIndex === undefined)
    return null;

  return types[typeIndex] ?? null;
}

type Value = {
  text: string;
  constant?: number;
};

function V(text: string, constant?: number): Value {
  return constant === undefined
    ? { text }
    : { text, constant };
}

function pop(stack: Value[]): Value {
  return stack.length
    ? stack.pop()!
    : V("<unknown-stack>");
}

function dumpStack(stack: Value[]) {

  console.log("      STACK:");

  if (!stack.length) {
    console.log("        <empty>");
    return;
  }

  const from = Math.max(0, stack.length - 12);

  for (let i = from; i < stack.length; i++) {

    const v = stack[i];

    console.log(
      `        [${i}] ${v.text}` +
      (v.constant !== undefined
        ? `  [CONST=${v.constant}]`
        : "")
    );
  }
}

function memArg(p: number) {

  const alignR = uleb(p);
  const offsetR = uleb(alignR.p);

  return {
    align: alignR.v,
    offset: offsetR.v,
    p: offsetR.p,
  };
}

function decodeFunction(
  bodyStart: number,
  bodyEnd: number,
  target: number,
  globalFunction: number
) {

  let p = bodyStart;

  /*
     locals:
       first byte = local declaration vector count
    */
  const localCountR = uleb(p);
  p = localCountR.p;

  const localTypes: number[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < localCountR.v; i++) {

    const countR = uleb(p);
    p = countR.p;

    const type = b[p++];

    for (let j = 0; j < countR.v; j++)
      localTypes.push(type);
  }

  /*
     Parameter count.
  */
  const sig = signature(globalFunction);

  const paramCount = sig?.params ?? 0;

  const stack: Value[] = [];

  const locals = new Map<number, Value>();

  for (let i = 0; i < paramCount; i++) {
    locals.set(i, V(`param[${i}]`));
  }

  console.log(
    `      function body ${hex(bodyStart)} .. ${hex(bodyEnd)}`
  );

  console.log(
    `      params=${paramCount} locals=${localTypes.length}`
  );

  while (p < bodyEnd) {

    const at = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    /*
       Control
    */

    if (op === 0x00) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)} unreachable`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x01) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)} nop`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x02) {

      const bt = b[p++];

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} block type=${hex(bt)}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x03) {

      const bt = b[p++];

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} loop type=${hex(bt)}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x04) {

      const bt = b[p++];

      const cond = pop(stack);

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} if type=${hex(bt)} cond=${cond.text}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x05) {

      console.log(`${hex(at)} else`);

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0b) {

      console.log(`${hex(at)} end`);

      if (at >= target)
        break;

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c) {

      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} br ${r.v}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0d) {

      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;

      const cond = pop(stack);

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} br_if ${r.v}  cond=${cond.text}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0e) {

      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;

      for (let i = 0; i < r.v; i++) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.p;
      }

      const def = uleb(p);
      p = def.p;

      pop(stack);

      console.log(`${hex(at)} br_table`);

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0f) {

      console.log(`${hex(at)} return`);

      continue;
    }

    /*
       Calls
    */

    if (op === 0x10) {

      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;

      const fn = r.v;
      const s = signature(fn);

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} call ${fn}` +
        (s
          ? `  (${s.params}->${s.results})`
          : "")
      );

      if (s) {

        for (let i = 0; i < s.params; i++)
          pop(stack);

        for (let i = 0; i < s.results; i++)
          stack.push(
            V(`result(call ${fn})`)
          );

      } else {

        /*
           Unknown imported signature.
           Do not destroy existing symbolic stack.
        */

        console.log(
          `      imported/unknown signature`
        );
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
       call_indirect
    */

    if (op === 0x11) {

      const typeR = uleb(p);
      p = typeR.p;

      const tableR = uleb(p);
      p = tableR.p;

      console.log("");
      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} ========================================`
      );

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} CALL_INDIRECT`
      );

      console.log(
        `      type=${typeR.v} table=${tableR.v}`
      );

      if (typeR.v === TARGET_TYPE) {

        const index =
          stack.length
            ? stack[stack.length - 1]
            : V("<NO INDEX>");

        console.log(
          `      INDEX = ${index.text}`
        );

        if (index.constant !== undefined) {

          console.log(
            `      INDEX CONSTANT = ${index.constant}`
          );

          if (index.constant === TARGET_SLOT) {

            console.log("");
            console.log(
              "      *** TARGET MATCH ***"
            );
            console.log(
              `      table[${TARGET_SLOT}] = Function 5079`
            );
            console.log("");
          }
        }

        /*
           Print the five values exactly in the order
           expected immediately before indirect call.
        */

        console.log(
          "      FIVE VALUES BEFORE CALL:"
        );

        const n = stack.length;

        for (
          let i = Math.max(0, n - 5);
          i < n;
          i++
        ) {
          console.log(
            `        stack[${i}] = ${stack[i].text}`
          );
        }

        dumpStack(stack);
      }

      const indirectSig =
        types[typeR.v];

      if (indirectSig) {

        /*
           args + function index
        */

        pop(stack);

        for (let i = 0; i < indirectSig.params; i++)
          pop(stack);

        for (let i = 0; i < indirectSig.results; i++)
          stack.push(
            V("<call_indirect result>")
          );

      } else {

        /*
           For target type 4:
             4 args + index
        */

        pop(stack);

        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++)
          pop(stack);

        stack.push(
          V("<call_indirect result>")
        );
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
       Locals
    */

    if (op === 0x20) {

      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;

      const v =
        locals.get(r.v) ??
        V(`local[${r.v}]`);

      stack.push(v);

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} local.get ${r.v} -> ${v.text}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {

      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;

      const v = pop(stack);

      locals.set(r.v, v);

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} local.set ${r.v} <- ${v.text}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {

      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;

      const v =
        stack.length
          ? stack[stack.length - 1]
          : V("<unknown>");

      locals.set(r.v, v);
      stack.push(v);

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} local.tee ${r.v} <- ${v.text}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x23) {

      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;

      stack.push(
        V(`global[${r.v}]`)
      );

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} global.get ${r.v}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x24) {

      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;

      const v = pop(stack);

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} global.set ${r.v} <- ${v.text}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    /*
       Constants
    */

    if (op === 0x41) {

      const r = sleb32(p);
      p = r.p;

      stack.push(
        V(`const(${r.v})`, r.v)
      );

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} i32.const ${r.v}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    /*
       Loads
    */

    if (
      op === 0x28 ||
      op === 0x29 ||
      op === 0x2a ||
      op === 0x2b ||
      op === 0x2c ||
      op === 0x2d ||
      op === 0x2e ||
      op === 0x2f
    ) {

      const m = memArg(p);
      p = m.p;

      const addr = pop(stack);

      const width =
        op === 0x28 ? "i32.load" :
        op === 0x29 ? "i64.load" :
        op === 0x2c ? "i32.load8_s" :
        op === 0x2d ? "i32.load8_u" :
        op === 0x2e ? "i32.load16_s" :
        op === 0x2f ? "i32.load16_u" :
        `load(0x${op.toString(16)})`;

      stack.push(
        V(
          `${width}(${addr.text}+${m.offset})`
        )
      );

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} ${width} offset=${m.offset}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    /*
       Stores
    */

    if (
      op === 0x36 ||
      op === 0x37 ||
      op === 0x38 ||
      op === 0x39 ||
      op === 0x3a ||
      op === 0x3b ||
      op === 0x3c ||
      op === 0x3d
    ) {

      const m = memArg(p);
      p = m.p;

      const val = pop(stack);
      const addr = pop(stack);

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} store offset=${m.offset}` +
        `  addr=${addr.text} value=${val.text}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    /*
       Drop
    */

    if (op === 0x1a) {

      const v = pop(stack);

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} drop ${v.text}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    /*
       i32 comparisons
    */

    if (
      op === 0x45 ||
      op === 0x46 ||
      op === 0x47 ||
      op === 0x48 ||
      op === 0x49 ||
      op === 0x4a ||
      op === 0x4b ||
      op === 0x4c ||
      op === 0x4d ||
      op === 0x4e ||
      op === 0x4f ||
      op === 0x50
    ) {

      if (op === 0x45) {

        const a = pop(stack);

        stack.push(
          V(`eqz(${a.text})`)
        );

      } else {

        const right = pop(stack);
        const left = pop(stack);

        stack.push(
          V(
            `( ${left.text} cmp ${right.text} )`
          )
        );
      }

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} i32 comparison`
      );

      continue;
    }

    /*
       i32 arithmetic / bit operations
    */

    if (
      op === 0x67 ||
      op === 0x68 ||
      op === 0x69 ||
      op === 0x6a ||
      op === 0x6b ||
      op === 0x6c ||
      op === 0x6d ||
      op === 0x6e ||
      op === 0x6f ||
      op === 0x70 ||
      op === 0x71 ||
      op === 0x72 ||
      op === 0x73 ||
      op === 0x74 ||
      op === 0x75 ||
      op === 0x76 ||
      op === 0x77
    ) {

      const right = pop(stack);
      const left = pop(stack);

      let constant: number | undefined;

      if (
        left.constant !== undefined &&
        right.constant !== undefined
      ) {

        switch (op) {

          case 0x6a:
            constant =
              (left.constant + right.constant) | 0;
            break;

          case 0x6b:
            constant =
              (left.constant - right.constant) | 0;
            break;

          case 0x74:
            constant =
              (left.constant << right.constant) | 0;
            break;
        }
      }

      stack.push(
        V(
          `( ${left.text} op0x${op.toString(16)} ${right.text} )`,
          constant
        )
      );

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} i32 op=0x${op.toString(16)}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    /*
       Anything else:
       consume the instruction conservatively if possible.
    */

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} UNKNOWN op=0x${op.toString(16)}`
    );

    /*
       Do not continue blindly through unknown bytes.
       They may contain immediates.
    */

    break;
  }
}

/*
   Build code-body table.
*/

function getBodies() {

  let p = CODE_START;

  const countR = uleb(p);
  p = countR.p;

  const bodies: {
    ordinal: number;
    global: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
  }[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < countR.v; i++) {

    const sizeR = uleb(p);
    p = sizeR.p;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + sizeR.v;

    bodies.push({
      ordinal: i,
      global: i + IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS,
      start,
      end,
    });

    p = end;
  }

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = getBodies();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CALL_INDIRECT INDEX TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("");
console.log(
  `TARGET TABLE SLOT : ${TARGET_SLOT}`
);
console.log(
  `TARGET FUNCTION   : 5079`
);
console.log(
  `TARGET TYPE       : ${TARGET_TYPE}`
);
console.log("");

for (const target of CANDIDATES) {

  const body = bodies.find(
    x => target >= x.start && target < x.end
  );

  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`TARGET @ ${hex(target)}`);
  console.log("============================================================");

  if (!body) {

    console.log("FUNCTION BODY NOT FOUND");
    continue;
  }

  console.log(
    `body ordinal     = ${body.ordinal}`
  );

  console.log(
    `global function  = ${body.global}`
  );

  console.log(
    `body range       = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`
  );

  console.log("");

  decodeFunction(
    body.start,
    body.end,
    target,
    body.global
  );
}

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = [0x11aaa2, 0x129f72];

let p = 8;

function u() {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return v >>> 0;
}

let codeStart = 0;

  const size = u();
  const end = p + size;

    codeStart = p;

  p = end;
}

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET = 4275;

function uleb(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: result, next: pos };

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) return null;
  }

  return null;
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        result |= (~0 << shift);

      return {
        value: result | 0,
        next: pos
      };
    }
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function decode(pos, end) {
  const start = pos;

  if (pos >= end) return null;

  const op = buf[pos++];

  const ins = {
    start,
    end: pos,
    op,
    text: `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`
  };

  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    ins.end = a.next;

    const n =
      op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
      op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
      "local.tee";

    ins.text = `${n} local=${a.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const a = sleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    ins.end = a.next;
    ins.value = a.value;
    ins.text = `i32.const ${a.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    ins.end = a.next;
    ins.text = `call ${a.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.text =
      `call_indirect type=${a.value} table=${b.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x2d
  ) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.isLoad = true;

    const name =
      op === 0x28 ? "i32.load" :
      op === 0x29 ? "i64.load" :
      "i32.load8_u";

    ins.text =
      `${name} align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x36) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.isStore = true;

    ins.text =
      `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x37) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.isStore = true;

    ins.text =
      `i64.store align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x3a) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.isStore = true;

    ins.text =
      `i32.store8 align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  const simple = {
    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor",
    0x74: "i32.shl",
    0x75: "i32.shr_s",
    0x76: "i32.shr_u",
    0x67: "i32.clz",
    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",
    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u"
  };

  if (simple[op]) {
    ins.text = simple[op];
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    if (pos >= end) return null;

    const bt = buf[pos++];

    ins.end = pos;

    ins.text =
      op === 0x02 ? `block type=0x${bt.toString(16)}` :
      op === 0x03 ? `loop type=0x${bt.toString(16)}` :
      `if type=0x${bt.toString(16)}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x05) {
    ins.text = "else";
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x0b) {
    ins.text = "end";
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    ins.end = a.next;
    ins.text =
      `${op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if"} ${a.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  return ins;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Find Code Section
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < buf.length) {
  const sectionId = buf[p++];

  const s = uleb(p);
  if (!s) throw new Error("Bad section");

  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = start + s.value;

  if (sectionId === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
  throw new Error("Code section not found");

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Scan all functions
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

let q = codeStart;

const countInfo = uleb(q);
q = countInfo.next;

const bodyCount = countInfo.value;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE CONSTANT 4275");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("BODY COUNT:", bodyCount);
console.log("TARGET    :", TARGET);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let hits = 0;

for (let func = 0; func < bodyCount; func++) {

  const sizeInfo = uleb(q);

  if (!sizeInfo)
    throw new Error("Bad body size");

  const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
  const bodyStart = sizeInfo.next;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

  let x = bodyStart;

  const localInfo = uleb(x);

  if (!localInfo) {
    q = bodyEnd;
    continue;
  }

  const groups = localInfo.value;
  x = localInfo.next;

  let localDeclOK = true;

  for (let g = 0; g < groups; g++) {
    const n = uleb(x);

    if (!n) {
      localDeclOK = false;
      break;
    }

    x = n.next + 1;
  }

  if (!localDeclOK) {
    q = bodyEnd;
    continue;
  }

  const ins = [];

  while (x < bodyEnd) {
    const z = decode(x, bodyEnd);

    if (!z)
      break;

    ins.push(z);
    x = z.end;
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {

    const z = ins[i];

    if (
      z.text === `i32.const ${TARGET}`
    ) {

      hits++;

      console.log("");
      console.log("============================================================");
      console.log("HIT #", hits);
      console.log("FUNC :", func);
      console.log("CONST:", hex(z.start));
      console.log("============================================================");

      const from = Math.max(0, i - 12);
      const to = Math.min(ins.length, i + 13);

      for (let k = from; k < to; k++) {

        const a = ins[k];

        let mark = "  ";

        if (k === i)
          mark = ">>";

        console.log(
          mark,
          hex(a.start),
          a.text
        );
      }

      /*
       * Detect immediate store after constant.
       */
      for (
        let k = i + 1;
        k < Math.min(ins.length, i + 12);
        k++
      ) {
        if (ins[k].isStore) {

          console.log("");
          console.log(
            "NEAR STORE:",
            hex(ins[k].start),
            ins[k].text
          );

          break;
        }

        if (
          ins[k].text === "call_indirect type=3 table=0" ||
          ins[k].text === "call_indirect type=7 table=0"
        ) {
          console.log("");
          console.log(
            "NEAR INDIRECT:",
            hex(ins[k].start),
            ins[k].text
          );

          break;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

FILE: scripts\traceConstantDispatchRoots.ts

import fs from "fs";

const FILE =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

const TARGET_INDEX = new Set([2053, 2101, 2612]);

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: p };
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

type Ins = {
    pos: number;
    op: number;
    imm?: number;
    offset?: number;
    text: string;
};

function decode(start: number, end: number): Ins[] {
    const out: Ins[] = [];
    let p = start;

    while (p < end) {
        const at = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;

            const n =
                op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
                op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
                "local.tee";

            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op,
                imm: r.value,
                text: `${n} ${r.value}`
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;

            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op,
                imm: r.value,
                text:
                    `${op === 0x23 ? "global.get" : "global.set"} ${r.value}`
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;

            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op,
                imm: r.value,
                text: `i32.const ${r.value}`
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;

            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op,
                imm: r.value,
                text: `call ${r.value}`
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x11) {
            const a = uleb(p);
            p = a.next;

            const b = uleb(p);
            p = b.next;

            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op,
                imm: a.value,
                text:
                    `call_indirect type=${a.value} table=${b.value}`
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (
            op >= 0x28 &&
            op <= 0x35
        ) {
            const align = wasm[p++];

            const r = uleb(p);
            p = r.next;

            const names: Record<number, string> = {
                0x28: "i32.load",
                0x29: "i64.load",
                0x2a: "f32.load",
                0x2b: "f64.load",
                0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
                0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
                0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
                0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
                0x30: "i64.load8_s",
                0x31: "i64.load8_u",
                0x32: "i64.load16_s",
                0x33: "i64.load16_u",
                0x34: "i64.load32_s",
                0x35: "i64.load32_u"
            };

            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op,
                offset: r.value,
                text:
                    `${names[op]} align=${align} offset=${r.value}`
            });

            continue;
        }

        const names: Record<number, string> = {
            0x45: "i32.eqz",
            0x46: "i32.eq",
            0x47: "i32.ne",
            0x48: "i32.lt_s",
            0x49: "i32.lt_u",
            0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
            0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
            0x4c: "i32.le_s",
            0x4d: "i32.le_u",
            0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
            0x4f: "i32.ge_u",
            0x6a: "i32.add",
            0x6b: "i32.sub",
            0x6c: "i32.mul",
            0x71: "i32.and",
            0x72: "i32.or",
            0x73: "i32.xor",
            0x74: "i32.shl",
            0x75: "i32.shr_s",
            0x76: "i32.shr_u",
            0x77: "i32.rotl",
            0x78: "i32.rotr",
            0x1a: "drop",
            0x1b: "select",
            0x0f: "return",
            0x0b: "end"
        };

        out.push({
            pos: at,
            op,
            text: names[op] ?? `op=0x${op.toString(16)}`
        });
    }

    return out;
}

/*
 * Locate code section.
 */
let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const r = uleb(p);
    const start = r.next;
    const end = start + r.value;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (!codeStart)
    throw new Error("Code section not found");

/*
 * Decode function bodies.
 */
const bodies: {
    fn: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
    ins: Ins[];
}[] = [];

p = codeStart;

const count = uleb(p);
p = count.next;

const IMPORTS = 83;

for (let body = 0; body < count.value; body++) {

    const size = uleb(p);
    const bodyStart = size.next;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + size.value;

    let x = bodyStart;

    const groups = uleb(x);
    x = groups.next;

    for (let g = 0; g < groups.value; g++) {
        const n = uleb(x);
        x = n.next;

        x += 1;
    }

    bodies.push({
        fn: IMPORTS + body,
        start: x,
        end: bodyEnd,
        ins: decode(x, bodyEnd)
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" CONSTANT DISPATCH ROOT TRACE");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Functions : ${bodies.length}`);
console.log();

let roots = 0;
let indirects = 0;

/*
 * Pattern:
 *
 *     i32.const ADDRESS
 *     ...
 *     i32.load
 *     ...
 *     call_indirect type=4
 *
 * We track constant-derived stack expressions.
 */
for (const fn of bodies) {

    const stack: string[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < fn.ins.length; i++) {

        const ins = fn.ins[i];

        if (ins.op === 0x20) {
            stack.push(
                ins.imm! < 2
                    ? `param[${ins.imm}]`
                    : `local[${ins.imm}]`
            );
            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x41) {
            stack.push(`const(${ins.imm})`);
            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x23) {
            stack.push(`global[${ins.imm}]`);
            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x28) {

            const base =
                stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";

            stack.push(
                `MEM[${base}+${ins.offset}]`
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x2d) {

            const base =
                stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";

            stack.push(
                `MEM8[${base}+${ins.offset}]`
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (
            ins.op === 0x6a ||
            ins.op === 0x6b ||
            ins.op === 0x6c ||
            ins.op === 0x71 ||
            ins.op === 0x72 ||
            ins.op === 0x73
        ) {

            const b =
                stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";

            const a =
                stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";

            const symbol =
                ins.op === 0x6a ? "+" :
                ins.op === 0x6b ? "-" :
                ins.op === 0x6c ? "*" :
                ins.op === 0x71 ? "&" :
                ins.op === 0x72 ? "|" :
                "^";

            stack.push(
                `(${a}${symbol}${b})`
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x10) {

            stack.push(
                `RESULT(call ${ins.imm})`
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x22) {

            const v =
                stack.length
                    ? stack[stack.length - 1]
                    : "UNKNOWN";

            stack.push(
                `local[${ins.imm}]=${v}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (ins.op === 0x21) {

            if (stack.length)
                stack.pop();

            continue;
        }

        if (
            ins.op === 0x11 &&
            ins.imm === 4
        ) {

            indirects++;

            const index =
                stack.length
                    ? stack[stack.length - 1]
                    : "UNKNOWN";

            /*
             * Only constant-rooted expressions.
             */
            if (
                index.includes("const(")
            ) {

                roots++;

                const hit =
                    [...TARGET_INDEX]
                        .filter(v =>
                            index.includes(
                                `const(${v})`
                            )
                        );

                console.log(
                    "------------------------------------------------------------"
                );

                console.log(
                    `ROOT DISPATCH @ ${hex(ins.pos)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `Function : ${fn.fn}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `Index    : ${index}`
                );

                if (hit.length) {
                    console.log(
                        `TARGET   : ${hit.join(", ")}`
                    );
                }

                /*
                 * Show up to 16 instructions before
                 * the indirect call.
                 */
                const from =
                    Math.max(0, i - 16);

                for (
                    let j = from;
                    j <= i;
                    j++
                ) {
                    console.log(
                        `  ${hex(fn.ins[j].pos)}  ${fn.ins[j].text}`
                    );
                }

                console.log();
            }

            /*
             * Conservative reset.
             */
            stack.length = 0;

            continue;
        }

        if (
            ins.op === 0x1a ||
            ins.op === 0x0f
        ) {
            if (stack.length)
                stack.pop();
        }
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(
    `type=4 indirect calls : ${indirects}`
);

console.log(
    `constant-rooted roots : ${roots}`
);

console.log();

console.log("Target table indices:");
console.log("  2053 -> 8952");
console.log("  2101 -> 8927");
console.log("  2612 -> 8637");

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceCoreCalls.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = new Set([202, 1151, 1152]);

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(
                "ULEB too long at 0x" + pos.toString(16)
            );
        }
    }

    throw new Error(
        "Unexpected EOF at 0x" + pos.toString(16)
    );
}

function parseSections() {
    const sections = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const sizeR = readULEB(p);
        const size = sizeR.value;
        p = sizeR.next;

        sections.push({
            id,
            start: p,
            end: p + size
        });

        p += size;
    }

    return sections;
}

function parseFunctionSection(section) {
    const types = [];

    let p = section.start;

    const countR = readULEB(p);
    const count = countR.value;
    p = countR.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const r = readULEB(p);

        types.push(r.value);

        p = r.next;
    }

    return types;
}

function parseCodeSection(section, functionTypes) {
    const bodies = [];

    let p = section.start;

    const countR = readULEB(p);
    const count = countR.value;
    p = countR.next;

    const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

        const sizeR = readULEB(p);
        const bodySize = sizeR.value;
        p = sizeR.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

        const localsR = readULEB(p);
        const localGroups = localsR.value;
        p = localsR.next;

        for (let g = 0; g < localGroups; g++) {
            const nR = readULEB(p);
            p = nR.next;

            p++; // value type
        }

        bodies.push({
            func: IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + i,
            typeIndex: functionTypes[i],
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            codeStart: p,
            codeEnd: bodyEnd
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function hex(v) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function decodeTargetFunction(body) {

    let p = body.codeStart;

    console.log("");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(`FUNC ${body.func}`);
    console.log(
        `BODY : ${hex(body.bodyStart)} .. ${hex(body.bodyEnd)}`
    );
    console.log(`TYPE : ${body.typeIndex}`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    while (p < body.codeEnd) {

        const offset = p;
        const op = buf[p++];

        let text = null;

        switch (op) {

            case 0x0b:
                text = "end";
                break;

            case 0x10: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                text = `call ${r.value}`;

                if (TARGETS.has(r.value)) {
                    text += "  <== TARGET";
                }

                break;
            }

            case 0x11: {
                const typeR = readULEB(p);
                p = typeR.next;

                const tableR = readULEB(p);
                p = tableR.next;

                text =
                    `call_indirect type=${typeR.value} table=${tableR.value}`;

                break;
            }

            case 0x20: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x21: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x22: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x28: {
                const align = buf[p++];

                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                text =
                    `i32.load align=${align} offset=${r.value}`;

                break;
            }

            case 0x29: {
                const align = buf[p++];

                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                text =
                    `i64.load align=${align} offset=${r.value}`;

                break;
            }

            case 0x2d: {
                const align = buf[p++];

                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                text =
                    `i32.load8_u align=${align} offset=${r.value}`;

                break;
            }

            case 0x36: {
                const align = buf[p++];

                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                text =
                    `i32.store align=${align} offset=${r.value}`;

                break;
            }

            case 0x37: {
                const align = buf[p++];

                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                text =
                    `i64.store align=${align} offset=${r.value}`;

                break;
            }

            case 0x41: {
                let q = p;
                let bytes = [];

                while (q < body.codeEnd) {
                    const b = buf[q++];
                    bytes.push(b);

                    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
                        break;
                    }

                    if (bytes.length > 5) {
                        break;
                    }
                }

                p = q;

                text =
                    `i32.const [${bytes
                        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
                        .join(" ")}]`;

                break;
            }

            case 0x42: {
                let q = p;
                let bytes = [];

                while (q < body.codeEnd) {
                    const b = buf[q++];
                    bytes.push(b);

                    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
                        break;
                    }

                    if (bytes.length > 10) {
                        break;
                    }
                }

                p = q;

                text =
                    `i64.const [${bytes
                        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
                        .join(" ")}]`;

                break;
            }

            case 0x46:
                text = "i32.eq";
                break;

            case 0x6a:
                text = "i32.add";
                break;

            case 0x6b:
                text = "i32.sub";
                break;

            case 0x7c:
                text = "i64.add";
                break;

            case 0x7d:
                text = "i64.sub";
                break;

            case 0x1b:
                text = "select";
                break;

            case 0x45:
                text = "i32.eqz";
                break;

            default:
                text =
                    `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
                break;
        }

        console.log(
            `${hex(offset)}  ${text}`
        );
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CORE CALL TARGET TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM SIZE : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);

const sections = parseSections();

const funcSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 3);
const codeSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 10);

if (!funcSection || !codeSection) {
    throw new Error("Required WASM sections not found.");
}

const functionTypes = parseFunctionSection(funcSection);

const bodies =
    parseCodeSection(codeSection, functionTypes);

const selected =
    bodies.filter(b => TARGETS.has(b.func));

console.log("");
console.log(
    `TARGET FUNCTIONS : ${[...TARGETS].join(", ")}`
);

console.log(
    `FOUND : ${selected.length}`
);

for (const body of selected) {
    decodeTargetFunction(body);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");
