import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const TARGET_PTON_AMOUNT = 500000000n;

const OP_JETTON_TRANSFER = 0x0f8a7ea5;

function opHex(op: number | null): string {
    if (op === null) return 'UNKNOWN';
    return '0x' + op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function ton(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;
    const frac = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return frac ? `${whole}.${frac}` : whole.toString();
}

function getOpcode(body: any): number | null {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return null;
        }

        return s.loadUint(32);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

function separator(title: string) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(` ${title}`);
    console.log('============================================================');
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    separator('CURRENT EXACT pTON LEG v2.2');

    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON WALLET:');
    console.log(PTON_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET pTON AMOUNT RAW:');
    console.log(TARGET_PTON_AMOUNT.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET pTON AMOUNT:');
    console.log(ton(TARGET_PTON_AMOUNT));

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint:
            'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(
        PTON_WALLET,
        {
            limit: 20,
        }
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(`TRANSACTIONS FETCHED: ${txs.length}`);

    let found = 0;

    for (const tx of txs) {

        if (!tx.inMessage) {
            continue;
        }

        const body = tx.inMessage.body;
        const op = getOpcode(body);

        if (op !== OP_JETTON_TRANSFER) {
            continue;
        }

        try {

            const s = body.beginParse();

            s.loadUint(32);

            const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

            const amount = s.loadCoins();

            if (amount !== TARGET_PTON_AMOUNT) {
                continue;
            }

            found++;

            separator('MATCHING CURRENT pTON TRANSFER');

            console.log('LT:');
            console.log(tx.lt.toString());

            console.log('HASH:');
            console.log(
                tx.hash().toString('base64')
            );

            console.log('TIME:');
            console.log(
                new Date(tx.now * 1000).toISOString()
            );

            console.log('OPCODE:');
            console.log(opHex(op));

            console.log('QUERY ID:');
            console.log(queryId.toString());

            console.log('');
            console.log('pTON RAW:');
            console.log(amount.toString());

            console.log('');
            console.log('pTON:');
            console.log(ton(amount));

            if (tx.inMessage.info.type === 'internal') {

                console.log('');
                console.log('SOURCE:');
                console.log(
                    tx.inMessage.info.src.toString()
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log('DESTINATION:');
                console.log(
                    tx.inMessage.info.dest.toString()
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log('TON VALUE:');
                console.log(
                    ton(tx.inMessage.info.value.coins)
                );
            }

            console.log('');
            console.log('FULL pTON TRANSFER BOC:');
            console.log(
                body.toBoc().toString('base64')
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('REMAINING BITS AFTER AMOUNT:');
            console.log(s.remainingBits);

            console.log('');
            console.log('REMAINING REFS:');
            console.log(s.remainingRefs);

            /*
             * Decode standard Jetton transfer.
             */

            const destination =
                s.loadAddress();

            const responseDestination =
                s.loadAddress();

            console.log('');
            console.log('DESTINATION FIELD:');
            console.log(destination.toString());

            console.log('');
            console.log('RESPONSE DESTINATION:');
            console.log(responseDestination.toString());

            const customPayload =
                s.loadMaybeRef();

            console.log('');
            console.log('CUSTOM PAYLOAD:');

            if (customPayload) {

                console.log('PRESENT');

                console.log(
                    customPayload
                        .toBoc()
                        .toString('base64')
                );

            } else {

                console.log('NONE');
            }

            const forwardTonAmount =
                s.loadCoins();

            console.log('');
            console.log('FORWARD TON AMOUNT:');
            console.log(
                `${ton(forwardTonAmount)} TON`
            );

            const forwardIsRef =
                s.loadBit();

            console.log('');
            console.log('FORWARD PAYLOAD TYPE:');

            let forwardPayload;

            if (forwardIsRef) {

                console.log('REFERENCE');

                forwardPayload =
                    s.loadRef();

            } else {

                console.log('INLINE');

                forwardPayload =
                    s.asCell();
            }

            console.log('');
            console.log('FORWARD PAYLOAD BOC:');
            console.log(
                forwardPayload
                    .toBoc()
                    .toString('base64')
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('FORWARD PAYLOAD BITS:');
            console.log(
                forwardPayload.beginParse().remainingBits
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('FORWARD PAYLOAD REFS:');
            console.log(
                forwardPayload.refs.length
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('FORWARD PAYLOAD OPCODE:');

            console.log(
                opHex(
                    getOpcode(forwardPayload)
                )
            );

            separator('END MATCH');

        } catch (e) {

            console.log('');
            console.log('DECODE ERROR:');
            console.log(String(e));
        }
    }

    separator('RESULT');

    console.log(
        `MATCHES FOUND: ${found}`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
}

import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const PTON_PAYLOAD_WALLET = Address.parse(
    '0:9220C181A6CFEACD11B7B8F62138DF1BB9CC82B6ED2661D2F5FAEE204B3EFB20'
);

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const TARGET_DEADLINE = 1790588068n;
const TARGET_MIN_LP = 300329052918n;

const OP_PTON_TRANSFER = 0x01f3835d;
const OP_JETTON_TRANSFER = 0x0f8a7ea5;
const OP_JETTON_NOTIFY = 0x7362d09c;
const OP_PROVIDE_LP = 0x37c096df;

function opHex(op: number | null): string {
    if (op === null) return 'UNKNOWN';
    return '0x' + op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function getOpcode(body: any): number | null {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return null;
        }

        return s.loadUint(32);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

function ton(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;

    const frac = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return frac
        ? `${whole}.${frac}`
        : whole.toString();
}

function printSeparator(title: string) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(` ${title}`);
    console.log('============================================================');
}

async function inspectAddress(
    client: TonClient,
    label: string,
    address: Address
) {
    printSeparator(label);

    console.log('');
    console.log('ADDRESS:');
    console.log(address.toString());

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(address, {
        limit: 60,
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log(`TRANSACTIONS FETCHED: ${txs.length}`);

    let index = 0;

    for (const tx of txs) {
        const inMessage = tx.inMessage;

        if (!inMessage) {
            index++;
            continue;
        }

        const op = getOpcode(inMessage.body);
        const lt = BigInt(tx.lt);

        let relevant =
            op === OP_PTON_TRANSFER ||
            op === OP_JETTON_TRANSFER ||
            op === OP_JETTON_NOTIFY ||
            op === OP_PROVIDE_LP;

        if (
            lt >= 99491281000000n &&
            lt <= 99491281000020n
        ) {
            relevant = true;
        }

        if (!relevant) {
            index++;
            continue;
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log(`---------------- TX ${index} ----------------`);

        console.log('LT:');
        console.log(tx.lt.toString());

        console.log('HASH:');
        console.log(tx.hash().toString('base64'));

        console.log('TIME:');
        console.log(
            new Date(tx.now * 1000).toISOString()
        );

        console.log('OPCODE:');
        console.log(opHex(op));

        if (inMessage.info.type === 'internal') {
            console.log('SOURCE:');
            console.log(inMessage.info.src.toString());

            console.log('DESTINATION:');
            console.log(inMessage.info.dest.toString());

            console.log('VALUE:');
            console.log(
                `${inMessage.info.value.coins} nanoTON`
            );

            console.log('VALUE TON:');
            console.log(
                ton(inMessage.info.value.coins)
            );
        }

        if (op === OP_PTON_TRANSFER) {
            try {
                const s = inMessage.body.beginParse();

                s.loadUint(32);

                const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

                console.log('QUERY ID:');
                console.log(queryId.toString());

                if (s.remainingBits >= 16) {
                    const tonAmount = s.loadCoins();

                    console.log('PTON TON AMOUNT:');
                    console.log(
                        `${tonAmount} nanoTON`
                    );

                    console.log('PTON AMOUNT:');
                    console.log(
                        ton(tonAmount)
                    );
                }

                console.log('REMAINING BITS:');
                console.log(s.remainingBits);

                console.log('REMAINING REFS:');
                console.log(s.remainingRefs);

            } catch (e) {
                console.log('PTON DECODE ERROR:');
                console.log(String(e));
            }
        }

        if (op === OP_JETTON_TRANSFER) {
            try {
                const s = inMessage.body.beginParse();

                s.loadUint(32);

                const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

                console.log('QUERY ID:');
                console.log(queryId.toString());

                if (s.remainingBits >= 16) {
                    const amount = s.loadCoins();

                    console.log('JETTON AMOUNT RAW:');
                    console.log(amount.toString());

                    console.log('JETTON AMOUNT 9 DECIMAL:');
                    console.log(ton(amount));
                }

            } catch (e) {
                console.log('JETTON DECODE ERROR:');
                console.log(String(e));
            }
        }

        if (
            lt >= 99491281000000n &&
            lt <= 99491281000020n
        ) {
            console.log('BODY BOC:');
            console.log(
                inMessage.body
                    .toBoc()
                    .toString('base64')
            );
        }

        index++;
    }
}

FILE: scripts\traceCurrentV22Messages.ts

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const TONCENTER =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v3';

const POOL =
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf';

const TARGET_LT = '99491281000009';

const DELAY_MS = 1300;

function sleep(ms: number) {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

async function getJson(
    url: string,
    label: string
): Promise<any> {

    for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 5; attempt++) {

        const response = await fetch(url);

        if (response.ok) {
            return await response.json();
        }

        if (response.status === 429) {
            const wait = DELAY_MS * attempt;

            console.log(
                `429 for ${label}; waiting ${wait} ms...`
            );

            await sleep(wait);
            continue;
        }

        throw new Error(
            `${label}: HTTP ${response.status} ${response.statusText}`
        );
    }

    throw new Error(
        `${label}: too many 429 responses`
    );
}

function txUrlByMessage(
    msgHash: string,
    direction: 'in' | 'out'
): string {

    const params = new URLSearchParams();

    params.set('msg_hash', msgHash);
    params.set('direction', direction);
    params.set('limit', '20');
    params.set('offset', '0');

    return `${TONCENTER}/transactionsByMessage?${params}`;
}

function normalizeBoc(
    value: any
): string | null {

    if (typeof value !== 'string') {
        return null;
    }

    return value.trim();
}

function printMessage(
    msg: any,
    label: string
) {

    console.log('');
    console.log(`----- ${label} -----`);

    console.log(
        'hash:',
        msg.hash ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'source:',
        msg.source ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'destination:',
        msg.destination ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'value:',
        msg.value ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'opcode:',
        msg.opcode ??
        msg.decoded_opcode ??
        'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'decoded_opcode:',
        msg.decoded_opcode ?? 'N/A'
    );

    const boc =
        normalizeBoc(
            msg.message_content?.body ??
            msg.body ??
            msg.boc
        );

    console.log(
        'possible BOC:',
        boc ?? 'NOT AVAILABLE'
    );
}

function printTx(
    tx: any,
    label: string
) {

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `================================================`
    );

    console.log(label);

    console.log(
        'account:',
        tx.account ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'tx hash:',
        tx.hash ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'lt:',
        tx.lt ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'trace_id:',
        tx.trace_id ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'utime:',
        tx.utime ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'total_fees:',
        tx.total_fees ?? 'N/A'
    );

    if (tx.in_msg) {
        printMessage(
            tx.in_msg,
            'IN MESSAGE'
        );
    }

    if (Array.isArray(tx.out_msgs)) {

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'OUT MESSAGE COUNT:',
            tx.out_msgs.length
        );

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < tx.out_msgs.length;
            i++
        ) {

            printMessage(
                tx.out_msgs[i],
                `OUT MESSAGE #${i + 1}`
            );
        }
    }
}

async function lookupMessage(
    msgHash: string,
    direction: 'in' | 'out',
    label: string
) {

    const url =
        txUrlByMessage(
            msgHash,
            direction
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `Looking up ${label}`
    );

    console.log(
        'URL:',
        url
    );

    const data =
        await getJson(
            url,
            label
        );

    const txs =
        data.transactions ?? [];

    console.log(
        'Matches:',
        txs.length
    );

    for (const tx of txs) {
        printTx(
            tx,
            `${label} TRANSACTION`
        );
    }

    return txs;
}

export async function run(
    provider: NetworkProvider
) {

    void provider;

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' STON.fi v2.2 CURRENT TX MESSAGE TRACE'
    );
    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT'
    );
    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Pool:',
        POOL
    );

    console.log(
        'Target LT:',
        TARGET_LT
    );

    console.log('');

    /*
     * First locate the exact Pool transaction.
     */

    const params =
        new URLSearchParams();

    params.set(
        'account',
        Address.parse(POOL).toRawString()
    );

    params.set(
        'limit',
        '20'
    );

    params.set(
        'offset',
        '0'
    );

    const txUrl =
        `${TONCENTER}/transactions?${params}`;

    const txData =
        await getJson(
            txUrl,
            'POOL TRANSACTIONS'
        );

    const txs =
        txData.transactions ?? [];

    const target =
        txs.find(
            (tx: any) =>
                String(tx.lt) === TARGET_LT
        );

    if (!target) {
        throw new Error(
            `Target transaction LT ${TARGET_LT} not found`
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'TARGET TRANSACTION FOUND'
    );

    printTx(
        target,
        'POOL TARGET TRANSACTION'
    );

    /*
     * Trace the exact input message.
     */

    if (
        target.in_msg &&
        target.in_msg.hash
    ) {

        await sleep(DELAY_MS);

        await lookupMessage(
            target.in_msg.hash,
            'out',
            'POOL INPUT MESSAGE / PREVIOUS TRANSACTION'
        );
    }

    /*
     * Trace every exact output message.
     */

    if (
        Array.isArray(target.out_msgs)
    ) {

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < target.out_msgs.length;
            i++
        ) {

            const msg =
                target.out_msgs[i];

            if (!msg.hash) {
                continue;
            }

            await sleep(DELAY_MS);

            await lookupMessage(
                msg.hash,
                'in',
                `POOL OUTPUT MESSAGE #${i + 1} / NEXT TRANSACTION`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' TRACE COMPLETE'
    );
    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY'
    );
    console.log(
        ' NO SEND / TRANSFER / BROADCAST'
    );
    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceDataSegments19534C.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x0019534c;
const VALUE = 0x000012b4;

function hex(v: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readUleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= -1 << shift;
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: pos,
  };
}

function findSection(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sid = wasm[p++];

    const size = readUleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    if (sid === id) {
      return {
        start,
        end,
        size: size.value,
      };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

function readInitExpr(pos: number) {
  const start = pos;
  const op = wasm[pos++];

  /*
   * Expected:
   *   i32.const <value>
   *   end
   */

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = readSleb(pos);
    pos = x.next;

    const end = wasm[pos++];

    return {
      start,
      next: pos,
      kind: "i32.const",
      value: x.value >>> 0,
      end,
    };
  }

  /*
   * global.get
   */
  if (op === 0x23) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);
    pos = x.next;

    const end = wasm[pos++];

    return {
      start,
      next: pos,
      kind: "global.get",
      value: x.value >>> 0,
      end,
    };
  }

  /*
   * Unknown init expression.
   * Scan until END.
   */
  while (pos < wasm.length) {
    if (wasm[pos++] === 0x0b) {
      break;
    }
  }

  return {
    start,
    next: pos,
    kind: "unknown",
    value: 0,
    end: 0,
  };
}

console.log("TRACE DATA SEGMENTS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET MEMORY = ${hex(TARGET)}`);
console.log(`TARGET VALUE  = ${hex(VALUE)}`);
console.log("============================================================");

const data = findSection(11);

if (!data) {
  console.log("DATA SECTION NOT FOUND");
  process.exit(0);
}

let p = data.start;

const count = readUleb(p);
p = count.next;

console.log(`DATA SEGMENT COUNT = ${count.value}`);
console.log("");

let targetSegments = 0;
let valueSegments = 0;

for (let seg = 0; seg < count.value; seg++) {
  const segStart = p;

  const flags = readUleb(p);
  p = flags.next;

  /*
   * MVP active segment:
   *
   * flags = 0
   * memory index implicit 0
   * offset expression follows
   *
   * flags = 2
   * memory index follows
   * offset expression follows
   *
   * passive = 1
   */
  let memoryIndex = 0;
  let offsetExpr: any = null;

  if (flags.value === 0) {
    offsetExpr = readInitExpr(p);
    p = offsetExpr.next;
  } else if (flags.value === 2) {
    const mi = readUleb(p);
    memoryIndex = mi.value;
    p = mi.next;

    offsetExpr = readInitExpr(p);
    p = offsetExpr.next;
  } else if (flags.value === 1) {
    /*
     * Passive data segment:
     * no runtime linear-memory initialization offset.
     */
    const size = readUleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = p + size.value;

    let foundValue = false;

    for (let q = payloadStart; q + 4 <= payloadEnd; q++) {
      const v =
        wasm[q] |
        (wasm[q + 1] << 8) |
        (wasm[q + 2] << 16) |
        (wasm[q + 3] << 24);

      if ((v >>> 0) === VALUE) {
        foundValue = true;
        console.log(
          `PASSIVE SEGMENT #${seg} ` +
          `contains 0x12B4 @ file ${hex(q)}`
        );
        valueSegments++;
      }
    }

    p = payloadEnd;

    continue;
  } else {
    console.log(
      `SEGMENT #${seg}: unsupported flags=${flags.value}`
    );

    /*
     * Cannot safely continue without knowing format.
     */
    break;
  }

  const sizeInfo = readUleb(p);
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const payloadStart = p;
  const payloadEnd = p + sizeInfo.value;

  /*
   * Search payload for 0x12B4.
   */
  const hits: number[] = [];

  for (let q = payloadStart; q + 4 <= payloadEnd; q++) {
    const v =
      wasm[q] |
      (wasm[q + 1] << 8) |
      (wasm[q + 2] << 16) |
      (wasm[q + 3] << 24);

    if ((v >>> 0) === VALUE) {
      hits.push(q);
    }
  }

  /*
   * Search whether the segment itself starts at TARGET.
   */
  let exactTarget = false;

  if (
    offsetExpr &&
    offsetExpr.kind === "i32.const" &&
    offsetExpr.value === TARGET
  ) {
    exactTarget = true;
  }

  if (hits.length > 0 || exactTarget) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`DATA SEGMENT #${seg}`);
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`SEGMENT FLAGS = ${flags.value}`);
    console.log(`MEMORY INDEX  = ${memoryIndex}`);
    console.log(
      `OFFSET EXPR  = ${offsetExpr?.kind ?? "unknown"}`
    );

    if (offsetExpr?.value !== undefined) {
      console.log(
        `OFFSET VALUE  = ${hex(offsetExpr.value)}`
      );
    }

    console.log(`PAYLOAD FILE  = ${hex(payloadStart)}`);
    console.log(`PAYLOAD SIZE  = ${sizeInfo.value}`);

    if (exactTarget) {
      console.log("");
      console.log(">>> EXACT TARGET INITIALIZATION <<<");
      console.log(
        `RUNTIME ADDRESS = ${hex(TARGET)}`
      );
      targetSegments++;
    }

    if (hits.length > 0) {
      console.log("");
      console.log("0x12B4 PAYLOAD HITS:");

      for (const q of hits) {
        const runtime =
          offsetExpr?.kind === "i32.const"
            ? (
                offsetExpr.value +
                (q - payloadStart)
              ) >>> 0
            : null;

        console.log(
          `FILE ${hex(q)}`
          +
          (runtime !== null
            ? ` -> RUNTIME ${hex(runtime)}`
            : "")
        );

        if (runtime === TARGET) {
          console.log(
            ">>> 0x12B4 INITIALIZES TARGET 0x19534C <<<"
          );

          targetSegments++;
        }

        valueSegments++;
      }
    }
  }

  p = payloadEnd;

  /*
   * Segment boundary sanity check.
   */
  if (p !== payloadEnd) {
    console.log(
      `WARNING segment ${seg} parse mismatch`
    );
    break;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(
  `EXACT TARGET SEGMENTS = ${targetSegments}`
);
console.log(
  `0x12B4 DATA PAYLOAD HITS = ${valueSegments}`
);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

FILE: scripts\traceDirect656Args.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 656;

const CALLS = [
  { func: 473,  addr: 0x00020087 },
  { func: 599,  addr: 0x000284fb },
  { func: 1249, addr: 0x00057842 },
  { func: 1601, addr: 0x00071954 },
  { func: 2161, addr: 0x0009b0b8 },
  { func: 2161, addr: 0x0009b0fe },
  { func: 3490, addr: 0x0010da53 },
  { func: 6665, addr: 0x0022fa9e },
  { func: 7020, addr: 0x0023d95e },
];

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u32(p: number): number {
  return (
    wasm[p] |
    (wasm[p + 1] << 8) |
    (wasm[p + 2] << 16) |
    (wasm[p + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function findSection(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sid = wasm[p++];
    const s = readULEB(p);
    const start = s.next;
    const end = start + s.value;

    if (sid === id) {
      return {
        id: sid,
        start,
        end,
        size: s.value,
      };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

/*
 * Decode only the function section.
 *
 * Section 3 contains type indices for defined functions.
 * Import functions are excluded from this vector.
 */
function getDefinedFunctionTypes(): number[] {
  const sec = findSection(3);

  if (!sec) {
    throw new Error("FUNCTION SECTION NOT FOUND");
  }

  let p = sec.start;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  const types: number[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const t = readULEB(p);
    p = t.next;
    types.push(t.value);
  }

  return types;
}

/*
 * Parse code section and return exact body ranges
 * indexed by defined-function ordinal.
 */
function getDefinedFunctionBodies() {
  const sec = findSection(10);

  if (!sec) {
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
  }

  let p = sec.start;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  const bodies: {
    bodyStart: number;
    bodyEnd: number;
    codeStart: number;
    codeEnd: number;
  }[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const size = readULEB(p);
    p = size.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + size.value;

    const localCount = readULEB(p);
    p = localCount.next;

    for (let j = 0; j < localCount.value; j++) {
      const n = readULEB(p);
      p = n.next;

      // value type
      p++;
    }

    const codeStart = p;

    bodies.push({
      bodyStart,
      bodyEnd,
      codeStart,
      codeEnd: bodyEnd,
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  return bodies;
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function dumpBytes(start: number, end: number) {
  const safeStart = Math.max(0, start);
  const safeEnd = Math.min(wasm.length, end);

  for (let p = safeStart; p < safeEnd; p += 16) {
    const e = Math.min(p + 16, safeEnd);

    const bytes: string[] = [];

    for (let q = p; q < e; q++) {
      bytes.push(wasm[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
    }

    console.log(
      `${hex(p)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
    );
  }
}

/*
 * Find the actual code-body containing the CALL address.
 * This avoids relying on a separate function-address table.
 */
function findBodyForCall(addr: number, bodies: ReturnType<typeof getDefinedFunctionBodies>) {
  for (let i = 0; i < bodies.length; i++) {
    const b = bodies[i];

    if (addr >= b.codeStart && addr < b.codeEnd) {
      return {
        ordinal: i,
        ...b,
      };
    }
  }

  return null;
}

console.log("TRACE DIRECT CALLERS OF FUNC 656");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET FUNC = ${TARGET_FUNC}`);

const types = getDefinedFunctionTypes();
const bodies = getDefinedFunctionBodies();

console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS = ${bodies.length}`);
console.log(`FUNCTION TYPES    = ${types.length}`);

for (const item of CALLS) {
  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`CALLER FUNC = ${item.func}`);
  console.log(`CALL @       = ${hex(item.addr)}`);

  const found = findBodyForCall(item.addr, bodies);

  if (!found) {
    console.log("BODY = NOT FOUND");
    continue;
  }

  console.log(
    `BODY        = ${hex(found.bodyStart)} .. ${hex(found.bodyEnd)}`
  );

  console.log(
    `CODE        = ${hex(found.codeStart)} .. ${hex(found.codeEnd)}`
  );

  console.log(
    `DEFINED ORD = ${found.ordinal}`
  );

  if (found.ordinal < types.length) {
    console.log(
      `TYPE INDEX  = ${types[found.ordinal]}`
    );
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("BYTES AROUND CALL");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  const start = Math.max(found.codeStart, item.addr - 40);
  const end = Math.min(found.codeEnd, item.addr + 40);

  dumpBytes(start, end);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTION");

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';

const dir = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const scannerPath = path.join(dir, 'disassembleAddressUsers.mjs');
const tempPath = path.join(dir, '__traceDispatch4769_decoder_temp.mjs');
const outputPath = path.join(dir, 'traceDispatch4769_output.txt');

const source = fs.readFileSync(scannerPath, 'utf8');
const marker = "console.log('\\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');";
const cut = source.indexOf(marker);

if (cut < 0) {
    throw new Error('Could not find decoder bootstrap marker');
}

let decoder;

try {
    fs.writeFileSync(
        tempPath,
        source.slice(0, cut) +
            '\nexport { bodies, decodeFunction, hex };\n',
        'utf8'
    );

    decoder = await import(
        pathToFileURL(tempPath).href + '?traceDispatch4769'
    );
} finally {
    fs.rmSync(tempPath, { force: true });
}

const { bodies, decodeFunction, hex } = decoder;

const GLOBAL_ADDR = 0x195354;
const TARGET_SLOT = 4769;

const lines = [];
const out = value => lines.push(String(value ?? ''));

let decodedCount = 0;
let decodeErrors = 0;
let globalRefs = 0;
let slotConstants = 0;
let indirectSites = 0;
let relevantFunctions = 0;

const results = [];

function isGlobalReference(text) {
    return text.includes(`0x${GLOBAL_ADDR.toString(16)}`) ||
        new RegExp(`\\b${GLOBAL_ADDR}\\b`).test(text);
}

function isSlotConstant(text) {
    return new RegExp(`\\b${TARGET_SLOT}\\b`).test(text);
}

function isIndirectCall(text) {
    return /\bcall_indirect\b/.test(text);
}

function contextBlock(funcIndex, instructions, index, label, radius = 8) {
    const start = Math.max(0, index - radius);
    const end = Math.min(instructions.length - 1, index + radius);
    const block = [];

    block.push('');
    block.push('----------------------------------------------------');
    block.push(`FUNCTION: ${funcIndex}`);
    block.push(`MATCH: ${label}`);
    block.push(`MATCH ADDRESS: ${hex(instructions[index].start)}`);
    block.push('CONTEXT:');

    for (let j = start; j <= end; j++) {
        const ins = instructions[j];
        const prefix = j === index ? '>>> ' : '    ';

        block.push(
            `${prefix}${hex(ins.start)}  ${ins.raw}  ${ins.text}`
        );
    }

    return block;
}

for (const funcIndex of bodies.keys()) {
    let decoded;

    try {
        decoded = decodeFunction(funcIndex);
        decodedCount++;
    } catch {
        decodeErrors++;
        continue;
    }

    const instructions = decoded.instructions;
    const anchors = [];

    let hasGlobalRef = false;
    let hasSlotConstant = false;
    let hasIndirect = false;

    for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
        const text = String(instructions[i].text);

        if (isGlobalReference(text)) {
            hasGlobalRef = true;
            globalRefs++;
            anchors.push({
                index: i,
                label: 'GLOBAL ADDRESS 0x195354'
            });
        }

        if (isSlotConstant(text)) {
            hasSlotConstant = true;
            slotConstants++;
            anchors.push({
                index: i,
                label: 'CONSTANT 4769'
            });
        }

        if (isIndirectCall(text)) {
            hasIndirect = true;
            indirectSites++;
        }
    }

    if (!hasGlobalRef && !hasSlotConstant) {
        continue;
    }

    relevantFunctions++;

    results.push({
        funcIndex,
        instructions,
        anchors,
        hasGlobalRef,
        hasSlotConstant,
        hasIndirect
    });
}

out('ROOSTER WASM — DISPATCH SLOT 4769 TRACE');
out('READ ONLY / OFFLINE / NO WASM EXECUTION');
out('====================================================');
out(`TARGET TABLE SLOT: ${TARGET_SLOT}`);
out(`DISPATCH GLOBAL ADDRESS: 0x${GLOBAL_ADDR.toString(16)}`);
out('');
out('SCAN SUMMARY');
out('----------------------------------------------------');
out(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS DECODED: ${decodedCount}`);
out(`FUNCTION DECODE ERRORS: ${decodeErrors}`);
out(`GLOBAL ADDRESS REFERENCES: ${globalRefs}`);
out(`CONSTANT 4769 REFERENCES: ${slotConstants}`);
out(`INDIRECT CALL SITES IN ALL DECODED FUNCTIONS: ${indirectSites}`);
out(`RELEVANT FUNCTIONS: ${relevantFunctions}`);

for (const item of results) {
    const {
        funcIndex,
        instructions,
        anchors,
        hasGlobalRef,
        hasSlotConstant,
        hasIndirect
    } = item;

    out('');
    out('====================================================');
    out(`FUNCTION ${funcIndex}`);
    out(
        `GLOBAL_195354=${hasGlobalRef} ` +
        `CONST_4769=${hasSlotConstant} ` +
        `HAS_CALL_INDIRECT=${hasIndirect}`
    );

    const seen = new Set();

    for (const anchor of anchors) {
        const key = `${anchor.index}:${anchor.label}`;

        if (seen.has(key)) continue;
        seen.add(key);

        for (const line of contextBlock(
            funcIndex,
            instructions,
            anchor.index,
            anchor.label
        )) {
            out(line);
        }
    }

    if (hasIndirect) {
        for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
            if (!isIndirectCall(String(instructions[i].text))) continue;

            for (const line of contextBlock(
                funcIndex,
                instructions,
                i,
                'CALL_INDIRECT IN RELEVANT FUNCTION',
                10
            )) {
                out(line);
            }
        }
    }
}

out('');
out('====================================================');
out('INTERPRETATION LIMITS');
out('1. A constant 4769 does not prove it is a table index.');
out('2. A reference to 0x195354 does not prove it writes the dispatch value.');
out('3. call_indirect context alone may not resolve runtime stack values.');
out('4. Decoder failures may hide additional references.');
out('No WASM execution performed.');

fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, lines.join('\n') + '\n', 'utf8');

console.log('Dispatch trace scan complete.');
console.log(`Decoded functions: ${decodedCount}`);
console.log(`Decode errors: ${decodeErrors}`);
console.log(`Relevant functions: ${relevantFunctions}`);
console.log(`Output: ${outputPath}`);

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceDispatchAddressCalc.ts

import fs from "fs";

const FILE =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

const TARGETS = [
    0x189dec, // table index 2053
    0x189f9c, // table index 2101
    0x18a8b0, // table index 2612
];

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: p,
    };
}

function readSection(pos: number) {
    const id = wasm[pos++];
    const len = readULEB(pos);

    return {
        id,
        start: len.next,
        end: len.next + len.value,
    };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" DISPATCH ADDRESS CALCULATION TRACE");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);

let p = 8;

let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
    const s = readSection(p);

    if (s.id === 10) {
        codeStart = s.start;
        codeEnd = s.end;
        break;
    }

    p = s.end;
}

if (!codeStart)
    throw new Error("Code section not found");

console.log(
    `Code : 0x${codeStart.toString(16)}..0x${codeEnd.toString(16)}`
);

console.log();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGETS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of TARGETS) {
    console.log(
        `0x${target.toString(16)} = ${target}`
    );
}

console.log();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SEARCHING GLOBAL.GET + CONSTANT ADDRESS PATTERNS");
console.log("============================================================");

let hits = 0;

/*
    We search for:

        global.get <index>
        i32.const <offset>
        i32.add

    and also:

        i32.const <offset>
        global.get <index>
        i32.add

    because Emscripten commonly forms static addresses from
    the wasm global stack/global-base pointer.
*/

for (let i = codeStart; i < codeEnd - 8; i++) {

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Pattern A:
    //
    // global.get X
    // i32.const TARGET_OFFSET
    // i32.add
    //
    // 0x23 <uleb> 0x41 <uleb> 0x6a
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (wasm[i] !== 0x23)
        continue;

    const g = readULEB(i + 1);

    if (wasm[g.next] !== 0x41)
        continue;

    const c = readULEB(g.next + 1);

    if (wasm[c.next] !== 0x6a)
        continue;

    const value = c.value >>> 0;

    for (const target of TARGETS) {

        if (value === target) {

            hits++;

            const from = Math.max(codeStart, i - 24);
            const to = Math.min(codeEnd, c.next + 24);

            console.log();
            console.log(
                `HIT A @ 0x${i.toString(16)}`
            );
            console.log(
                `  global index = ${g.value}`
            );
            console.log(
                `  constant     = 0x${value.toString(16)}`
            );
            console.log(
                `  target       = 0x${target.toString(16)}`
            );

            console.log(
                "  bytes:"
            );

            console.log(
                Array.from(wasm.slice(from, to))
                    .map(v =>
                        v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
                    )
                    .join(" ")
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SEARCHING CONSTANT + GLOBAL.GET + ADD");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let i = codeStart; i < codeEnd - 8; i++) {

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Pattern B:
    //
    // i32.const OFFSET
    // global.get X
    // i32.add
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (wasm[i] !== 0x41)
        continue;

    const c = readULEB(i + 1);

    if (wasm[c.next] !== 0x23)
        continue;

    const g = readULEB(c.next + 1);

    if (wasm[g.next] !== 0x6a)
        continue;

    const value = c.value >>> 0;

    for (const target of TARGETS) {

        if (value === target) {

            hits++;

            const from = Math.max(codeStart, i - 24);
            const to = Math.min(codeEnd, g.next + 24);

            console.log();
            console.log(
                `HIT B @ 0x${i.toString(16)}`
            );
            console.log(
                `  constant     = 0x${value.toString(16)}`
            );
            console.log(
                `  global index = ${g.value}`
            );
            console.log(
                `  target       = 0x${target.toString(16)}`
            );

            console.log(
                "  bytes:"
            );

            console.log(
                Array.from(wasm.slice(from, to))
                    .map(v =>
                        v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
                    )
                    .join(" ")
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SEARCHING NEAR-TARGET CONSTANTS");
console.log("============================================================");

/*
    The actual address may be:

        global + base

    while the target is reached after another add/sub.

    Therefore inspect constants within +/- 0x1000 of each
    target. This helps identify the base offset.
*/

for (const target of TARGETS) {

    console.log();
    console.log(
        `TARGET 0x${target.toString(16)}`
    );

    let count = 0;

    for (let i = codeStart; i < codeEnd - 2; i++) {

        if (wasm[i] !== 0x41)
            continue;

        const c = readULEB(i + 1);

        const value = c.value >>> 0;

        if (
            value >= target - 0x1000 &&
            value <= target + 0x1000
        ) {

            count++;

            if (count <= 20) {

                const from = Math.max(codeStart, i - 12);
                const to = Math.min(codeEnd, c.next + 20);

                console.log();
                console.log(
                    `  const @ 0x${i.toString(16)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  value = 0x${value.toString(16)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  delta = ${value - target}`
                );

                console.log(
                    "  bytes:"
                );

                console.log(
                    Array.from(wasm.slice(from, to))
                        .map(v =>
                            v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
                        )
                        .join(" ")
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log(
        `  TOTAL NEAR CONSTANTS = ${count}`
    );
}

console.log();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SCAN COMPLETE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(
    `TOTAL DIRECT ADDRESS CALC HITS = ${hits}`
);
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\traceDispatchBaseChain.ts

import fs from "fs";

const FILE =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: p,
    };
}

function readSection(pos: number) {
    const id = wasm[pos++];
    const len = readULEB(pos);

    return {
        id,
        start: len.next,
        end: len.next + len.value,
    };
}

function hex(v: number): string {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" DISPATCH BASE CHAIN TRACE");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

    const s = readSection(p);

    if (s.id === 10) {
        codeStart = s.start;
        codeEnd = s.end;
        break;
    }

    p = s.end;
}

if (!codeStart)
    throw new Error("Code section not found");

console.log(
    `Code : ${hex(codeStart)}..${hex(codeEnd)}`
);

console.log();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("EXACT load(+4) -> call_indirect(type=4)");
console.log("============================================================");

let total = 0;

for (let i = codeStart; i < codeEnd - 8; i++) {

    /*
       i32.load offset=4

       0x28 0x02 0x04

       immediately followed by

       call_indirect type=4

       0x11 0x04 0x00
    */

    if (
        wasm[i] !== 0x28 ||
        wasm[i + 1] !== 0x02 ||
        wasm[i + 2] !== 0x04 ||
        wasm[i + 3] !== 0x11 ||
        wasm[i + 4] !== 0x04 ||
        wasm[i + 5] !== 0x00
    ) {
        continue;
    }

    total++;

    console.log();
    console.log(
        `DISPATCH #${total} @ ${hex(i)}`
    );

    const from =
        Math.max(codeStart, i - 80);

    const to =
        Math.min(codeEnd, i + 24);

    console.log(
        `WINDOW ${hex(from)}..${hex(to)}`
    );

    console.log(
        Array.from(wasm.slice(from, to))
            .map(v =>
                v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
            )
            .join(" ")
    );

    /*
       Look backwards for the nearest local.get.
       This identifies the base local used by load(+4).
    */

    let local = -1;
    let localPos = -1;

    for (let j = i - 1; j >= Math.max(codeStart, i - 40); j--) {

        if (wasm[j] === 0x20) {

            const r = readULEB(j + 1);

            if (r.next <= i) {
                local = r.value;
                localPos = j;
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    if (local >= 0) {

        console.log(
            `BASE CANDIDATE = local ${local} @ ${hex(localPos)}`
        );

        /*
           Search backwards for writes to this local.
        */

        let writes = 0;

        for (
            let j = Math.max(codeStart, i - 400);
            j < i;
            j++
        ) {

            if (
                wasm[j] === 0x21
            ) {

                const r = readULEB(j + 1);

                if (r.value === local) {

                    writes++;

                    console.log(
                        `  local.set ${local} @ ${hex(j)}`
                    );

                    const from2 =
                        Math.max(codeStart, j - 20);

                    const to2 =
                        Math.min(codeEnd, r.next + 32);

                    console.log(
                        "  " +
                        Array.from(
                            wasm.slice(from2, to2)
                        )
                            .map(v =>
                                v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
                            )
                            .join(" ")
                    );
                }
            }

            /*
               local.tee
            */

            if (
                wasm[j] === 0x22
            ) {

                const r = readULEB(j + 1);

                if (r.value === local) {

                    writes++;

                    console.log(
                        `  local.tee ${local} @ ${hex(j)}`
                    );

                    const from2 =
                        Math.max(codeStart, j - 20);

                    const to2 =
                        Math.min(codeEnd, r.next + 32);

                    console.log(
                        "  " +
                        Array.from(
                            wasm.slice(from2, to2)
                        )
                            .map(v =>
                                v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
                            )
                            .join(" ")
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        console.log(
            `  LOCAL WRITES FOUND = ${writes}`
        );
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(
    `TOTAL EXACT DISPATCHES = ${total}`
);
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceDispatchDataRefs.ts

import fs from "fs";

const FILE = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

const TARGET_FILE_OFFSETS = [
    0x004a9035, // index 2053
    0x004a91e5, // index 2101
    0x004a9af9, // index 2612
];

function u32(p: number): number {
    return (
        wasm[p] |
        (wasm[p + 1] << 8) |
        (wasm[p + 2] << 16) |
        (wasm[p + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readSection(pos: number) {
    const id = wasm[pos++];
    const len = readULEB(pos);
    const start = len.next;
    const end = start + len.value;

    return { id, start, end };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" DISPATCH DATA REFERENCE TRACE");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);

//
// Find data section
//
let p = 8;
let dataStart = 0;
let dataEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
    const s = readSection(p);

    if (s.id === 11) {
        dataStart = s.start;
        dataEnd = s.end;
        break;
    }

    p = s.end;
}

if (!dataStart) {
    throw new Error("Data section not found");
}

console.log(
    `Data section : 0x${dataStart.toString(16)}..0x${dataEnd.toString(16)}`
);

//
// Parse data segments and locate target file offsets
//
let q = dataStart;
const segCount = readULEB(q);
q = segCount.next;

console.log(`Data segments : ${segCount.value}`);
console.log();

type TargetInfo = {
    fileOffset: number;
    segment: number;
    segmentFileStart: number;
    segmentMemStart: number;
    relative: number;
    memoryAddress: number;
};

const found: TargetInfo[] = [];

for (let seg = 0; seg < segCount.value; seg++) {
    const flags = readULEB(q);
    q = flags.next;

    let memStart = 0;

    if (flags.value === 0) {
        // active, memory 0
        if (wasm[q] !== 0x41)
            throw new Error(`Unexpected data offset opcode at segment ${seg}`);

        q++;

        const off = readULEB(q);
        memStart = off.value;
        q = off.next;

        if (wasm[q++] !== 0x0b)
            throw new Error(`Missing end of init expr at segment ${seg}`);
    }
    else if (flags.value === 2) {
        const memoryIndex = readULEB(q);
        q = memoryIndex.next;

        if (wasm[q] !== 0x41)
            throw new Error(`Unexpected data offset opcode at segment ${seg}`);

        q++;

        const off = readULEB(q);
        memStart = off.value;
        q = off.next;

        if (wasm[q++] !== 0x0b)
            throw new Error(`Missing end of init expr at segment ${seg}`);
    }
    else if (flags.value === 1) {
        // passive segment
        // no memory address
        memStart = 0;
    }
    else {
        throw new Error(`Unsupported data segment flags ${flags.value}`);
    }

    const size = readULEB(q);
    q = size.next;

    const bytesStart = q;
    const bytesEnd = q + size.value;

    for (const target of TARGET_FILE_OFFSETS) {
        if (target >= bytesStart && target < bytesEnd) {
            found.push({
                fileOffset: target,
                segment: seg,
                segmentFileStart: bytesStart,
                segmentMemStart: memStart,
                relative: target - bytesStart,
                memoryAddress:
                    flags.value === 1
                        ? 0
                        : memStart + (target - bytesStart),
            });
        }
    }

    q = bytesEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET MEMORY ADDRESSES");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const x of found) {
    console.log();
    console.log(
        `FILE 0x${x.fileOffset.toString(16)}`
    );
    console.log(
        `  segment       = ${x.segment}`
    );
    console.log(
        `  segment start = 0x${x.segmentFileStart.toString(16)}`
    );
    console.log(
        `  relative      = 0x${x.relative.toString(16)}`
    );
    console.log(
        `  memory addr   = 0x${x.memoryAddress.toString(16)}`
    );
}

//
// Locate code section
//
p = 8;

let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
    const s = readSection(p);

    if (s.id === 10) {
        codeStart = s.start;
        codeEnd = s.end;
        break;
    }

    p = s.end;
}

if (!codeStart)
    throw new Error("Code section not found");

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CODE REFERENCE SEARCH");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
    `Code : 0x${codeStart.toString(16)}..0x${codeEnd.toString(16)}`
);

//
// Search little-endian i32 constants equal to target memory addresses.
// Also search byte sequences surrounding the references.
//
for (const x of found) {
    const a = x.memoryAddress;

    console.log();
    console.log(
        `TARGET FILE 0x${x.fileOffset.toString(16)}`
    );
    console.log(
        `MEMORY ADDRESS 0x${a.toString(16)}`
    );

    const b0 = a & 0xff;
    const b1 = (a >>> 8) & 0xff;
    const b2 = (a >>> 16) & 0xff;
    const b3 = (a >>> 24) & 0xff;

    let hits = 0;

    for (let i = codeStart; i + 5 <= codeEnd; i++) {

        // i32.const opcode 0x41
        if (
            wasm[i] === 0x41 &&
            wasm[i + 1] === b0 &&
            wasm[i + 2] === b1 &&
            wasm[i + 3] === b2 &&
            wasm[i + 4] === b3
        ) {
            hits++;

            const from = Math.max(codeStart, i - 16);
            const to = Math.min(codeEnd, i + 32);

            const hex = Array.from(
                wasm.slice(from, to)
            )
                .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
                .join(" ");

            console.log();
            console.log(
                `  i32.const HIT @ 0x${i.toString(16)}`
            );
            console.log(
                `  bytes [0x${from.toString(16)}..0x${to.toString(16)}]`
            );
            console.log(`  ${hex}`);
        }
    }

    console.log();
    console.log(`  TOTAL i32.const REFERENCES = ${hits}`);
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SCAN COMPLETE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceDispatchFamilyFull.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const FIRST_FUNC = 8948;
const LAST_FUNC  = 8956;

function u8(p) {
  return wasm[p];
}

function readULEB(p) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = u8(p++);

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: p
      };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(p) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = u8(p++);

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    value |= (~0 << shift);

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: p
  };
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   SECTIONS
--------------------------------------------------------- */

function parseSections() {
  let p = 8;
  const sections = [];

  while (p < wasm.length) {

    const id = u8(p++);

    const sizeR = readULEB(p);
    const size = sizeR.value;

    p = sizeR.next;

    sections.push({
      id,
      start: p,
      end: p + size,
      size
    });

    p += size;
  }

  return sections;
}

const sections = parseSections();

const sec3  = sections.find(x => x.id === 3);
const sec10 = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!sec3 || !sec10)
  throw new Error("Function/Code sections not found");

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   FUNCTION TYPES
--------------------------------------------------------- */

function parseFunctionTypes() {

  let p = sec3.start;

  const countR = readULEB(p);
  const count = countR.value;

  p = countR.next;

  const result = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

    const r = readULEB(p);

    result.push(r.value);

    p = r.next;
  }

  return result;
}

const definedTypes = parseFunctionTypes();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   CODE BODIES
--------------------------------------------------------- */

function parseCodeBodies() {

  let p = sec10.start;

  const countR = readULEB(p);
  const count = countR.value;

  p = countR.next;

  const bodies = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

    const sizeR = readULEB(p);
    const bodySize = sizeR.value;

    const sizeStart = p;

    p = sizeR.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

    bodies.push({
      func: 83 + i,
      bodyStart,
      bodyEnd,
      bodySize,
      sizeStart
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = parseCodeBodies();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   SAFE IMMEDIATE DECODER
--------------------------------------------------------- */

function readMemArg(p) {

  const alignR = readULEB(p);
  p = alignR.next;

  const offsetR = readULEB(p);
  p = offsetR.next;

  return {
    align: alignR.value,
    offset: offsetR.value,
    next: p
  };
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   FUNCTION DECODER
--------------------------------------------------------- */

function decodeFunction(body) {

  let p = body.bodyStart;

  const localCountR = readULEB(p);
  const localCount = localCountR.value;

  p = localCountR.next;

  /*
   * Skip local declarations.
   */

  for (let i = 0; i < localCount; i++) {

    const nR = readULEB(p);
    p = nR.next;

    p++; // value type
  }

  const instructions = [];

  while (p < body.bodyEnd) {

    const off = p;
    const op = u8(p++);

    let text =
      `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

    let imm = null;

    try {

      switch (op) {

        /* -------------------------------------------------
           CONTROL
        ------------------------------------------------- */

        case 0x00:
          text = "unreachable";
          break;

        case 0x01:
          text = "nop";
          break;

        case 0x02: {
          const r = readSLEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text = `block type=${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x03: {
          const r = readSLEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text = `loop type=${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x04: {
          const r = readSLEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text = `if type=${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x05:
          text = "else";
          break;

        case 0x0b:
          text = "end";
          instructions.push({
            off,
            op,
            text,
            imm
          });

          return instructions;

        case 0x0c: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `br ${r.value}`;
          imm = r.value;
          break;
        }

        case 0x0d: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `br_if ${r.value}`;
          imm = r.value;
          break;
        }

        case 0x0e: {

          const nR = readULEB(p);
          const n = nR.value;

          p = nR.next;

          const labels = [];

          for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            labels.push(r.value);
            p = r.next;
          }

          const defR = readULEB(p);
          p = defR.next;

          text =
            `br_table [${labels.join(",")}] default=${defR.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
          text = "return";
          break;

        case 0x10: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `call ${r.value}`;
          imm = r.value;
          break;
        }

        case 0x11: {

          const typeR = readULEB(p);
          p = typeR.next;

          const tableR = readULEB(p);
          p = tableR.next;

          text =
            `call_indirect type=${typeR.value} table=${tableR.value}`;

          imm = typeR.value;

          break;
        }

        /* -------------------------------------------------
           PARAMETRIC
        ------------------------------------------------- */

        case 0x1a:
          text = "drop";
          break;

        case 0x1b:
          text = "select";
          break;

        /* -------------------------------------------------
           VARIABLES
        ------------------------------------------------- */

        case 0x20: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
          imm = r.value;
          break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
          imm = r.value;
          break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
          imm = r.value;
          break;
        }

        case 0x23: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `global.get ${r.value}`;
          imm = r.value;
          break;
        }

        case 0x24: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `global.set ${r.value}`;
          imm = r.value;
          break;
        }

        /* -------------------------------------------------
           MEMORY
        ------------------------------------------------- */

        case 0x28:
        case 0x29:
        case 0x2a:
        case 0x2b:
        case 0x2c:
        case 0x2d:
        case 0x2e:
        case 0x2f:
        case 0x30:
        case 0x31:
        case 0x32:
        case 0x33:
        case 0x34:
        case 0x35: {

          const r = readMemArg(p);
          p = r.next;

          const names = {
            0x28: "i32.load",
            0x29: "i64.load",
            0x2a: "f32.load",
            0x2b: "f64.load",
            0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
            0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
            0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
            0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
            0x30: "i64.load8_s",
            0x31: "i64.load8_u",
            0x32: "i64.load16_s",
            0x33: "i64.load16_u",
            0x34: "i64.load32_s",
            0x35: "i64.load32_u"
          };

          text =
            `${names[op]} align=${r.align} offset=${r.offset}`;

          imm = r.offset;

          break;
        }

        case 0x36:
        case 0x37:
        case 0x38:
        case 0x39:
        case 0x3a:
        case 0x3b:
        case 0x3c:
        case 0x3d:
        case 0x3e: {

          const r = readMemArg(p);
          p = r.next;

          const names = {
            0x36: "i32.store",
            0x37: "i64.store",
            0x38: "f32.store",
            0x39: "f64.store",
            0x3a: "i32.store8",
            0x3b: "i32.store16",
            0x3c: "i64.store8",
            0x3d: "i64.store16",
            0x3e: "i64.store32"
          };

          text =
            `${names[op]} align=${r.align} offset=${r.offset}`;

          imm = r.offset;

          break;
        }

        case 0x3f: {

          const mem = u8(p++);
          text = `memory.size mem=${mem}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x40: {

          const mem = u8(p++);
          text = `memory.grow mem=${mem}`;

          break;
        }

        /* -------------------------------------------------
           CONSTANTS
        ------------------------------------------------- */

        case 0x41: {

          const r = readSLEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text =
            `i32.const ${r.value} (${hex(r.value >>> 0)})`;

          imm = r.value;

          break;
        }

        case 0x42: {

          const r = readSLEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `i64.const ${r.value}`;
          imm = r.value;

          break;
        }

        case 0x43: {

          p += 4;

          text = "f32.const";

          break;
        }

        case 0x44: {

          p += 8;

          text = "f64.const";

          break;
        }

        /* -------------------------------------------------
           INTEGER COMPARISON
        ------------------------------------------------- */

        case 0x45:
          text = "i32.eqz";
          break;

        case 0x46:
          text = "i32.eq";
          break;

        case 0x47:
          text = "i32.ne";
          break;

        case 0x48:
          text = "i32.lt_s";
          break;

        case 0x49:
          text = "i32.lt_u";
          break;

        case 0x4a:
          text = "i32.gt_s";
          break;

        case 0x4b:
          text = "i32.gt_u";
          break;

        case 0x4c:
          text = "i32.le_s";
          break;

        case 0x4d:
          text = "i32.le_u";
          break;

        case 0x4e:
          text = "i32.ge_s";
          break;

        case 0x4f:
          text = "i32.ge_u";
          break;

        case 0x50:
          text = "i64.eqz";
          break;

        case 0x51:
          text = "i64.eq";
          break;

        case 0x52:
          text = "i64.ne";
          break;

        /* -------------------------------------------------
           INTEGER ARITHMETIC
        ------------------------------------------------- */

        case 0x67:
          text = "i32.clz";
          break;

        case 0x68:
          text = "i32.ctz";
          break;

        case 0x69:
          text = "i32.popcnt";
          break;

        case 0x6a:
          text = "i32.add";
          break;

        case 0x6b:
          text = "i32.sub";
          break;

        case 0x6c:
          text = "i32.mul";
          break;

        case 0x6d:
          text = "i32.div_s";
          break;

        case 0x6e:
          text = "i32.div_u";
          break;

        case 0x6f:
          text = "i32.rem_s";
          break;

        case 0x70:
          text = "i32.rem_u";
          break;

        case 0x71:
          text = "i32.and";
          break;

        case 0x72:
          text = "i32.or";
          break;

        case 0x73:
          text = "i32.xor";
          break;

        case 0x74:
          text = "i32.shl";
          break;

        case 0x75:
          text = "i32.shr_s";
          break;

        case 0x76:
          text = "i32.shr_u";
          break;

        case 0x77:
          text = "i32.rotl";
          break;

        case 0x78:
          text = "i32.rotr";
          break;

        /* -------------------------------------------------
           I64 INTEGER OPERATIONS
        ------------------------------------------------- */

        case 0x7c:
          text = "i64.add";
          break;

        case 0x7d:
          text = "i64.sub";
          break;

        case 0x7e:
          text = "i64.mul";
          break;

        case 0x7f:
          text = "i64.div_s";
          break;

        case 0x80:
          text = "i64.div_u";
          break;

        case 0x81:
          text = "i64.rem_s";
          break;

        case 0x82:
          text = "i64.rem_u";
          break;

        case 0x83:
          text = "i64.and";
          break;

        case 0x84:
          text = "i64.or";
          break;

        case 0x85:
          text = "i64.xor";
          break;

        case 0x86:
          text = "i64.shl";
          break;

        case 0x87:
          text = "i64.shr_s";
          break;

        case 0x88:
          text = "i64.shr_u";
          break;

        case 0x89:
          text = "i64.rotl";
          break;

        case 0x8a:
          text = "i64.rotr";
          break;

        /* -------------------------------------------------
           CONVERSIONS
        ------------------------------------------------- */

        case 0xa7:
          text = "i32.wrap_i64";
          break;

        case 0xac:
          text = "i64.extend_i32_s";
          break;

        case 0xad:
          text = "i64.extend_i32_u";
          break;

        /* -------------------------------------------------
           REFERENCE / OTHER
        ------------------------------------------------- */

        case 0xd0: {

          const r = u8(p++);

          text = `ref.null type=0x${r.toString(16)}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0xd1:
          text = "ref.is_null";
          break;

        case 0xd2: {

          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          text = `ref.func ${r.value}`;

          imm = r.value;

          break;
        }

        /* -------------------------------------------------
           PREFIX 0xfc
        ------------------------------------------------- */

        case 0xfc: {

          const subR = readULEB(p);
          p = subR.next;

          text =
            `0xfc subopcode=${subR.value}`;

          /*
           * Most 0xfc operations have immediates.
           * Keep the instruction visible without
           * risking desynchronization.
           */

          break;
        }

        /* -------------------------------------------------
           UNKNOWN
        ------------------------------------------------- */

        default:

          text =
            `UNKNOWN op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

          /*
           * Do not consume arbitrary bytes.
           * The raw offset remains visible.
           */

          break;
      }

    } catch (err) {

      text =
        `DECODE_ERROR op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")} ${err.message}`;

      instructions.push({
        off,
        op,
        text,
        imm
      });

      return instructions;
    }

    instructions.push({
      off,
      op,
      text,
      imm
    });
  }

  return instructions;
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   HEADER
--------------------------------------------------------- */

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" DISPATCH FAMILY FULL TRACE");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

console.log(
  `WASM SIZE : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`
);

console.log(
  `FUNCTIONS : ${FIRST_FUNC} .. ${LAST_FUNC}`
);

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   TRACE
--------------------------------------------------------- */

let totalInstructions = 0;
let unknownCount = 0;

for (
  let func = FIRST_FUNC;
  func <= LAST_FUNC;
  func++
) {

  const body =
    bodies.find(x => x.func === func);

  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  if (!body) {

    console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
    console.log("NOT FOUND");

    continue;
  }

  const typeIndex =
    definedTypes[func - 83];

  console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);

  console.log(
    `BODY : ${hex(body.bodyStart)} .. ${hex(body.bodyEnd)}`
  );

  console.log(
    `SIZE : ${body.bodySize}`
  );

  console.log(
    `TYPE : ${typeIndex}`
  );

  console.log("");
  console.log("INSTRUCTIONS");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  const ins =
    decodeFunction(body);

  totalInstructions += ins.length;

  for (const i of ins) {

    if (i.text.startsWith("UNKNOWN"))
      unknownCount++;

    console.log(
      `${hex(i.off)}  ${i.text}`
    );
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log(
    `INSTRUCTION COUNT : ${ins.length}`
  );
}
