   SUMMARY
--------------------------------------------------------- */

console.log(" SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
  `TOTAL INSTRUCTIONS : ${totalInstructions}`
);

console.log(
  `UNKNOWN OPCODES    : ${unknownCount}`
);

);

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceDispatchHandlers.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_FUNCS = [122, 656, 91, 787];

const TARGET_ADDRS = new Set([
  0x195348,
  0x19534c,
  0x195350,
  0x195354,
  0x195358,
]);

function readUleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(v: number, width = 8): string {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function findSection(idWanted: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = readUleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === idWanted) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`Section ${idWanted} not found`);
}

const importSec = findSection(2);
const funcSec = findSection(3);
const codeSec = findSection(10);

let importedFunctionCount = 0;

{
  let p = importSec.start;

  const count = readUleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const ml = readUleb(p);
    p = ml.next + ml.value;

    const nl = readUleb(p);
    p = nl.next + nl.value;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      const x = readUleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      importedFunctionCount++;
    } else if (kind === 1) {
      p++;
      const flags = readUleb(p);
      p = flags.next;
      const min = readUleb(p);
      p = min.next;
      if (flags.value & 1) {
        const max = readUleb(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 2) {
      const flags = readUleb(p);
      p = flags.next;
      const min = readUleb(p);
      p = min.next;
      if (flags.value & 1) {
        const max = readUleb(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }
}

const bodies = new Map<number, { start: number; end: number }>();

{
  let p = codeSec.start;

  const count = readUleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const size = readUleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    bodies.set(importedFunctionCount + i, { start, end });

    p = end;
  }
}

function signedLeb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= -1 << shift;
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: pos,
  };
}

function memarg(pos: number) {
  const align = readUleb(pos);
  pos = align.next;

  const offset = readUleb(pos);
  pos = offset.next;

  return {
    align: align.value,
    offset: offset.value,
    next: pos,
  };
}

function scan(func: number) {
  const body = bodies.get(func);

  if (!body) {
    console.log(`FUNC ${func}: BODY NOT FOUND`);
    return;
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
  console.log(`BODY = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
  console.log(`SIZE = ${body.end - body.start}`);
  console.log("============================================================");

  let p = body.start;

  while (p < body.end) {
    const at = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = signedLeb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const value = x.value >>> 0;

      if (TARGET_ADDRS.has(value)) {
        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  i32.const ${hex(value)}  <<< TARGET ADDRESS`
        );
      } else {
        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  i32.const ${hex(value)}`
        );
      }

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = readUleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  call FUNC ${x.value}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
      const type = readUleb(p);
      p = type.next;

      const table = readUleb(p);
      p = table.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  call_indirect TYPE ${type.value} TABLE ${table.value}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x28) {
      const m = memarg(p);
      p = m.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  i32.load offset=${hex(m.offset)}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x29) {
      const m = memarg(p);
      p = m.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  i64.load offset=${hex(m.offset)}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x2d) {
      const m = memarg(p);
      p = m.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  i32.load8_u offset=${hex(m.offset)}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x36) {
      const m = memarg(p);
      p = m.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  i32.store offset=${hex(m.offset)}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x3a) {
      const m = memarg(p);
      p = m.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  i32.store8 offset=${hex(m.offset)}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x20) {
      const x = readUleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  local.get ${x.value}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
      const x = readUleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  local.set ${x.value}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
      const x = readUleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  local.tee ${x.value}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0b) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  end`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c) {
      const x = readUleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  br ${x.value}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0d) {
      const x = readUleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  br_if ${x.value}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0e) {
      const x = readUleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const labels: number[] = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < x.value; i++) {
        const y = readUleb(p);
        p = y.next;
        labels.push(y.value);
      }

      const def = readUleb(p);
      p = def.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  br_table [${labels.join(",")}] default=${def.value}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0f) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  return`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  control opcode ${hex(op, 2)}`);

      if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03) {
        const blockType = wasm[p++];
        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  blocktype=${hex(blockType, 2)}`
        );
      } else {
        const blockType = wasm[p++];
        console.log(
          `${hex(at)}  if blocktype=${hex(blockType, 2)}`
        );
      }

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x40) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  blocktype=empty`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x45) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  i32.eqz`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x46) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  i32.eq`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x47) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  i32.ne`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x4a) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  i32.gt_u`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6a) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  i32.add`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x71) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  i32.and`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x72) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  i32.or`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x74) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  i32.shl`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x1a) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  drop`);
      continue;
    }

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)}  opcode ${hex(op, 2)}`
    );
  }
}

  scan(func);
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const CODE_START = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_END   = 0x00324ef5;
const IMPORTED   = 83;

const TARGETS = new Set([8637, 8927, 8952]);

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
    if (pos + i >= b.length) return null;

    const x = b[pos + i];
    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, end: pos + i + 1 };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function getBodies() {
  let p = CODE_START;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  if (!count) throw new Error("Cannot read body count");

  p = count.end;

  const bodies: any[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const size = readULEB(p);
    if (!size) throw new Error(`Bad body size at ${hex(p)}`);

    p = size.end;

    bodies.push({
      ordinal: i,
      global: i + IMPORTED,
      start: p,
      end: p + size.value
    });

    p += size.value;
  }

  return bodies;
}

function decodeInstruction(pos: number) {
  const op = b[pos];

  if (op === 0x20) {
    const x = readULEB(pos + 1);
    if (!x) return null;

    return {
      op,
      end: x.end,
      text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
      local: x.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x28) {
    const align = readULEB(pos + 1);
    if (!align) return null;

    const offset = readULEB(align.end);
    if (!offset) return null;

    return {
      op,
      end: offset.end,
      text: `i32.load offset=${offset.value}`,
      offset: offset.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x2d) {
    const align = readULEB(pos + 1);
    if (!align) return null;

    const offset = readULEB(align.end);
    if (!offset) return null;

    return {
      op,
      end: offset.end,
      text: `i32.load8_u offset=${offset.value}`,
      offset: offset.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const type = readULEB(pos + 1);
    if (!type) return null;

    const table = readULEB(type.end);
    if (!table) return null;

    return {
      op,
      end: table.end,
      text: `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`,
      type: type.value
    };
  }

  return {
    op,
    end: pos + 1,
    text: `op=0x${op.toString(16)}`
  };
}

function expressionBefore(
  bodyStart: number,
  callPos: number,
  maxBack = 20
) {
  const ins: any[] = [];

  let p = bodyStart;

  while (p < callPos) {
    const d = decodeInstruction(p);

    if (!d || d.end <= p || d.end > callPos) break;

    ins.push({
      pos: p,
      ...d
    });

    p = d.end;
  }

  return ins.slice(-maxBack);
}

const bodies = getBodies();

console.log("============================================================");

console.log("");

console.log("");

let total = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {

  for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

    if (b[p] !== 0x28)
      continue;

    const align = readULEB(p + 1);
    if (!align) continue;

    const offset = readULEB(align.end);
    if (!offset) continue;

    if (offset.value !== 4)
      continue;

    const q = offset.end;

    if (
      b[q] !== 0x11 ||
      b[q + 1] !== 0x04 ||
      b[q + 2] !== 0x00
    )
      continue;

    total++;

    const important = TARGETS.has(body.global);

    if (!important)
      continue;

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`TARGET FUNCTION ${body.global}`);
    console.log(`BODY            ${body.ordinal}`);
    console.log(`LOAD(+4)        ${hex(p)}`);
    console.log(`CALL_INDIRECT   ${hex(q)}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    const before = expressionBefore(body.start, p, 24);

    console.log("");
    console.log("STACK / INDEX SOURCE CANDIDATES");
    console.log("--------------------------------------------");

    for (const x of before) {
      console.log(`${hex(x.pos)}  ${x.text}`);
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("RAW CONTEXT");

    const from = Math.max(body.start, p - 48);
    const to = Math.min(body.end, q + 8);

    console.log(
      `${hex(from)} .. ${hex(to)}`
    );

    console.log(
      Array.from(b.slice(from, to))
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ")
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("PARAM[0] REFERENCES IN LOCAL WINDOW");

    let param0Hits = 0;

    for (
      let x = Math.max(body.start, p - 96);
      x < p;
      x++
    ) {
      if (b[x] !== 0x20)
        continue;

      const idx = readULEB(x + 1);

      if (!idx)
        continue;

      if (idx.value === 0) {
        console.log(`  local.get 0 @ ${hex(x)}`);
        param0Hits++;
      }
    }

    console.log(
      `PARAM[0] HITS = ${param0Hits}`
    );
  }
}

FILE: scripts\traceDispatchMemoryWriters.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = [
    {
        name: "Function 3621 dispatch source",
        address: 2428392,
        hex: "0x251088",
    },
    {
        name: "Function 3658 root pointer",
        address: 1837608,
        hex: "0x1C0188",
    },
];

const TARGET_INDEX = 7969;

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: p };

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35)
            throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF in ULEB");
}

function readSLEB(buf: Buffer, p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        b = buf[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        if (shift > 35)
            throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (-1 << shift);

    return { value, next: p };
}

function ulebBytes(value: number): number[] {
    const out: number[] = [];

    do {
        let b = value & 0x7f;
        value >>>= 7;

        if (value !== 0)
            b |= 0x80;

        out.push(b);
    } while (value !== 0);

    return out;
}

function findSection(
    wasm: Buffer,
    wantedId: number
): { id: number; payloadStart: number; payloadEnd: number } | null {

    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];

        const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
        const payloadStart = sizeInfo.next;
        const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sizeInfo.value;

        if (payloadEnd > wasm.length)
            return null;

        if (id === wantedId) {
            return {
                id,
                payloadStart,
                payloadEnd,
            };
        }

        p = payloadEnd;
    }

    return null;
}

function parseCodeBodies(wasm: Buffer) {
    const sec = findSection(wasm, 10);

    if (!sec)
        throw new Error("Code section not found");

    let p = sec.payloadStart;

    const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    p = countInfo.next;

    const bodies: {
        func: number;
        bodyStart: number;
        bodyEnd: number;
        codeStart: number;
        codeEnd: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
        const bodyStart = sizeInfo.next;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + sizeInfo.value;

        if (bodyEnd > sec.payloadEnd)
            throw new Error(`Invalid code body ${i}`);

        const localCountInfo = readULEB(wasm, bodyStart);
        let q = localCountInfo.next;

        for (let j = 0; j < localCountInfo.value; j++) {
            const n = readULEB(wasm, q);
            q = n.next;

            // valtype
            q++;
        }

        bodies.push({
            func: 83 + i,
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            codeStart: q,
            codeEnd: bodyEnd,
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function matchBytes(
    wasm: Buffer,
    pos: number,
    bytes: number[]
): boolean {
    if (pos + bytes.length > wasm.length)
        return false;

    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) {
        if (wasm[pos + i] !== bytes[i])
            return false;
    }

    return true;
}

function findPattern(
    wasm: Buffer,
    start: number,
    end: number,
    pattern: number[]
): number[] {
    const hits: number[] = [];

    for (let p = start; p + pattern.length <= end; p++) {
        if (matchBytes(wasm, p, pattern))
            hits.push(p);
    }

    return hits;
}

function formatHex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function u32LE(wasm: Buffer, p: number): number {
    if (p + 4 > wasm.length)
        return 0;

    return (
        wasm[p] |
        (wasm[p + 1] << 8) |
        (wasm[p + 2] << 16) |
        (wasm[p + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function inspectDataSegments(wasm: Buffer, addresses: number[]) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" DATA SEGMENT INITIALIZATION");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const sec = findSection(wasm, 11);

    if (!sec) {
        console.log("Data section not found.");
        return;
    }

    let p = sec.payloadStart;

    const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    p = countInfo.next;

    console.log(`Data segments: ${count}`);
    console.log("");

    const wanted = new Set(addresses);

    for (let seg = 0; seg < count; seg++) {
        const flagsInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
        const flags = flagsInfo.value;
        p = flagsInfo.next;

        let memoryIndex = 0;
        let offset = 0;

        if (flags === 0) {
            // active, memory 0
            // offset expression

            if (wasm[p] !== 0x41) {
                console.log(
                    `segment ${seg}: unsupported offset expression opcode ${formatHex(wasm[p])}`
                );
                return;
            }

            p++;

            const offInfo = readSLEB(wasm, p);
            offset = offInfo.value >>> 0;
            p = offInfo.next;

            if (wasm[p] !== 0x0b)
                throw new Error(`segment ${seg}: missing end`);

            p++;
        }
        else if (flags === 2) {
            // active with explicit memory index
            const memInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
            memoryIndex = memInfo.value;
            p = memInfo.next;

            if (wasm[p] !== 0x41) {
                console.log(
                    `segment ${seg}: unsupported offset expression`
                );
                return;
            }

            p++;

            const offInfo = readSLEB(wasm, p);
            offset = offInfo.value >>> 0;
            p = offInfo.next;

            if (wasm[p] !== 0x0b)
                throw new Error(`segment ${seg}: missing end`);

            p++;
        }

        const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
        const size = sizeInfo.value;
        const dataStart = sizeInfo.next;
        const dataEnd = dataStart + size;

        for (const addr of addresses) {
            if (addr >= offset && addr + 4 <= offset + size) {
                const rel = addr - offset;

                console.log(
                    `TARGET ${formatHex(addr)}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `  segment       : ${seg}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `  memory index  : ${memoryIndex}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `  segment start : ${formatHex(offset)}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `  segment size  : ${size}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `  relative      : ${formatHex(rel)}`
                );

                if (rel + 4 <= size) {
                    const value = u32LE(wasm, dataStart + rel);

                    console.log(
                        `  initial i32   : ${value} (${formatHex(value)})`
                    );

                    if (value === TARGET_INDEX) {
                        console.log(
                            `  *** INITIAL VALUE IS TABLE INDEX ${TARGET_INDEX} ***`
                        );
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        p = dataEnd;
    }
}

function inspectStaticAddressWriters(
    wasm: Buffer,
    bodies: ReturnType<typeof parseCodeBodies>,
    target: number
) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(` STATIC ADDRESS WRITERS FOR ${formatHex(target)}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    const imm = ulebBytes(target);

    // i32.const target
    const constPattern = [0x41, ...imm];

    let totalConst = 0;
    let store8 = 0;
    let store16 = 0;
    let store32 = 0;

    for (const body of bodies) {
        const hits = findPattern(
            wasm,
            body.codeStart,
            body.codeEnd,
            constPattern
        );

        if (hits.length === 0)
            continue;

        for (const hit of hits) {
            totalConst++;

            console.log("");
            console.log(
                `Function ${body.func}  const ${formatHex(target)} @ ${formatHex(hit)}`
            );

            const end = Math.min(body.codeEnd, hit + 20);

            let p = hit;

            while (p < end) {
                const op = wasm[p];

                if (op === 0x41) {
                    const x = readSLEB(wasm, p + 1);
                    console.log(
                        `  ${formatHex(p)}  i32.const ${x.value}`
                    );
                    p = x.next;
                    continue;
                }

                if (op === 0x21) {
                    const x = readULEB(wasm, p + 1);
                    console.log(
                        `  ${formatHex(p)}  local.set ${x.value}`
                    );
                    p = x.next;
                    continue;
                }

                if (op === 0x22) {
                    const x = readULEB(wasm, p + 1);
                    console.log(
                        `  ${formatHex(p)}  local.tee ${x.value}`
                    );
                    p = x.next;
                    continue;
                }

                if (op === 0x20) {
                    const x = readULEB(wasm, p + 1);
                    console.log(
                        `  ${formatHex(p)}  local.get ${x.value}`
                    );
                    p = x.next;
                    continue;
                }

                if (op === 0x3a) {
                    const align = wasm[p + 1];
                    const off = readULEB(wasm, p + 2);
                    console.log(
                        `  ${formatHex(p)}  i32.store8 align=${align} offset=${off.value}`
                    );
                    store8++;
                    p = off.next;
                    continue;
                }

                if (op === 0x3b) {
                    const align = wasm[p + 1];
                    const off = readULEB(wasm, p + 2);
                    console.log(
                        `  ${formatHex(p)}  i32.store16 align=${align} offset=${off.value}`
                    );
                    store16++;
                    p = off.next;
                    continue;
                }

                if (op === 0x36) {
                    const align = wasm[p + 1];
                    const off = readULEB(wasm, p + 2);
                    console.log(
                        `  ${formatHex(p)}  i32.store align=${align} offset=${off.value}`
                    );
                    store32++;
                    p = off.next;
                    continue;
                }

                // stop when another control-flow boundary appears
                if (
                    op === 0x0b ||
                    op === 0x0f ||
                    op === 0x0c ||
                    op === 0x0d
                ) {
                    break;
                }

                p++;
            }
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("SUMMARY");
    console.log(`  i32.const hits : ${totalConst}`);
    console.log(`  store8 nearby  : ${store8}`);
    console.log(`  store16 nearby : ${store16}`);
    console.log(`  store32 nearby : ${store32}`);
}

function inspectImmediateValueWrites(
    wasm: Buffer,
    bodies: ReturnType<typeof parseCodeBodies>,
    value: number
) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(` WRITERS OF VALUE ${value} (${formatHex(value)})`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    const imm = ulebBytes(value);
    const pattern = [0x41, ...imm];

    let count = 0;

    for (const body of bodies) {
        const hits = findPattern(
            wasm,
            body.codeStart,
            body.codeEnd,
            pattern
        );

        for (const hit of hits) {
            count++;

            const end = Math.min(body.codeEnd, hit + 18);

            console.log("");
            console.log(
                `Function ${body.func} @ ${formatHex(hit)}`
            );

            let p = hit;

            while (p < end) {
                const op = wasm[p];

                if (op === 0x41) {
                    const x = readSLEB(wasm, p + 1);
                    console.log(
                        `  ${formatHex(p)} i32.const ${x.value}`
                    );
                    p = x.next;
                    continue;
                }

                if (op === 0x20) {
                    const x = readULEB(wasm, p + 1);
                    console.log(
                        `  ${formatHex(p)} local.get ${x.value}`
                    );
                    p = x.next;
                    continue;
                }

                if (op === 0x36) {
                    const off = readULEB(wasm, p + 2);
                    console.log(
                        `  ${formatHex(p)} i32.store offset=${off.value}`
                    );
                    p = off.next;
                    break;
                }

                if (op === 0x3a) {
                    const off = readULEB(wasm, p + 2);
                    console.log(
                        `  ${formatHex(p)} i32.store8 offset=${off.value}`
                    );
                    p = off.next;
                    break;
                }

                if (op === 0x3b) {
                    const off = readULEB(wasm, p + 2);
                    console.log(
                        `  ${formatHex(p)} i32.store16 offset=${off.value}`
                    );
                    p = off.next;
                    break;
                }

                p++;
            }
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(`Total CONST(${value}) sites: ${count}`);
}

export async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" DISPATCH MEMORY WRITER TRACE");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("");

    const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
    console.log("");

    const bodies = parseCodeBodies(wasm);

    console.log(`Defined functions : ${bodies.length}`);
    console.log(`Function range    : ${bodies[0].func} .. ${bodies.at(-1)!.func}`);

    for (const t of TARGETS) {
        console.log("");
        console.log(
            `${t.name}: ${t.address} (${t.hex})`
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        `Target Function 4815 table index : ${TARGET_INDEX}`
    );

    inspectDataSegments(
        wasm,
        TARGETS.map(x => x.address)
    );

    for (const t of TARGETS) {
        inspectStaticAddressWriters(
            wasm,
            bodies,
            t.address
        );
    }

    inspectImmediateValueWrites(
        wasm,
        bodies,
        TARGET_INDEX
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" END");
    console.log("============================================================");
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const CODE_START = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_END   = 0x00324ef5;

const IMPORTED = 83;

const TARGETS = [8637, 8927, 8952];

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
    if (pos + i >= b.length)
      return null;

    const x = b[pos + i];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0)
      return { value, end: pos + i + 1 };

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

type Body = {
  ordinal: number;
  global: number;
  start: number;
  end: number;
};

function getBodies(): Body[] {

  let p = CODE_START;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  if (!count)
    throw new Error("Cannot read body count");

  p = count.end;

  const out: Body[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const size = readULEB(p);
    if (!size)
      throw new Error(`Bad body size at ${hex(p)}`);

    p = size.end;

    out.push({
      ordinal: i,
      global: i + IMPORTED,
      start: p,
      end: p + size.value
    });

    p += size.value;
  }

  return out;
}

const bodies = getBodies();

function ulebBytes(pos: number) {
  const r = readULEB(pos);
  return r ? r.end : pos;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DISPATCH PARAM[0] TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

for (const target of TARGETS) {

  const body = bodies.find(x => x.global === target);

  if (!body) {
    console.log(`TARGET ${target}: NOT FOUND`);
    continue;
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`FUNCTION ${target}`);
  console.log(
    `body=${body.ordinal} ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`
  );
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let hits = 0;

  for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

    const op = b[p];

    /*
       local.get 0
       20 00
    */
    if (op === 0x20 && b[p + 1] === 0x00) {

      hits++;

      /*
         Only print nearby instructions.
         We intentionally keep this compact.
      */

      const from = Math.max(body.start, p - 12);
      const to   = Math.min(body.end, p + 32);

      console.log("");
      console.log(`param[0] @ ${hex(p)}`);

      console.log(
        Array.from(b.slice(from, to))
          .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
          .join(" ")
      );
    }
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log(`PARAM[0] USE SITES = ${hits}`);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\traceDispatchQuartet.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const SELECTORS = [
  { addr: 0x19534c, value: 4788, name: "SELECTOR0" },
  { addr: 0x195350, value: 4789, name: "SELECTOR1" },
  { addr: 0x195354, value: 4790, name: "SELECTOR2" },
  { addr: 0x195358, value: 4791, name: "SELECTOR3" },
];

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value, next: p };
}

function findCodeSection() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = readULEB(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === 10) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

function parseBodies() {
  const code = findCodeSection();

  let p = code.start;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  const bodies: any[] = [];

  for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
    const size = readULEB(p);
    p = size.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + size.value;

    const locals = readULEB(p);
    p = locals.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < locals.value; i++) {
      const n = readULEB(p);
      p = n.next;

      // local type
      p++;
    }

    bodies.push({
      ordinal,
      bodyStart,
      bodyEnd,
      instrStart: p,
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  return bodies;
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function bytes(start: number, end: number) {
  return Array.from(wasm.subarray(start, end))
    .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join(" ");
}

function decodeInstruction(p: number) {
  const start = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = readSLEB(p);

    return {
      next: x.next,
      op,
      text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = readULEB(p);

    return {
      next: x.next,
      op,
      text: `call ${x.value}`,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const type = readULEB(p);
    p = type.next;

    const table = readULEB(p);
    p = table.next;

    return {
      next: p,
      op,
      text: `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`,
    };
  }

  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22 ||
    op === 0x23 ||
    op === 0x24
  ) {
    const x = readULEB(p);

    const names: Record<number, string> = {
      0x20: "local.get",
      0x21: "local.set",
      0x22: "local.tee",
      0x23: "global.get",
      0x24: "global.set",
    };

    return {
      next: x.next,
      op,
      text: `${names[op]} ${x.value}`,
    };
  }

  if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x2a ||
    op === 0x2b ||
    op === 0x2c ||
    op === 0x2d ||
    op === 0x2e ||
    op === 0x2f ||
    op === 0x36 ||
    op === 0x37 ||
    op === 0x38 ||
    op === 0x39
  ) {
    const align = readULEB(p);
    p = align.next;

    const offset = readULEB(p);
    p = offset.next;

    return {
      next: p,
      op,
      text: `memory-op align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`,
    };
  }

  const names: Record<number, string> = {
    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x71: "i32.xor",
    0x0b: "end",
    0x0f: "return",
    0x04: "if",
    0x05: "else",
    0x02: "block",
    0x03: "loop",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
  };

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const x = readULEB(p);

    return {
      next: x.next,
      op,
      text: `${names[op]} ${x.value}`,
    };
  }

  return {
    next: p,
    op,
    text: names[op] ?? `op_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
  };
}

const bodies = parseBodies();

console.log("TRACE DISPATCH QUARTET");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS = ${bodies.length}`);
console.log("");

for (const selector of SELECTORS) {
  console.log(
    `${selector.name}: ADDRESS=${hex(selector.addr)} VALUE=${selector.value}`
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DIRECT ADDRESS / VALUE REFERENCES");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const body of bodies) {
  let p = body.instrStart;

  while (p < body.bodyEnd) {
    const ins = decodeInstruction(p);

    if (
      ins.text === "i32.const 1659724" ||
      ins.text === "i32.const 1659728" ||
      ins.text === "i32.const 1659732" ||
      ins.text === "i32.const 1659736" ||
      ins.text === "i32.const 4788" ||
      ins.text === "i32.const 4789" ||
      ins.text === "i32.const 4790" ||
      ins.text === "i32.const 4791" ||
      ins.text.includes("call_indirect")
    ) {
      console.log(
        `FUNC ORDINAL ${body.ordinal} @ ${hex(p)}`
      );
      console.log(`  ${ins.text}`);
      console.log(`  BYTES = ${bytes(p, ins.next)}`);
    }

    p = ins.next;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("LOCAL WINDOWS AROUND SELECTOR REFERENCES");
console.log("============================================================");

const interesting: {
  ordinal: number;
  addr: number;
  next: number;
}[] = [];

for (const body of bodies) {
  let p = body.instrStart;

  while (p < body.bodyEnd) {
    const ins = decodeInstruction(p);

    if (
      ins.text === "i32.const 1659724" ||
      ins.text === "i32.const 1659728" ||
      ins.text === "i32.const 1659732" ||
      ins.text === "i32.const 1659736" ||
      ins.text === "i32.const 4788" ||
      ins.text === "i32.const 4789" ||
      ins.text === "i32.const 4790" ||
      ins.text === "i32.const 4791"
    ) {
      interesting.push({
        ordinal: body.ordinal,
        addr: p,
        next: ins.next,
      });
    }

    p = ins.next;
  }
}

for (const hit of interesting) {
  const body = bodies[hit.ordinal];

  let p = body.instrStart;
  const list: any[] = [];

  while (p < body.bodyEnd) {
    const ins = decodeInstruction(p);

    if (p >= hit.addr - 24 && p <= hit.addr + 32) {
      list.push({
        addr: p,
        next: ins.next,
        text: ins.text,
      });
    }

    if (p > hit.addr + 32) break;

    p = ins.next;
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log(
    `FUNC ${hit.ordinal} TARGET @ ${hex(hit.addr)}`
  );

  for (const x of list) {
    console.log(
      `  ${hex(x.addr)}  ${x.text}`
    );
  }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceDispatchRegistration.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET_START = 0x00189dc0;
const TARGET_END   = 0x00189e24;

const TARGET_VALUES = new Set([
  2049, 2050, 2051, 2052, 2053, 2054, 2055
]);

function u8(p) {
  return wasm[p];
}

function readULEB(p) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = u8(p++);
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(p) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = u8(p++);
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    value |= (~0 << shift);

  return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readString(p) {
  const r = readULEB(p);

  return {
    text: Buffer.from(
      wasm.subarray(r.next, r.next + r.value)
    ).toString(),
    next: r.next + r.value
  };
}

function skipLimits(p) {
  const flags = u8(p++);

  let r = readULEB(p);
  p = r.next;

  if (flags & 1) {
    r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;
  }

  return p;
}

function parseSections() {
  let p = 8;
  const sections = [];

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = u8(p++);

    const sizeR = readULEB(p);
    const size = sizeR.value;
    p = sizeR.next;

    sections.push({
      id,
      start: p,
      end: p + size,
      size
    });

    p += size;
  }

  return sections;
}

const sections = parseSections();

const sec1  = sections.find(x => x.id === 1);
const sec3  = sections.find(x => x.id === 3);
const sec10 = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!sec1 || !sec3 || !sec10) {
  throw new Error("Required WASM sections not found");
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   TYPE SECTION
--------------------------------------------------------- */

function parseTypes() {
  let p = sec1.start;

  const countR = readULEB(p);
  const count = countR.value;
  p = countR.next;

  const types = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const form = u8(p++);

    if (form !== 0x60)
      throw new Error(
        `Unexpected type form at ${hex(p - 1)}`
      );

    let r = readULEB(p);
    const nParams = r.value;
    p = r.next;

    const params = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < nParams; j++)
      params.push(u8(p++));

    r = readULEB(p);
    const nResults = r.value;
    p = r.next;

    const results = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < nResults; j++)
      results.push(u8(p++));

    types.push({
      params,
      results
    });
  }

  return types;
}

const types = parseTypes();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   FUNCTION SECTION
--------------------------------------------------------- */

function parseFunctionTypes() {
  let p = sec3.start;

  const countR = readULEB(p);
  const count = countR.value;
  p = countR.next;

  const result = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const r = readULEB(p);

    result.push(r.value);
    p = r.next;
  }

  return result;
}

const definedTypes = parseFunctionTypes();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   CODE SECTION
--------------------------------------------------------- */

function parseCodeBodies() {
  let p = sec10.start;

  const countR = readULEB(p);
  const count = countR.value;
  p = countR.next;

  const bodies = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const sizeR = readULEB(p);
    const bodySize = sizeR.value;
    const sizeStart = p;

    p = sizeR.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

    bodies.push({
      func: 83 + i,
      bodyStart,
      bodyEnd,
      bodySize,
      sizeStart
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = parseCodeBodies();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   CONSTANT / ADDRESS DECODER
--------------------------------------------------------- */

function decodeBody(body) {
  let p = body.bodyStart;

  const localCountR = readULEB(p);
  const localCount = localCountR.value;
  p = localCountR.next;

  const locals = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < localCount; i++) {
    const nR = readULEB(p);
    const n = nR.value;
    p = nR.next;

    const type = u8(p++);

    for (let j = 0; j < n; j++)
      locals.push(type);
  }

  const instructions = [];

  while (p < body.bodyEnd) {
    const off = p;
    const op = u8(p++);

    let text =
      `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

    let imm = null;

    switch (op) {

      case 0x0b:
        instructions.push({
          off,
          op,
          text: "end"
        });

        return instructions;

      case 0x0f:
        instructions.push({
          off,
          op,
          text: "return"
        });

        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        text = `call ${r.value}`;
        imm = r.value;

        break;
      }

      case 0x11: {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        const table = readULEB(p);
        p = table.next;

        text =
          `call_indirect type=${r.value} table=${table.value}`;

        imm = r.value;

        break;
      }

      case 0x20: {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        text = `local.get ${r.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        text = `local.set ${r.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x28: {
        const align = u8(p++);

        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        text =
          `i32.load align=${align} offset=${r.value}`;

        imm = r.value;

        break;
      }

      case 0x2d: {
        const align = u8(p++);

        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        text =
          `i32.load8_u align=${align} offset=${r.value}`;

        imm = r.value;

        break;
      }

      case 0x29: {
        const align = u8(p++);

        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        text =
          `i64.load align=${align} offset=${r.value}`;

        imm = r.value;

        break;
      }

      case 0x36: {
        const align = u8(p++);

        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        text =
          `i32.store align=${align} offset=${r.value}`;

        imm = r.value;

        break;
      }

      case 0x37: {
        const align = u8(p++);

        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        text =
          `i64.store align=${align} offset=${r.value}`;

        imm = r.value;

        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        const r = readSLEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        text =
          `i32.const ${r.value} (${hex(r.value >>> 0)})`;

        imm = r.value;

        break;
      }

      case 0x42: {
        const r = readSLEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        text =
          `i64.const ${r.value}`;

        imm = r.value;

        break;
      }

      case 0x6a:
        text = "i32.add";
        break;

      case 0x46:
        text = "i32.eq";
        break;

      case 0x1b:
        text = "select";
        break;

      case 0x00:
        text = "unreachable";
        break;

      default:
        text =
          `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

        break;
    }

    instructions.push({
      off,
      op,
      text,
      imm
    });
  }

  return instructions;
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   Runtime address -> Data Segment file offset
--------------------------------------------------------- */

const dataAddressMap = new Map();

function findRuntimeDataOffset(runtimeAddress) {
  /*
   * Data segment parser below identifies all active
   * segments and maps runtime addresses to file offsets.
   */
  return dataAddressMap.get(runtimeAddress) ?? null;
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   DATA SEGMENTS
--------------------------------------------------------- */

function parseDataSegments() {
  const sec11 = sections.find(x => x.id === 11);

  if (!sec11)
    return;

  let p = sec11.start;

  const countR = readULEB(p);
  const count = countR.value;
  p = countR.next;

  for (let seg = 0; seg < count; seg++) {

    const flagsR = readULEB(p);
    const flags = flagsR.value;
    p = flagsR.next;

    let memoryIndex = 0;
    let runtimeBase = null;

    if (flags === 0) {

      /* active, implicit memory 0 */

      const op = u8(p++);

      if (op !== 0x41)
        throw new Error(
          `Unexpected data offset opcode ${hex(p - 1)}`
        );

      const offR = readSLEB(p);

      runtimeBase = offR.value >>> 0;
      p = offR.next;

      if (u8(p++) !== 0x0b)
        throw new Error(
          "Missing data segment end"
        );

    } else if (flags === 2) {

      /* active, explicit memory index */

      const memR = readULEB(p);

      memoryIndex = memR.value;
      p = memR.next;

      const op = u8(p++);

      if (op !== 0x41)
        throw new Error(
          "Unexpected data offset opcode"
        );

      const offR = readSLEB(p);

      runtimeBase = offR.value >>> 0;
      p = offR.next;

      if (u8(p++) !== 0x0b)
        throw new Error(
          "Missing data segment end"
        );

    } else {

      /*
       * Passive segments are ignored here.
       */

      if (flags !== 1)
        throw new Error(
          `Unsupported data flags ${flags}`
        );

      const lenR = readULEB(p);

      p = lenR.next + lenR.value;

      continue;
    }

    const lenR = readULEB(p);
    const len = lenR.value;
    p = lenR.next;

    const bytesStart = p;
    const bytesEnd = p + len;

    if (
      memoryIndex === 0 &&
      runtimeBase !== null
    ) {

      const lo =
        Math.max(runtimeBase, TARGET_START);

      const hi =
        Math.min(
          runtimeBase + len,
          TARGET_END + 1
        );

      for (
        let addr = lo;
        addr < hi;
        addr++
      ) {

        dataAddressMap.set(
          addr,
          bytesStart + (addr - runtimeBase)
        );
      }
    }

    p = bytesEnd;
  }
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   BUILD DATA ADDRESS MAP
--------------------------------------------------------- */

/*
 * IMPORTANT:
 * This MUST happen before STATIC DATA WINDOW,
 * because findRuntimeDataOffset() depends on
 * dataAddressMap being populated.
 */

parseDataSegments();

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   STATIC DATA WINDOW
--------------------------------------------------------- */

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" DISPATCH REGISTRATION TRACE");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

console.log(
  `WASM SIZE : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`
);

console.log(
  `TARGET    : ${hex(TARGET_START)} .. ${hex(TARGET_END)}`
);

console.log("");
console.log("STATIC WORDS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (
  let addr = TARGET_START;
  addr <= TARGET_END - 4;
  addr += 4
) {

  const fileOffset =
    findRuntimeDataOffset(addr);

  if (fileOffset === null)
    continue;

  const value =
    wasm.readUInt32LE(fileOffset);

  const mark =
    TARGET_VALUES.has(value)
      ? "  <== TABLE VALUE"
      : "";

  console.log(
    `${hex(addr)}  value=${value} (${hex(value)})${mark}`
  );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   TARGET VALUES
--------------------------------------------------------- */

console.log("");
console.log("TARGET VALUES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (
  let addr = TARGET_START;
  addr <= TARGET_END - 4;
  addr += 4
) {

  const fileOffset =
    dataAddressMap.get(addr);

  if (fileOffset === undefined)
    continue;

  const value =
    wasm.readUInt32LE(fileOffset);

  const mark =
    TARGET_VALUES.has(value)
      ? "  <== 2049..2055"
      : "";

  console.log(
    `${hex(addr)} -> ${value} (${hex(value)})${mark}`
  );
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   SCAN ALL FUNCTIONS
--------------------------------------------------------- */

console.log("");
console.log("FUNCTION REFERENCES TO TARGET DATA");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let totalHits = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {

  let ins;

  try {
    ins = decodeBody(body);
  } catch {
    continue;
  }

  const relevant = [];

  for (const i of ins) {

    if (i.op !== 0x41)
      continue;

    const v = i.imm >>> 0;

    if (
      (v >= TARGET_START && v <= TARGET_END) ||
      TARGET_VALUES.has(v)
    ) {
      relevant.push(i);
    }
  }

  if (!relevant.length)
    continue;

  const typeIndex =
    body.func < 83
      ? null
      : definedTypes[body.func - 83];

  console.log("");
  console.log(`FUNC ${body.func}`);

  console.log(
    `BODY : ${hex(body.bodyStart)} .. ${hex(body.bodyEnd)}`
  );

  console.log(
    `TYPE : ${typeIndex}`
  );

  for (const i of relevant) {

    console.log(
      `  ${hex(i.off)}  ${i.text}`
    );
  }

  totalHits += relevant.length;
}

console.log("");
console.log(
  `TOTAL CODE HITS : ${totalHits}`
);

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
   EXACT LOAD/STORE OFFSETS IN TARGET ADDRESS RANGE
--------------------------------------------------------- */

console.log("");
console.log("LOAD/STORE CONTEXT");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let memoryHits = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {

  let ins;

  try {
    ins = decodeBody(body);
  } catch {
    continue;
  }

  for (let k = 0; k < ins.length; k++) {

    const i = ins[k];

    if (
      i.op !== 0x28 &&
      i.op !== 0x29 &&
      i.op !== 0x2d &&
      i.op !== 0x36 &&
      i.op !== 0x37
    )
      continue;

    /*
     * We cannot prove the runtime address
     * from an offset alone.
     *
     * Print nearby constants and
     * address-producing instructions.
     */

    const before = ins.slice(
      Math.max(0, k - 5),
      k
    );

    const hasTargetConst =
      before.some(x =>
        x.op === 0x41 &&
        (
          (x.imm >>> 0) >= TARGET_START &&
          (x.imm >>> 0) <= TARGET_END
        )
      );

    if (!hasTargetConst)
      continue;

    console.log("");
    console.log(
      `FUNC ${body.func} @ ${hex(i.off)}`
    );

    console.log(
      `  ${i.text}`
    );

    for (const b of before) {

      console.log(
        `    ${hex(b.off)}  ${b.text}`
      );
    }

    memoryHits++;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log(
  `LOAD/STORE CONTEXT HITS : ${memoryHits}`
);

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" END");
console.log("============================================================");


====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceDispatchRuntimeRecords.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = [2053, 2101, 2612];

const ROOTS = [
    1633928, // 0x18ee88
    1633988, // 0x18eec4
    1634104, // 0x18ef38
    1634128, // 0x18ef50
    1634268, // 0x18efdc
    1634408, // 0x18f068
    1617084, // 0x18acbc
];

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value,
                next: pos,
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40) !== 0) {
        value -= Math.pow(2, shift);
    }

    return {
        value,
        next: pos,
    };
}

function hex(v: number): string {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readMemU32(mem: Uint8Array, addr: number): number | null {
    if (addr < 0 || addr + 4 > mem.length) {
        return null;
    }

    return (
        mem[addr] |
        (mem[addr + 1] << 8) |
        (mem[addr + 2] << 16) |
        (mem[addr + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function region(addr: number): string {
    if (addr >= 0x0018744c && addr < 0x0018ab8d) {
        return "SEG412";
    }

    if (addr >= 0x0018ab98 && addr < 0x0018f512) {
        return "SEG413";
    }

    return "OTHER";
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE DISPATCH RUNTIME RECORDS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log();
console.log(`WASM size = ${buf.length}`);

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * FIND DATA SECTION
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

let pos = 8;

let dataStart = -1;
let dataEnd = -1;

while (pos < buf.length) {
    const sectionId = buf[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;

    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const payloadStart = pos;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 11) {
        dataStart = payloadStart;
        dataEnd = payloadEnd;
    }

    pos = payloadEnd;
}

if (dataStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("DATA SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

console.log();
console.log("DATA SECTION");
console.log(`  payload start = ${hex(dataStart)}`);
console.log(`  payload end   = ${hex(dataEnd)}`);

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * PARSE DATA SEGMENTS
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

let p = dataStart;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const dataCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

console.log(`  segments      = ${dataCount}`);

type Segment = {
    memoryIndex: number;
    offset: number;
    bytes: Uint8Array;
};

const segments: Segment[] = [];

let maxAddress = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < dataCount; i++) {
    const flagsInfo = readULEB(p);
    const flags = flagsInfo.value;
    p = flagsInfo.next;

    let memoryIndex = 0;
    let offset = -1;

    if (flags === 0) {
        const opcode = buf[p++];

        if (opcode !== 0x41) {
            throw new Error(
                `Segment ${i}: unexpected offset opcode ${hex(opcode)}`
            );
        }

        const offInfo = readSLEB(p);
        offset = offInfo.value;
        p = offInfo.next;

        if (buf[p++] !== 0x0b) {
            throw new Error(`Segment ${i}: missing end opcode`);
        }
    } else if (flags === 2) {
        const memInfo = readULEB(p);
        memoryIndex = memInfo.value;
        p = memInfo.next;

        const opcode = buf[p++];

        if (opcode !== 0x41) {
            throw new Error(
                `Segment ${i}: unexpected offset opcode ${hex(opcode)}`
            );
        }

        const offInfo = readSLEB(p);
        offset = offInfo.value;
        p = offInfo.next;

        if (buf[p++] !== 0x0b) {
            throw new Error(`Segment ${i}: missing end opcode`);
        }
    } else if (flags === 1) {
        const lenInfo = readULEB(p);
        const len = lenInfo.value;
        p = lenInfo.next;

        const bytes = buf.subarray(p, p + len);
        p += len;

        segments.push({
            memoryIndex: -1,
            offset: -1,
            bytes,
        });

        continue;
    } else {
        throw new Error(
            `Unsupported data segment flags=${flags} at segment ${i}`
        );
    }

    const lenInfo = readULEB(p);
    const len = lenInfo.value;
    p = lenInfo.next;

    const bytes = buf.subarray(p, p + len);
    p += len;

    segments.push({
        memoryIndex,
        offset,
        bytes,
    });

    if (memoryIndex === 0 && offset >= 0) {
        const end = offset + len;

        if (end > maxAddress) {
            maxAddress = end;
        }
    }
}

console.log(`  parser end    = ${hex(p)}`);
console.log(`  expected end  = ${hex(dataEnd)}`);

if (p !== dataEnd) {
    throw new Error(
        `DATA PARSER MISMATCH: ${hex(p)} != ${hex(dataEnd)}`
    );
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * BUILD LINEAR MEMORY
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

const memorySize = Math.max(
    maxAddress + 0x1000,
    0x02000000
);

const memory = new Uint8Array(memorySize);

let mappedBytes = 0;

for (const seg of segments) {
    if (seg.memoryIndex !== 0) {
        continue;
    }

    if (seg.offset < 0) {
        continue;
    }

    memory.set(seg.bytes, seg.offset);
    mappedBytes += seg.bytes.length;
}

console.log();
console.log("MEMORY IMAGE");
console.log(`  allocated     = ${memory.length}`);
console.log(`  mapped bytes  = ${mappedBytes}`);
console.log(`  max data addr = ${hex(maxAddress)}`);

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * ROOT VALUES
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("ROOT VALUES FROM REAL WASM MEMORY");
console.log("============================================================");

const rootValues = new Map<number, number>();

for (const root of ROOTS) {
    const value = readMemU32(memory, root);

    console.log();
    console.log(`${hex(root)} (${root})`);

    if (value === null) {
        console.log("  OUT OF RANGE");
        continue;
    }

    rootValues.set(root, value);

    console.log(`  VALUE  = ${hex(value)} (${value})`);
    console.log(`  REGION = ${region(value)}`);
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * ROOT POINTERS
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("ROOT POINTERS");
console.log("============================================================");

const structures = new Set<number>();

for (const value of rootValues.values()) {
    if (
        value >= 0x10000 &&
        value + 0x200 < memory.length
    ) {
        structures.add(value);
    }
}

for (const addr of structures) {
    console.log(
        `${hex(addr)} -> ${region(addr)}`
    );
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * STRUCTURE SCAN
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("STRUCTURE FIELD SCAN");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const addr of structures) {
    console.log();
    console.log(
        `STRUCTURE @ ${hex(addr)} [${region(addr)}]`
    );

    for (let off = 0; off <= 0x180; off += 4) {
        const value = readMemU32(memory, addr + off);

        if (value === null) {
            break;
        }

        const isTarget = TARGETS.includes(value);

        const isPointer =
            value >= 0x00180000 &&
            value < 0x00200000;

        if (isTarget || isPointer) {
            console.log(
                `  +0x${off.toString(16).padStart(3, "0")}` +
                ` = ${hex(value)} (${value})` +
                (isTarget ? "  <<< TARGET >>>" : "") +
                ` [${region(value)}]`
            );
        }
    }
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * TARGET SEARCH
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET TABLE INDEX SEARCH");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of TARGETS) {
    const hits: number[] = [];

    for (let addr = 0; addr + 4 <= maxAddress; addr += 4) {
        const value = readMemU32(memory, addr);

        if (value === target) {
            hits.push(addr);
        }
    }

    console.log();
    console.log(`TARGET ${target}`);
    console.log(`  MEMORY HITS = ${hits.length}`);

    for (const addr of hits.slice(0, 100)) {
        console.log(
            `  ${hex(addr)} -> ${target} [${region(addr)}]`
        );
    }

    if (hits.length > 100) {
        console.log(`  ... ${hits.length - 100} more`);
    }
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * RELEVANT DISPATCH FIELDS
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DISPATCHER RELEVANT FIELDS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const addr of structures) {
    console.log();
    console.log(`STRUCTURE ${hex(addr)}`);

    for (const off of [
        4,
        16,
        24,
        40,
        132,
        140,
        144,
        148,
    ]) {
        const value = readMemU32(memory, addr + off);

        if (value === null) {
            continue;
        }

        const target = TARGETS.includes(value);

        console.log(
            `  +${off.toString().padStart(3, " ")} = ` +
            `${hex(value)} (${value})` +
            (target ? "  <<< TARGET >>>" : "")
        );
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\traceDispatchSlotStores.js
