(async () => {
    const fs = require("fs");
    const wabt = await require("wabt")();

    const file =
        "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

    const targets = new Map([
        [1659724, "SLOT_19534C"],
        [1659728, "SLOT_195350"],
        [1659732, "SLOT_195354"],
        [1659736, "SLOT_195358"]
    ]);

    const features = {
        exceptions: true,
        mutable_globals: true,
        sat_float_to_int: true,
        sign_extension: true,
        simd: true,
        threads: true,
        function_references: true,
        multi_value: true,
        tail_call: true,
        bulk_memory: true,
        reference_types: true,
        annotations: true,
        code_metadata: true,
        gc: true,
        memory64: true,
        multi_memory: true,
        extended_const: true,
        relaxed_simd: true
    };

    const mod = wabt.readWasm(fs.readFileSync(file), {
        readDebugNames: false,
        ...features
    });

    mod.validate();

    const wat = mod.toText({
        foldExprs: false,
        inlineExport: false
    });

    const lines = wat.split(/\r?\n/);
    let funcId = null;
    let hits = 0;

    console.log("DISPATCH SLOT STORE SCAN");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("Direct-address stores and nearby context only");

    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
        const start = lines[i].match(/^\s*\(func \(;(\d+);\)/);

        if (start) {
            funcId = Number(start[1]);
        }

        // A direct-address store usually has i32.const ADDRESS
        // immediately before the store, possibly with a value
        // pushed between them. Print candidate stores whose
        // nearby instructions mention a target address.
        if (!/\bi32\.store(?:8|16)?\b|\bi64\.store\b|\bf32\.store\b|\bf64\.store\b/.test(lines[i])) {
            continue;
        }

        const from = Math.max(0, i - 8);
        const context = lines.slice(from, i + 1);
        const found = [];

        for (const [address, label] of targets) {
            if (context.some(line =>
                new RegExp(`\\bi32\\.const\\s+${address}\\b`).test(line)
            )) {
                found.push(`${label} (${address})`);
            }
        }

        if (found.length === 0) continue;

        hits++;
        console.log("");
        console.log("------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(`FUNC ${funcId} | STORE LINE ${i + 1}`);
        console.log("TARGET CANDIDATE:", found.join(", "));

        for (let j = from; j <= i; j++) {
            console.log(`${j + 1}: ${lines[j].trimEnd()}`);
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("DIRECT STORE CANDIDATES:", hits);
    console.log("NOTE: Pointer-based writes may not appear in this scan.");

    mod.destroy();
})().catch(error => {
    console.error(error);
    process.exitCode = 1;
});

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceDispatchSlotWriters.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = [
  0x195348,
  0x19534c,
  0x195350,
  0x195354,
  0x195358,
];

function readUleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= -1 << shift;
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(v: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function findSection(idWanted: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = readUleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === idWanted) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`Section ${idWanted} not found`);
}

/*
 * Import section
 */
const importSec = findSection(2);

let importedFunctions = 0;

{
  let p = importSec.start;

  const count = readUleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const ml = readUleb(p);
    p = ml.next + ml.value;

    const nl = readUleb(p);
    p = nl.next + nl.value;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      const t = readUleb(p);
      p = t.next;
      importedFunctions++;
    } else if (kind === 1) {
      p++;
      const flags = readUleb(p);
      p = flags.next;

      const min = readUleb(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags.value & 1) {
        const max = readUleb(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 2) {
      const flags = readUleb(p);
      p = flags.next;

      const min = readUleb(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags.value & 1) {
        const max = readUleb(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }
}

/*
 * Function + Code sections
 */
const funcSec = findSection(3);
const codeSec = findSection(10);

const definedTypes: number[] = [];

{
  let p = funcSec.start;

  const count = readUleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const t = readUleb(p);
    p = t.next;
    definedTypes.push(t.value);
  }
}

const bodies = new Map<number, { start: number; end: number }>();

{
  let p = codeSec.start;

  const count = readUleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const size = readUleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    bodies.set(importedFunctions + i, { start, end });

    p = end;
  }
}

/*
 * Scan every defined function.
 *
 * We specifically search:
 *
 * A) i32.const TARGET
 *
 * B) i32.store with offset 0
 *
 * C) i32.store8 with offset 0
 *
 * D) nearby patterns:
 *
 *    const TARGET
 *    ...
 *    store
 *
 * The purpose is to catch both obvious and slightly
 * separated absolute-address writes.
 */
console.log("TRACE DISPATCH SLOT WRITERS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of TARGETS) {
  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`TARGET ${hex(target)}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let hitCount = 0;

  for (const [func, body] of bodies.entries()) {
    const hits: string[] = [];

    let p = body.start;

    while (p < body.end) {
      const at = p;
      const op = wasm[p++];

      if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = readSleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        if ((x.value >>> 0) === target) {
          hits.push(
            `${hex(at)} i32.const ${hex(target)}`
          );
        }

        continue;
      }

      /*
       * i32.store
       */
      if (op === 0x36) {
        const align = readUleb(p);
        p = align.next;

        const offset = readUleb(p);
        p = offset.next;

        if (offset.value === 0) {
          hits.push(
            `${hex(at)} i32.store offset=0`
          );
        }

        continue;
      }

      /*
       * i32.store8
       */
      if (op === 0x3a) {
        const align = readUleb(p);
        p = align.next;

        const offset = readUleb(p);
        p = offset.next;

        if (offset.value === 0) {
          hits.push(
            `${hex(at)} i32.store8 offset=0`
          );
        }

        continue;
      }

      /*
       * Skip memarg instructions that are loads.
       */
      if (
        op === 0x28 || // i32.load
        op === 0x29 || // i64.load
        op === 0x2a || // f32.load
        op === 0x2b || // f64.load
        op === 0x2c || // i32.load8_s
        op === 0x2d || // i32.load8_u
        op === 0x2e || // i32.load16_s
        op === 0x2f || // i32.load16_u
        op === 0x30 || // i64.load8_s
        op === 0x31 || // i64.load8_u
        op === 0x32 || // i64.load16_s
        op === 0x33 || // i64.load16_u
        op === 0x34 || // i64.load32_s
        op === 0x35    // i64.load32_u
      ) {
        const align = readUleb(p);
        p = align.next;

        const offset = readUleb(p);
        p = offset.next;

        continue;
      }

      /*
       * Simple instructions with immediates.
       */
      if (
        op === 0x10 || // call
        op === 0x0c || // br
        op === 0x0d || // br_if
        op === 0x20 || // local.get
        op === 0x21 || // local.set
        op === 0x22    // local.tee
      ) {
        const x = readUleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        continue;
      }

      if (op === 0x11) {
        const type = readUleb(p);
        p = type.next;

        const table = readUleb(p);
        p = table.next;

        continue;
      }

      if (
        op === 0x02 ||
        op === 0x03 ||
        op === 0x04
      ) {
        p++;
        continue;
      }

      /*
       * br_table
       */
      if (op === 0x0e) {
        const n = readUleb(p);
        p = n.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
          const x = readUleb(p);
          p = x.next;
        }

        const d = readUleb(p);
        p = d.next;

        continue;
      }
    }

    /*
     * Only report functions where the target address
     * itself was found.
     */
    if (hits.some(x => x.includes("i32.const"))) {
      console.log("");
      console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
      console.log(
        `BODY ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`
      );

      for (const h of hits) {
        console.log(`  ${h}`);
      }

      hitCount++;
    }
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log(`FUNCTIONS WITH TARGET CONST = ${hitCount}`);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceDispatchTargets.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_CALLS = new Map([
    [202, "CALL_202"],
    [1151, "CALL_1151"],
    [1152, "CALL_1152"],
]);

const TARGET_VALUES = new Map([
    [0x0003ef51, "ID_3EF51"],
    [0x0003f0a0, "ID_3F0A0"],
    [2049, "TABLE_2049"],
    [2050, "TABLE_2050"],
    [2051, "TABLE_2051"],
    [2052, "TABLE_2052"],
    [2053, "TABLE_2053"],
    [2054, "TABLE_2054"],
    [2055, "TABLE_2055"],
]);

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(
                `ULEB too long at 0x${pos.toString(16)}`
            );
        }
    }

    throw new Error(
        `Unexpected EOF while reading ULEB at 0x${pos.toString(16)}`
    );
}

function parseSections() {
    const sections = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const sizeR = readULEB(p);
        const size = sizeR.value;
        p = sizeR.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + size;

        sections.push({
            id,
            start,
            end
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

function parseFunctionSection(section) {
    const result = [];

    let p = section.start;

    const countR = readULEB(p);
    const count = countR.value;
    p = countR.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const r = readULEB(p);

        result.push(r.value);

        p = r.next;
    }

    return result;
}

function parseCodeSection(section, functionTypes) {
    const bodies = [];

    let p = section.start;

    const countR = readULEB(p);
    const count = countR.value;
    p = countR.next;

    const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

        const sizeR = readULEB(p);
        const bodySize = sizeR.value;
        p = sizeR.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

        const localsR = readULEB(p);
        const localGroups = localsR.value;
        p = localsR.next;

        for (let g = 0; g < localGroups; g++) {
            const nR = readULEB(p);
            p = nR.next;

            // local value type
            p++;
        }

        bodies.push({
            func: IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + i,
            typeIndex: functionTypes[i],
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            codeStart: p,
            codeEnd: bodyEnd
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function hex(v, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function bytesAt(start, end) {
    const arr = [];

    for (let p = start; p < end && p < buf.length; p++) {
        arr.push(buf[p].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
    }

    return arr.join(" ");
}

function showContext(pos, bodyStart, bodyEnd) {
    const from = Math.max(bodyStart, pos - 12);
    const to = Math.min(bodyEnd, pos + 20);

    console.log(
        `    CONTEXT ${hex(from)} .. ${hex(to)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `    ${bytesAt(from, to)}`
    );
}

function scanFunction(body) {

    const hits = [];

    let p = body.codeStart;

    while (p < body.codeEnd) {

        const op = buf[p];

        /*
         * call
         * 0x10 + ULEB function index
         */
        if (op === 0x10) {

            const r = readULEB(p + 1);

            if (TARGET_CALLS.has(r.value)) {

                hits.push({
                    kind: "call",
                    pos: p,
                    label: TARGET_CALLS.get(r.value),
                    value: r.value
                });
            }

            p = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * i32.const
         * 0x41 + SLEB32
         *
         * We do not need to decode signedness here.
         * Read the immediate bytes and compare the unsigned
         * 32-bit representation when it terminates.
         */
        if (op === 0x41) {

            let q = p + 1;
            let value = 0;
            let shift = 0;

            while (q < body.codeEnd) {

                const b = buf[q++];

                value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

                if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {

                    const u32 = value >>> 0;

                    if (TARGET_VALUES.has(u32)) {

                        hits.push({
                            kind: "i32.const",
                            pos: p,
                            label: TARGET_VALUES.get(u32),
                            value: u32
                        });
                    }

                    break;
                }

                shift += 7;

                if (shift > 35) {
                    break;
                }
            }

            /*
             * Even if this is an unknown/invalid immediate,
             * only move to the first byte after the encoded
             * immediate when termination was found.
             */
            if (q > p + 1) {
                p = q;
            } else {
                p++;
            }

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * call_indirect
         *
         * opcode = 0x11
         * immediate = typeidx ULEB + tableidx ULEB
         */
        if (op === 0x11) {

            const typeR = readULEB(p + 1);
            const tableR = readULEB(typeR.next);

            hits.push({
                kind: "call_indirect",
                pos: p,
                typeIndex: typeR.value,
                table: tableR.value
            });

            p = tableR.next;
            continue;
        }

        p++;
    }

    return hits;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DISPATCH TARGET TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
    `WASM SIZE : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`
);

const sections = parseSections();

const funcSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 3);
const codeSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 10);

if (!funcSec || !codeSec) {
    throw new Error("Function or Code section not found.");
}

const functionTypes = parseFunctionSection(funcSec);
const bodies = parseCodeSection(codeSec, functionTypes);

const TARGET_MIN = 8948;
const TARGET_MAX = 8956;

console.log(
    `FUNCTION RANGE : ${TARGET_MIN} .. ${TARGET_MAX}`
);

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("TARGET FUNCTION BODIES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const selected = bodies.filter(
    b => b.func >= TARGET_MIN && b.func <= TARGET_MAX
);

for (const body of selected) {

    console.log(
        `FUNC ${body.func}  BODY ${hex(body.bodyStart)} .. ${hex(body.bodyEnd)}`
    );

    const hits = scanFunction(body);

    if (hits.length === 0) {
        console.log("    no target hits");
        continue;
    }

    for (const hit of hits) {

        if (hit.kind === "call") {

            console.log(
                `    ${hex(hit.pos)}  ${hit.label}  call ${hit.value}`
            );

            showContext(
                hit.pos,
                body.codeStart,
                body.codeEnd
            );
        }

        else if (hit.kind === "i32.const") {

            console.log(
                `    ${hex(hit.pos)}  ${hit.label}  ` +
                `${hit.value} (${hex(hit.value)})`
            );

            showContext(
                hit.pos,
                body.codeStart,
                body.codeEnd
            );
        }

        else if (hit.kind === "call_indirect") {

            console.log(
                `    ${hex(hit.pos)}  CALL_INDIRECT ` +
                `type=${hit.typeIndex} table=${hit.table}`
            );

            showContext(
                hit.pos,
                body.codeStart,
                body.codeEnd
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("GLOBAL TARGET SCAN");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const globalHits = [];

for (const body of bodies) {

    const hits = scanFunction(body);

    for (const hit of hits) {

        if (
            hit.kind === "call" ||
            hit.kind === "i32.const"
        ) {
            globalHits.push({
                func: body.func,
                ...hit
            });
        }
    }
}

for (const hit of globalHits) {

    console.log(
        `FUNC ${hit.func}  ${hex(hit.pos)}  ` +
        `${hit.label}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("INDIRECT DISPATCH SCAN");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let indirectCount = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {

    const hits = scanFunction(body);

    for (const hit of hits) {

        if (hit.kind !== "call_indirect") {
            continue;
        }

        indirectCount++;

        console.log(
            `FUNC ${body.func}  ${hex(hit.pos)}  ` +
            `type=${hit.typeIndex} table=${hit.table}`
        );

        if (body.func >= TARGET_MIN &&
            body.func <= TARGET_MAX) {

            showContext(
                hit.pos,
                body.codeStart,
                body.codeEnd
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log(
    `TOTAL CALL_INDIRECT : ${indirectCount}`
);

console.log("");

console.log("END");

FILE: scripts\traceExactSelectorAddressExpressions.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const TARGETS = [
    0x19534c,
    0x195350,
    0x195354,
    0x195358,
];

const TARGET_SET = new Set(TARGETS);

function hex(n: number, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function readULEB(p: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: p };
}

function readSLEB(p: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = bytes[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value -= 2 ** shift;

    return { value, next: p };
}

interface Ins {
    pc: number;
    op: number;
    text: string;
    imm?: number;
}

function decode(p: number): Ins {

    const op = bytes[p];

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        return {
            pc: p,
            op,
            text: `local.get ${r.value}`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        return {
            pc: p,
            op,
            text: `local.set ${r.value}`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        return {
            pc: p,
            op,
            text: `local.tee ${r.value}`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = readSLEB(p + 1);

        return {
            pc: p,
            op,
            text: `i32.const ${r.value}`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x23) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        return {
            pc: p,
            op,
            text: `global.get ${r.value}`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x24) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        return {
            pc: p,
            op,
            text: `global.set ${r.value}`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x28) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 2);

        return {
            pc: p,
            op,
            text: `i32.load offset=${hex(r.value)}`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x36) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 2);

        return {
            pc: p,
            op,
            text: `i32.store offset=${hex(r.value)}`,
            imm: r.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x6a)
        return { pc: p, op, text: "i32.add" };

    if (op === 0x6b)
        return { pc: p, op, text: "i32.sub" };

    if (op === 0x74)
        return { pc: p, op, text: "i32.shl" };

    if (op === 0x75)
        return { pc: p, op, text: "i32.shr_s" };

    if (op === 0x76)
        return { pc: p, op, text: "i32.shr_u" };

    if (op === 0x77)
        return { pc: p, op, text: "i32.rotl" };

    if (op === 0x78)
        return { pc: p, op, text: "i32.rotr" };

    if (op === 0xa7)
        return { pc: p, op, text: "i32.wrap_i64" };

    if (op === 0xfc) {

        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        if (r.value === 10)
            return { pc: p, op, text: "memory.copy" };

        if (r.value === 11)
            return { pc: p, op, text: "memory.fill" };
    }

    return {
        pc: p,
        op,
        text: `opcode ${hex(op, 2)}`
    };
}

function nextPC(p: number) {

    const op = bytes[p];

    if (
        op === 0x20 ||
        op === 0x21 ||
        op === 0x22 ||
        op === 0x23 ||
        op === 0x24
    )
        return readULEB(p + 1).next;

    if (op === 0x41)
        return readSLEB(p + 1).next;

    if (
        op === 0x28 ||
        op === 0x29 ||
        op === 0x2a ||
        op === 0x2b ||
        op === 0x36 ||
        op === 0x37 ||
        op === 0x38 ||
        op === 0x39
    )
        return readULEB(p + 2).next;

    if (op === 0xfc)
        return readULEB(p + 1).next;

    return p + 1;
}

/*
 * Find every literal target address in the entire WASM.
 */
console.log("TRACE EXACT SELECTOR ADDRESS EXPRESSIONS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of TARGETS)
    console.log(`TARGET = ${hex(target)}`);

console.log("");

let literalHits = 0;
let storeCandidates = 0;

let p = 0;

while (p < bytes.length) {

    /*
     * Only inspect opcode 0x41 because selector addresses
     * are i32 constants.
     */
    if (bytes[p] === 0x41) {

        const r = readSLEB(p + 1);

        if (TARGET_SET.has(r.value)) {

            literalHits++;

            console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
            console.log(
                `LITERAL TARGET @ ${hex(p)} = ${hex(r.value)}`
            );

            /*
             * Show nearby raw bytes.
             */
            const start = Math.max(0, p - 16);
            const end = Math.min(bytes.length, r.next + 24);

            console.log(
                `RAW RANGE ${hex(start)} .. ${hex(end)}`
            );

            for (let q = start; q < end; q += 16) {

                const row: string[] = [];

                for (
                    let k = q;
                    k < Math.min(q + 16, end);
                    k++
                ) {
                    row.push(
                        bytes[k].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
                    );
                }

                console.log(
                    `${hex(q)}  ${row.join(" ")}`
                );
            }

            /*
             * Decode the following instructions.
             */
            console.log("FOLLOWING:");

            let q = r.next;

            for (let n = 0; n < 20 && q < bytes.length; n++) {

                const ins = decode(q);

                console.log(
                    `  ${hex(ins.pc)} ${ins.text}`
                );

                /*
                 * Any store shortly after the exact target
                 * literal is a candidate, but NOT yet proven.
                 */
                if (ins.op === 0x36) {
                    storeCandidates++;

                    console.log(
                        "  >>> STORE CANDIDATE AFTER TARGET CONSTANT"
                    );
                }

                q = nextPC(q);
            }
        }
    }

    p++;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log(`EXACT TARGET LITERALS = ${literalHits}`);
console.log(`STORE CANDIDATES      = ${storeCandidates}`);
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");

FILE: scripts\traceExactStateStores.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = new Set([
  0x250fb4,
  0x250fc0,
]);

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function u32(pos) {
  return wasm.readUInt32LE(pos);
}

function lebU32(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let i = pos;

  while (i < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[i++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: result >>> 0, next: i };
}

function lebI32(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let i = pos;
  let b = 0;

  while (i < wasm.length) {
    b = wasm[i++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    result |= (~0 << shift);

  return { value: result | 0, next: i };
}

function opcodeName(op) {
  const map = {
    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x3a: "i32.store8",
    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",
    0x10: "call",
    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x6c: "i32.mul",
    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor",
    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",
    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",
  };

  return map[op] || `0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("EXACT STATE STORE TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGETS : 0x250fb4 / 0x250fc0");
console.log("WASM    :", wasmPath);
console.log("SIZE    :", wasm.length);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let hits = [];

for (let i = 0; i < wasm.length; i++) {
  const op = wasm[i];

  if (op !== 0x36 && op !== 0x3a && op !== 0x28 && op !== 0x2d)
    continue;

  let p = i + 1;

  const align = lebU32(p);
  p = align.next;

  const offset = lebU32(p);
  p = offset.next;

  if (!TARGETS.has(offset.value))
    continue;

  hits.push({
    pos: i,
    op,
    offset: offset.value
  });
}

console.log(`TOTAL TARGET HITS : ${hits.length}`);
console.log("");

for (const h of hits) {
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`WASM OFFSET : 0x${h.pos.toString(16)}`);
  console.log(`OPCODE      : ${opcodeName(h.op)}`);
  console.log(`ADDRESS     : 0x${h.offset.toString(16)}`);

  const start = Math.max(0, h.pos - 18);
  const end = Math.min(wasm.length, h.pos + 28);

  console.log("BYTES:");
  let line = "";

  for (let p = start; p < end; p++) {
    line += wasm[p].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
  }

  console.log(line);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY BY ADDRESS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of TARGETS) {
  const arr = hits.filter(x => x.offset === target);

  console.log(
    `0x${target.toString(16)} : ${arr.length} exact instruction hits`
  );

  for (const h of arr) {
    console.log(
      `  ${opcodeName(h.op).padEnd(14)} @ 0x${h.pos.toString(16)}`
    );
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\traceExactStore4.js

const fs = require("fs");

const FILE = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const IMPORTS = 83;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

function uleb(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;
    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
        s += 7;
    }
    return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sleb(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0, b;
    while (true) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
        s += 7;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    }
    if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        v |= (~0 << s);
    return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function findSection(type) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const t = wasm[p++];

        const z = uleb(p);
        p = z.next;

        if (t === type)
            return { start: p, end: p + z.value };

        p += z.value;
    }

    return null;
}

function decode(start, end) {

    let p = start;

    const lg = uleb(p);
    p = lg.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < lg.value; i++) {
        const n = uleb(p);
        p = n.next;
        p++;
    }

    const a = [];

    while (p < end) {

        const addr = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        let text = `opcode_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`;

        switch (op) {

            case 0x20: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `local.get ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x21: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `local.set ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x22: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `local.tee ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x41: {
                const x = sleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `i32.const ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x28: {
                const al = uleb(p);
                p = al.next;

                const of = uleb(p);
                p = of.next;

                text = `i32.load align=${al.value} offset=${of.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x2d: {
                const al = uleb(p);
                p = al.next;

                const of = uleb(p);
                p = of.next;

                text = `i32.load8_u align=${al.value} offset=${of.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x36: {
                const al = uleb(p);
                p = al.next;

                const of = uleb(p);
                p = of.next;

                text = `i32.store align=${al.value} offset=${of.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x10: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `call ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x11: {
                const t = uleb(p);
                p = t.next;

                const tab = uleb(p);
                p = tab.next;

                text = `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x23: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `global.get ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x24: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `global.set ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x45:
                text = "i32.eqz";
                break;

            case 0x46:
                text = "i32.eq";
                break;

            case 0x47:
                text = "i32.ne";
                break;

            case 0x6a:
                text = "i32.add";
                break;

            case 0x6b:
                text = "i32.sub";
                break;

            case 0x1a:
                text = "drop";
                break;

            case 0x0b:
                text = "end";
                break;
        }

        a.push({ addr, text });
    }

    return a;
}

function getBodies() {

    const sec = findSection(10);

    if (!sec)
        throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

    let p = sec.start;

    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next;

    const bodies = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

        const z = uleb(p);
        p = z.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + z.value;

        bodies.push({
            func: IMPORTS + i,
            start,
            end
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function hx(x) {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8,"0");
}

const bodies = getBodies();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("EXACT i32.store OFFSET=4 ANALYSIS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let count = 0;

for (const b of bodies) {

    const ins = decode(b.start, b.end);

    for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {

        if (
            ins[i].text !== "i32.store align=2 offset=4" &&
            ins[i].text !== "i32.store align=0 offset=4"
        )
            continue;

        count++;

        console.log("");
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(`HIT #${count}`);
        console.log(`FUNC ${b.func}`);
        console.log(`STORE ${hx(ins[i].addr)}`);
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        const from = Math.max(0, i - 7);
        const to = Math.min(ins.length, i + 4);

        for (let j = from; j < to; j++) {

            const mark = j === i ? ">>" : "  ";

            console.log(
                `${mark} ${hx(ins[j].addr)} ${ins[j].text}`
            );
        }

        /*
         * Detect the important pattern:
         *
         * local.get A
         * i32.load offset=4
         * ...
         * i32.store offset=4
         *
         * and:
         *
         * local.get A
         * ...
         * i32.store offset=4
         */

        let baseLocal = null;

        for (let j = i - 1; j >= Math.max(0, i - 8); j--) {

            const m = ins[j].text.match(/^local\.get (\d+)$/);

            if (m) {
                baseLocal = Number(m[1]);
                break;
            }
        }

        if (baseLocal !== null) {
            console.log("NEARBY BASE LOCAL :", baseLocal);
        }

        /*
         * Show whether this is the known decrement pattern.
         */
        const p1 = ins[i - 1]?.text || "";
        const p2 = ins[i - 2]?.text || "";

        if (
            p1 === "i32.sub" &&
            p2 === "i32.const 1"
        ) {
            console.log("CLASSIFICATION : DECREMENT");
        } else {
            console.log("CLASSIFICATION : NON-DECREMENT");
        }
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("EXACT OFFSET=4 STORES :", count);

console.log("NETWORK               : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS          : NO");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from 'fs';

const WASM_PATH =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

const START = 61125;
const SIZE = 127;
const END = START + SIZE;

function readU32(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readI32(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                value |= (~0 << shift);
            }

            return { value, next: pos };
        }
    }
}

function skipBlockType(pos: number) {
    const b = wasm[pos];

    if (
        b === 0x40 ||
        b === 0x7f ||
        b === 0x7e ||
        b === 0x7d ||
        b === 0x7c ||
        b === 0x7b ||
        b === 0x70 ||
        b === 0x6f
    ) {
        return pos + 1;
    }

    return readI32(pos).next;
}

function decode(pos: number) {
    const start = pos;
    const op = wasm[pos++];

    let text = `0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')}`;

    switch (op) {

        case 0x20: {
            const r = readU32(pos);
            text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
            pos = r.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const r = readU32(pos);
            text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
            pos = r.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const r = readU32(pos);
            text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
            pos = r.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const r = readI32(pos);
            text = `i32.const ${r.value}`;
            pos = r.next;
            break;
        }

        case 0x28: {
            const align = readU32(pos);
            pos = align.next;

            const offset = readU32(pos);
            pos = offset.next;

            text = `i32.load align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const r = readU32(pos);
            pos = r.next;
            text = `call ${r.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const type = readU32(pos);
            pos = type.next;

            const table = readU32(pos);
            pos = table.next;

            text =
                `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x47:
            text = 'i32.gt_u';
            break;

        case 0x45:
            text = 'i32.eqz';
            break;

        case 0x0d: {
            const r = readU32(pos);
            pos = r.next;
            text = `br_if ${r.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x0c: {
            const r = readU32(pos);
            pos = r.next;
            text = `br ${r.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x0e: {
            const n = readU32(pos);
            pos = n.next;

            const labels: number[] = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
                const x = readU32(pos);
                pos = x.next;
                labels.push(x.value);
            }

            const def = readU32(pos);
            pos = def.next;

            text =
                `br_table [${labels.join(',')}] default=${def.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x04:
            pos = skipBlockType(pos);
            text = 'if';
            break;

        case 0x05:
            text = 'else';
            break;

        case 0x0b:
            text = 'end';
            break;
    }

    return {
        start,
        end: pos,
        text,
        op,
    };
}

console.log('');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log(' F#122 LOAD / INDIRECT TRACE v22');
console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE WASM');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log('');

console.log(`Function : F#122`);
console.log(`Start    : ${START}`);
console.log(`End      : ${END}`);
console.log('');

let p = START;

// Skip local declaration vector
const localGroups = readU32(p);
p = localGroups.next;

console.log(`local groups: ${localGroups.value}`);

for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
    const count = readU32(p);
    p = count.next;

    const type = wasm[p++];

    console.log(
        `  locals group ${i}: count=${count.value} type=0x${type.toString(16)}`
    );
}

console.log('');
console.log('Instructions:');
console.log('');

while (p < END) {
    const x = decode(p);

    console.log(
        `${x.start.toString().padStart(7)}  ${x.text}`
    );

    p = x.end;

    if (x.op === 0x0b && p >= END) {
        break;
    }
}

console.log('============================================================');

FILE: scripts\traceF176V22.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

function readULEB(
    data: Uint8Array,
    pos: { v: number }
): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = data[pos.v++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("Invalid ULEB128");
        }
    }
}

function hex(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
    return Buffer.from(bytes)
        .toString("hex")
        .match(/.{1,2}/g)
        ?.join(" ") ?? "";
}

function u32le(bytes: Uint8Array): number {
    return (
        bytes[0] |
        (bytes[1] << 8) |
        (bytes[2] << 16) |
        (bytes[3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function readVecBytes(
    data: Uint8Array,
    pos: { v: number }
): Uint8Array {
    const len = readULEB(data, pos);
    const start = pos.v;
    pos.v += len;

    return data.subarray(start, start + len);
}

function decodeDataSection(
    data: Uint8Array,
    sectionStart: number,
    sectionSize: number
) {
    const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sectionSize;

    const pos = {
        v: sectionStart,
    };

    const count = readULEB(data, pos);

    console.log(`Data segments : ${count}`);

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const flags = readULEB(data, pos);

        let memoryIndex = 0;
        let offsetValue: number | null = null;

        /*
         * flags:
         *
         * 0 = active, memory 0, offset expr
         * 1 = passive
         * 2 = active, explicit memory index
         */

        if (flags === 0) {
            const opcode = data[pos.v++];

            if (opcode !== 0x41) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Unexpected offset opcode 0x${opcode.toString(16)} at ${pos.v - 1}`
                );
            }

            offsetValue = readULEB(data, pos);

            const endOpcode = data[pos.v++];

            if (endOpcode !== 0x0b) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Unexpected end opcode 0x${endOpcode.toString(16)}`
                );
            }
        } else if (flags === 1) {
            /*
             * passive segment
             */
        } else if (flags === 2) {
            memoryIndex = readULEB(data, pos);

            const opcode = data[pos.v++];

            if (opcode !== 0x41) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Unexpected offset opcode 0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
                );
            }

            offsetValue = readULEB(data, pos);

            const endOpcode = data[pos.v++];

            if (endOpcode !== 0x0b) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Unexpected end opcode 0x${endOpcode.toString(16)}`
                );
            }
        } else {
            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported data segment flags ${flags}`
            );
        }

        const segmentData = readVecBytes(data, pos);

        if (i === 669) {
            console.log("\n============================================================");
            console.log(" DATA SEGMENT 669");
            console.log("============================================================");

            console.log(`segment       : ${i}`);
            console.log(`flags         : ${flags}`);
            console.log(`memory index  : ${memoryIndex}`);
            console.log(
                `segment offset: ${offsetValue ?? "passive"}`
            );

            if (offsetValue !== null) {
                console.log(
                    `offset hex    : 0x${offsetValue
                        .toString(16)
                        .toUpperCase()}`
                );
            }

            console.log(
                `data size     : ${segmentData.length}`
            );

            console.log("\nRAW DATA:");

            console.log(hex(segmentData));

            console.log("\nMEMORY MAP:");

            if (offsetValue !== null) {
                for (let j = 0; j < segmentData.length; j++) {
                    const addr = offsetValue + j;

                    const mark =
                        addr === 1_659_724
                            ? " <<< TARGET"
                            : "";

                    console.log(
                        `addr=${addr
                            .toString()
                            .padStart(9, " ")} ` +
                        `0x${addr
                            .toString(16)
                            .padStart(8, "0")} ` +
                        `byte=0x${segmentData[j]
                            .toString(16)
                            .padStart(2, "0")}${mark}`
                    );
                }
            }

            console.log("\n32-BIT VALUES:");

            for (
                let j = 0;
                j + 4 <= segmentData.length;
                j += 4
            ) {
                const value = u32le(
                    segmentData.subarray(j, j + 4)
                );

                console.log(
                    `relative=${j
                        .toString()
                        .padStart(3, " ")} ` +
                    `addr=0x${(
                        (offsetValue ?? 0) + j
                    )
                        .toString(16)
                        .padStart(8, "0")} ` +
                    `value=${value} ` +
                    `0x${value
                        .toString(16)
                        .padStart(8, "0")}`
                );
            }

            console.log("\nNEXT SEGMENTS:");

            for (
                let k = i + 1;
                k < Math.min(i + 5, count);
                k++
            ) {
                /*
                 * We cannot jump here without parsing,
                 * so this is intentionally only a marker.
                 */
            }
        }
    }

    console.log(
        `\nData section parser ended at ${pos.v}, expected ${sectionEnd}`
    );
}

async function main() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" WASM DATA SEGMENT 669 TRACE v22");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    if (!fs.existsSync(WASM)) {
        throw new Error(`WASM not found:\n${WASM}`);
    }

    const wasm = new Uint8Array(
        fs.readFileSync(WASM)
    );

    console.log(`\nWASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);

    /*
     * Parse WASM sections.
     */

    let p = 8;

    let dataSectionStart = -1;
    let dataSectionSize = -1;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const sectionId = wasm[p++];

        const pos = {
            v: p,
        };

        const sectionSize = readULEB(wasm, pos);

        const payloadStart = pos.v;
        const payloadEnd =
            payloadStart + sectionSize;

        console.log(
            `section id=${sectionId} ` +
            `offset=${payloadStart} ` +
            `size=${sectionSize}`
        );

        if (sectionId === 11) {
            dataSectionStart = payloadStart;
            dataSectionSize = sectionSize;
            break;
        }

        p = payloadEnd;
    }

    if (dataSectionStart < 0) {
        throw new Error("WASM Data section not found");
    }

    console.log("\n============================================================");
    console.log(" DATA SECTION");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log(
        `offset : ${dataSectionStart}`
    );

    console.log(
        `size   : ${dataSectionSize}`
    );

    console.log(
        `end    : ${dataSectionStart + dataSectionSize}`
    );

    decodeDataSection(
        wasm,
        dataSectionStart,
        dataSectionSize
    );

    console.log("\n============================================================");
    console.log(" DONE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log("READ ONLY: no TON connection");
    console.log("READ ONLY: no wallet");
    console.log("READ ONLY: no transaction");
    console.log("READ ONLY: no signing");
}

main().catch(err => {
    console.error("\nERROR:");
    console.error(err);
    process.exit(1);
});

FILE: scripts\traceFamily8460.ts

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = new Set([8459, 8460, 8461, 8462, 8465, 8466, 8467, 8468, 8479, 8480]);

let p = 8;

function u(): number {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x: number;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return v >>> 0;
}

let codeStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const size = u();
  const end = p + size;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

p = codeStart;
const count = u();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 8459..8480 MEMORY SELECTOR TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let f = 0; f < count; f++) {
  const bodySize = u();
  const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

  const groups = u();

  for (let g = 0; g < groups; g++) {
    u();
    p++;
  }

  const func = f + 91;

  if (!TARGETS.has(func)) {
    p = bodyEnd;
    continue;
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("############################################################");
  console.log("FUNC", func);
  console.log("BODY START 0x" + (bodyEnd - bodySize).toString(16));
  console.log("BODY END   0x" + bodyEnd.toString(16));
  console.log("############################################################");

  const start = Math.max(codeStart, bodyEnd - bodySize);
  const end = bodyEnd;

  // Print raw bytes, compactly.
  for (let q = start; q < end; q += 16) {
    const z = Math.min(end, q + 16);

    console.log(
      "0x" + q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
      [...b.slice(q, z)]
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ")
    );
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
